16.I.60.8 Full-E8 quasi-linear spectrum, moving-normal stabilizer, and D8 recurrence.


The quasi-linear branch can now be promoted beyond its P/K compression. Let


Y_mu=P_mu-K_mu,
W_-=eta^{mu nu}Y_mu odot Y_nu,
E_0=S(W_-)-600 e_A,


with transported seed


E(w)=exp[-(w/2) ad D] E_0.


Because the Chayet-Garibaldi map S is E8-equivariant, the transport is similarity on the Killing-raised endomorphism. Therefore the full spectrum, rank, and nullity are independent of w. Define


T_w:=G^{-1} Kcal_w=-(1/480)E(w),


where Kcal_w=-(1/480)G E(w) is the covariant bilinear carrier.


Using the earned branching


248=(45,1)+(1,15)+(10,6)+(16,4)+(bar16,bar4)


under Spin(10) x Spin(4,2), W_- acts only through the conformal factor. The exact spectrum is


spec S(W_-)={0^(55),120^(172),240^(6),480^(11),600^(4)}.


The multiplicities sum to 248. The independent trace check is


120(172)+240(6)+480(11)+600(4)=29760,


in agreement with the Chayet-Garibaldi trace identity Tr S(W)=(h^vee+1)K(W)=31K(W) and the present normalization K(W_-)=960.


Hence


spec E_0={-600^(55),-480^(172),-360^(6),-120^(11),0^(4)},


and therefore


boxed{spec T_w={0^(4),(1/4)^(11),(3/4)^(6),1^(172),(5/4)^(55)}}.


Thus


boxed{rank T_w=244,  nullity T_w=4}


for every w. There are no accidental transverse zero modes. The nonzero generalized spectrum is separated from zero by a gap 1/4. In addition,


Tr T_w=248,


so the generalized mean eigenvalue is exactly one, consistent with a unit plus traceless-3875 carrier.


The D8 geometry makes the eigenspaces transparent. Split the earned so(12,4) vector space as


V_{12,4}=I_{10} direct-sum L_{1,3} direct-sum <a,b>,


with


Y_mu=2 L_mu wedge a,
X_mu=2 L_mu wedge b,
D=a wedge b,


and choose the real-form signs so that a is spacelike and b timelike. Set


U_{10,1}=I_{10} direct-sum <b>.


Then on the bosonic adjoint 120=Lambda^2 V,


lambda=0:      L wedge a, dim 4,
lambda=1/4:    U wedge a, dim 11,
lambda=3/4:    Lambda^2 L, dim 6,
lambda=1:      U tensor L, dim 44,
lambda=5/4:    Lambda^2 U, dim 55,


while the 128-dimensional spinor complement carries lambda=1. This reproduces the full multiplicity 172 at eigenvalue one as 44+128.


At the seed,


ker T_0=span{P_mu-K_mu}.


Under rapidity transport,


ker T_w=span{N_mu(w)},
N_mu(w)=e^{-w/2}P_mu-e^{w/2}K_mu,


for the epsilon_P=+ orientation, while the retained neutral generalized eigenvector is


E_mu(w)=e^{-w/2}P_mu+e^{w/2}K_mu.


Equivalently, rapidity is a boost of the two conformal normals,


a(w)=cosh(w/2)a-sinh(w/2)b,
b(w)=cosh(w/2)b-sinh(w/2)a,


so that


ker T_w=L wedge a(w),


whereas the retained neutral coframe lies in L wedge b(w). The quasi-linear carrier is therefore a moving-normal coframe selector: it annihilates one conformal normal and retains the orthogonal one.


The carrier alone has an accidental continuous stabilizer of Spin(10,1) x Spin(1,3)-type. This follows from the distinct bosonic eigenspaces above: the 44-dimensional lambda=1 space is U tensor L and contains both the retained neutral coframe and the 40 mixed (10,4) vectors as one 11 x 4 multiplet. The already-earned dilation generator removes this accidental enlargement. Since D=a wedge b singles out b inside U_{10,1},


boxed{stab(T_w,D)=so(10)+so(1,3)}.


Thus the pair (T_w,D) has exactly the desired continuous residual Spin(10) x Spin(3,1) algebra at the representation level. No additional selector is required to distinguish the neutral coframe from the 40 mixed vectors. This is an algebraic stabilizer statement only; action-level vacuum selection remains open.


The D8 mixed sector is not a separate mechanism. For each i=1,...,10, let U_i^a=e_i wedge a and U_i^b=e_i wedge b. In the D-grade basis U_i^+,U_i^- the transported block is


T_U(w)=[[3/4,(1/2)e^{-w}],[(1/2)e^w,3/4]],


whereas the physical P/K block is


T_PK(w)=[[1/2,(1/2)e^{-w}],[(1/2)e^w,1/2]].


Therefore


boxed{T_U(w)=T_PK(w)+(1/4)I}.


The same rapidity transport recurs in every mixed D8 pair, shifted by exactly one quarter, with eigenvalues 1/4 and 5/4. These are precisely the mixed generators whose opposite D grades satisfy


P_D[U_i^+,U_j^-]=kappa_D delta_ij D.


Hence the earlier non-Abelian D8 cohomology repair and the quasi-linear coframe carrier are two blocks of the same transported E8 operator. The same D transport that moves the physical coframe also moves the ten mixed pairs whose bracket sources the D curvature.


The exact kernel projector is the spectral polynomial


Pi_0(T)=((T-I)(4T-5I)(4T-3I)(4T-I))/15.


It obeys Pi_0^2=Pi_0 and T Pi_0=0 and is transported covariantly with T_w. For the matched-kernel first-order candidate


S=integral[beta B^T Kcal_w F-(alpha/2)B^T Kcal_w B],


there is therefore an exact local algebraic null-shift redundancy


delta B=Pi_0(T_w)Lambda.


The B equation


Kcal_w(beta F-alpha B)=0


determines B on im Kcal_w and leaves only the Pi_0 component arbitrary. Modulo the null shift,


B_im=(beta/alpha)F_im,


and elimination gives


S_eff=(beta^2/(2alpha)) integral F^T Kcal_w F.


No inverse of Kcal_w is required. The finite-invertible-heavy determinant no-go remains exact; this branch lies outside its hypotheses because the heavy quadratic form is singular and shares the same kernel as the BF coupling. The precise escape mechanism is therefore


boxed{singular matched kernel + quasi-linear transport},


not a failure of the determinant theorem.


At the principal-symbol level, the null P-K combination does not acquire an independent high-frequency kinetic channel from w(x)-dependence: derivatives of w and of the moving projectors enter below the highest derivative on the connection. The four-dimensional null bundle can nevertheless re-enter through the full non-Abelian curvature. With X_mu=2L_mu wedge b and Y_mu=2L_mu wedge a,


[X_mu,X_nu] subset M_{mu nu},
[Y_mu,Y_nu] subset M_{mu nu},
[X_mu,Y_nu] subset eta_{mu nu}D,


and the M and D sectors have nonzero carrier eigenvalues 3/4 and 1/4. This identifies the nonlinear/constraint couplings erased by the fixed linear P/K truncation.


However, the present calculation does not yet prove that the null co-soldering field is completely auxiliary. Around a nondegenerate retained coframe e^mu, write f^mu=f^mu{}_nu e^nu for the Y-sector. Then


e_mu wedge f^mu=2f_[mu nu] e^mu wedge e^nu,


and


(e_mu wedge f^mu)^2=-e_mu wedge e_nu wedge f^mu wedge f^nu.


Therefore the purely algebraic M/D terms control the antisymmetric part f_[mu nu], but they do not by themselves supply a quadratic Hessian for the ten symmetric components f_(mu nu). The exact present result is therefore


boxed{no second Y principal kinetic channel},


not yet


boxed{Y fully auxiliary}.


The next live calculation is correspondingly narrow: determine whether the already-required D8 mixed activation and full BRST/constraint system remove or lift f_(mu nu), or whether those ten symmetric components remain strongly coupled. A4 now has a representation/principal-symbol pass for uniqueness of the neutral coframe direction, A2 has a partial pass from the exact covariant B-null-shift symmetry, and A5 has an exact transverse spectrum with no extra zero modes; healthy action-level single-spin-2 closure remains open.




§16.I.60.9 — D4 Trace-Pair Response Closure


On the scalar-null physical branch define the quasi-linear carrier


Khat_w=-(1/480)G E(w).


On the P/K label plane,


Khat_w=(1/2)[[e^w,epsilon_P],[epsilon_P,e^(-w)]]
       =(1/2)(A I+B Z+E X),


with


A=cosh w,
B=sinh w,
E=epsilon_P,
A^2-B^2=1,
E^2=1.


The two coordinates of the already-earned D4 polarization carrier are reconstructed from the same quasi-linear object:


Pi_C=2AB/(A^2+B^2)=tanh(2w)=tanh zeta,


Xi_C=2AE/(A^2+E^2)=4 epsilon_P cosh w/(cosh zeta+3).


Equivalently, using K:=Khat_w and R in {Z,X},


R_R[K]:=2 tr(R K^2)/[tr(K^2)+tr(K R K R)],


so that


R_Z[K]=Pi_C,
R_X[K]=Xi_C.


The apparently special denominator satisfies the exact identity


cosh zeta+3=2[tr(K^2)+tr(K X K X)],


so it is not a new microscopic eigenvalue. It is a reflected quadratic norm of the same transported rank-one carrier. The already-earned D4 quarter-turn locks the two response-channel normalizations equal. A reduced local polynomial auxiliary Hessian can generate these rational responses, but derivation of that Hessian from the complete microscopic BF/PCBF parent remains open.


The exact even norm may therefore be written either as


N_C^2=Pi_C^2+Xi_C^2


or, with c=sqrt(1-Pi_C^2)=sech zeta,


N_C^2=((1+c)^2(1+12c-9c^2))/(1+3c)^2.


The earlier naive full-E8 quadratic X/Z norm uplift remains a NO-GO: the trace-pair reconstruction does not identify the inequivalently normalized 1820 and 135 directions with one inherited Euclidean norm.


§16.I.60.10 — PhiBB Plus Purely Radial Stabilization No-Go


For fixed topological sector n, the admitted filtered PhiBB contribution has reduced angular dependence


S_PhiBB=-(12/7)g_PhiBB n mu cosh zeta,


up to the fixed action/orientation convention. Hence the most general potential obtained by adding an arbitrary purely radial term V_0(mu) has the form


V(mu,zeta)=V_0(mu)-A mu cosh zeta,


with A=(12/7)g_PhiBB n after absorbing the overall sign. Then


partial_zeta V=-A mu sinh zeta.


For A nonzero and activated mu>0, every stationary point has zeta=0. Eliminating mu by its own stationarity equation does not evade this result: the on-shell envelope derivative remains -A mu_*(zeta)sinh zeta. Therefore


PhiBB linear overlap + arbitrary purely radial stabilization


cannot generate the finite mirror-related D4 vacua.


For comparison, if an independent microscopic coefficient kappa_N>0 multiplying -N_C^2 is ever earned, the already-established D4 geometry fixes


V''(0)=-2 kappa_N,


and at the selected orbit


cosh zeta_*=3,
V''(±zeta_*)=(16/27)kappa_N>0.


Thus the remaining problem is the microscopic origin of the D4 angular response, not its reduced stability analysis.


§16.I.60.11 — Exact Full-Bosonic Exchange Reflection and Chiral Obstruction


To avoid a notation collision, define


C_Y:=eta^{mu nu}rho(Y_mu)rho(Y_nu),
Y_mu=P_mu-K_mu,


for the conformal quadratic operator, while reserving Q_PD for the later product-duality involution on the scalar-heavy sector.


The complete E8-adjoint spectrum is


spec(C_Y)={0^55,4^172,8^6,12^4,16^11}.


The 12-eigenspace is exactly the four-dimensional Y_mu sector. On the D8 bosonic 120=Lambda^2 V_{12,4}, the 12-eigenspace is L wedge a and the 16-eigenspace is


(I_10 wedge a) direct-sum <a wedge b>.


Together they are exactly


a wedge a^perp,


dimension 15. Hence the spectral sign reversal on the 12 and 16 eigenspaces is precisely the normal reflection a->-a on the full bosonic 120. It gives


Y_mu->-Y_mu,
X_mu->X_mu,
D->-D,
P_mu<->K_mu.


A pure reflection of only the 12-eigenspace is not bracket-equivariant because [Y,X] is proportional to D. The completed bosonic reflection repairs that defect.


However, a bare one-normal reflection is odd in the D8 Clifford algebra and exchanges chirality. Therefore its naive extension by the identity on the chiral 128 is NOT a full-E8 automorphism. This is the exact chiral obstruction.


§16.I.60.12 — Compensated Spin(12,4) Full-E8 Exchange Involution


The chiral obstruction is removed by pairing the conformal normal reflection with an orientation-reversing internal Pin(I_10) transformation.


Let r_a reflect the Lorentzian normal a. Choose an internal compensator r_I built from an odd number of mutually orthogonal internal unit reflections, and define


g_X=r_I r_a.


Because the total number of reflected directions is even, g_X has even Clifford parity and lies in Spin(12,4), acting within the same real chiral spin module S^+. The compensator can be chosen so the induced action is involutive: one internal reflection suffices when the a norm has the opposite sign to an internal positive unit vector; otherwise three mutually orthogonal internal positive reflections give an even four-vector Clifford product with square +1. In either case the induced adjoint action is an involution.


On the physical conformal sector,


a->-a,
b->b,
L->L,
Y_mu->-Y_mu,
X_mu->X_mu,
D->-D,
P_mu<->K_mu.


On the exact branching


248=(45,1) direct-sum (1,15) direct-sum (10,6) direct-sum (16,bar4) direct-sum (bar16,4),


the conformal orientation reversal exchanges 4<->bar4, while the internal orientation reversal exchanges 16<->bar16. Their simultaneous action therefore exchanges


(16,bar4)<->(bar16,4)


inside the actual E8 adjoint content rather than leaving the chiral 128.


Therefore:


PASS — the reduced P_mu<->K_mu exchange has a genuine full-E8(-24) representation/group-level involutive lift by a compensated Spin(12,4) element.


NO-GO RETAINED — the bare one-normal reflection, or the full-bosonic reflection extended trivially on the 128, is not a full-E8 automorphism.


OPEN — invariance of one complete microscopic action and selected vacuum under this compensated involution is not yet proved.


OPEN — the exact relation between this compensated exchange involution and the independently earned scalar-heavy product-duality involution


Q_PD=(*_10) tensor (E_N H_N)


remains to be derived. Do not identify the two merely because both induce a Y reflection on their certified sectors.


OPEN — quantum/discrete-anomaly consistency, the independent Hermitian 30380 contact, nonzero radial/positive-degree activation, BRST survival, N_4>0, and healthy single-spin-2 closure remain downstream.

§16.I.59 — Quartic 135 Necessity-and-Existence and the qSaw Weight Bridge
The direct microscopic square remains subject to the representation no-go: the symmetric square of the chiral SO(16) spinor supplies 1⊕1820⊕6435 and therefore contains no direct 135 channel. This does not obstruct the next even rung. Writing V=R^16, the standard identifications are 1820≅Λ^4V and 135≅Sym^2_0V. For F,G∈Λ^4V define


Q(F,G)_{ab}=F_{(a|cde|}G_{b)}{}^{cde}-(1/16)g_{ab}F_{cdef}G^{cdef}.


This map is symmetric in F,G, symmetric and traceless in a,b, SO(16)-equivariant, and nonzero. Hence 135 occurs in Sym^2(1820). Therefore, among even pair-built polynomial routes, the first available 135 after the degree-two obstruction occurs at degree four:


128 →[square] 1820 →[Q] 135⊂3875,


with primitive polynomial degree ladder 1→2→4.


For a qSaw/rapidity-generated 1820 response
F(w)=e^wF_+ + ε_P F_0 + e^{-w}F_-,
the quartic 135 response H_135(w)=Q(F(w),F(w)) expands as


H_135(w)=H_{+2}e^{2w}+ε_P H_{+1}e^w+H_0+ε_P H_{-1}e^{-w}+H_{-2}e^{-2w},


where
H_{+2}=Q(F_+,F_+),
H_{+1}=2Q(F_+,F_0),
H_0=2Q(F_+,F_-)+Q(F_0,F_0),
H_{-1}=2Q(F_0,F_-),
H_{-2}=Q(F_-,F_-).


Thus the microscopic quartic 135 carries the exact rapidity-weight spectrum (+2,+1,0,-1,-2), matching the reduced binary-quartic/conic-defect weight spectrum. This is a structural match, not yet an identification of the five-dimensional reduced defect with the E8 30380 or with the 135 itself.


If Z_ID denotes the physical relative internal/dilation direction inside 135⊂3875, then the scalar physical response
h(w)=<Z_ID,H_135(w)>
is determined by five coefficients h_{+2},h_{+1},h_0,h_{-1},h_{-2}. The decisive comparison with the reduced conic defect is coefficient-vector proportionality after one fixed invariant normalization; weight agreement alone is insufficient.


The remaining E8 factorization gate is whether the explicit SO(16) quartic contraction is the physical 135 projection of the already-earned bracket-recoupled E8 map. On the qSaw-generated physical corridor, the required statement is


π_135 ∘ Φ ∘ J = c Q,


for some nonzero normalization c, where J is the still-to-be-derived embedding/recoupling from the relevant symmetric 1820-pair data into the E8 source feeding the live 30380→3875 map Φ. OPEN — comparison of the five physical quartic coefficients with the reduced conic-defect coefficients under one fixed normalization.


§16.I.60 — SO(16) Quartic-135 Uniqueness and Restricted E8 Factorization Gate


The §16.I.59 existence theorem can be strengthened to uniqueness on the full SO(16)-covariant 1820-pair domain:


dim Hom_SO(16)(Sym^2(1820),135)=1.


Proof. Identify 1820≅Λ^4V and 135≅Sym^2_0V for V=R^16. An equivariant bilinear map Λ^4V×Λ^4V→Sym^2V corresponds to an SO(16)-invariant tensor with ten natural-vector indices: four from each input and two from the output. Since 10<16, the orientation tensor ε_16 cannot contribute; the relevant invariant tensors are generated by metric pairings. Antisymmetry eliminates every pairing that contracts two indices belonging to the same four-form. If both free output indices are taken from the same four-form, the remaining indices force such a forbidden self-contraction and the term vanishes. Therefore every nonzero metric contraction with two free output indices takes one free index from F and one from G, and pairs the remaining three indices of F with the remaining three indices of G. The 3! cross-pairings differ only by the antisymmetry signs and span one tensor structure,


S(F,G)_{ab}=F_{(a|cde|}G_{b)}{}^{cde}.


The only additional symmetric two-tensor structure is the pure trace g_ab<F,G>. Projecting Sym^2V→Sym^2_0V removes that trace and leaves, up to scale, precisely


Q(F,G)_{ab}=F_{(a|cde|}G_{b)}{}^{cde}-(1/16)g_ab F_cdef G^{cdef}.


Since §16.I.59 already gives a nonzero witness for Q, the Hom space is exactly one-dimensional.


Corollary — restricted proportionality. Let R:Sym^2(1820)→135 be any SO(16)-equivariant linear map on the symmetric pair input. Then either R=0 or


R=cQ


for one nonzero scalar c.


This sharply reduces the live E8 factorization gate. Book 16 independently has


dim Hom_E8(Sym^2(30380),3875)=2,


with the raw-square E8 direction carrying zero physical 135 response and the bracket-recoupled direction carrying a genuinely nonzero 135/Z_ID source. Therefore no uniqueness is claimed at the full E8 30380-pair level. The uniqueness arises only after a lawful SO(16)-equivariant restriction from the 1820-pair domain and projection to the 135 output.


Let J denote the still-open microscopic recoupling from Sym^2(1820) into the source domain on which the live bracket-recoupled E8 map Φ_2 acts, and define


R=π_135∘Φ_2∘J.


Because Φ_2 is E8-equivariant, hence SO(16)-equivariant, and π_135 is SO(16)-equivariant, a fully SO(16)-equivariant J makes R automatically SO(16)-equivariant. The factorization claim


π_135∘Φ_2∘J=cQ


then requires only one further substantive test: R must be nonzero. A single physical witness F_* with


π_135 Φ_2 J(F_*,F_*)≠0


would force proportionality on the entire Sym^2(1820) domain. Five independent rapidity-coefficient comparisons would then become normalization/consistency checks rather than the primary proof.


Caveat — symmetry breaking. If J is defined only after a background or vacuum choice that reduces SO(16) to a proper subgroup, the one-dimensional Hom theorem cannot be invoked automatically. In that case the restricted subgroup Hom multiplicity must be computed, or the original five-coefficient comparison must be retained. Likewise, the already-known nonzero 135 response of Φ_2 on some 30380 state does not prove nonvanishing on im J; the witness must lie in the actual 1820-pair image.


Gate ledger update:
PASS — dim Hom_SO(16)(Sym^2(1820),135)=1.
PASS — Q is the unique nonzero SO(16)-equivariant symmetric quartic 135 map up to scale.
PASS — if J is SO(16)-equivariant, equivariance of π_135Φ_2J is automatic.
REDUCED OPEN GATE — construct the physical J and show one nonzero 135 witness in im J.
FALLBACK — if J breaks SO(16), compute the subgroup multiplicity or retain the five-coefficient test.


§16.I.61 — 1820→30380 Linear No-Go and Common-Parent Bifurcation
The §16.I.60 factorization diagram requires a degree and representation correction. Under SO(16), the E8 antisymmetric-pair representation branches as


30380 = 120 ⊕ 1920 ⊕ 7020 ⊕ 8008 ⊕ 13312.


In particular, 1820 is absent. Therefore


Hom_SO(16)(1820,30380)=0.


There is no lawful SO(16)-equivariant linear embedding of the first symmetric spinor-square 1820 into the live 30380 condensate. The open map J in §16.I.60 must not be interpreted as such an embedding.


The correct first-square structure is bifurcated. For a chiral 128 spinor,


Sym^2(128)=1⊕1820⊕6435,
Λ^2(128)=120⊕8008.


Meanwhile E8 gives


248↓SO(16)=120⊕128,
Λ^2(248)=248⊕30380.


Because the 248 contains a 120 but no 8008, the 8008 component of Λ^2(128) cannot lie in the adjoint 248 summand of Λ^2(248); it lies canonically in the 30380. Denote this SO(16)-equivariant inclusion by


j_8008:8008→30380.


Thus the primitive spinor-pair square has two distinct degree-two descendants relevant to the present problem. For an ordered microscopic pair tensor T=ψ⊗φ, define


F(T)=P_1820 Sym(T) ∈1820,
C(T)=j_8008 P_8008 Alt(T) ∈30380.


Both F and C are quadratic in the primitive spinor amplitudes, but they are different symmetry projections of the same parent. The quartic 135 responses are then


H_sym(T)=Q(F(T),F(T)) ∈135,
H_E8(T)=π_135 Φ_2(C(T),C(T)) ∈135.


The corrected physical bridge question is not whether 1820 embeds into 30380. It is whether the two quartic descendants of the same microscopic parent agree up to one invariant normalization on the physical qSaw/rapidity locus:


π_135 Φ_2(C(T),C(T)) = c Q(F(T),F(T)),


with c≠0 and T restricted to the actual physical pair domain.


Degree audit. F(T) and C(T) are both degree two in the primitive spinors; both H_sym and H_E8 are degree four. Hence the common-parent bifurcation preserves the earned polynomial ladder 1→2→4. By contrast, any attempt to construct C quadratically from F would make C degree four and Φ_2(C,C) degree eight, producing an octic mismatch with the quartic Q(F,F) route in the homogeneous polynomial lift. Projective normalizations can change displayed rational degree but do not remove the underlying homogeneous mismatch.


Fermionic caveat. If one replaces the ordered microscopic pair by an ordinary commuting rank-one tensor T=ψ⊗ψ, then Alt(T)=0 and the 8008 branch vanishes identically. Therefore the live 8008/30380 route requires genuine two-fermion exchange structure: two ordered spinors, Grassmann antisymmetry, or an equivalent microscopic construction. The two-component qSaw Veronese projector χχ^T by itself is only the reduced symmetric/projective carrier and must not be identified with the full antisymmetric spinor-pair condensate.


This relocates the microscopic bottleneck. The required calculation is now:
(1) specify the genuine ordered/fermionic parent T(w) carrying the qSaw/rapidity data;
(2) compute both F(w)=P_1820 Sym T(w) and C(w)=j_8008P_8008 Alt T(w);
(3) verify C(w)≠0 on the physical corridor;
(4) compare the two quartic 135 descendants under one fixed normalization.


Gate ledger update:
NO-GO — Hom_SO(16)(1820,30380)=0; no linear 1820→30380 bridge.
PASS — 8008 is the canonical antisymmetric spinor-pair branch inside 30380.
PASS — symmetric 1820 and antisymmetric 8008 are sibling degree-two channels of one ordered spinor-pair parent.
PASS — both candidate 135 mechanisms remain quartic in primitive spinor amplitudes.
OPEN — microscopic ordered/Grassmann parent T(w).
OPEN — common-parent Fierz/recoupling identity relating H_E8 and H_sym.
OPEN — nonzero physical 8008 witness on the qSaw/rapidity corridor.


§16.I.62 — 8008 Quartic-135 Uniqueness and Six-Form Reduction of the Live E8 Route


The physical 8008 branch admits the same representation-level uniqueness simplification as the 1820 branch. Identify 8008≅Λ^6V and 135≅Sym^2_0V for V=R^16. Then


dim Hom_SO(16)(Sym^2(8008),135)=1.


Proof. An SO(16)-equivariant symmetric bilinear map Λ^6V×Λ^6V→Sym^2V corresponds to an invariant tensor with fourteen natural-vector indices. An ε_16 contribution cannot survive: two orientation indices would remain and contraction of those two antisymmetric indices with the symmetric metric vanishes. Hence the relevant invariants are generated by metric pairings. Antisymmetry again forces one free output index to come from each six-form and pairs the remaining five indices crosswise. All 5! such contractions span one tensor structure up to sign. After removing the pure trace, the unique nonzero candidate is


Q_6(C,D)_{ab}=C_{(a|i2…i6|}D_{b)}{}^{i2…i6}-(1/16)g_{ab}C_{i1…i6}D^{i1…i6}.


A simple six-form witness shows Q_6 is nonzero, so the Hom space is exactly one-dimensional.


Now restrict the already-earned bracket-recoupled E8 map Φ_2 to the canonical 8008⊂30380 and project its output to 135⊂3875. Define


R_6(C,D)=π_135 Φ_2(j_8008 C,j_8008 D).


Book 16 independently establishes that Φ_2 has a genuinely nonzero 135/Z_ID response on the physical 8008 branch. Therefore R_6 is a nonzero SO(16)-equivariant symmetric bilinear map Sym^2(8008)→135. By uniqueness,


R_6=c_6 Q_6


for one nonzero normalization constant c_6.


Thus the representation shape of the live E8 135 response on the 8008 branch is closed: up to normalization, it is the traceless six-form contraction. No further E8 Clebsch search is required merely to determine its SO(16) tensor form.


Combining with §16.I.60, the common-parent bridge is now reduced to a comparison of two explicit tensor covariants:


c_6 Q_6(C(T),C(T)) ?= c_4 Q_4(F(T),F(T)),


where F(T)=P_1820 Sym T and C(T)=P_8008 Alt T for the same microscopic ordered/fermionic parent T. Each side is unique within its own representation channel; what remains open is the Fierz/common-parent relation between the four-form and six-form bilinears on the physical qSaw/rapidity locus.


Gate ledger update:
PASS — dim Hom_SO(16)(Sym^2(8008),135)=1.
PASS — the live nonzero E8 8008→135 quartic response is proportional to Q_6.
PASS — the E8-side tensor shape is fixed up to one scalar normalization.
OPEN — the common-parent relation between Q_6(C,C) and Q_4(F,F).
OPEN — the microscopic qSaw/rapidity parent that makes the 8008 branch nonzero while retaining the required three first-square weights.


§16.I.63 — Conditional Two-Frame Fermion Veronese Lift and Three-State Microscopic Reconstruction


The common-parent gate admits a minimal qSaw-compatible microscopic ansatz. This section is a conditional construction, not yet a derived dynamical identification.


Let U≅R^2 be the qSaw projective doublet space and S the chiral 128-dimensional SO(16) spinor. Choose two fixed linear maps


A,B:U→S.


For χ=(r,s)^T∈U define two ordered fermion spinors


ψ(χ)=Aχ=r a_+ + s a_-,
φ(χ)=Bχ=r b_+ + s b_-,


where a_±=Ae_± and b_±=Be_±. The ordered microscopic pair is


T(χ)=ψ(χ)⊗φ(χ).


Because both fermions depend linearly on the same qSaw doublet, T is exactly quadratic in χ:


T(χ)=r^2 T_+ + rs T_0 + s^2 T_-,


with
T_+=a_+⊗b_+,
T_0=a_+⊗b_-+a_-⊗b_+,
T_-=a_-⊗b_-.


Projecting the symmetric and antisymmetric spinor-exchange parts gives sibling degree-two channels


F(χ)=P_1820 Sym T(χ)=r^2F_+ + rsF_0 + s^2F_-,
C(χ)=P_8008 Alt T(χ)=r^2C_+ + rsC_0 + s^2C_-.


On the rapidity half-rung
r=e^{w/2},
s=ε_P e^{-w/2},
these become


F(w)=e^wF_+ + ε_PF_0 + e^{-w}F_-,
C(w)=e^wC_+ + ε_PC_0 + e^{-w}C_-.


Thus the same primitive qSaw half-rung supplies the required (+1,0,-1) first-square weights simultaneously to the symmetric 1820 and antisymmetric 8008 branches. If the normalized qSaw representative carries an overall factor c(w), both channels acquire the common factor c(w)^2; their projective rapidity direction is unchanged and their quartic descendants acquire c(w)^4.


Two elementary no-gos explain why two frames are required within this linear-in-χ class.


Scalar-amplitude no-go. If ψ(w)=r(w)a and φ(w)=s(w)b for fixed spinors a,b, then T(w)=r(w)s(w)a⊗b. On the reciprocal half-rung r∝e^{w/2}, s∝e^{-w/2}, the ordered pair has a fixed projective direction. It cannot generate the required e^w:1:e^{-w} rapidity orbit.


Single-frame no-go. If A=B, then ψ(χ)=φ(χ) for every χ, so Alt T(χ)=0 and hence C(χ)=0. The physical 8008/30380 branch disappears. Therefore a nontrivial qSaw rapidity orbit with a nonzero antisymmetric condensate requires two nonproportional spinor frames A and B, or a more general microscopic structure beyond this ansatz.


Three-state reconstruction. In the normalized qSaw coordinate λ, take
χ_+=(1,0)^T,
χ_-=(0,1)^T,
χ_b=2^{-1/2}(1,ε_P)^T.
Because T, F, and C are quadratic in χ, their values at these three projective states determine the entire qSaw orbit exactly. Equivalently, with q=2λ-1 and u=sqrt(1-q^2), any one of X∈{T,F,C} obeys


X(q)=u X_b + (1-u+q)X_+/2 + (1-u-q)X_-/2,


with the parity sign carried by the definition of the balanced anchor. Thus the earlier three-state qSaw tomography extends from the reduced Hessian to the full sibling microscopic pair channels under the two-frame lift.


The remaining nonvanishing condition is explicit:


P_8008 Alt(Aχ⊗Bχ)≠0


on the physical qSaw/rapidity corridor. Nonproportional A and B are necessary for a nonzero antisymmetric pair but are not sufficient to ensure a nonzero 8008 projection, because the antisymmetric bispinor also contains the 120 channel.


With §§16.I.60–62, the quartic comparison becomes


c_6 Q_6(C(w),C(w)) ?= c_4 Q_4(F(w),F(w)).


Both sides now inherit the same five rapidity weights (+2,+1,0,-1,-2) from a single explicit degree-two parent construction. What remains is no longer the rapidity bookkeeping: it is the selection of the two spinor frames A,B and the Fierz/recoupling condition that relates their four-form and six-form quartic 135 covariants.


Gate ledger update:
PASS — a two-frame Veronese lift gives simultaneous 1820 and 8008 first-square weights (+1,0,-1).
NO-GO — reciprocal scalar rescaling of two fixed spinors cannot generate the rapidity orbit.
NO-GO — a single shared spinor frame A=B kills the 8008 branch.
PASS — three canonical qSaw states determine both microscopic sibling channels under the two-frame lift.
OPEN — derive or select A and B from the existing E8/physical subgroup structure rather than postulate them.
OPEN — verify nonzero 8008 projection on the physical corridor.
OPEN — test the common-parent quartic identity between Q_6 and Q_4.




BOOK 16 FRONTIER ADDENDUM — ALGEBRAIC NULL-SHIFT COLLAPSE, TOPOLOGICAL LORENTZ ACTIVATION, AND CURVATURE-HESSIAN RANK GATE


Theorem 16.I.57.T1 — Algebraic Null-Shift Collapse of the Soft-B Escape
On the matched-kernel branch, suppose the exact soft sector carries a genuine global algebraic gauge symmetry


δB_s = Π_w Λ_2,


with Λ_2 an arbitrary globally defined kernel-valued two-form. Then every globally defined soft configuration B_s is gauge-equivalent to zero: choose Λ_2 = −B_s. Therefore the earlier CHI-type soft-B escape, which survived the weaker differential equivalence B_s ∼ B_s + Dη through global cohomology, does not survive this stronger algebraic null shift under the stated global hypothesis.


This conclusion is conditional on the unrestricted global availability of Λ_2. Boundary conditions, restricted large-gauge transformations, flux quantization, gerbe-like patching, or another independently derived global restriction could leave a residual global class; none is promoted here without a separate derivation.


Corollary 16.I.57.C1 — Activation Must Move to Protected Curvature
Under the hypotheses of Theorem 16.I.57.T1, nonzero activation cannot be carried solely by the exact soft B kernel. A lawful seed must instead occur in curvature/image data or in another independently protected global sector.


Theorem 16.I.57.T2 — Conditional Premetric Topological Curvature Activation
Let the invariant bilinear define the Chern–Weil four-form


Θ_4 = ⟨F ∧ F⟩.


By invariance and the Bianchi identity, dΘ_4 = 0. Hence, if a closed four-cycle Σ_4 carries


ν_Σ = ∫_{Σ_4} ⟨F ∧ F⟩ ≠ 0,


then F is not identically zero. No coframe or spacetime metric is required for this implication.


On a lawful matched-kernel image branch satisfying Π_w F = 0, one has F = (I − Π_w)F, so the curvature is entirely in the nonsingular image of the constitutive operator. After the matched-kernel gauge fixing B = (β/α)F,


ν_Σ ≠ 0  ⇒  F_im ≠ 0  ⇒  B_im ≠ 0.


This is a conditional sector-activation theorem, not a dynamical sector-selection theorem. It shows that a chosen nontrivial topological sector can forbid the globally trivial curvature configuration; it does not explain why that sector is selected by the unified dynamics.


Corollary 16.I.57.C2 — Rapidity Topological-Charge Invariance
For the covariantly transported branch


F_w = U(w) F_0 U(w)^{-1},


invariance of the bilinear gives


⟨F_w ∧ F_w⟩ = ⟨F_0 ∧ F_0⟩,


and therefore


ν_Σ(w) = ν_Σ(0).


Thus qSaw/rapidity transport carries an already-existing topological curvature class; it cannot manufacture topological charge from F_0 = 0.


Theorem 16.I.57.T3 — Lorentz-Stabilizer Curvature Reduction
At balance define


h_a = (P_a + ε_P K_a)/√2,


n_a = (P_a − ε_P K_a)/√2.


The Lorentz generators preserve the image/null splitting,


[M_{ab},h_c] ∈ span{h},    [M_{ab},n_c] ∈ span{n},


whereas dilation exchanges the two lines,


[D,h_a] ∝ n_a,    [D,n_a] ∝ h_a.


With the already-earned moving-normal compensator in half-rapidity convention,


A_D = −(1/2)dw,


a compatible pre-soldering geometric connection may be written schematically as


A_geom = ω^{ab} M_{ab} − (1/2)dw D.


Because [D,M_{ab}] = 0 and d²w = 0, its curvature reduces to


F_geom = R^{ab} M_{ab},


so in particular


F_D = 0.


Therefore the natural protected geometric topological carrier on the covariantly transported pre-soldering branch is Lorentz/stabilizer curvature, not persistent dilation curvature. This is consistent with the earlier Book-16 result that persistent dilation curvature is blind to the desired proportional coframe line, while Lorentz curvature survives downstream as the Palatini-type coupling.


Corollary 16.I.57.C3 — Auxiliary Π_wF Multiplier Demotion
On the strictly covariantly transported pre-soldering stabilizer branch, Π_wF = 0 follows from the stabilizer-valued curvature split and need not be imposed by an independent auxiliary multiplier merely to define that background. Such a multiplier may still re-enter if hard coframe fluctuations enlarge the admissible field space and permit curvature components outside the stabilizer branch.


Theorem 16.I.57.T4 — Curvature-Induced Coframe Hessian
A nonzero Lorentz curvature supplies a Palatini-type quadratic coframe term of the schematic form


S_e^(2) ∼ ∫ ε_{abcd} R^{ab} ∧ e^c ∧ e^d.


Consequently the coframe Hessian at the unsoldered point is no longer identically zero:


δ²S_e ∼ ∫ ε_{abcd} R^{ab} ∧ δe^c ∧ δe^d.


The prior flat-unsoldered zero-Hessian obstruction is therefore escaped on this branch. However, R ≠ 0 by itself does not imply N_4 > 0, full hard-coframe rank, or exactly one healthy physical spin-2 sector after BRST reduction.


Definition 16.I.57.G1 — Lorentz Curvature-Hessian Rank Gate
Define the curvature-induced coframe operator


𝓗_R(e)_d = ε_{abcd} R^{ab} ∧ e^c.


The live microscopic task is to classify rank and kernel of 𝓗_R over admissible nontrivial Lorentz/stabilizer topological sectors and then remove BRST/gauge null directions. A successful soldering seed requires, on the selected hard P_cf line,


ker 𝓗_R = gauge/BRST directions only.


If every lawful Lorentz curvature leaves additional physical zero directions, record a new no-go rather than promoting a soldered vacuum.


Frontier Promotion
The live question is promoted from “can a lawful nonzero flux be activated?” to:


Does any admissible topological Lorentz curvature produce sufficient BRST-reduced coframe-Hessian rank for soldering?


The following remain open and must not be promoted prematurely: topology does not select its own sector; N_4 > 0 has not yet been proved; a healthy single-spin-2 closure has not yet been proved; the old CHI soft-B global class is not inherited through the stronger algebraic null shift without an additional global restriction; and no numerical E8 realization is claimed unless explicitly computed.




§16.I.61 — PULLBACK EXTERIOR-RANK OBSTRUCTION, CHERN–WEIL STATIONARITY, AND HARD POSITIVE-DEGREE SOURCE GATE


Dependency note. This section is downstream of the positive-degree exterior-rank obstruction, the strong algebraic null-shift result, and the premetric Lorentz curvature-Hessian rank analysis. It does not assume a coframe, spacetime metric, spacetime Hodge star, Einstein background, or a microscopic E8 source not yet earned. The already-earned internal Lorentz metric, orientation, and Lorentz Hodge operator remain admissible.


Definition 16.I.61.D1 — Exterior generating rank.
Let Φ:M^4→P be a smooth parameter map and let A=Φ*𝒜 be a connection pulled back from a parameter-space connection 𝒜, with curvature F=Φ*𝓕. Define
r_ext(p):=rank(dΦ_p).
Equivalently, in a local scalar presentation A=Σ_i A_i(Φ)dφ^i, all exterior one-form dependence lies in W_Φ(p)=span{dφ^i|_p} and r_ext(p)=dim W_Φ(p) after removal of linear dependence.


Theorem 16.I.61.T1 — Pullback exterior-rank obstruction.
If r_ext(p)<4, then
(F∧F)_p=0.
Proof. Since F=Φ*𝓕,
F∧F=Φ*(𝓕∧𝓕).
The pullback of every four-form through a differential of rank less than four vanishes. Equivalently, F_p∈Λ²W_Φ(p), so F_p∧F_p∈Λ⁴W_Φ(p)=0 whenever dim W_Φ(p)<4. □


Corollary 16.I.61.C1 — Exterior-rank staircase.
For pullback-generated connections, r_ext≤1 forces F=0. At r_ext=2 or 3, nonzero F is possible but F∧F=0. The first exterior generating rank at which F∧F≠0 can occur is r_ext=4. Thus the older one-scalar connection no-go is the r_ext≤1 endpoint of a stronger four-degree obstruction.


Corollary 16.I.61.C2 — Premetric Hessian consequence.
On a separable Lorentz-curvature branch R=XF, the previously derived factorization H_R=K_X⊗L_F shows that full coframe-Hessian rank requires both Pf_V(X)≠0 and F∧F≠0. Therefore, within the present pullback-generated class, r_ext=4 is necessary for rank H_R=16. It is not sufficient: r_ext=4 does not itself force F∧F≠0.


Boundary statement. The rank r_ext is an upstream exterior generating rank. It is not the soldering/coframe rank N₄. No implication r_ext=4⇒N₄>0 has been proved; that bridge remains open.


Corollary 16.I.61.C3 — Rapidity cannot supply the missing exterior rank by itself.
A connection whose positive-degree dependence is generated only by one scalar x or its rapidity w has r_ext≤1 and therefore cannot by itself generate a nonzero base Pfaffian F∧F or a four-form topological density. This is consistent with the independent result that qSaw/rapidity transport cannot create topological charge from zero.


Theorem 16.I.61.T2 — One-generator homogeneous Chern–Weil stationarity.
On the already-derived separated Lorentz constitutive branch
W_L=a(w)I+b(w)J_L,
with homogeneous connection equations
da∧F=0,
 db∧F=0,
if F∧F≠0 at a point, then da=db=0 there.
Proof. In four dimensions a nonsimple two-form F with F∧F≠0 makes the wedge map L_F:Λ¹→Λ³, α↦F∧α, invertible. Hence da∧F=0 and db∧F=0 imply da=db=0. □


Boundary statement. This theorem does not assert that arbitrary multichannel coefficient functions must be constant. In a general multichannel constitutive sector the correct object is the coefficient-gradient map entering the connection equation, and stationarity follows only where that map is injective.


Research Diagnostic 16.I.61.R1 — Inhomogeneous hard two-form source.
To identify the missing resource without promoting an unearned microscopic action, consider the diagnostic parent
S_parent=∫[½ BᵀMB−BᵀKF−BᵀJ₂].
The algebraic B equation is
MB=KF+J₂,
so after eliminating B the effective action contains, in addition to the homogeneous curvature-quadratic term, a cross-term
∫FᵀΞ₂,
Ξ₂:=KᵀM⁻¹J₂.
Its connection variation supplies schematically a covariant inhomogeneous current DΞ₂. Thus the homogeneous stationarity equation is replaced schematically by
da∧R+db∧J_LR+DΞ₂=0.
If DΞ₂≠0, the previous implication from a full-rank base two-form to local scalar stationarity can be evaded. If DΞ₂=0, this diagnostic source does not provide the needed local selection mechanism. This is a resource test only; no microscopic E8 origin for J₂ is asserted here.


Proposition 16.I.61.P1 — Null-shift compatibility condition.
An unrestricted soft kernel-valued two-form B_s remains gauge-removable under the already-established algebraic null shift δB_s=Π_wΛ₂. Therefore a viable J₂-like activation mechanism cannot obtain physical activation merely by reintroducing that soft branch. It must couple to a hard/image-valued sector, or acquire independently derived global or gauge structure that prevents the old arbitrary soft mode from carrying the claimed physics.


Gate 16.I.61.G1 — Common hard positive-degree resource target.
Three presently distinct obstruction classes now identify a common admissible target:
(1) the pullback exterior-rank obstruction, which forbids F∧F≠0 below exterior generating rank four;
(2) the homogeneous scalar Chern–Weil stationarity obstruction, which prevents zero-form coefficient variation from locally selecting a nonsimple curvature on the separated one-generator branch; and
(3) the soft-B null-shift obstruction, which removes unrestricted kernel-valued two-form activation.
A single lawful microscopic mechanism would count as obstruction complementarity only if it supplies a hard positive-degree source/current capable of supporting or inducing a nonsimple base two-form and also produces the required nonzero covariant current in the connection equation without reviving the soft gauge branch.


Separation of microscopic jobs. A ΦBB interaction can modify the algebraic constitutive matrix M(Φ) and may participate in the still-open microscopic Lorentz-Hodge transfer, but that fact alone does not create an inhomogeneous connection current after B elimination. The live search must therefore keep distinct:
ΦBB → candidate ρ_PK≠0 → internal Lorentz Pfaffian,
versus
hard positive-degree source coupling → candidate DΞ₂≠0 plus nonsimple base curvature → exterior activation/selection.
Only a single microscopic E8 parent that lawfully realizes both jobs would close the proposed obstruction-complementarity pairing.


Status. Pullback exterior-rank obstruction: PROVED for pullback-generated connection classes. One-generator homogeneous Chern–Weil stationarity: PROVED on the stated separated branch. Common hard-source gate: OPEN. No theorem of N₄>0, BRST survival, or a unique healthy spin-2 mode is promoted by this section.


§16.I.62 — HOMOGENEOUS AUXILIARY-B CLOSURE NO-GO AND CLOSED-CONTENT TWO-FORM SOURCE CLASSIFICATION


Dependency note. This section sharpens §16.I.61. It assumes only the pre-soldering differential-form structure already in use. No coframe, spacetime metric, exterior-index contraction, spacetime Hodge operator, or unearned E8 coefficient is introduced.


Theorem 16.I.62.T1 — Homogeneous algebraic auxiliary-B closure no-go.
Let B denote any finite collection of algebraic two-form auxiliaries and let F denote the curvature channels of the connection. Consider the hard-sector action class
S[B,A,φ]=∫[½ BᵀM(φ)B−BᵀK(φ)F(A)],
where M is invertible on the hard/image sector under consideration and there is no term linear in B whose two-form coefficient is independent of B and F. Then eliminating B yields a homogeneous curvature-quadratic action
S_eff[A,φ]=−½∫FᵀW(φ)F,
W=KᵀM⁻¹K,
up to the fixed overall sign and wedge-pairing convention.
Proof. The algebraic equation is MB=KF, hence B=M⁻¹KF. Direct substitution gives the displayed quadratic form. The number of B channels and off-diagonal mixing do not alter the argument. □


Corollary 16.I.62.C1 — No additive connection current from homogeneous algebraic B systems.
Within the hypotheses of T1, the connection Euler–Lagrange equation is covariantly homogeneous in F, schematically
D(WF)=0.
After use of the curvature Bianchi identity, all remaining terms are still proportional to F. In particular there is no additive three-form current analogous to DΞ₂ from §16.I.61.


Corollary 16.I.62.C2 — Connection-sector persistence of the flat branch.
At F=0 the algebraic hard-sector equation gives B=0 and the connection equation from this homogeneous sector is automatically satisfied. Thus this BF/B² sector by itself cannot dynamically remove the flat-curvature branch. This is a connection-sector statement only: a complete coupled theory could exclude a particular flat configuration through additional field equations or global restrictions not contained in the hypotheses.


Corollary 16.I.62.C3 — ΦBB changes constitutive data but does not by itself close positive-degree activation.
The already-identified full-E8 representation-admissible invariant
I_{ΦBB}=g_{ΦBB}⟨Φ₂(C,C),P_{3875}(B∧B)⟩
is quadratic in B. When inserted into the algebraic hard sector it contributes to M(φ). Therefore it can change W=KᵀM⁻¹K and may participate in the microscopic internal Lorentz-Hodge/Pfaffian problem, but by itself it does not generate an additive source current. The independent questions whether the microscopic action supplies g_{ΦBB}≠0 and whether positive-degree activation occurs remain open.


Definition 16.I.62.D1 — Closed-content source class.
For the following classification only, restrict candidate local two-form sources J₂ to polynomials generated by zero-form selectors φ^i, their ordinary differentials dφ^i, and the already-present algebraic two-forms B and F, with zero-form-dependent internal linear maps and no additional positive-degree generator or covariant derivative carrying new exterior directions.


Theorem 16.I.62.T2 — Degree-two source normal form in the closed-content class.
Every candidate J₂ in D1 can be written schematically as
J₂=C_B(φ)B+C_F(φ)F+½Q_{ij}(φ)dφ^i∧dφ^j.
The C_BB contribution to −BᵀJ₂ is another quadratic B term and is absorbed, after the appropriate symmetric projection, into M_eff. The C_FF contribution is another BF term and is absorbed into K_eff. Hence the only potentially inhomogeneous source in this restricted class is the scalar-gradient wedge
J_φ=½Q_{ij}(φ)dφ^i∧dφ^j.


Corollary 16.I.62.C4 — Ordinary-gradient 2→3→4 exterior hierarchy.
Let W_φ=span{dφ^i} and r_φ=dim W_φ. For J_φ as above,
r_φ<2 ⇒ J_φ=0,
r_φ<3 ⇒ dJ_φ=0,
r_φ<4 ⇒ J_φ∧J_φ=0.
Indeed
 dJ_φ=½(∂_kQ_{ij})dφ^k∧dφ^i∧dφ^j,
so a nonzero ordinary derivative current first becomes possible at exterior scalar rank three, while a nonsimple/Pfaffian two-form source first becomes possible at rank four.


Corollary 16.I.62.C5 — One-rapidity source collapse.
If w is the only ordinary scalar carrying positive degree, every source of the gradient-wedge type vanishes because dw∧dw=0. Thus rapidity cannot supply the missing J₂ through ordinary scalar gradients alone.


Boundary statement. T2 and C4 do not cover a genuinely covariant derivative D_AΦ of a noncentral dynamical order parameter, an independent curvature/two-form channel, a gerbe/global flux variable, or any other separately earned positive-degree generator. Such structures lie outside the closed-content class and are precisely the surviving candidate escape routes.


Research Candidate 16.I.62.R1 — Covariant-order-parameter escape.
If a noncentral selector Φ is promoted by the microscopic theory to a genuine dynamical field with an earned covariant derivative D_AΦ, then a source of schematic form
J₂=𝒬(D_AΦ∧D_AΦ)
would lie outside the ordinary pullback theorem. Moreover D_A²Φ=F·Φ can make D_AJ₂ contain curvature-dependent three-form terms. This is only a structural candidate. The present manuscript has not derived the required E8-equivariant map 𝒬, its nonzero hard/image projection, its action coefficient, or its BRST survival.


Gate 16.I.62.G1 — Nonhomogeneous positive-degree origin gate.
The live activation search can now be stated more sharply. Either derive an admissible microscopic ingredient outside the homogeneous algebraic-B closure class, or prove that every available E8(-24) invariant remains inside that class. A successful ingredient must produce a hard/image-valued effective two-form source whose connection variation contains a genuinely nonzero additive current, must permit the exterior rank needed for a nonsimple base curvature, and must survive the relevant gauge/BRST audit. Merely adding further algebraic B channels, off-diagonal B² mixing, BF mixing, or a ΦBB constitutive term does not pass this gate.


Status. Homogeneous auxiliary-B closure no-go: PROVED under T1 hypotheses. Closed-content source normal form and ordinary-gradient 2→3→4 hierarchy: PROVED under D1. Covariant-order-parameter escape: OPEN. Microscopic hard-source origin: OPEN. N₄>0, BRST survival, and healthy single-spin-2 closure remain OPEN.






§16.I.64 — COVARIANT-CONDENSATE MOMENT MAP, POSITIVE-DEGREE BF SOURCE, AND SAME-FORM LORENTZ-HODGE TRANSFER GATE


The positive-degree activation problem admits a sharper representation-theoretic resolution than the direct 3875 self-wedge ansatz. Let C be the live 30380-valued condensate/order parameter. The already-established quadratic-map closure gives


dim Hom_E8(Sym²(30380),3875)=2,
dim Hom_E8(Λ²(30380),3875)=0.


Therefore a direct antisymmetric derivative source D_A C ∧ D_A C → 3875 is forbidden. This no-go must not be confused with the surviving symmetric zero-form maps Φ₁, Φ₂: C⊙C → 3875. In particular, the live bracket-recoupled Φ₂ channel remains available for the internal P/K transfer theorem.


The correct positive-degree target is instead the adjoint. Let R=30380 carry its invariant nondegenerate symmetric bilinear form h_R, and let κ denote the E8 Killing form. Define the canonical moment map μ by


κ(μ(u,v),X)=h_R(X·u,v).


E8 invariance makes X act h_R-skew, so μ(u,v)=-μ(v,u). Hence


μ: Λ²(30380) → 248


is E8-equivariant. Because R is nontrivial and E8 is simple, μ cannot vanish identically: otherwise h_R(Xu,v)=0 for all u,v would force the E8 action to be trivial. Its image is a nonzero E8 ideal in the adjoint and is therefore all of 248. Thus


Λ²(30380) → 3875: FAIL,
Λ²(30380) → 248: PASS,


with the second arrow canonically realized by μ.


Definition 16.I.64.A — Covariant-condensate moment-map two-form. Set


u=D_A C,
J_C=(1/2) μ(u∧u) ∈ Ω²⊗248.


This construction is pre-soldering lawful. It uses no gravitational coframe, spacetime metric, or spacetime Hodge operator.


Definition 16.I.64.B — Condensate derivative rank. At p define


r_C(p)=rank(D_A C|_p:T_pM→30380).


If r_C<2 then J_C=0. If r_C<4 then every four-form generated solely from J_C∧J_C vanishes. Consequently r_C=4 is necessary for J_C itself to carry a nonsimple/Pfaffian exterior structure. This rank is not the soldering rank N₄; no implication r_C=4 ⇒ N₄>0 is asserted.


The equivariant moment map also obeys a covariant transgression identity. With


Q_C=μ(C,D_A C),


one has, up to fixed nonzero normalization constants determined by the convention for μ,


D_A Q_C=c₁J_C+c₂ μ(C,F·C),


while D_AJ_C is proportional to μ(F·C∧D_A C). Hence a bare term ∫κ(F∧J_C) is, modulo a boundary contribution and the Bianchi identity D_AF=0, reducible to a C-dependent curvature-quadratic constitutive term. By itself it does not generically escape the homogeneous constitutive closure already isolated upstream.


A genuinely B-linear parent is


I_BJ=g_J∫κ(B∧J_C).


Using the same transgression, this is equivalent modulo a boundary term to a combination containing D_AB∧Q_C together with a C-dependent BF correction. It therefore lies outside the previous purely algebraic-B closure theorem: the connection variation now sees D_AB. This does not by itself establish a physical soft-B mode; the null-shift/BRST complex must be rechecked.


Definition 16.I.64.C — Single-parent 3875-filtered moment-map interaction. Let Φ₂(C,C)∈3875 be the live bracket-recoupled symmetric source and define the Killing-self-adjoint adjoint operator H_C by


κ(X,H_CY)=⟨Φ₂(C,C),P_3875(X⊙Y)⟩.


Then the representation-admissible parent


I_*=g_*∫⟨Φ₂(C,C),P_3875(B∧J_C)⟩
   =g_*∫κ(B∧H_CJ_C)


uses the allowed exchange symmetries in separate places: C C is a zero-form symmetric square Sym²(30380)→3875; D_AC∧D_AC is an exterior antisymmetric square Λ²(30380)→248; and B,J_C are both two-forms, so their wedge product is exchange-symmetric and can enter Sym²(248), which contains 3875. No forbidden Λ²(30380)→3875 map is used.


This gives representation-level obstruction complementarity: the same 30380 field can supply the internal constitutive/Lorentz-Hodge operator through C² and positive exterior degree through D_AC∧D_AC. This is not yet a microscopic action theorem; g_*≠0 remains to be derived or falsified.


Theorem 16.I.64.D — Same-exterior-form preservation. Suppose on a branch the adjoint moment-map source factorizes as


J_C=X F


for X∈248 and a base two-form F. Since H_C acts only on internal adjoint indices,


H_CJ_C=(H_CX)F.


Therefore, if the live internal Lorentz-Hodge transfer gives


H_CX=αE_35+ρ_PK E_12,


then necessarily


H_CJ_C=(αE_35+ρ_PK E_12)F.


Within this single-parent construction, the E_12 Hodge partner lands on the same exterior two-form F; it cannot acquire an unrelated exterior form without an additional microscopic operator. Accordingly, if this source is transmitted to Lorentz curvature,


R=(αE_35+ρ_PK E_12)F,


then the previously derived shared-form curvature-Hessian discriminant gives


Δ_H(R) ∝ (αρ_PK)^8 Pf_U(F)^8.


Thus full premetric coframe-Hessian rank on this branch requires both the internal condition αρ_PK≠0 and the exterior condition F∧F≠0. This remains a curvature-transfer criterion, not a theorem that either condition is dynamically realized.


Lemma 16.I.64.E — Ambient rank-four witness. Inside the standard 120⊂30380|_SO(16), choose v_a=M_{a5}, a=0,1,2,3, and ordinary coordinate covectors θ^a. At one point let


D_AC=Σ_a v_a θ^a.


The Lorentz projection of the moment map satisfies


P_L μ(v_a,v_b) ∝ [M_{a5},M_{b5}] ∝ M_ab,


so P_LJ_C is proportional to Σ_{a<b}M_ab θ^a∧θ^b. If a hard field equation transmits this source to R^{ab}=k θ^a∧θ^b with k≠0, then, on a 4×4 component matrix E, the curvature Hessian acts as


H_R(E)=2k[(tr E)I-E^T]


up to the fixed orientation normalization. On 1⊕Sym²_0⊕Λ² the eigenvalues are respectively 6k, -2k, +2k with multiplicities 1,9,6. Hence rank H_R=16. In the displayed normalization det M_R=-3·2^16 k^16. This is an ambient local witness only; it does not prove that the physical 30380 condensate orbit realizes the required four tangent directions or that the microscopic field equation transmits J_C to curvature.


Definition 16.I.64.F — Physical condensate-orbit tangent coefficient. On the selected hard P/K line set


h_a=(P_a+ε_P K_a)/√2,
v_a=h_a·C_0.


Lorentz covariance permits


h_R(v_a,v_b)=s_C η_ab.


Equivalently,


s_C η_ab=-(1/2)h_R(C_0,{h_a,h_b}C_0),


and contraction gives the corresponding quadratic-orbit invariant. The next physical-orbit gate is


s_C ?≠ 0.


If s_C=0 on the actual condensate branch, the ambient moment-map witness can degenerate there despite the existence of μ. If s_C≠0, the selected four P/K tangent directions form a nondegenerate Lorentz-vector sector and the Lorentz projection P_L μ(h_aC_0,h_bC_0) is forced to be nonzero up to the unique covariant normalization.


Definition 16.I.64.G — Null-shift compatibility test. Under the strong algebraic shift δB=Π_wΛ_2,


δI_*=g_*∫κ(Λ_2,Π_w^†H_CJ_C).


Therefore the old null shift survives this parent precisely when


Π_w^†H_CJ_C=0.


If the condition fails, one must rederive the complete gauge/constraint/BRST complex; failure of the old shift is not by itself proof that the former soft mode becomes a ghost-number-zero physical excitation.


Gate ledger after §16.I.64. The direct antisymmetric 30380→3875 derivative route is closed. The adjoint moment-map route Λ²(30380)→248 passes representation theory, the ambient rank-four/Hessian witness exists, and I_* is representation-admissible. Still open are: s_C≠0 on the actual physical condensate orbit; a nonzero microscopic same-parent coefficient ρ_PK and coupling g_*; dynamic activation r_C=4; compatibility with or replacement of the strong null-shift BRST complex; transmission to a Lorentz curvature with nonzero exterior Pfaffian; N₄>0; and exactly one healthy physical spin-2 mode.


Status: positive-degree moment-map representation gate — PASS. Physical-orbit, microscopic-transfer, BRST, soldering-rank, and healthy-spin-2 gates — OPEN.




§16.I.65 — Normal-Plane Reality, Orientation-Tangent Noncollapse, and the Microscopic Lorentz-Hodge Transmission Gate


Scope. This section closes the finite algebraic questions left by the normal-condensate and curvature-Hessian analyses. It does not promote any undetermined microscopic action coefficient, BRST class, N₄ theorem, or spin-2 health claim.


The physical normal condensate is
C₀ = a n₊ + b n₋ + ā s₊ + b̄ s₋,
with n₊, n₋ the distinguished Lorentz-singlet normal pair in the six-dimensional factor. Let the D3-normal amplitude basis and the true conformal null basis be related by
(z₊,z₋)ᵀ = T_N (a,b)ᵀ.
For the selected hard generator
h_a = (P_a + ε_P K_a)/√2,
the normal-plane action reduces to
h_a C₀ ∝ (z₋ + ε_P z₊) e_a/√2.
Writing
(u_{ε_P},v_{ε_P}) = (ε_P,1)T_N,
we therefore have the exact two-amplitude reduction
h_a C₀ ∝ (u_{ε_P}a + v_{ε_P}b)e_a.


Theorem 16.I.65.1 — Real-normal-plane orientation-tangent noncollapse. Assume only that the two physical normal bases are related by a real invertible change of basis T_N ∈ GL(2,R). Then
χ_C := 2 Im(a b̄) ≠ 0  ⇒  h_a C₀ ≠ 0.
Proof. Since T_N is real and invertible, (u,v):=(u_{ε_P},v_{ε_P}) is a nonzero real row. If ua+vb=0, then (a,b)=c(v,−u) for some c∈C. Hence a b̄ = −uv|c|² is real, so Im(a b̄)=0. Contraposition proves the result. ∎


Interpretation. The theorem does not require the exact normalization or the exact assignment of the D3 ± labels to the E8 conformal five-grading lines. It requires only the real-form statement T_N∈GL(2,R). Consequently the orientation-breaking condition is sufficient to prevent collapse of the selected hard P/K orbit tangent whenever that real-plane identification is established. The exact D3-weight/five-grade line matching and exact T_N remain open. Moreover h_aC₀≠0 is a tangent-noncollapse statement, not automatically a scalar norm statement: because the invariant form is indefinite, s_C≠0 remains open unless the relevant pairing is independently shown nonnull on this tangent.


Proposition 16.I.65.2 — Representation/action separation for Lorentz-Hodge transfer. The current E8 analysis establishes the multiplicity-one orientation invariant I₁₄₆ with nonzero representation normalization κ₁₄₆, and separately establishes the internal Lorentz-Hodge/rank-four E₁₂ partner. The same microscopic analysis leaves the coefficient/dynamical weight of this invariant in the Cartan–Plebanski/BF action undetermined. Therefore
κ₁₄₆ ≠ 0  ⇏  ρ_PK ≠ 0.
The remaining ρ_PK gate is an action-level transmission problem, not an unfinished representation decomposition. Representation theory alone is exhausted at this point.


Proposition 16.I.65.3 — Curvature-Hessian consequence of the transmission coefficient. On the complementary internal-plane branch
R = α E₃₅ F + ρ_PK E₁₂ G,
with L_F(η)=F∧η and L_G(η)=G∧η, the Hessian splits into the two complementary internal blocks. For αρ_PK≠0,
rank H_R = 2 rank L_F + 2 rank L_G.
Therefore
rank H_R = 16
iff
αρ_PK≠0,  F∧F≠0,  G∧G≠0,
and, up to one fixed nonzero convention factor,
Δ_H(R) ∝ (αρ_PK)^8 Pf_U(F)^4 Pf_U(G)^4.
In the shared-form branch
R = (αE₃₅ + ρ_PK E₁₂)F,
this becomes
Δ_H(R) ∝ (αρ_PK)^8 Pf_U(F)^8,
so full rank is equivalent to αρ_PK≠0 and F∧F≠0.


Corollary 16.I.65.4 — Rank-eight obstruction when the Hodge transmission vanishes. If ρ_PK=0 on the complementary-plane route, then rank H_R≤8 regardless of any topological charge carried by the surviving E₃₅ exterior form. Thus the internal Hodge-transmission coefficient and the positive-degree/base-Pfaffian activation are independent microscopic gates. Topological activation cannot substitute for a vanishing ρ_PK.


BRST consequence. Once H_R is invertible on the selected hard P/K line, write the full ghost-number-zero quadratic operator as
𝓗 = [[H_R,C],[C†,D]].
Then the remaining null problem is governed by the Schur complement
S_R = D − C† H_R⁻¹ C,
with ker 𝓗 ≅ ker S_R. Hence a full-rank coframe Hessian removes independent pure-coframe zero modes, but it does not prove BRST physical soldering; any surviving gauge or physical null carrying a coframe component is necessarily mixed with the remaining fields.


Gate ledger after §16.I.65.
PASS: physical (126,6) normal condensate pair; reduction of the normal-basis problem to a 2×2 map; E8 orientation source I₁₄₆; internal rank-four Lorentz-Hodge E₁₂ partner; strict Lorentz-stabilizer algebraic rank-16 existence; P_cf label-factor compatibility.
CONDITIONAL PASS: χ_C≠0 implies selected hard-orbit tangent noncollapse, conditional only on T_N∈GL(2,R).
OPEN: exact D3-weight/five-grade line matching and exact T_N; scalar s_C≠0; action-level ρ_PK; microscopic positive-degree/base-Pfaffian activation; BRST ghost-number-zero physical soldering; N₄>0; exactly one healthy physical spin-2.
NOT ESTABLISHED: κ₁₄₆≠0 ⇒ ρ_PK≠0.


Next exact calculations. (A) Derive or falsify ρ_PK from one actual microscopic BF/PCBF parent action coefficient; representation existence cannot decide it further. (B) If ρ_PK≠0, prove the relevant base two-form Pfaffian(s) are nonzero without importing a spacetime Hodge operator or a pre-soldering coframe. (C) Only then compute the Schur-reduced BRST kernel of the complete quadratic system. Exact T_N is required for line labels and normalization, but not for the orientation-tangent theorem once the real-plane identification is independently certified.




§16.I.66 — Orientation-Parent Degree Obstruction, BF/3875 Action Separation, and the Two-Pfaffian Topology Gap


Scope. This section tests the currently audited microscopic parents against the seed-level Lorentz-Hodge curvature gate. It proves a parent-specific seed obstruction and a topology/rank separation theorem. It does not prove a universal E8 no-go, nonzero ρ_PK, N₄>0, or a healthy spin-2 mode.


Theorem 16.I.66.1 — Quartic orientation-parent seed obstruction. The audited full-E8 orientation parent reduces on the physical slice to
I₁₄₆ = κ₁₄₆ χ_C E₂(e,f),
χ_C = Im(a b̄),
with κ₁₄₆≠0 and E₂ homogeneous of bidegree (2,2) in (e,f). Hence at the fully unsoldered point (e,f)=(0,0),
D^j_{e,f} I₁₄₆|₀ = 0,  j=1,2,3.
In particular,
D²_{e,f} I₁₄₆|₀ = 0.
Therefore I₁₄₆ cannot seed a quadratic Palatini-type R e e soldering Hessian and cannot create positive exterior rank from the exactly unsoldered point. Its first possible unsoldered coframe response is quartic. If an independent nonzero e or f background is already present, lower variations in the complementary field may occur; in that case I₁₄₆ is a post-rank orientation transmitter, not the seed of rank.
Proof. E₂ is homogeneous total coframe degree four. Every derivative of order j<4 remains homogeneous of positive degree 4−j and therefore vanishes at the origin. ∎


Proposition 16.I.66.2 — BF/3875 action separation. The audited I_{ΦBB} is a genuine 3875 B-sector invariant of schematic form
I_{ΦBB}=g_{ΦBB}⟨Φ₂(C,C),Q(B,B)⟩₃₈₇₅.
Its restricted representation overlap is nonzero, but g_{ΦBB} remains an action coefficient not fixed by representation theory. Because I_{ΦBB} is quadratic in B and has no direct e/f factor, it does not by itself supply the pure e/f soldering Hessian and does not establish ρ_PK≠0. Eliminating B in a specified BF+BB parent may generate an effective curvature term only conditionally on the BF kinetic map, the relevant B-sector inversion or constraint structure, and a nonzero same-parent transmission into the Lorentz-Hodge P/K curvature channel.


Corollary 16.I.66.3 — Audited-parent closure of the seed calculation. Among the Book-16 parents audited so far, neither closes the seed-level ρ_PK gate: I₁₄₆ is too high in exterior/coframe degree at the unsoldered origin; I_{ΦBB} is a B-sector term whose dynamical coefficient and same-parent Lorentz-Hodge curvature transmission remain unproved. Therefore the microscopic Lorentz-Hodge transmission gate remains OPEN. This is not a theorem that every E8 action has ρ_PK=0.


Theorem 16.I.66.4 — Two-Pfaffian topology gap. Let
R = X F + Y G
with X,Y complementary internal Lorentz bivectors and F,G base two-forms. Then
Θ₄ = ⟨R∧R⟩
= ⟨X,X⟩ F∧F + 2⟨X,Y⟩ F∧G + ⟨Y,Y⟩ G∧G.
On the complementary-plane Hessian branch, full rank requires both
F∧F≠0,
G∧G≠0,
together with nonzero internal coefficients. A single nonzero Θ₄ does not imply these two conditions.


(i) If ⟨X,Y⟩≠0, take
F=θ⁰∧θ¹,
G=θ²∧θ³.
Then F∧F=G∧G=0 but F∧G≠0. Thus Θ₄ can be nonzero while
rank H_R = 2·2 + 2·2 = 8.


(ii) If ⟨X,Y⟩=0 and ⟨X,X⟩≠0, take F nonsimple and G simple, for example
F=θ⁰∧θ¹+θ²∧θ³,
G=θ⁰∧θ¹.
Then F∧F≠0 and G∧G=0. Θ₄ can be nonzero from the F∧F term while
rank H_R = 2·4 + 2·2 = 12.
The analogous statement holds with X/F and Y/G exchanged.
Therefore one Chern-Weil charge does not close the two exterior-Pfaffian rank gate. ∎


Corollary 16.I.66.5 — Shared-form exception is conditional. If a same-parent microscopic derivation forces
R=(X+Y)F,
the split two-Pfaffian condition collapses to the single condition F∧F≠0. In that special branch a nonzero topological density
Θ₄=⟨X+Y,X+Y⟩F∧F
with nonnull internal invariant can force F∧F≠0 somewhere. But existence of the shared-form curvature sector, its global nontrivial characteristic class, and nonzero Lorentz-Hodge transmission remain independent microscopic/global gates.


Gate ledger after §16.I.66.
PASS: parent-specific theorem that I₁₄₆ cannot seed a quadratic soldering Hessian at the fully unsoldered point; BF/3875 representation-versus-action separation; split-curvature two-Pfaffian topology insufficiency theorem.
OPEN: explicit lower-exterior-degree same-parent orientation/Hodge BF term or another derivation of ρ_PK≠0; actual base Pfaffian(s) on the E8-admissible curvature; BRST ghost-number-zero physical soldering; N₄>0; exactly one healthy physical spin-2.
NO UNIVERSAL NO-GO: a different earned microscopic action term could in principle supply ρ_PK.


Next exact calculation. Classify lower-exterior-degree orientation-odd E8 invariants compatible with the BF/curvature parent. If the current E8 channels admit none, the action-level ρ_PK gate sharpens to a finite action-space no-go. If one exists, compute its coefficient and whether it lands on the same exterior curvature two-form or an independent one before reopening BRST.




§16.I.67 — Mixed-3875 BF Uniqueness, the Audited Hodge-Channel No-Go, and the Minimal Odd Microscopic Escape


Scope. Section 16.I.66 separated the orientation source, the BF/3875 action sector, and the two-Pfaffian rank requirement. The remaining question is whether the already-earned symmetric 30380²→3875 source can itself enter the BF constitutive operator and supply the missing Lorentz-Hodge E₁₂ component. The answer on the presently audited source branch is no. This is a current-parent no-go, not a universal E₈ no-go.


Definition 16.I.67.A — Mixed 3875 BF invariant. Let
Φ₂(C,C)∈3875
be the live symmetric condensate source and let H_C∈End(248) be its Killing-self-adjoint operator,
κ(H_CX,Y)=⟨Φ₂(C,C),P₃₈₇₅(X⊙Y)⟩₃₈₇₅.
The corresponding pre-soldering BF four-form is
I_{ΦBF}=g_{ΦBF}∫⟨Φ₂(C,C),P₃₈₇₅(B⊙F)⟩₃₈₇₅
          =g_{ΦBF}∫κ(B∧H_CF).
This is of the existing PCBF form Bᵀ∧K(S,C)F; it is therefore a candidate contribution to K(S,C), not a new action architecture.


Lemma 16.I.67.1 — Uniqueness of the Φ₂-linear 3875 BF contraction. Within the class of E₈-invariant pre-soldering terms that are linear in the fixed 3875 source Φ₂ and bilinear in the adjoint-valued two-forms B and F through the symmetric adjoint product, I_{ΦBF} is unique up to one overall scalar.
Proof. Since two exterior two-forms commute under wedge, the internal bilinear lies in Sym²(248). The 3875 occurs with multiplicity one in Sym²(248)=1⊕3875⊕27000. Hence the 3875 projection and its invariant pairing with Φ₂ are unique up to normalization. ∎


Theorem 16.I.67.2 — Audited symmetric-3875 Lorentz-Hodge no-go. On the source restriction already tested in §§16.I.53–54, the complete presently tested symmetric quadratic 30380²→3875 family has its antisymmetric Lorentz/P–K component confined to one simple plane. In the explicit physical basis this direct plane is
A₀=E₃₅,
while its unique internal Lorentz-Hodge complement is
*_L A₀=rE₁₂,
r≠0.
The tested symmetric source does not contain an independent E₁₂ block. Therefore the mixed invariant I_{ΦBF} cannot generate the missing Hodge coefficient on that audited branch:
π₁₂(H_CX)=0,
β_{PK}^{(ΦBF)}=0,
ρ_{PK}^{(ΦBF)}=0,
for the selected direct carrier X used in the audited reduction. A nonzero g_{ΦBF} can renormalize or transmit the direct E₃₅ coefficient, but it cannot by itself complete the internal antisymmetric rank.


Proof. I_{ΦBF} acts on F only through the same operator H_C determined by Φ₂. Multiplicity one removes any second independent 3875 contraction that could change the internal image while keeping the same source. The explicit §§16.I.53–54 reduction establishes that the tested symmetric-source image has only the direct E₃₅ antisymmetric block and that E₁₂ is the distinct Lorentz-Hodge complement. Hence no choice of the scalar coupling g_{ΦBF} creates a component absent from the image of H_C. ∎


Corollary 16.I.67.3 — The §16.I.64 single-parent interaction does not jointly close the two Pfaffian gates on the audited branch. Recall
I_*=g_*∫κ(B∧H_CJ_C),
with J_C the adjoint-valued two-form moment-map current. If locally
J_C=XF
for a base two-form F, then
H_CJ_C=(H_CX)F.
Thus I_* lawfully preserves the exterior two-form and can in principle carry positive exterior degree. However, on the audited symmetric-3875 branch, H_CX has no E₁₂ component. If the surviving internal carrier is proportional to the simple plane E₃₅, then
Pf_V(E₃₅)=0,
and therefore
rank H_R≤8
for the corresponding separable curvature R∝E₃₅F, even when
F∧F≠0.
Consequently the same-parent 3875 route may address the base/exterior Pfaffian while failing the independent internal Pfaffian. It does not satisfy obstruction complementarity.


Proposition 16.I.67.4 — Reduced ΦBB inversion cannot manufacture an absent Hodge block. The existing term
I_{ΦBB}=g_{ΦBB}⟨Φ₂(C,C),Q(B,B)⟩₃₈₇₅
modifies the B-sector constitutive matrix M. Formally eliminating B gives
B=M⁻¹KF,
S_eff⊃(1/2)FᵀKᵀM⁻¹KF.
If K and M are restricted to the same audited reduced block and neither contains an E₁₂-transmitting component, matrix inversion and multiplication cannot create an independent complementary E₁₂ channel. Hence I_{ΦBB} alone does not rescue ρ_{PK} inside that reduced branch.


Caveat. This is not a theorem against full-E₈ heavy-field mixing. Off-block couplings, an odd bridge current, an odd heavy Hessian, or another independently earned microscopic source can alter the Schur complement before restriction and can in principle generate the missing Hodge component. Such a mechanism must be exhibited explicitly; it cannot be inferred from the reduced ΦBB term.


Theorem 16.I.67.5 — Current-parent exhaustion statement. Among the presently audited microscopic parents:
(1) I₁₄₆ supplies the orientation pseudoscalar but has quartic coframe degree and cannot seed the quadratic unsoldered Hessian;
(2) the Φ₂-linear mixed 3875 BF invariant is representation-admissible and unique, but its audited image has no independent E₁₂ block;
(3) I_* preserves a positive-degree exterior two-form but uses the same H_C and therefore inherits the same internal Hodge obstruction;
(4) I_{ΦBB} changes the reduced B-sector mass/constitutive operator but does not create E₁₂ without independently earned transverse or odd full-E₈ mixing.
Therefore none of the currently audited parents, taken in their established reduced action roles, proves
ρ_{PK}≠0.
This closes the attempt to obtain the Lorentz-Hodge coefficient merely by reusing the live symmetric 3875 source.


Definition 16.I.67.B — Minimal odd microscopic escape criterion. A successful pre-soldering completion must provide an E₈-covariant microscopic contribution whose restriction to the selected Lorentz/P–K sector has
π₁₂(K_{odd}E₃₅)≠0
or the corresponding nonzero off-block Schur-complement transfer. Its coefficient must be dynamically nonzero on the physical condensate orbit. If such a contribution enters the BF constitutive operator K directly, then it acts on the same exterior curvature two-form F and automatically yields the favorable shared-form structure
R=(αE₃₅+ρ_{PK}E₁₂)F.
The curvature-Hessian gate then reduces to the two independent nonvanishing conditions
ρ_{PK}≠0,
F∧F≠0.
No spacetime Hodge operator is used in this criterion; *_L remains the earned internal Lorentz Hodge only.


Corollary 16.I.67.6 — The next search space is genuinely smaller. A candidate based only on another scalar rescaling of the same Φ₂/3875 operator cannot work. The next lawful microscopic search must instead test one of:
(a) an independently nontrivial 27000 constitutive channel not among the already tested images;
(b) an odd full-E₈ bridge/heavy-field mixing channel whose pre-restriction Schur complement has nonzero E₁₂ transfer;
(c) another E₈-covariant zero-form constitutive source with the required mirror-odd/Hodge character.
A post-split expression such as χ_C κ(B∧*_LF) is not a derivation: χ_C and *_L must descend from an independently established full-E₈ parent before such a term is admissible microscopically.


Gate ledger after §16.I.67.
PASS: representation/action admissibility and multiplicity-one uniqueness of the Φ₂-linear mixed 3875 BF term; same-exterior-form preservation when a constitutive Hodge component enters K directly.
NO-GO ON THE AUDITED CURRENT PARENT: the live symmetric Φ₂/3875 operator cannot supply the independent E₁₂ Lorentz-Hodge block; the §16.I.64 I_* route cannot simultaneously close the base and internal Pfaffian gates; reduced I_{ΦBB} inversion cannot create the absent block without new transverse/odd mixing.
OPEN: a genuinely new odd/full-E₈ microscopic Hodge-transfer source, including an untested nontrivial 27000 or heavy-mixing mechanism; its nonzero physical coefficient ρ_{PK}; the actual nonzero base Pfaffian; BRST ghost-number-zero survival after the resulting action is fixed; N₄>0; exactly one healthy physical spin-2.
NOT ESTABLISHED: any universal E₈ no-go against ρ_{PK}; any identification of the post-split Hodge term with a full-E₈ invariant before its parent is derived.


Next exact calculation. Audit the remaining full-E₈ constitutive possibilities by representation channel and parity. First test whether an independently available 27000-valued zero-form source can contribute to K with a nonzero E₁₂ restriction. If the tested 27000 family is already exhaustive, promote its existing failure to a 27000 no-go. Otherwise isolate the untested multiplicity/channel. In parallel, derive the minimal off-block heavy-field Schur complement capable of producing an odd E₁₂ transfer without importing a spacetime metric or Hodge operator.




§16.I.68 — Recovery of the Linear-30380 Mixed-BF Channel and the Odd Schur-Transfer Commutator Gate


Scope. Section 16.I.67 closes reuse of the live symmetric 3875 source as the missing Lorentz-Hodge channel, but it does not exhaust the PCBF action class. An earlier exact Book-16 theorem, §16.0.H.T4, already established that C∈30380 is a Killing-skew endomorphism of the adjoint and that a mixed term B^T∧CF is lawful even though a bare C insertion cannot appear linearly in a symmetric BB or FF kernel. This section brings that earned channel forward and computes what it can and cannot do.


Definition 16.I.68.A — Linear-30380 mixed BF invariant. Use the Killing form κ to identify C∈30380⊂Λ²(248) with the κ-skew endomorphism K_C of the adjoint,
κ(K_C X,Y)=-κ(X,K_C Y).
Define
I_{CBF}=g_{CBF}∫κ(B∧K_C F).
Equivalently, I_{CBF} pairs C with the 30380 projection of the antisymmetric internal part of the distinct-field tensor B⊗F. It is a pre-soldering four-form. No spacetime metric, spacetime Hodge operator, inverse coframe, or nonzero coframe is used.


Lemma 16.I.68.1 — Multiplicity-one uniqueness of the linear-30380 BF channel. Within the class of E8-invariant terms linear in C∈30380 and bilinear in the distinct adjoint-valued two-forms B and F through the antisymmetric internal product, I_{CBF} is unique up to one scalar coefficient.
Proof. The exact adjoint tensor decomposition is Λ²(248)=248⊕30380, with the 30380 occurring once. The 248 summand is the bracket image and is orthogonal to C∈30380. Therefore the 30380 projection and its invariant pairing with C are unique up to normalization. ∎


Corollary 16.I.68.2 — Same-field cancellation. If the two field slots are identified, the linear skew insertion vanishes:
κ(B∧K_C B)=0,
and likewise for F. Thus the new channel is genuinely mixed BF data; it is not a hidden BB or FF invariant. This is consistent with §16.0.H.T4.


Theorem 16.I.68.3 — Finite Lorentz-Hodge transmission test. Let the even BF constitutive operator already transmit the direct physical Lorentz bivector A₀=E₃₅, and write
K=K_even+g_{CBF}K_C.
On a local separable curvature input XF with direct reduced carrier X→E₃₅, the linear-30380 term multiplies the same exterior two-form F. Its missing complementary coefficient is therefore determined by the single reduced matrix element
ρ_{CBF}:=g_{CBF} π₁₂(K_C E₃₅),
with π₁₂ denoting projection onto the earned E₁₂ Lorentz-Hodge partner after the full-E8 operator has been restricted to the physical P/K sector. Hence, if
π₁₂(K_C E₃₅)≠0
and g_{CBF}≠0, the BF Hessian contains the favorable shared-form structure
R_eff=(αE₃₅+ρ_{CBF}E₁₂)F
for nonzero direct coefficient α. The curvature-Hessian rank gate then reduces to
αρ_{CBF}≠0,   F∧F≠0.
No Lorentz Hodge operator is inserted into the parent action: E₁₂ is only the already-earned reduced target used to test the full-E8 matrix element. ∎


Status warning. Representation theory proves that I_{CBF} exists and is unique in this candidate class. It does not prove that the physical condensate C₀ gives π₁₂(K_{C₀}E₃₅)≠0, and it does not prove g_{CBF}≠0. Those are now a finite matrix-element gate and an action-coefficient gate respectively.


Proposition 16.I.68.4 — 27000 exhaustiveness is not yet available. Section 16.I.54 already records that the tested 27000 images do not supply an independent E₁₂ block. However, the August-25 microscopic addendum explicitly leaves
m_{27000}=dim Hom_E8(Sym²(30380),27000)
and the full common-parent 27000 projection unresolved. Therefore the tested failure cannot yet be promoted to a universal 27000-channel no-go. The linear-30380 mixed-BF term is an already-admitted lower-degree candidate and should be tested before a new unrestricted 27000 Clebsch search is treated as necessary.


Theorem 16.I.68.5 — Odd Schur-transfer commutator theorem. Let M=M† be the invertible even heavy-B kernel and let
K=K₀+gK_C,
with K₀†=K₀ and K_C†=-K_C relative to κ. After algebraic elimination of B, the reduced curvature operator is
W=K†M⁻¹K.
Its expansion is
W=W₀+gW₁+g²W₂,
where
W₀=K₀M⁻¹K₀,
W₁=K₀M⁻¹K_C-K_CM⁻¹K₀,
W₂=-K_CM⁻¹K_C.
The first orientation-sensitive term W₁ is Killing-self-adjoint. In the canonical reduced case K₀=I,
W₁=[M⁻¹,K_C].
Therefore a Killing-skew linear 30380 BF bridge can generate a Killing-self-adjoint odd effective curvature channel only through noncommutation with the even heavy constitutive kernel. ∎


Corollary 16.I.68.6 — Scalar-heavy-kernel cancellation. If K₀=I and M=mI on the relevant block, or more generally [M⁻¹,K_C]=0, then W₁=0. For M=mI one has exactly
W=m⁻¹(I-g²K_C²),
