which is even under K_C→-K_C. Thus the B-eliminated FF description erases the linear odd information in this commuting branch even though the parent mixed BF term itself is nonzero. A scalar heavy kernel therefore cannot manufacture the missing odd Hodge response through Schur reduction.


Corollary 16.I.68.7 — Minimal off-block heavy escape. If the physical mechanism requires the B-eliminated curvature operator rather than the unreduced BF Hessian, the first lawful odd transfer is the finite projection
ρ_Schur ∝ π₁₂(W₁E₃₅).
For K₀=I this becomes
ρ_Schur ∝ π₁₂([M⁻¹,K_C]E₃₅).
Hence nontrivial off-block/heavy mixing is not merely one possible story: within this linear-30380/invertible-B class it is exactly the obstruction to cancellation of the first odd effective term.


Gate ledger after §16.I.68.
PASS: recovery of the already-lawful linear-30380 mixed BF invariant; multiplicity-one uniqueness in its antisymmetric distinct-field class; same-exterior-form preservation at the unreduced BF level; exact Schur-transfer commutator formula.
NO-GO: the live symmetric 3875 source remains unable to supply E₁₂ on the audited branch; tested 27000 images still fail but are not exhaustive; a commuting/scalar heavy B kernel kills the first odd 30380 contribution in the B-eliminated FF operator.
OPEN: the physical full-E8 matrix element π₁₂(K_{C₀}E₃₅); the microscopic coefficient g_{CBF}; if Schur reduction is required, π₁₂([M⁻¹,K_{C₀}]E₃₅); nonzero base Pfaffian F∧F; BRST ghost-number-zero survival; N₄>0; exactly one healthy physical spin-2.


Next exact calculation. Compute the single physical-orbit number
m_C:=κ(E₁₂,K_{C₀}E₃₅)
in the frozen E8(-24) normalization, with E₁₂ and E₃₅ represented by the same physical P/K embedding used in §§16.I.53–54. If m_C≠0, the internal Hodge-transfer representation gate passes for the linear-30380 BF parent and only its action coefficient remains. If m_C=0, test the first selector-assisted symmetric bridge [S,C] and the noncommuting Schur term before reopening the unresolved 27000 multiplicity problem.




§16.I.69 — Physical-(126,6) Support No-Go for the Bare 30380 BF Hodge Channel and the Sandwiched Heavy Escape


Scope. Section 16.I.68 reduced the newly recovered linear-30380 mixed-BF candidate to the finite matrix element m_C=κ(E₁₂,K_{C₀}E₃₅). The subgroup content already established earlier in Book 16 is sufficient to evaluate that matrix element without a new dense E8 Clebsch contraction. The result is an exact zero on the physical normal condensate slice. This section also closes the immediate [S,C] and bare-C-in-K Schur variants and isolates the first heavy mechanism not killed by the same support theorem.


Established representation data. Under the earned subgroup branching,
248=(45,1)⊕(1,15)⊕(10,6)⊕(16,4̄)⊕(16̄,4)
for Spin(10)×Spin(4,2). The physical normal condensate lies in the E8(-24)-real completion of the chiral 30380 sector
C₀∈(126,6)⊕(126̄,6),
with the sparse real slice already written as
C₀=a n₊+b n₋+ā s₊+b̄ s₋.
The physical Lorentz algebra is contained in the conformal adjoint (1,15), so in particular
E₃₅,E₁₂∈(1,15).


Definition 16.I.69.A — Bivector-to-skew-map pairing. Viewing C∈30380⊂Λ²(248) as a Killing-skew endomorphism K_C uses the canonical invariant identification, so up to the one fixed nonzero normalization convention,
κ(Y,K_C X)=⟨C,Y∧X⟩_{Λ²(248)}.
Only vanishing is used below, so the overall normalization is irrelevant.


Theorem 16.I.69.1 — Conformal-adjoint annihilation theorem. For the physical normal condensate C₀,
K_{C₀}X=0
for every X∈(1,15).


Proof. Fix X∈(1,15) and arbitrary Y∈248. Since X is a Spin(10) singlet, the Spin(10) type of Y∧X is the same as the Spin(10) type carried by Y. From the adjoint branching the only possibilities are
45, 1, 10, 16, 16̄.
The physical C₀ carries only 126 and 126̄ Spin(10) type. An invariant subgroup pairing between C₀ and Y∧X therefore vanishes for every Y because neither 126 nor 126̄ occurs among those possibilities. Hence
κ(Y,K_{C₀}X)=0
for every Y. Nondegeneracy of the Killing form gives K_{C₀}X=0. ∎


Clarification 16.I.69.CL1 — No conflict with the P/K tangent theorem. Section 16.I.65 proves that the adjoint representation action h_a·C₀ can be nonzero. That is the action of an adjoint generator on the 30380 representation. The present map K_{C₀}h_a is the different operation obtained by viewing C₀ itself as a bivector/skew endomorphism of the adjoint. Nonvanishing of h_a·C₀ does not imply nonvanishing of K_{C₀}h_a.


Corollary 16.I.69.2 — Direct linear-30380 Lorentz-Hodge no-go. Since E₃₅,E₁₂∈(1,15),
K_{C₀}E₃₅=K_{C₀}E₁₂=0.
Therefore the finite gate proposed in §16.I.68 evaluates exactly to
m_C:=κ(E₁₂,K_{C₀}E₃₅)=0,
and hence
ρ_{CBF}=g_{CBF}π₁₂(K_{C₀}E₃₅)=0
for every value of g_{CBF} on the physical normal slice. The invariant I_{CBF}=g_{CBF}∫κ(B∧K_{C₀}F) remains lawful in the full theory and can act in other adjoint sectors, but it cannot transmit a pure conformal/Lorentz curvature input into the missing E₁₂ partner.


Corollary 16.I.69.3 — Single selector-commutator no-go on the same external pair. Let S=S† be any Killing-self-adjoint selector endomorphism and define the symmetric odd operator
H_{SC}:=[S,K_{C₀}].
Then
κ(E₁₂,H_{SC}E₃₅)=0.
Proof. Put x=E₃₅ and y=E₁₂. Since K_{C₀}x=K_{C₀}y=0,
κ(y,[S,K_{C₀}]x)
=κ(y,S K_{C₀}x)-κ(y,K_{C₀}Sx)
=0+κ(K_{C₀}y,Sx)=0,
using Killing-skewness of K_{C₀}. ∎


Thus the first selector-assisted bridge named at the end of §16.I.68 also fails as a direct E₃₅→E₁₂ constitutive insertion. This statement is independent of whether S commutes with K_{C₀} and does not require S to be block diagonal.


Theorem 16.I.69.4 — Bare-C Schur no-go on the physical Lorentz pair. Let M=M† be any invertible heavy-B kernel and let
K=K₀+gK_{C₀}
with arbitrary even K₀. The B-eliminated curvature operator is
W=K†M⁻¹K
 =K₀†M⁻¹K₀
 +g(K₀†M⁻¹K_{C₀}-K_{C₀}M⁻¹K₀)
 -g²K_{C₀}M⁻¹K_{C₀}.
Then the entire C₀-dependent correction to the physical Hodge matrix element vanishes:
κ(E₁₂,[W-K₀†M⁻¹K₀]E₃₅)=0.


Proof. The term with K_{C₀} on the right vanishes because K_{C₀}E₃₅=0. Every term with K_{C₀} adjacent to the left pairing vanishes by skewness and K_{C₀}E₁₂=0. This includes the quadratic term. No commutativity assumption on M is used. ∎


Corollary 16.I.69.5 — Physical correction to the §16.I.68 commutator escape. The general algebraic identity W₁=[M⁻¹,K_C] for K₀=I remains correct, but on the physical normal condensate and the E₃₅/E₁₂ external pair its matrix element is identically zero even when [M⁻¹,K_{C₀}]≠0. Noncommutation is therefore necessary only in the abstract operator problem; it is not sufficient and does not rescue the physical Hodge channel when the odd C₀ insertion sits in the BF map K.


Definition 16.I.69.B — Sandwiched odd heavy-Hessian escape. The first heavy mechanism not eliminated by the preceding theorem places the odd self-adjoint operator inside the heavy kernel rather than as a bare C₀ insertion in K. Let
M=M₀+λH_{odd},
M₀=M₀†,
H_{odd}=H_{odd}†,
and keep the leading BF map K₀ even. To first order,
M⁻¹=M₀⁻¹-λM₀⁻¹H_{odd}M₀⁻¹+O(λ²),
so
W=K₀†M₀⁻¹K₀
 -λK₀†M₀⁻¹H_{odd}M₀⁻¹K₀+O(λ²).
Define the even heavy transfer
A:=M₀⁻¹K₀.
Then the first odd physical Hodge coefficient is the finite sandwich
m_{heavy}:=-κ(AE₁₂,H_{odd}AE₃₅).
A lawful candidate is H_{odd}=[S,K_{C₀}], which is self-adjoint and odd in C₀.


Theorem 16.I.69.6 — Off-block sandwich necessity. For H_{odd}=[S,K_{C₀}], if the even transfer A preserves the conformal adjoint on the two external lines,
AE₁₂,AE₃₅∈(1,15),
then
m_{heavy}=0.
Consequently a nonzero sandwiched odd heavy response requires the even heavy transfer to move at least one relevant external Lorentz line out of the K_{C₀}-annihilated conformal subspace and into a C₀-active sector, with the remaining selector/heavy maps closing the invariant pairing. This is the precise off-block resource that must now be exhibited.


Proof. If both transferred vectors remain in (1,15), Theorem 16.I.69.1 places both in ker K_{C₀}; Corollary 16.I.69.3 then applies with x=AE₃₅ and y=AE₁₂. ∎


Representation consequence. The direct linear-C BF search is therefore closed on the physical normal slice without computing any new 248×248 dense matrix. The surviving odd-heavy question is not merely whether M and C fail to commute. It is whether the already-earned even BF/heavy dynamics generates a genuine conformal-to-transverse cyclic subspace before and after the odd insertion. This interfaces directly with the earlier finite Krylov/cyclic-heavy theorem: only the action-derived heavy directions reached from E₃₅ and E₁₂ through A need to be tested.


Gate ledger after §16.I.69.
PASS: exact evaluation of the §16.I.68 finite matrix-element gate by subgroup selection; distinction between adjoint action on C₀ and the bivector endomorphism K_{C₀}; exact reduction of the first surviving odd-heavy mechanism to one sandwiched matrix element.
NO-GO: m_C=0; direct linear-30380 BF Hodge transmission from E₃₅ to E₁₂ on the physical normal slice; direct [S,C₀] constitutive insertion on the same external pair; every C₀-dependent term generated by K=K₀+gK_{C₀} in K†M⁻¹K, even for noncommuting M.
OPEN: an action-derived odd heavy Hessian H_{odd}, especially [S,K_{C₀}], together with an even off-block transfer A=M₀⁻¹K₀ giving m_{heavy}≠0; the unresolved nontrivial 27000 route; positive-degree/base-Pfaffian activation; BRST ghost-number-zero survival; N₄>0; exactly one healthy spin-2.


Next exact calculation. Restrict the already-computed live Φ₂(C₀,C₀) heavy/B-sector operator and the admissible selector operators to the finite cyclic subspace generated from the two physical Lorentz lines E₃₅ and E₁₂. Determine whether the even map A=M₀⁻¹K₀ has any nonzero projection out of (1,15) into a K_{C₀}-active sector. If no such projection exists, the entire odd-heavy [S,C₀] escape closes. If it exists, compute the single sandwiched number m_{heavy}=-κ(AE₁₂,[S,K_{C₀}]AE₃₅) before reopening the unresolved 27000 multiplicity problem.




§16.I.70 — SO(16) Block-Parity Firewall, Closure of the Current Odd-Heavy Sandwich, and Return to the 27000 Gate


Scope. Section 16.I.69 showed that a nonzero odd-heavy sandwich requires an even transfer A=M₀⁻¹K₀ to move the physical Lorentz external lines into a sector on which K_{C₀} acts. Earlier Book-16 SO(16) branching data now make that requirement exact. On the presently earned physical 8008 condensate and quadratic zero-form source class, the required boson-spinor off-block is absent. This closes the current Φ₂-mediated odd-heavy escape without claiming a universal no-go against independently activated off-block fields.


Established SO(16) data. Book 16 already has
248↓SO(16)=120⊕128,
3875↓SO(16)=135⊕1820⊕1920,
and
30380↓SO(16)=120⊕1920⊕7020⊕8008⊕13312.
The physical antisymmetric spinor-pair condensate is the canonical
C₀∈8008⊂Λ²(128)⊂30380.
The physical conformal/Lorentz algebra, including E₃₅ and E₁₂, lies in the bosonic 120.


Theorem 16.I.70.1 — Bosonic-block annihilation strengthening. The skew endomorphism induced by the physical condensate satisfies
K_{C₀}|_{120}=0.


Proof. The canonical identification Λ²(248)≅so(248,κ) sends a bivector supported in Λ²(128) to a skew endomorphism whose only nonzero matrix block has both legs in the 128 sector. Since C₀ lies in the 8008 component of Λ²(128), it has no 120∧120 or 120∧128 support. Therefore K_{C₀} annihilates every vector in the 120. ∎


This strengthens §16.I.69.1 from the conformal (1,15) subalgebra to the full SO(16)-bosonic adjoint block.


Lemma 16.I.70.2 — The 1920 is the 3875 off-block carrier. Relative to 248=120⊕128, a Killing-self-adjoint endomorphism has diagonal blocks in Sym²(120) and Sym²(128), while its boson-spinor off-diagonal block lies in 120⊗128. Inside the 3875 branching, the only spinorial SO(16) summand is 1920. Hence the 1920 component is the only 3875 channel capable of 120↔128 mixing; the 135 and 1820 components are block preserving.


Theorem 16.I.70.3 — Quadratic-8008 source has no 1920 component. Any SO(16)-equivariant quadratic source built from C₀⊙C₀ with C₀∈8008 is tensorial under the Spin(16) center and therefore has no spinorial 1920 projection. In particular, on the physical 8008 branch,
π₁₉₂₀ Φ₁(C₀,C₀)=0,
π₁₉₂₀ Φ₂(C₀,C₀)=0.


Proof. The 8008 is a tensor representation, explicitly identified in Book 16 with Λ⁶(16). The symmetric square of a tensor representation has tensor center character. The 1920 is spinorial: it is the off-diagonal 120⊗128 representation inside the 3875. Therefore an equivariant map from Sym²(8008) to 1920 vanishes by center-character selection. ∎


Corollary 16.I.70.4 — Live Φ₂ even transfer is block preserving on the physical branch. The Killing-raised endomorphism supplied by Φ₂(C₀,C₀) has only 135⊕1820 support in the 3875 and hence preserves
120⊕128
blockwise. The same conclusion holds for any even operator obtained by sums, products, inverses on nonsingular subblocks, and spectral polynomials of the currently admitted block-preserving tensorial constitutive operators.


Theorem 16.I.70.5 — Current odd-heavy sandwich no-go. Let the even heavy transfer
A=M₀⁻¹K₀
be generated entirely from the presently admitted tensorial/block-preserving zero-form constitutive data, including the live Φ₂(C₀,C₀) 3875 response and the existing geometric 135/1820 selector/carrier operators. Then
A(120)⊂120.
For any odd heavy insertion of the immediate selector-condensate form
H_{odd}=[S,K_{C₀}]
with S block preserving on the same background,
κ(AE₁₂,H_{odd}AE₃₅)=0.
Hence
m_{heavy}=0
through this current source class.


Proof. E₁₂,E₃₅∈120 and block preservation gives AE₁₂,AE₃₅∈120. Theorem 16.I.70.1 then gives K_{C₀}AE₁₂=K_{C₀}AE₃₅=0. The commutator matrix element vanishes exactly as in §16.I.69.3. ∎


Corollary 16.I.70.6 — What would actually evade the firewall. A nonzero one-C₀ odd-heavy sandwich requires an independently earned operator with a genuine 120↔128 off-block before or after the C₀ insertion. Within a 3875-valued symmetric operator this means a nonzero 1920 component. Such a component cannot be sourced quadratically by the physical 8008 condensate alone. It would have to come from an independently activated spinorial/off-block field, a positive-degree background with the required SO(16) center character, or another microscopic parent not contained in the currently audited homogeneous tensorial zero-form class.


27000 consequence. The parity firewall does not kill a direct even 27000 constitutive channel acting wholly inside the bosonic 120. Such a channel does not need to pass through the C₀-active 128 block and can in principle supply an E₃₅→E₁₂ matrix element. Therefore the unresolved 27000 common-parent/multiplicity problem is no longer merely one option among comparable current routes: after the §16.I.69–70 no-gos it is the first remaining representation-level constitutive candidate within the existing bosonic zero-form architecture.


Gate ledger after §16.I.70.
PASS: stronger K_{C₀}|₁₂₀=0 support theorem; identification of 1920 as the 3875 boson-spinor off-block; center-parity proof that the physical quadratic 8008 source cannot populate 1920.
NO-GO ON THE CURRENT SOURCE CLASS: direct bare-C BF Hodge transfer; direct [S,C₀] transfer; bare-C Schur transfer; Φ₂/C₀²-mediated odd-heavy sandwich when all even constitutive maps are generated from the admitted tensorial block-preserving zero-form data.
OPEN ESCAPE OUTSIDE THAT CLASS: an independently activated 1920/off-block field or other spinorial-background resource.
PRIMARY LIVE REPRESENTATION GATE: the unresolved 27000 source, including m₂₇₀₀=dim Hom_E8(Sym²(30380),27000), construction of the common unprojected source tensor, and its physical 120-sector E₃₅→E₁₂ restriction.
DOWNSTREAM OPEN: action coefficient, positive-degree/base-Pfaffian activation, BRST survival, N₄>0, and exactly one healthy spin-2.


Next exact calculation. Compute or externally certify the E8 tensor-product multiplicity
m₂₇₀₀=dim Hom_E8(Sym²(30380),27000).
If m₂₇₀₀=0, the current quadratic-condensate 27000 route closes outright. If m₂₇₀₀>0, construct a basis of the independent quadratic 27000 maps, evaluate their restrictions on the physical 8008 normal slice, and test the finite matrix element
m₂₇:=κ(E₁₂,H₂₇(C₀,C₀)E₃₅).
Only a nonzero m₂₇ should be promoted to the next action-level coefficient gate.




§16.I.71 — Exact Two-Copy 27000 Multiplicity, Common-Source 3:4 Split, and Exhaustive Quadratic-Condensate Hodge No-Go


Scope. Section 16.I.70 reduced the surviving homogeneous zero-form search to the quadratic 30380²→27000 sector. The two questions left there can now be answered exactly: the symmetric multiplicity is two, and the same bracket-recoupled raw tensor whose 3875 projection is Φ₂ has a nonzero 27000 projection. Nevertheless, neither of the two independent quadratic 27000 maps supplies the missing physical E₁₂ Lorentz-Hodge plane. The quadratic-condensate 27000 route is therefore closed on the physical normal slice, while the August-25 common-preprojection-source question is positively resolved.


Theorem 16.I.71.1 — Exact symmetric 27000 multiplicity. In the frozen E8 highest-weight convention, an independent LieART tensor-product calculation gives two copies of the 27000 in 30380⊗30380. Combining the complete tensor-square decomposition with the already-established Book-16 facts


dim Hom_E8(Sym²(30380),3875)=2,
dim Hom_E8(Λ²(30380),3875)=0,


and the exact dimension


dim Sym²(30380)=30380·30381/2=461487390,


fixes the exchange parity uniquely. Both 27000 copies lie in the symmetric square:


boxed: dim Hom_E8(Sym²(30380),27000)=2,
boxed: dim Hom_E8(Λ²(30380),27000)=0.


One corresponding exact symmetric-square decomposition is


Sym²(30380)=1⊕2·3875⊕2·27000⊕147250⊕779247⊕2·2450240⊕4096000⊕4881384⊕26411008⊕70680000⊕146325270⊕203205000.


The dimensions sum to 461487390. ∎


SO(16) audit. Independently,


27000↓SO(16)=1⊕128⊕1820⊕5304⊕6435⊕13312,


while


Sym²(120)=1⊕135⊕1820⊕5304,
Sym²(128)=1⊕1820⊕6435,
120⊗128=128⊕1920⊕13312.


Thus the direct bosonic 120→120 part of a 27000 can use only 1⊕1820⊕5304. The scalar 1 cannot provide an off-diagonal E₃₅↔E₁₂ matrix element; the physically relevant direct-Hodge test therefore lies in the 1820/5304 content.


Definition 16.I.71.A — The two canonical quadratic 27000 maps. Retain the two already-established raw quadratic tensors


Q^(1)_MN=C_MP C_N{}^P,
Q^(2)_MN=f_M{}^{PQ}f_N{}^{RS}C_PR C_QS,


with


Φ_r=P_3875 Q^(r).


Define


Ψ_r=P_27000 Q^(r),  r=1,2.


Both Ψ_r are E8-equivariant symmetric quadratic maps into 27000.


Lemma 16.I.71.2 — First-map Hodge zero. On the physical 8008⊂Λ²(128) normal condensate, Q^(1) has support only in the spinor-spinor symmetric block. Hence for the physical dilation D∈120,


Q^(1)D=0.


Its E8 singlet projection acts on D by a scalar multiple of D, and the already-audited 3875 projection satisfies


Φ₁D∝W,
W=(0,1,1,2,2,2,2,2).


The physical missing-plane functional is


X₁₂(h)=h₁−2h₇+2h₈.


One has


X₁₂(D)=0,
X₁₂(W)=0.


Since Q^(1)=P₁Q^(1)+Φ₁+Ψ₁,


boxed: X₁₂(Ψ₁D)=0.


Therefore the first quadratic 27000 intertwiner cannot supply the E₁₂ Lorentz-Hodge partner. ∎


Theorem 16.I.71.3 — Exact raw bracket-recoupled action and common-source split. Let


A=aa',  B=bb'.


Reconstruct Q^(2) from the frozen 240-root ordering, canonical Chevalley signs, exact bracket, η=B_Killing/60, and the four sparse normal wedges. The calculation reproduces the manuscript certificate of exactly 27 ordered nonzero entries and exact symmetry. Its Killing-raised action on the physical dilation line is


Q^(2)D=
−(
4(A+B),
2(3A+2B),
2(4A+3B),
4(3A+2B),
2(5A+3B),
4(2A+B),
2(3A+B),
4A
).


The invariant singlet trace of Q^(2) is zero on this normal slice. Comparing the displayed vector with the already-published §16.I.53.3 coefficients gives the exact projector relation


boxed: Φ₂D=(3/7)Q^(2)D,
boxed: Ψ₂D=(4/7)Q^(2)D=(4/3)Φ₂D.


On the E8(-24)-real slice A=|a|², B=|b|². Since the first component of Q^(2)D is −4(A+B), Ψ₂ is nonzero on every nonzero physical normal condensate. Thus the August-25 common-source existence question is closed positively: the same unprojected bracket-recoupled tensor Q^(2) has simultaneous nonzero 3875 and 27000 projections with fixed physical D-transfer ratio 3:4. ∎


Corollary 16.I.71.4 — Fixed physical P/K reduction of the common source. Because the physical transfer matrix G is linear in the D-action vector,


G_Ψ2=(4/3)G_Φ2,
G_Q2=(7/3)G_Φ2.


Using §16.I.53,


G_Φ2=(12/7)
[[-Σ_I,0,0,0],
 [0,-Σ_I,0,0],
 [0,0,-Σ_I,Δ_I],
 [0,0,-Δ_I,Σ_I]],


so


G_Ψ2=(16/7)
[[-Σ_I,0,0,0],
 [0,-Σ_I,0,0],
 [0,0,-Σ_I,Δ_I],
 [0,0,-Δ_I,Σ_I]].


The common 27000 projection is therefore genuinely nonzero but is collinear with the already-earned 3875 physical transfer. It changes normalization, not the Lorentz bivector plane.


Theorem 16.I.71.5 — Second-map Hodge zero. Applying X₁₂ to the exact raw vector gives identically


X₁₂(Q^(2)D)=0.


The §16.I.54 audit already gives


X₁₂(Φ₂D)=0.


Since the singlet trace is zero and Ψ₂=Q^(2)−Φ₂,


boxed: X₁₂(Ψ₂D)=0.


Equivalently, the nonzero Ψ₂ transfer retains the same direct E₃₅ plane and does not populate E₁₂. ∎


Lemma 16.I.71.6 — Ψ₁ and Ψ₂ are independent. On the physical real sample a=a'=1, b=b'=2, the exact projected D-actions include


Ψ₁D:
H₁=−10/31,  H₈=58/217,


while


Ψ₂D:
H₁=−80/7,  H₈=−16/7.


Thus


(H₁/H₈)_Ψ1=−35/29,
(H₁/H₈)_Ψ2=5,


so the two output vectors are not proportional. Hence Ψ₁ and Ψ₂ are linearly independent E8 intertwiners. Together with Theorem 16.I.71.1 they form a basis of


Hom_E8(Sym²(30380),27000). ∎


Theorem 16.I.71.7 — Exhaustive quadratic-condensate 27000 Lorentz-Hodge no-go. Every E8-equivariant symmetric quadratic map


Ψ:Sym²(30380)→27000


has the form


Ψ=uΨ₁+vΨ₂.


On the physical normal 8008 slice,


X₁₂(ΨD)=uX₁₂(Ψ₁D)+vX₁₂(Ψ₂D)=0.


Therefore no quadratic 30380-condensate source in the complete 27000 Hom space supplies the missing E₁₂ Lorentz-Hodge partner of the direct E₃₅ plane.


This is an exhaustive no-go for the quadratic-condensate 27000 class. It is not a universal no-go against arbitrary 27000 fields, positive-degree backgrounds, independently activated spinorial/off-block sources, or higher-order microscopic parents. ∎


Corollary 16.I.71.8 — Common-parent synchronization without obstruction complementarity. The single unprojected source Q^(2) does solve the August-25 common-preprojection question in the limited sense that one parent simultaneously generates Φ₂ and Ψ₂ with a fixed 3:4 physical D-transfer split. A canonical invariant of the form


I_Q2BB=g_Q2∫⟨Q^(2)(C,C),B⊙B⟩


decomposes orthogonally into its 3875 and 27000 projector pieces with one common parent coefficient. However, because G_Ψ2 is proportional to G_Φ2 and both have X₁₂=0, this synchronization does not satisfy obstruction complementarity for the Lorentz curvature-Hessian rank gate. It can correlate channel normalizations, but it cannot manufacture the missing Hodge plane.


Gate ledger after §16.I.71.
PASS: m_27000=2 exactly; both copies are symmetric; complete SO(16) branching of 27000; explicit two-map basis Ψ₁,Ψ₂; nonzero common 27000 projection of Q^(2); exact common-source 3:4 split on D; fixed G_Ψ2=(4/3)G_Φ2.

CLOSED SEARCH CLASS: all homogeneous quadratic zero-form condensate maps through 3875 or 27000 as the source of the missing Lorentz-Hodge plane.
OPEN OUTSIDE THAT CLASS: an independently activated boson-spinor off-block resource, especially a 1920/other spinorial constitutive field; a positive-degree background capable of producing such off-block transfer; or another higher/nonquadratic microscopic parent not reduced to the audited C₀² maps.
DOWNSTREAM OPEN: base/exterior Pfaffian activation, BRST ghost-number-zero survival, N₄>0, and exactly one healthy physical spin-2.


Next exact calculation. Determine the minimal E8/SO(16) representation content capable of evading the block-parity firewall. In particular test whether the first spinorial resource already present in the adjoint, 128, can combine with the physical tensorial condensate 8008 to source the required 3875 off-block 1920 (or the 27000 off-block 128/13312), and compute the relevant tensor-product multiplicities. If no such low-degree off-block source exists, the current local polynomial escape class narrows again before any BRST calculation is attempted.




§16.I.72 — Quasi-Linear Hodge Descent, Escape from the Fixed-Polynomial Firewall, and the Single-Coefficient Transmission Gate


Scope. Sections 16.I.67–71 close the searched fixed-polynomial zero-form routes to the missing Lorentz-Hodge plane: the complete quadratic 30380²→3875 and 30380²→27000 spaces have zero E₁₂ functional on the physical normal slice, and the first bare-C/Schur/off-block variants do not repair that failure. Those no-gos do not apply to the already-earned quasi-linear branch of §§16.I.52, 16.I.56, 16.I.59, and 16.I.60. The quasi-linear branch is generated by half-rapidity E8 transport / inverse-qSaw and therefore is not another homogeneous polynomial intertwiner of the condensate. It must be tested separately.


Definition 16.I.72.A — Doubled-rung microscopic state. On the scalar-null physical condensate branch write


Σ_I=|a|²+|b|²,
Δ_I=|a|²−|b|²,
μ=2|a||b|>0,
Π:=Δ_I/Σ_I,
c:=μ/Σ_I.


Then


Π²+c²=1,
Π=tanh(2w),
c=sech(2w).


The direct bracket-recoupled Lorentz source of §16.I.53 is proportional to Δ_I E₃₅ and therefore carries the completed/doubled angular coordinate Π after normalization by Σ_I.


Definition 16.I.72.B — Canonical inverse-qSaw / half-rapidity descent. The exact positive-chart descent of §16.I.59 is


Q=Π/(1+c)=tanh w,
S=sqrt[2c/(1+c)]=sech w,
Q²+S²=1.


The orientation sign ε_P=sgn Im(a b̄) supplies the signed second coordinate ε_P S. This map contains a projective denominator and a square root when expressed in the doubled-rung quadratic invariants. It is the projective shadow of the group-level half transport g(w)=exp[-(w/2)ad D], not an ordinary matrix square root and not a polynomial map Sym²(30380)→R.


Theorem 16.I.72.1 — Fixed-polynomial firewall separation. The no-go theorems of §§16.I.67–71 constrain fixed local polynomial maps built directly from C₀, C₀², and the audited finite heavy insertions. The descended pair (Q,ε_P S) is state-dependent and quasi-linear in the projective sense above. Therefore those no-go theorems neither produce nor forbid the descended Hodge response. Using the quasi-linear branch is not a counterexample to the polynomial no-go; it lies outside that hypothesis class. ∎


Theorem 16.I.72.2 — Unique quasi-linear Saw–Hodge completion. Section 16.I.56 already proves


dim_R Hom_D4(V_Saw,V_PK)=1,


for


V_Saw=span_R{Q,S},
V_PK=span_R{E₃₅,E₁₂},


with the quarter-turn and reflection actions identified. Hence every nonzero D4-equivariant transfer from the descended raw Saw circle to the Lorentz-Hodge plane has the form


A_QL(w)=ρ_PK [ Q E₃₅ + ε_P S E₁₂ ]


up to the fixed overall orientation/basis sign convention.


Thus the missing E₁₂ coefficient is not an independent angular function once the quasi-linear carrier is admitted. It is the second component of the same one-parameter D4 intertwiner as the E₃₅ component. ∎


Theorem 16.I.72.3 — Quasi-linear internal-rank formula. Using *_L E₃₅=−E₁₂ and *_L E₁₂=E₃₅, the two displayed bivectors are complementary simple Lorentz planes. Therefore, up to the fixed nonzero convention sign,


Pf(A_QL)=ρ_PK² ε_P Q S,


det(A_QL)=ρ_PK⁴ Q²S².


In doubled-rung microscopic variables,


Q²S² = [Π²/(1+c)²][2c/(1+c)]
      = 2Π²c/(1+c)³.


Hence


boxed: det(A_QL)=ρ_PK⁴ · 2Π²c/(1+c)³.


For every finite scalar-null condensate with μ>0 one has c>0. Therefore


boxed: rank A_QL=4 iff ρ_PK≠0 and Π≠0,


that is, iff the common quasi-linear transfer is activated and the physical intensities are not exactly balanced. At Π=0 the E₃₅ leg vanishes and the internal antisymmetric block has rank two; in the asymptotic |w|→∞ limit S→0 and the Hodge leg disappears. ∎


Corollary 16.I.72.4 — The quadratic E₁₂ zero does not kill the quasi-linear rank branch. The exact results


X₁₂(Φ₁D)=X₁₂(Φ₂D)=X₁₂(Ψ₁D)=X₁₂(Ψ₂D)=0


say that the doubled-rung fixed polynomial source has no literal E₁₂ component. They do not imply S=0 after half-rapidity descent. On every finite scalar-null branch, S=sech w>0. Therefore a nonzero quasi-linear transmission coefficient ρ_PK produces a nonzero E₁₂ partner even though every fixed quadratic source has zero E₁₂ functional.


Definition 16.I.72.C — One coefficient, not two. The quasi-linear completion changes the action-level question. One no longer seeks unrelated microscopic coefficients α₃₅ and β₁₂ generated by separate polynomial intertwiners. The required action parent must transmit the entire descended D4 doublet with one common coefficient ρ_PK. Once the E₃₅ leg is identified as the Q component of that transported doublet, D4 equivariance fixes the E₁₂ leg and its relative state dependence.


The normalized doubled-rung direct source already has the exact ancestry


(7/12) G_[35]/Σ_I = Π = qSaw(Q).


Thus the canonical inverse-qSaw map sends the known direct angular coordinate to Q. The remaining microscopic issue is not representation availability but action placement: does the same BF/PCBF parent that carries the direct E₃₅ source implement the already-earned half-rapidity transport and D4 Saw–Hodge intertwiner with one nonzero coefficient?


Relation to the earlier successful quasi-linear escape. Section 16.I.60.8 established that the finite-invertible-heavy determinant no-go is evaded by


boxed: singular matched kernel + quasi-linear transport,


with the same singular carrier in BF and BB terms and with rapidity moving the physical kernel while preserving the full generalized spectrum. The present Hodge problem has the same logical structure. The fixed polynomial truncation erases the complementary response; the quasi-linear transported carrier can recover a moving physical response without contradicting the fixed-truncation theorem. No inverse of a singular heavy kernel is required.


Gate ledger after §16.I.72.
PASS: exact separation between the fixed-polynomial no-go class and the quasi-linear half-transport class; exact inverse-qSaw formulas; unique D4 Saw–Hodge angular form; exact internal determinant/rank formula in both raw and doubled variables.
CONDITIONAL PASS: if one microscopic parent transmits the descended D4 doublet with ρ_PK≠0, the internal Lorentz-Hodge rank gate is passed for every finite nonbalanced scalar-null branch.
NO LONGER REQUIRED: an independent polynomial C₀²→E₁₂ map or a separate angular function for the Hodge leg.
OPEN: derivation of a nonzero single-parent ρ_PK in the BF/PCBF action; proof that the same half-rapidity transport acts in the antisymmetric Lorentz curvature channel rather than only in the coframe-label carrier; positive-degree/base-Pfaffian activation; BRST ghost-number-zero survival; N₄>0; exactly one healthy spin-2.


Next exact calculation. Do not continue the fixed-polynomial 30380²/27000 search. Instead restrict the already-earned full-E8 quasi-linear carrier Kcal_w=−(1/480)G E(w), together with the actual BF/PCBF parent contraction, to the physical antisymmetric Lorentz curvature sector. Test whether its descended response is proportional to


Q E₃₅ + ε_P S E₁₂


with one nonzero coefficient. If yes, ρ_PK is earned and the remaining curvature-Hessian gate is the exterior Pfaffian F∧F≠0. If the E₁₂ response still vanishes after this transported action-level restriction, then the quasi-linear escape itself—not merely the polynomial source class—fails for the Lorentz-Hodge gate.




§16.I.73 — Direct Application of the Quasi-Linear Technique: Trace-Weighted Matched-Kernel PCBF Response


Scope. Section 16.I.72 identified the correct escape class but stopped before applying it to the actual first-order BF/PCBF structure. This section performs that application. Two preliminary exact results matter. First, plain similarity transport of the quasi-linear carrier does not rotate the physical Lorentz 35 plane into its Hodge 12 partner. Second, the unweighted matched-kernel PCBF parent cancels the carrier out of the auxiliary B equation and therefore cannot by itself produce the completed D4 response pair. The minimal quasi-linear repair is to weight the BF numerator and BB Hessian by the two trace-pair polynomials already generated by the same transported carrier. This preserves the singular matched kernel and returns the exact completed response ratios Π_C and Ξ_C on shell.


16.I.73.1 — Plain transported-carrier Hodge no-go.


From §16.I.60.8 the Killing-raised quasi-linear carrier T_w has the exact bosonic spectrum


lambda=0 on L∧a(w),
lambda=1/4 on U∧a(w),
lambda=3/4 on Λ²L,
lambda=1 on U⊗L,
lambda=5/4 on Λ²U.


The physical Lorentz bivectors E₃₅ and E₁₂ lie in the Lorentz-generator block Λ²L. Hence


T_w E₃₅=(3/4)E₃₅,
T_w E₁₂=(3/4)E₁₂.


Therefore similarity transport of T_w cannot mix E₃₅ and E₁₂. The half-rapidity carrier is a moving P/K-normal selector, not a Lorentz-Hodge rotation operator.


The same conclusion follows from the moving coframe brackets. With


E_mu(w)=e^{-w/2}P_mu+e^{w/2}K_mu,
N_mu(w)=e^{-w/2}P_mu-e^{w/2}K_mu,


and the standard conformal brackets, the products of rapidity factors cancel:


[E_mu(w),E_nu(w)] ∝ M_mu nu,
[N_mu(w),N_nu(w)] ∝ -M_mu nu,
[E_mu(w),N_nu(w)] ∝ eta_mu nu D.


Thus the non-Abelian curvature generated purely by transporting the P/K legs changes the moving normal/coframe splitting but does not generate a new Lorentz-Hodge bivector plane by itself. This closes the naive reading of §16.I.72 in which exp[-(w/2)ad D] would directly rotate E₃₅ into E₁₂.


16.I.73.2 — Unweighted matched-kernel response cancellation.


The §16.I.60.8 matched-kernel parent has the form


S_0=∫[beta B^T∧Kcal_w F-(alpha/2)B^T∧Kcal_w B].


Its B equation is


Kcal_w(beta F-alpha B)=0.


Modulo the exact null shift generated by the transported kernel projector Pi_0(T_w), one obtains on im Kcal_w


B_im=(beta/alpha)F_im.


Therefore the same Kcal_w appearing in BF and BB cancels from the response equation. The unweighted parent preserves the singular matched-kernel escape and its healthy null-shift structure, but it does not reconstruct Π_C or Ξ_C. Merely inserting the quasi-linear carrier into both slots is insufficient.


16.I.73.3 — Exact trace-pair numerator and Hessian polynomials.


Use the unnormalized transported rank-one P/K carrier of §16.I.60.9,


Khat_w=(1/2)(A I+B Z+E X),
A=cosh w,
B=sinh w,
E=epsilon_P,
A²-B²=1,
E²=1.


Define for R in {Z,X}


N_R:=2 tr(R Khat_w²),
D_R:=tr(Khat_w²)+tr(Khat_w R Khat_w R).


Direct two-by-two evaluation gives


tr(Khat_w²)=A²,
N_Z=2AB,
D_Z=A²+B²,
N_X=2AE,
D_X=A²+E².


Hence


N_Z/D_Z=tanh(2w)=Π_C,
N_X/D_X=2 epsilon_P cosh w/(cosh²w+1)=Ξ_C.


Equivalently,


Π_C=qSaw(Q)=2Q/(1+Q²),
Ξ_C=qSaw(epsilon_P S)=2 epsilon_P S/(1+S²),


with Q=tanh w and S=sech w. The two completed D4 response coordinates are therefore exact numerator/Hessian ratios built from one and the same transported carrier.


16.I.73.4 — Trace-weighted matched-kernel PCBF parent.


On the already-selected broken-Lorentz Hodge response plane introduce the two B-channel amplitudes B_Z and B_X, corresponding respectively to the direct E₃₅ line and its covariant Lorentz-Hodge partner E₁₂. Let F_Z and F_X denote the corresponding curvature two-form channels; on the favorable shared-form branch they are carried by one base two-form F with internal anchors E₃₅ and E₁₂=−*_L E₃₅.


Consider the quasi-linear first-order parent


S_QL-PCBF
=Σ_{R=Z,X} ∫[
 beta N_R B_R^T∧Kcal_w F_R
 -(alpha/2) D_R B_R^T∧Kcal_w B_R
].


The scalar weights N_R and D_R are generated algebraically from Khat_w; the same singular full-E8 carrier Kcal_w remains in both BF and BB slots. Therefore the exact transported kernel and null-shift symmetry are unchanged.


The B_R equations are


Kcal_w(beta N_R F_R-alpha D_R B_R)=0.


Modulo the same null shift, on im Kcal_w,


B_R=(beta/alpha)(N_R/D_R)F_R.


Hence exactly


boxed: B_Z=(beta/alpha) Π_C F_Z,
boxed: B_X=(beta/alpha) Ξ_C F_X.


This is the direct application of the earlier quasi-linear technique to the PCBF response problem: the fixed carrier remains singular and matched, while different polynomial numerator and Hessian weights generated by that carrier survive the auxiliary equation as a nontrivial rational response.


The construction uses no inverse of Kcal_w. The only divisions are by the scalar Hessian factors D_Z and D_X, which are strictly positive for finite real w:


D_Z=cosh²w+sinh²w>0,
D_X=cosh²w+1>0.


Thus the finite-invertible-heavy determinant no-go remains untouched; the singular matched-kernel mechanism is retained exactly.


16.I.73.5 — Shared-form Lorentz-Hodge rank pass at reduced action level.


On the shared-form branch write the on-shell internal B response as


B_phys=(beta/alpha)[Π_C E₃₅+Ξ_C E₁₂]F.


Since E₁₂ is the Lorentz Hodge partner of E₃₅,


*_L E₃₅=-E₁₂,
*_L E₁₂=E₃₅,


one obtains, up to the fixed orientation sign,


Pf_internal(B_phys/F)=(beta/alpha)² Π_C Ξ_C,


det_internal(B_phys/F)=(beta/alpha)^4 Π_C² Ξ_C².


For every finite w,


Ξ_C=2 epsilon_P cosh w/(cosh²w+1)


is nonzero. Therefore


boxed: rank_internal B_phys=4 iff beta/alpha !=0 and w!=0.


Equivalently the only finite interior rank loss is the exactly balanced branch Π_C=0. The asymptotic |w|→∞ boundary also loses the Hodge leg because Ξ_C→0.


In terms of c=sech(2w),


Π_C² Ξ_C² = 8 c(1-c)(1+c)²/(1+3c)².


This is positive for 0<c<1 and vanishes only at the balanced point c=1 and the asymptotic boundary c=0.


16.I.73.6 — Direct-channel normalization match.


The certified bracket-recoupled quadratic source of §16.I.53 has


G_[35]=(12/7) Delta_I=(12/7) Sigma_I Π_C.


Therefore, if the Z response channel of S_QL-PCBF is identified with that already-certified direct microscopic source, the common reduced radial normalization is fixed by matching to


A_rad=(12/7) Sigma_I


up to the pre-existing overall BF orientation/action convention. D4 covariance then fixes the companion reduced Hodge response to


G_[12]^(QL)=A_rad Ξ_C
=(12/7) Sigma_I Ξ_C,


again up to the same fixed orientation sign.


This is not a second fitted coefficient. It is the quasi-linear response prediction obtained by matching the common D4 doublet to the already-known direct leg. What remains unproved is that the complete microscopic E8(-24) action realizes precisely this trace-weighted parent rather than setting its common coupling to zero or adding an uncontrolled orthogonal channel.


16.I.73.7 — Exterior-rank firewall.


The preceding pass is an internal Lorentz-Hodge response theorem. A common spacetime two-form F still has to satisfy


F∧F !=0


for the shared-form curvature/coframe Hessian to have full exterior rank. If the B route is used directly, the corresponding positive-degree background must likewise be nondecomposable. The quasi-linear response solves neither positive-degree activation nor exterior Pfaffian generation by itself.


16.I.73.8 — Full-E8 provenance gate after the reduced pass.


The trace-weighted construction is exact on the already-earned physical P/K and Lorentz-Hodge quotient. Its ingredients have partial full-E8 ancestry: Kcal_w comes from the full-E8 transported Chayet-Garibaldi carrier; the Z response is tied to the D grading; the X response is tied to the invariant P/K Killing pairing used in the lift. However the complete microscopic action-level covariantization of the two response functionals N_Z,D_Z,N_X,D_X, including their transverse extension and their placement into one E8(-24)-invariant PCBF parent, is not yet proved.


Accordingly the result is stronger than the §16.I.72 ansatz but remains one gate short of microscopic closure.


Gate ledger after §16.I.73.
PASS: plain Kcal_w transport does not directly rotate E₃₅ to E₁₂; unweighted matched-kernel response cancellation identified exactly; exact trace-pair numerator/Hessian formulas; exact trace-weighted matched-kernel B equations; reduced quasi-linear response B_Z∝Π_C and B_X∝Ξ_C; reduced internal rank-four pass for finite nonbalanced states; direct-channel normalization match fixes the relative Hodge response without a second coefficient.
NO-GO: naive similarity transport as Lorentz Hodge rotation; unweighted matched BF/BB kernel as a source of Π_C/Ξ_C response ratios.
OPEN MICROSCOPIC GATE: one full-E8(-24) PCBF invariant whose restriction is the trace-weighted matched-kernel doublet above, with nonzero common coefficient and no orthogonal contamination.
DOWNSTREAM OPEN: lawful positive-degree activation; F∧F!=0 or equivalent B∧B!=0; BRST survival; N4>0; exactly one healthy physical spin-2; absolute mirror selection.


Next exact calculation. Build the full-E8 covariant extensions of the two trace-pair response operators using only the already-earned transported carrier E(w), the Killing form, the D grading, and the physical selector data. Then contract those scalar weights into the full adjoint B∧F and B∧B invariants and test whether the resulting parent reduces to S_QL-PCBF with one common D4 coefficient. If yes, the microscopic Lorentz-Hodge coefficient gate is closed; the next independent obstruction is positive-degree/exterior Pfaffian activation.




§16.I.74 — Full-E8 Selector-Covariant Quasi-Linear Hodge Parent and Relative-Coefficient Closure


Scope and correction. Section 16.I.73 is a reduced PCBF response theorem. Its trace-pair ratios are exact, but the notation B_Z,B_X still assigns the second reduced response to the Lorentz-Hodge E12 direction after the physical split. Book 16 already forbids promoting such a post-split *_L insertion to a microscopic derivation unless the Hodge direction itself descends from lawful full-E8 data. The present section supplies that missing typed lift. The construction is field-dependent and selector-covariant; it does not contradict the fixed field-independent E8-linear quarter-turn no-go. It proves existence of one full-E8-hosted single-parent completion and fixes the Hodge/direct relative coefficient once that parent is activated. It does not force the overall action coupling or positive-degree flux to be nonzero.


16.I.74.1 — Relational-trace necessity theorem.


Let


T_w:=-(1/480)E(w),
E(w)=exp[-(w/2)ad D]E_0.


Any full-adjoint trace formed only from T_w, the Killing form, and powers of T_w is independent of w because T_w is conjugate to T_0. Inserting ad D alone does not restore w-dependence, because the transport is generated by D and hence [ad D,exp(-(w/2)ad D)]=0. Therefore the completed response scalars Pi_C and Xi_C cannot arise from conjugation-invariant traces of T_w alone.


Section 16.I.60.12 supplies the necessary second relational datum: a compensated Spin(12,4) involution g_X whose adjoint action Xcal:=Ad(g_X) obeys on the certified physical sector


Xcal P_mu=K_mu,
Xcal K_mu=P_mu,
Xcal D=-D.


Hence, on the physical P/K label plane,


Xcal|_(P/K)=X,
(ad D)|_(P/K)=Z,
X Z+Z X=0.


The noncommuting exchange involution is therefore the minimal already-earned datum capable of making the trace response depend on rapidity. ∎


16.I.74.2 — Exact physical P/K projector from the moving kernel.


The exact kernel projector of §16.I.60.8 is


Pi_0(T)=((T-I)(4T-5I)(4T-3I)(4T-I))/15.


For every w,


im Pi_0(T_w)=span{N_mu(w)},
N_mu(w)=e^{-w/2}P_mu-e^{w/2}K_mu.


Since


(ad D)N_mu(w)=e^{-w/2}P_mu+e^{w/2}K_mu=:E_mu(w),


and E_mu(w) is the retained coframe eigenline with T_w eigenvalue one, define


boxed:
Pi_PK:=Pi_0(T_w)+(ad D)Pi_0(T_w)(ad D).


On each physical P/K doublet, Pi_0 is the null-line projector and its D-conjugate is the complementary retained-line projector. Their product vanishes and their sum is the identity on that doublet. Globally, ker T_w has exactly dimension four and the D-conjugate image has dimension four, so


Pi_PK^2=Pi_PK,
rank Pi_PK=8.


Thus Pi_PK projects onto precisely span{P_mu,K_mu}_{mu=1,2,3,5} and contains no transverse E8 state. Although built from the moving kernel, the selected eight-dimensional P/K span is independent of w. ∎


16.I.74.3 — Full-adjoint trace-pair lift.


Define the normalized physical partial trace


tau_PK(A):=(1/4)Tr_248(Pi_PK A Pi_PK).


The factor 1/4 removes the four identical Lorentz-direction copies and leaves the two-by-two label trace. Define


Zcal:=Pi_PK(ad D)Pi_PK,
Xcal_PK:=Pi_PK Xcal Pi_PK,
Khatcal_w:=Pi_PK Xcal T_w Pi_PK,


with the inherited orientation branch understood. On each physical P/K label doublet,


Khatcal_w -> (1/2)(A I+B Z+E X),
A=cosh w,
B=sinh w,
E=epsilon_P.


Now define the full-adjoint scalar contractions


N_Z:=2 tau_PK(Zcal Khatcal_w^2),
D_Z:=tau_PK(Khatcal_w^2)+tau_PK(Khatcal_w Zcal Khatcal_w Zcal),


N_X:=2 tau_PK(Xcal_PK Khatcal_w^2),
D_X:=tau_PK(Khatcal_w^2)+tau_PK(Khatcal_w Xcal_PK Khatcal_w Xcal_PK).


Their physical reductions are exactly


N_Z=2AB,
D_Z=A^2+B^2,
N_X=2AE,
D_X=A^2+E^2,


and therefore


boxed: N_Z/D_Z=Pi_C,
boxed: N_X/D_X=Xi_C.


Under simultaneous E8 conjugation of T_w, D, Xcal, and Pi_PK, these are scalar traces. They are therefore full-E8 selector-covariant response functions, not fixed traces of a single conjugacy orbit. This resolves the full-lift problem for the angular scalar pair. ∎


16.I.74.4 — Exact Lorentz spectral projector from the same quasi-linear carrier.

with the 3/4 eigenspace exactly

boxed:

=(4/3) T_w(4T_w-I)(T_w-I)(4T_w-5I)


obeys


Pi_L^2=Pi_L,
rank Pi_L=6,

The polynomial evaluates to one at lambda=3/4 and zero at 0,1/4,1,5/4. Thus the physical Lorentz bivector sector is selected algebraically by the already-earned full-E8 quasi-linear carrier; no external fixed Lorentz projector is required. ∎


16.I.74.5 — Selector-derived Lorentz Hodge complex structure.


Let l:=im Pi_L. The D8 realization proves l is the physical real Lorentz algebra so(1,3). Book 16 already established


End_SO+(3,1)(Lambda^2 L)=span_R{I,*_L}.


Equivalently, the commutant of the adjoint l-action on l is two-dimensional. Therefore there is, up to sign, a unique nontrivial real endomorphism J_L on l satisfying


[J_L,ad_x]=0 for all x in l,
tr_l J_L=0,
J_L^2=-I_l.


The internal-normal relative orientation lock fixes the sign. Choose the Book-16 convention

Then

and under simultaneous E8 transport of the selector orbit it transforms by conjugation. Thus Jcal_L is a field-dependent E8-hosted Hodge operator derived from the selected Lorentz spectral subalgebra. It is not a forbidden fixed E8-equivariant linear quarter-turn on an irreducible 3875. The earlier Schur/Hom no-go is therefore preserved rather than evaded by fiat. ∎

§16.I.75 — Physical-Orbit Moment-Map Soldering, Topological Hard-Flux Rank Instability, and Premetric Quartic Stabilization


Scope. Section 16.I.74 closes the relative Lorentz-Hodge coefficient problem for one selector-covariant full-E8 parent, conditional on activation of its common action coefficient. The next obstruction is positive exterior degree and actual rank initiation. Section 16.I.64 already supplied the lawful covariant-condensate moment map J_C=(1/2)mu(D_A C wedge D_A C), but left three questions open: whether the physical orbit tangent norm s_C is nonzero, whether four physical tangent directions can define the coframe rather than merely an abstract derivative rank, and whether a pre-soldering action can make the zero-rank branch unstable. Those questions can now be separated and sharpened. The physical-orbit norm and coframe extraction close exactly. A protected hard curvature sector gives a split-signature quadratic instability. A selector-derived moment-map quartic supplies a lawful radial stabilizer. Dynamic selection of the nontrivial topological sector, the absolute signs of the remaining action coefficients, and the complete constrained/BRST spectrum remain open.


16.I.75.1 — Physical tangent norm closure.


Retain the physical E8(-24)-real condensate


C_0=a n_+ + b n_- + a_bar s_+ + b_bar s_-


in (126,6) plus (126bar,6), and the selected hard P/K generator


h_a=(P_a+epsilon_P K_a)/sqrt(2).


Section 16.I.65 reduces its action to


v_a:=h_a C_0 = c u tensor e_a + c_bar u_bar tensor e_a


up to one fixed nonzero normalization, where


c=u_epsilon a+v_epsilon b


with (u_epsilon,v_epsilon) a nonzero real row whenever T_N is real and invertible. The compact Spin(10) factor carries a positive Hermitian invariant form on the 126, while the real six-vector factor carries the Spin(4,2) metric. Consequently the unique real invariant bilinear on the conjugate pair restricts on this tensor line as


h_R(v_a,v_b)=gamma |c|^2 eta_ab


for one fixed gamma!=0 determined by normalization. Therefore


boxed: s_C=gamma |c|^2.


By Theorem 16.I.65.1,


chi_C=2 Im(a b_bar)!=0  implies  c!=0.


Hence


boxed: chi_C!=0  implies  s_C!=0


conditional only on the already-stated real invertible normal-plane identification T_N in GL(2,R). The earlier indefinite-form caution is resolved on this specific physical tensor line: indefiniteness resides in eta_ab, not in a vanishing scalar prefactor. ∎


16.I.75.2 — Derived physical coframe from the condensate derivative.


Let


V_C=span_R{v_a}


be the four-dimensional physical hard-orbit tangent subspace. Since its Gram matrix is s_C eta_ab with s_C!=0, define the four one-forms


boxed:
e^a=(1/s_C) eta^{ab} h_R(v_b,D_A C).


Then the physical-orbit projection of the condensate derivative is exactly


P_VC(D_A C)=v_a e^a.


Under the residual Lorentz stabilizer, v_a transforms as a Lorentz vector and therefore e^a transforms as the dual Lorentz-vector coframe. No spacetime metric or spacetime Hodge star enters this definition.


Define


r_phys=rank(P_VC D_A C:T_pM->V_C).


Then


boxed: r_phys=4  iff  e^1 wedge e^2 wedge e^3 wedge e^5 !=0.


Accordingly, on the already-selected hard P_cf orbit,


boxed: r_phys=4  implies  N_4>0


for the derived physical coframe. This does not alter the older generic firewall: an arbitrary rank-four derivative in the full 30380 need not lie in V_C and therefore generic r_C=4 still does not imply soldering. The implication is specific to the nondegenerate physical hard-orbit projection. ∎


16.I.75.3 — Exact physical Lorentz moment-map coefficient.


The moment map obeys


kappa(mu(u,v),X)=h_R(X dot u,v).


On V_C the Lorentz generators act in the vector representation,


M_cd dot v_a proportional to eta_ac v_d-eta_ad v_c.


Therefore


kappa(mu(v_a,v_b),M_cd)
proportional to
s_C(eta_ac eta_bd-eta_ad eta_bc).


Since the Killing form restricted to the Lorentz algebra has exactly the same invariant tensor structure with nonzero normalization kappa_L, uniqueness gives


boxed:
P_L mu(v_a,v_b)=m_C M_ab,


with


m_C=lambda_L s_C,


where lambda_L!=0 is fixed by the generator/Killing normalization. Hence chi_C!=0 implies m_C!=0 on the physical branch.


It follows that


boxed:
P_L J_C=(m_C/2) M_ab e^a wedge e^b.


Thus the physical moment-map source is not merely nonzero: it is the canonical Lorentz-valued two-form quadratic in the derived coframe. ∎


16.I.75.4 — Nonseparable moment-map Hessian survives quasi-linear Hodge completion.


For the canonical local witness


R_0^{ab}=k theta^a wedge theta^b,


apply the Lorentz selector operator

Hence the nonseparable moment-map witness remains full rank for every p!=0; adding the Hodge response does not destroy the ambient rank witness. For the §16.I.74 quasi-linear coefficients p=Pi_C and q=Xi_C, every finite nonbalanced state Pi_C!=0 retains rank sixteen on this witness. ∎


16.I.75.5 — Homogeneous quartic no-scale theorem for self-activation.


The preceding results prove availability of the physical rank-four tangent and its nonseparable moment map. They do not by themselves select a nonzero amplitude. Consider a local homogeneous unitary-gauge ray with no independently protected positive-degree background and scale the physical hard-orbit derivative by one amplitude q:


D_A C|_VC proportional to q,
J_C proportional to q^2.


On a homogeneous connection ray dA=0, curvature generated by the same broken connection component also begins at order q^2. Every local premetric four-form in the current BF+BB+BJ_C polynomial class therefore scales, after algebraic elimination of B, as q^4 times a zero-form coefficient. The reduced stationarity equation has the form


q^3 Gamma(C_0,w,couplings)=0.


Thus generically q=0 remains the only isolated stationary amplitude. Gamma=0 produces a tuned/flat branch rather than a stabilized finite scale.


boxed: the physical moment-map route evades the exterior-rank representation obstruction but does not self-select a finite homogeneous amplitude from the completely trivial sector within the same local polynomial no-scale class.


This is the moment-map refinement of the earlier PCBF no-self-start theorem. ∎


16.I.75.6 — Hard topological curvature is the compatible upstream seed.


Book 16 already proves that a nonzero Chern-Weil charge


nu_Sigma=integral_{Sigma_4} kappa(F wedge F)!=0


forces nonzero curvature somewhere and, on the matched-kernel image branch, forces a nonzero hard B response. This seed is compatible with the null-shift firewall because the activation resides in curvature/image data rather than in an unrestricted kernel-valued soft B amplitude.


Independent Lie-group topology confirms that such principal-bundle sectors are mathematically available for the E8(-24) real form. Its maximal compact subgroup has Lie algebra e7 plus su2; a connected semisimple group has the homotopy type of its maximal compact subgroup; and each compact simple factor has pi_3=Z. The finite central quotient does not change pi_3. Therefore


boxed: pi_3(E8(-24))=Z plus Z.


Hence nontrivial four-dimensional E8(-24) bundle sectors exist. This is an availability theorem, not a dynamical sector-selection theorem: the action still has to explain why the realized charge is nonzero.


16.I.75.7 — Matched hard-flux/moment-map parent and quadratic rank instability.

 -(alpha/2) Delta_w kappa(B wedge T_w B)
 +g_J kappa(B wedge T_w Pi_L J_C)

T_w[beta Ncal_w F+g_J Pi_L J_C-alpha Delta_w B]=0.

proportional to

16.I.75.8 — Split-signature theorem for every full-Pfaffian shared-form seed.


Suppose locally


B_0=X F


with X a Lorentz bivector of nonzero internal Pfaffian and F a base two-form with F wedge F!=0. After identifying three-forms with one-forms using only the two chosen orientations, the Hessian factorizes as


H_{B_0}=K_X tensor L_F,


where K_X and L_F are invertible real 4x4 antisymmetric matrices. Put them in canonical skew blocks. Their complex eigenvalues are


plus/minus i a_1, plus/minus i a_2


and


plus/minus i b_1, plus/minus i b_2


with all a_i,b_j nonzero. Every pair (i,j) contributes two positive and two negative real tensor-product eigenvalues. Therefore


boxed: signature H_{B_0}=(8,8).


Consequently every full-Pfaffian shared-form seed makes the zero-coframe point a saddle. This statement is independent of the overall sign of the coupling: reversing the sign exchanges the positive and negative subspaces but preserves the (8,8) split.


Because the set of invertible 4x4 coframe matrices is open and dense while the negative cone of an (8,8) quadratic form is open and nonempty, there exist negative directions with det(e^a_mu)!=0. Thus the instability contains genuinely nondegenerate coframe directions; it is not confined to rank-deficient perturbations. ∎


16.I.75.9 — Explicit protected shared-form witness.

F_0=E_12 f,


and


integral kappa(F_0 wedge F_0)
=kappa(E_12,E_12) integral f wedge f
!=0.

up to the fixed Hodge/orientation convention. For finite nonbalanced w, Pi_C Xi_C!=0, so the internal Pfaffian is nonzero; nonzero self-intersection forces f wedge f!=0 somewhere. Therefore at every such point


boxed: rank H_{B_0}=16,
boxed: signature H_{B_0}=(8,8).


This is an explicit global-to-local rank-instability witness. It does not assert that every base admits such a U(1) reduction, nor that the unified action selects that topological class. More general nonabelian E8(-24) topological sectors remain available through the pi_3 result above. ∎


16.I.75.10 — Premetric quartic volume from the same moment map.


The quadratic instability needs stabilization. The selector-derived Lorentz complex structure of §16.I.74 permits the local four-form


I_J4=lambda_J integral kappa(Pi_L J_C wedge Jcal_L Pi_L J_C).


This term is lawful before a spacetime metric exists: J_C is an adjoint-valued two-form and Jcal_L is an internal selector-derived endomorphism. On the physical tangent,

while the invariant contraction with Jcal_L is proportional to the Lorentz epsilon tensor. Hence


boxed:

for one nonzero normalization Lambda_C proportional to lambda_J m_C^2.


Thus the same condensate moment map that supplies the quadratic Palatini channel also admits a premetric quartic volume stabilizer, with no spacetime Hodge star inserted by hand. ∎


16.I.75.11 — Radial finite-rank stabilization witness.


Choose any full-rank negative direction e_bar in the split-signature quadratic cone and scale


e^a=q e_bar^a.


Along that ray the reduced action begins as


V(q)=A q^2+B q^4+O(q^6),


with A<0 supplied by the hard topological rank seed and
