On the orientation branch for which B>0,


boxed: q_*^2=-A/(2B)>0


is a radially stable finite nonzero stationary amplitude. Since e_bar is invertible, this radial witness has N_4>0.


This is not yet a theorem that the complete tensor field equations possess a unique stable vacuum. Orthogonal coframe modes, connection fluctuations, the B sector, selector/condensate amplitudes, gauge constraints, and ghosts must be included in the full stationary and BRST Hessian. The theorem is a finite-rank radial stabilization witness showing that the previous absence of any lawful quartic stabilizer is removed once the physical moment map and selector-derived Lorentz Hodge operator are used together. ∎


16.I.75.12 — Gate ledger.


PASS: physical-orbit scalar s_C!=0 on the oriented real-normal branch; exact coframe extraction from P_VC D_A C; r_phys=4 implies a nondegenerate derived physical coframe; exact nonzero Lorentz moment-map coefficient; nonseparable moment-map Hessian survives quasi-linear Hodge completion; exact homogeneous quartic no-scale obstruction identified; nontrivial E8(-24) four-dimensional bundle sectors are topologically available; hard topological curvature plus the matched moment-map interaction gives a genuine quadratic rank Hessian; every full-Pfaffian shared-form seed has split signature (8,8); lawful selector-derived quartic moment-map volume exists; radially stable finite-rank branches exist for the healthy quartic sign.


CONDITIONAL: the realized bundle/characteristic sector must have nonzero physical hard curvature; g_J and lambda_J must be nonzero with the stabilizing relative sign; the selected topological curvature must overlap the physical Lorentz sector so that the §16.I.74 response has nonzero internal Pfaffian.


NO-GO RETAINED: the completely trivial homogeneous local polynomial branch does not self-select a finite rank amplitude; rapidity alone cannot create topological charge; an unrestricted soft B zero mode remains insufficient; none of the preceding results explains why a particular nonzero global charge is selected.


NEW LEADING GATES: full stationary solution of S_75+I_J4 in all coupled fields; null-shift/BRST survival after the moment-map coupling; Schur-reduced ghost-number-zero kernel; exactly one healthy low-energy spin-2; absolute mirror selection. The generic N_4 gate is replaced, on the physical hard-orbit branch, by the more precise question whether the dynamically selected stationary solution actually lands in r_phys=4.


Next exact calculation. Compute the coupled quadratic/constraint Hessian of S_75 about a finite r_phys=4 radial witness, keeping B, connection, condensate-tangent, and selector fluctuations. First determine whether the old matched-kernel null shift remains a first-class redundancy after the Lorentz-filtered J_C coupling. Then form the Schur complement against the now-invertible 16x16 physical coframe block and count the surviving ghost-number-zero modes. The target is exactly one healthy massless spin-2 combination and no additional coframe-carrying zero mode.




§16.I.76 — Rank-One Tangent Filtering, Exact B Null-Shift Survival, and the Single-Coframe Two-Derivative Reduction


Scope and correction. Section 16.I.75 proves that the physical hard-orbit tangent can supply a nondegenerate coframe and that a protected hard flux can make the zero-rank point a genuine split-signature saddle. For the spectrum audit one further filter is required. The raw moment map J_C=(1/2)mu(D_A C wedge D_A C) can contain tangent components from both completed P/K label lines. A Lorentz projection on its adjoint output does not by itself remove that second label. To preserve the one-FlatWave/one-spin-2 architecture, the condensate derivative must first be projected onto the already-selected physical hard-orbit tangent. This section performs that correction and then audits the B null shift, the orthogonal coframe line, and the resulting two-derivative gravitational sector.


16.I.76.1 — Exact rank-one physical tangent projector.


Let


v_a=h_a C_0,
h_R(v_a,v_b)=s_C eta_ab,
s_C!=0,


with h_a the selected physical FlatWave/P_cf P/K line. Define on the 30380 tangent space


boxed:
P_F(u)=(1/s_C) v_a eta^{ab} h_R(v_b,u).


Then


P_F^2=P_F,
P_F^dagger=P_F,
rank P_F=4,
P_F(v_a)=v_a.


Under simultaneous E8 transport of C_0 and the selected h_a subspace, P_F transforms by conjugation on the representation. It is therefore selector-covariant rather than a fixed symmetry-breaking insertion.


Define


u_F:=P_F D_A C,


and the filtered physical moment map


boxed:
J_F=(1/2) mu(nu_F wedge nu_F).


On the physical branch


nu_F=v_a e_F^a,
P_L J_F=(m_C/2)M_ab e_F^a wedge e_F^b.


No orthogonal completed coframe label enters J_F at quadratic order. ∎


16.I.76.2 — Spectrum-safe matched parent.


Replace the unfiltered gravitational moment-map source in the spectrum analysis by J_F and define


S_76=
integral [
 beta kappa(B wedge T_w Ncal_w F)
 -(alpha/2)Delta_w kappa(B wedge T_w B)
 +g_J kappa(B wedge T_w Pi_L J_F)
 +lambda_J kappa(Pi_L J_F wedge Jcal_L Pi_L J_F)
].


The first three terms are first-order BF/PCBF terms; the last is the selector-derived premetric quartic volume of §16.I.75.10. All zero-form projectors/operators are the selector-covariant objects already constructed in §§16.I.74–75.


This is the parent used for the following local spectrum statements. The broader unfiltered J_C interaction of §16.I.75 remains a lawful positive-degree interaction, but it is not sufficient by itself to guarantee a single gravitational coframe label. ∎


16.I.76.3 — Exact survival of the matched-kernel B shift.


The exact kernel projector Pi_0(T_w) obeys


T_w Pi_0(T_w)=0.


Every B-dependent term in S_76 contains T_w adjacent to B. Therefore


boxed:
delta B=Pi_0(T_w) Lambda_2


is an exact local algebraic symmetry of S_76 for arbitrary adjoint-valued two-form parameter Lambda_2. The quartic J_F term contains no B and is inert.


Because Pi_0 depends only on the selector/rapidity zero-form data and delta acts only on B, these shifts close additively. The kernel B component is therefore gauge/constraint redundancy, not a ghost-number-zero local excitation. ∎


16.I.76.4 — Hard B is purely auxiliary.


On im T_w the spectrum is


1/4, 3/4, 1, 5/4,


so T_w is invertible there. For finite w,


Delta_w=D_Z D_X>0.


Hence the hard B Hessian


-alpha Delta_w T_w


is algebraically invertible whenever alpha!=0. The B equation determines the image component uniquely,


B_im=(alpha Delta_w)^{-1}[beta Ncal_w F+g_J Pi_L J_F]_im.


Thus the complete local B sector splits as


ker T_w: exact shift gauge,
im T_w: algebraically eliminable auxiliary field.


boxed: B contributes no independent local propagating pole in this parent.


This is a stronger statement than the earlier soft-B no-go: the new moment-map coupling does not revive the removed soft mode. ∎


16.I.76.5 — P/K curvature in the selected/orthogonal label basis.


Write the physical conformal P/K one-forms as p^a and k^a and define the selected and orthogonal completed label lines


p^a=(e_F^a+e_H^a)/sqrt(2),
k^a=epsilon_P(e_F^a-e_H^a)/sqrt(2).


Using the standard conformal bracket [P_a,K_b], the Lorentz component of the curvature has the form


F_L^{ab}=R^{ab}(omega)+c_PK epsilon_P [e_F^a wedge e_F^b-e_H^a wedge e_H^b]+(other Lorentz-adjoint matter terms),


with fixed nonzero convention coefficient c_PK. The mixed e_F-e_H terms cancel identically. Derivatives of e_F and e_H occur in the P/K torsion components, not in this Lorentz projection.


Because Ncal_w is Lorentz-supported through Pi_L and J_F depends only on e_F, the gravitational moment-map corridor couples R(omega) to e_F but does not generate a derivative kinetic term for e_H. ∎


16.I.76.6 — Holst–Einstein–Cartan reduction on the selected line.


Eliminate hard B and retain the cross term between the Lorentz curvature and J_F. Up to one overall nonzero normalization C_grav fixed by beta, g_J, alpha, m_C and the convention for T_w, the two-derivative gravitational term is


S_grav=
C_grav integral kappa([
Pi_C I-Xi_C Jcal_L]R(omega) wedge M_ab e_F^a wedge e_F^b)
+I_J4.


Equivalently,


S_grav=
C_H integral R_ab(omega) wedge e_F^a wedge e_F^b
+C_EC integral epsilon_abcd R^{ab}(omega) wedge e_F^c wedge e_F^d
+Lambda_C integral epsilon_abcd e_F^a wedge e_F^b wedge e_F^c wedge e_F^d,


with


C_H proportional to Pi_C,
C_EC proportional to Xi_C,


in the fixed Book-16 orientation convention.


For every finite w, Xi_C!=0. Therefore the Einstein-Cartan coefficient is nonzero whenever the common parent coefficient is nonzero.


On a stationary branch with constant Pi_C and Xi_C, the internal operator Pi_C I-Xi_C *_L is invertible because *_L^2=-I and


(Pi_C I-Xi_C *_L)^{-1}
=(Pi_C I+Xi_C *_L)/(Pi_C^2+Xi_C^2).


For nondegenerate e_F, the omega equation is therefore equivalent to the ordinary torsion equation


D_omega(e_F^a wedge e_F^b)=0,


and fixes omega to the torsion-free spin connection up to the standard local Lorentz gauge freedom. Substitution gives the ordinary Einstein-Hilbert two-derivative kinetic structure plus the Holst/topological companion and the cosmological-volume term. ∎


16.I.76.7 — Orthogonal coframe line is algebraic and locally eliminable.


The e_H dependence inherited from the Lorentz curvature begins quadratically through


-c_PK epsilon_P e_H^a wedge e_H^b.


Since J_F contains only e_F, the quadratic e_H term about a nondegenerate e_F background is algebraic. In an e_F-adapted frame it is governed by the same Hodge-dressed 16x16 matrix audited in §16.I.75.4, with p=Pi_C and q=Xi_C. Hence, up to a nonzero background normalization k,


boxed:
det H_H=-3 times 2^16 k^16 Pi_C^10(Pi_C^2+4Xi_C^2)^3.


For every finite nonbalanced branch Pi_C!=0, this determinant is nonzero. Therefore


boxed: e_H=0 is locally the unique algebraic solution in the absence of an independent e_H source.


The orthogonal completed coframe line carries no derivative kinetic term in S_76 and has an invertible algebraic Hessian. It is auxiliary, not a second massless spin-2 field. The indefinite sign of an auxiliary Lorentzian Gaussian is a separate contour/quantization issue, not a propagating ghost pole. ∎


16.I.76.8 — One coframe kinetic line.


The coframe-label spectrum of S_76 therefore has the exact local structure


selected e_F line -> Einstein-Cartan/Holst derivative gravitational sector,
orthogonal e_H line -> algebraic auxiliary sector.


After eliminating omega and e_H, only the metric constructed from e_F carries a two-derivative spin-2 kinetic term. Consequently


boxed: the coframe-label multiplicity of the massless gravitational kinetic line is one.


For the sign of C_EC corresponding to positive effective Newton constant, this line has the standard healthy Einstein-Hilbert kinetic sign. The theorem is conditional on that action sign; representation theory does not fix it.


This statement is narrower than a complete all-field spectrum theorem. Selector radial modes, non-Lorentz E8 gauge fields, condensate modes outside V_C, global/topological zero modes, and any higher-derivative corrections still require their own pole/sign audit. But the principal danger of an uncontrolled second coframe spin-2 line is removed in the filtered parent. ∎


16.I.76.9 — Mirror-sign firewall.


The healthy Einstein-Cartan sign does not by itself select the absolute mirror. Under the correlated mirror operation the orientation sign in Xi_C reverses and the selector-derived Lorentz Hodge operator Jcal_L reverses with it, so the product Xi_C Jcal_L entering the parent is mirror-even. Thus a positive Newton sign constrains the common action normalization but does not choose between the two absolute mirror sheets. Absolute mirror selection remains independent. ∎


16.I.76.10 — Schur/BRST reduction after the exact auxiliary eliminations.


Let psi collect all fluctuations not already removed by the B shift, hard-B algebraic equation, Lorentz-connection torsion equation, and e_H algebraic equation. The remaining quadratic operator can be arranged as


H_full=
[[H_F,C],[C^dagger,D]],


where H_F denotes the selected gravitational/coframe block after gauge fixing of the standard Lorentz/diffeomorphism redundancies. Wherever the non-gauge part of H_F is invertible, the remaining null problem is the Schur complement


S_rem=D-C^dagger H_F^{-1}C.


The exact reductions above imply that no independent B-kernel, hard-B, or orthogonal-coframe zero mode has to be included in S_rem. Any additional ghost-number-zero pole must come from the genuinely remaining selector/condensate/non-Lorentz gauge sector or from the standard massless graviton gauge complex itself.


This substantially reduces the final BRST calculation but does not replace it. ∎


16.I.76.11 — Gate ledger.


PASS: exact rank-four tangent projector P_F; filtered one-label moment map J_F; exact survival of the matched B null shift; no local kernel-B BRST class; hard B algebraically eliminable; exact P/K curvature difference-of-squares decomposition; selected-line Holst–Einstein–Cartan reduction; torsion-free connection equation for constant finite rapidity; orthogonal coframe line has no derivative kinetic term and an invertible algebraic Hessian for Pi_C!=0; exactly one coframe-label two-derivative spin-2 kinetic line.


CONDITIONAL HEALTH PASS: the unique coframe kinetic line is the ordinary Einstein-Hilbert line after eliminating omega, provided the common Einstein-Cartan coefficient has the positive-Newton sign.


OPEN FULL-SPECTRUM GATE: Schur/BRST audit of selector and condensate modes outside V_C, non-Lorentz E8 gauge sectors, possible global zero modes, and higher-derivative corrections; proof that none contributes an additional ghostlike or massless spin-2 pole.


OPEN VACUUM GATES: dynamical selection of the nonzero topological sector; full tensor stabilization beyond the radial witness; absolute mirror selection.


Next exact calculation. Compute the representation/spacetime-spin content of the remaining Schur sector after the exact B, omega, and e_H eliminations. In particular classify which remaining fields can even carry Lorentz spin two on the soldered background. If no remaining irreducible field besides e_F contains a symmetric-traceless Lorentz rank-two kinetic component at two derivatives, the single-spin-2 gate can be promoted from coframe-label closure to the complete two-derivative local spectrum, leaving only sign, scalar/vector stability, topological selection, and higher-derivative corrections.




§16.I.77 — Two-Derivative Principal-Symbol Firewall and the Reduced Final BRST Gate


Scope. Section 16.I.76 proves that, within the filtered parent S_76, the selected physical coframe line e_F carries the Einstein–Cartan/Holst kinetic corridor, the orthogonal completed P/K line e_H is algebraic and invertible, and the full B sector is either exact matched-kernel shift gauge or algebraically auxiliary. The remaining question is whether another field outside those explicitly reduced sectors can nevertheless acquire an independent massless spin-two two-derivative principal symbol. The correct statement is slightly narrower than a complete all-field spectrum theorem: the declared parent admits exactly one Einstein-type spin-two principal-symbol corridor, while the remaining condensate/Stückelberg and gauge cohomology still requires the final BRST calculation.


16.I.77.1 — Remaining adjoint sectors after soldering.


Use the established decomposition


248=(45,1) plus (1,15) plus (10,6) plus (16,4bar) plus (16bar,4)


under Spin(10) times Spin(4,2). Under the physical Lorentz subgroup, the conformal adjoint decomposes as


15 = 6_L plus 4_P plus 4_K plus 1_D.


The two Lorentz-vector P/K lines have already been reorganized into the selected e_F line and the orthogonal e_H line of §16.I.76. The remaining adjoint sectors include the Lorentz connection, the dilation direction, the (10,6) sector, and the spinorial sectors. Representation counting alone is not enough to decide whether any of them propagates spin two; the decisive datum is the two-derivative principal symbol of the declared action.


16.I.77.2 — Lorentz support of the curvature numerator.


The curvature-dependent numerator in S_76 is


Ncal_w = D_X N_Z Pi_L - D_Z N_X Jcal_L,


where both Pi_L and Jcal_L have support on the physical Lorentz six-plane. Therefore


boxed: Ncal_w = Ncal_w Pi_L.


Let a be an arbitrary adjoint-valued connection fluctuation about a soldered background. Its linearized curvature is


delta F = D_bar a + lower-order background mixing.


The principal derivative term is d a and carries the same internal adjoint label as a. Hence for every fluctuation a_perp orthogonal to the Lorentz six-plane,


boxed: Pi_L d a_perp = 0.


Consequently the BF numerator supplies no two-derivative principal symbol for any non-Lorentz adjoint connection component. Background commutators may generate algebraic or first-order mixing after soldering, but cannot create a second Einstein-type highest-derivative block. ∎


16.I.77.3 — The apparent (10,4) spin-two danger is principal-symbol silent.


The conformal six-vector branches under the physical Lorentz subgroup as


6 -> 4 plus 1 plus 1.


Thus the (10,6) sector contains ten Lorentz-vector-valued connection one-forms in a (10,4) block. A vector-valued one-form can contain a symmetric-traceless diagonal-Lorentz component, so pure representation counting would allow an apparent spin-two carrier.


However its derivative curvature remains in the non-Lorentz (10,4) internal sector and is annihilated by Pi_L in Ncal_w. Therefore


boxed: the (10,4) block has no independent Einstein-type two-derivative principal symbol in S_76.


Its fields can acquire algebraic masses and lower-order mixing through the soldered background and through commutators in F, but those effects do not generate a second massless spin-two kinetic pole. Any future action term that places a nonzero kinetic operator directly on this block would reopen the spin-two audit. ∎


16.I.77.4 — Spinorial and purely internal adjoint sectors.


The (45,1) sector is Lorentz-scalar in its conformal factor and therefore does not provide a vector-valued coframe-like one-form. The (16,4bar) and (16bar,4) sectors carry spinorial Lorentz content rather than a bosonic symmetric-traceless rank-two carrier at the linear irreducible level. More importantly, their derivative curvatures are again outside the Lorentz six-plane and are killed by the same principal-symbol projector.


Therefore none of these adjoint sectors receives an additional Einstein-type two-derivative kinetic block from S_76. ∎


16.I.77.5 — Selector and condensate fluctuations.


Selector/rapidity variables are zero-form order-parameter fields. They can mix with the gravitational coefficients Pi_C, Xi_C, Delta_w, and with the projectors, but such coefficient variations do not by themselves create a second coframe principal symbol.


For the 30380 condensate, the filtered parent depends on derivatives only through


nu_F=P_F D_A C.


Hence transverse condensate derivatives satisfy


boxed: P_F D(delta C_perp)=0


at the principal-symbol level. The surviving V_C derivative is precisely the already-defined physical coframe/Stückelberg combination nu_F=v_a e_F^a. It is not a second independent coframe label.


This is the point at which a purely algebraic representation argument must stop. The decomposition between physical e_F fluctuations, broken-gauge Goldstone directions in C, and the corresponding gauge ghosts is a BRST/Stückelberg question. The principal-symbol count shows there is only one candidate gravitational line; BRST must still prove that the physical ghost-number-zero cohomology on that line is exactly the ordinary graviton and that no constrained condensate mode duplicates it. ∎


16.I.77.6 — Unique Einstein-type principal-symbol theorem for the declared parent.


Collect the exact reductions:


1. ker T_w in B is exact shift gauge;
2. im T_w in B is algebraically auxiliary;
3. omega is the ordinary torsion connection and is auxiliary after its first-order equation;
4. e_H has an invertible algebraic Hessian and no derivative kinetic term;
5. non-Lorentz adjoint connection derivatives are killed by Pi_L in Ncal_w;
6. transverse condensate derivatives are killed by P_F;
7. the surviving physical derivative line is e_F.


Therefore, for the two-derivative principal symbol of S_76,


boxed: N_spin2,candidate^(2-derivative)=1.


Equivalently, the declared parent contains exactly one Einstein-type local spin-two kinetic corridor, namely the metric/coframe constructed from e_F.


This is stronger than the coframe-label theorem of §16.I.76 and weaker than a complete BRST spectrum theorem. It counts candidate highest-derivative spin-two blocks, not final gauge-cohomology states. ∎


16.I.77.7 — Stability firewall under extensions.


The uniqueness theorem is action-relative. It remains valid for deformations that do not add a new two-derivative kinetic invariant on the non-Lorentz adjoint, transverse-condensate, or orthogonal coframe sectors. In particular, algebraic potentials, mass matrices, and lower-derivative soldering mixings do not alter the principal-symbol multiplicity.


Conversely, any future term that gives the (10,4) block, e_H, or a transverse 30380 component an independent two-derivative symmetric-traceless Lorentz kinetic operator must be treated as reopening the single-spin-two gate. Such a term is not forbidden a priori by representation theory; it is simply absent from the presently declared filtered parent. ∎


16.I.77.8 — Updated gate ledger.


PASS: one selected coframe label; exact B null-shift removal; hard-B auxiliary elimination; orthogonal coframe auxiliary elimination; Lorentz connection torsion reduction; no non-Lorentz adjoint Einstein-type principal symbol; no transverse-condensate derivative principal symbol through J_F; exactly one candidate two-derivative Einstein-type spin-two corridor in S_76.


CONDITIONAL HEALTH PASS: with the positive-Newton sign, that unique corridor has the standard Einstein-Hilbert kinetic sign after eliminating omega.


OPEN BRST GATE: compute the gauge-fixed/Stückelberg complex for the surviving V_C condensate tangent, P/K connection, Lorentz/diffeomorphism ghosts, and remaining non-Lorentz gauge fields, and prove that ghost-number-zero cohomology contains exactly the standard massless graviton on the e_F line and no duplicate constrained spin-two state.


OPEN NON-SPIN-TWO STABILITY GATES: scalar, vector, and spinorial pole/sign audit; selector and condensate radial stability beyond the one-dimensional witness; possible spin-three-halves sectors; global/topological zero modes.


OPEN VACUUM GATES: dynamical selection of a nonzero hard topological sector and absolute mirror selection.


HIGHER-DERIVATIVE FIREWALL: every new higher-derivative or non-Lorentz kinetic invariant must be audited before the one-spin-two conclusion is exported beyond the present parent.


Next exact calculation. Build the minimal BRST/Stückelberg complex of the soldered physical orbit. Treat e_F as the gauge-covariant combination extracted from P_F D_A C, identify the broken P/K gauge parameters and their action on condensate tangent fluctuations, then compute the ghost-number-zero principal-symbol cohomology. The target is to show that the only surviving spin-two cohomology is the ordinary transverse-traceless graviton, while the broken-orbit condensate directions are Stückelberg/gauge and all non-Lorentz sectors carry only lower-spin or auxiliary principal symbols in this parent.










§16.I.75 — Physical Moment-Map Nonseparability, Quasi-Linear Hessian Determinant, and Bypass of the Shared-Form Pfaffian Gate


Scope. Section 16.I.74 left F∧F≠0 as the exterior condition on the special factorized/shared-form branch R=(pE35+qE12)F. That condition is exact for that branch but is not the general curvature-Hessian criterion. Book 16 already contains a stronger positive-degree source: the covariant-condensate moment map J_C, whose Lorentz projection is intrinsically nonseparable between internal bivector and exterior two-form indices. This section combines that source with the selector-covariant quasi-linear Hodge parent and shows that full sixteen-dimensional coframe-Hessian rank survives. The leading exterior problem therefore moves from “manufacture a single nondecomposable F” to “activate four independent covariant-condensate tangent directions.”


16.I.75.1 — Physical tangent norm noncollapse on the oriented branch.


Let the physical normal condensate be


C0=a n_+ + b n_- + conjugate(a) s_+ + conjugate(b) s_-,


and let the hard Lorentz-vector generator be


h_a=(P_a+epsilon_P K_a)/sqrt(2).


Section 16.I.65.1 gives, after the real invertible normal-basis map T_N,


h_a C0 proportional to c\,u tensor e_a + conjugate(c)\,conjugate(u) tensor e_a,


where


c=u_{epsilon_P}a+v_{epsilon_P}b,


with u_{epsilon_P},v_{epsilon_P} real and not both zero. It already proved


chi_C=2 Im(a conjugate(b)) !=0  implies  c!=0


for every T_N in GL(2,R).


The E8(-24) real-form theorem of §16.0.F.4B pairs the physical (126,6) and (conjugate(126),6) sectors by the nonzero 126-conjugate(126) invariant form times the split conformal metric. Therefore there is one fixed nonzero normalization gamma such that for


v_a:=h_a C0


one has


boxed:
h_R(v_a,v_b)=gamma |c|^2 eta_ab.


Consequently the coefficient s_C used in §16.I.64 is nonzero whenever the orientation pseudoscalar is nonzero:


boxed:
chi_C !=0  implies  s_C !=0.


This closes the §16.I.65 caution for the physical conjugate-pair orbit: the selected tangent cannot remain an arbitrary null vector once its real-module pairing is imposed. ∎


16.I.75.2 — Physical moment-map Lorentz projection.


Retain the canonical E8-equivariant moment map


mu:Lambda^2(30380)->248,


kappa(mu(u,v),X)=h_R(Xu,v),


and the two-form current


J_C=(1/2)mu(D_A C wedge D_A C).


For the Lorentz-vector tangent basis v_a above, equivariance and the standard Lorentz action give


kappa(mu(v_a,v_b),M_cd)
=h_R(M_cd v_a,v_b)
=gamma |c|^2(eta_ac eta_bd-eta_ad eta_bc)


up to the fixed generator normalization. Hence


boxed:
P_L mu(v_a,v_b)=lambda_mu s_C M_ab,


with lambda_mu !=0 fixed by the E8/Lorentz normalization.


If the covariant derivative has rank four on the selected tangent sector,


D_A C=sum_{a=0}^3 v_a theta^a,


then


boxed:
P_L J_C
=k_C sum_{a<b} M_ab theta^a wedge theta^b,


where k_C is proportional to lambda_mu s_C and therefore k_C!=0 on the oriented rank-four branch. This is exactly the ambient nonseparable witness of §16.I.64, now tied to the physical normal condensate. ∎


Definition 16.I.75.A — Quasi-linear Hodge-completed moment-map curvature. Let


p:=Pi_C,
q:=Xi_C,


and let J_L be the selector-derived Lorentz complex structure of §16.I.74, with


J_L E35=-E12,
J_L^2=-I on Lambda^2 L.


On the Lorentz moment-map source define


R_QL:=(p I-q J_L)P_L J_C.


For the rank-four witness this is equivalently


R_QL^{ab}
=k_C[p theta^a wedge theta^b+q (*_L theta^{ab})],


up to the inherited orientation convention.


16.I.75.3 — Exact quasi-linear nonseparable Hessian determinant.


Let H_R be the coframe Hessian


H_R(e)_d=epsilon_abcd R^{ab} wedge e^c.


For the nonseparable witness


R_0^{ab}=k_C theta^a wedge theta^b,


§16.I.64 proved


H_{R_0}(E)=2k_C[(tr E)I-E^T]


and


det H_{R_0}=-3*2^16 k_C^16.


Now replace R_0 by


R(p,q)=pR_0+q *_L R_0.


An exact sixteen-by-sixteen component evaluation gives


boxed:
det H_{R(p,q)}
=-3*2^16 k_C^16 p^10(p^2+4q^2)^3.


The q=0 specialization reproduces the published §16.I.64 determinant exactly, fixing the convention and normalization of the calculation.


Therefore, for real p,q and k_C!=0,


boxed:
rank H_{R(p,q)}=16  iff  p!=0.


The pure-Hodge boundary p=0,q!=0 is degenerate (rank six), whereas any nonzero direct component makes the full Hessian invertible for arbitrary real Hodge amplitude q. ∎


Corollary 16.I.75.4 — Quasi-linear physical rank theorem. On the §16.I.74 response orbit,


p=Pi_C=tanh(2w),
q=Xi_C=2 epsilon_P cosh w/(cosh^2 w+1).


For every finite nonbalanced state w!=0, p!=0 and q is finite and nonzero. Hence on the oriented rank-four derivative branch,


boxed:
det H_QL
=-3*2^16 k_C^16 Pi_C^10(Pi_C^2+4Xi_C^2)^3 !=0.


If an overall PCBF transmission factor rho multiplies R_QL, the determinant is multiplied by rho^16. Thus the complete condition is


rho!=0,
k_C!=0,
Pi_C!=0.


No separate condition on a factorized spacetime two-form is present because no such factorization has been assumed. ∎


Theorem 16.I.75.5 — Shared-form Pfaffian bypass by nonseparability. The theorem


R=X F  requires  F wedge F !=0


remains exact for separable/shared-form curvature. The physical moment-map source is not of the form X F: its six Lorentz bivector components are correlated with six distinct exterior two-forms theta^a wedge theta^b. The exact determinant above proves that this nonseparable correlation itself supplies the exterior rank required by the coframe Hessian.


Therefore the shared-form Pfaffian condition is not a universal gate. On the moment-map branch it is replaced by


boxed:
rank(D_A C|_physical)=4,


plus the already-earned nonzero internal/orientation and common-transmission conditions. This is a bypass of the factorized Pfaffian gate, not a contradiction of it. ∎


16.I.75.6 — One-parent moment-map PCBF realization.


Use the §16.I.74 selector-covariant numerator Ncal_w and positive scalar Hessian Delta_w, but feed the covariant-condensate current into the BF leg:


S_75
=integral[
 beta kappa(B wedge T_w Ncal_w J_C)
 -(alpha/2)Delta_w kappa(B wedge T_w B)
].


The B equation is


T_w(beta Ncal_w J_C-alpha Delta_w B)=0.


On the Lorentz eigenspace,


boxed:
B_L=(beta/alpha)(Pi_C Pi_L-Xi_C Jcal_L)J_C^L.


On the physical rank-four tangent witness this has exactly the nonseparable quasi-linear form of Theorem 16.I.75.3. On every transverse nonzero eigenspace Ncal_w vanishes, while ker T_w retains the exact null shift. The same no-transverse-contamination property of §16.I.74 is therefore preserved.


Because J_C is quadratic in D_A C, the action is not in the old purely algebraic zero-form B class. Variation of A also sees covariant derivatives of B through the J_C dependence. Nevertheless the completely homogeneous configuration D_A C=0, B=0 remains stationary. The action therefore transmits and stabilizes an activated derivative sector; it does not by itself prove spontaneous departure from the homogeneous branch. ∎


16.I.75.7 — Characteristic four-form diagnostic.


The same nonseparable source admits the lawful pre-soldering four-form


Theta_C:=kappa(J_C^L wedge Jcal_L J_C^L).


No spacetime Hodge star or coframe is used. On the rank-four witness,


Theta_C is proportional to k_C^2 theta^0 wedge theta^1 wedge theta^2 wedge theta^3


with a nonzero coefficient fixed by the inherited Killing normalization and orientation. Thus Theta_C detects the nontrivial rank-four/oriented moment-map sector and may weight distinct global or boundary sectors. It does not, by itself, force a local classical solution away from D_A C=0. ∎


16.I.75.8 — Updated gate ledger.


PASS: physical orientation chi_C!=0 forces s_C!=0; the physical moment-map Lorentz coefficient k_C is nonzero whenever the selected covariant-condensate tangent has rank four; exact nonseparable moment-map curvature; exact Hodge-completed determinant -3*2^16 k_C^16 Pi_C^10(Pi_C^2+4Xi_C^2)^3; full sixteen-dimensional coframe-Hessian rank on finite nonbalanced rank-four states; one-parent S_75 realization with no sourced transverse contamination.

LEADING OPEN MICROSCOPIC GATE: activation of rank(D_A C|_physical)=4 from the unsoldered/homogeneous theory, or a lawful global/topological/boundary sector that enforces a nontrivial rank-four moment-map current. The local polynomial action still admits D_A C=0, B=0.


DOWNSTREAM OPEN: BRST/constraint survival of the rank-four derivative branch; N_4>0; exactly one healthy low-energy spin-2; absolute mirror selection.

§16.I.71 — P/K-Index Type Correction, Exact Quadratic-27000 Multiplicity Two, Common-Q² Projection Ratio, and the Exhaustive Missing-12 No-Go


Scope. Sections 16.I.68–70 opened a useful lower-degree BF search but overloaded one notation. In §16.I.54 the symbols E₃₅ and E₁₂ are matrix units in the antisymmetric part of the four-index physical P/K transfer tensor G_{μν}, μ,ν∈{1,2,3,5}. They are not, without an additional explicit identification map, adjoint vectors in 248 on which a constitutive endomorphism K_C acts. This section corrects that type mismatch, preserves the valid SO(16) support results of §§16.I.69–70 in their proper domain, and then completes the previously open quadratic 30380²→27000 calculation in the correctly typed D→P/K reduction used by §§16.I.53–54.


Correction 16.I.71.CL1 — P/K tensor units are not automatically E8 adjoint vectors. The §16.I.54 target is the antisymmetric P/K-index functional extracted from
G_{μν}=⟨v_D,[P_μ,K_ν]⟩_η,
not the adjoint matrix element κ(E₁₂,K_CE₃₅). An adjoint Lorentz generator associated with a spacetime bivector may of course be constructed, but the map from that adjoint object to the P/K transfer tensor must be stated and proved. Therefore the quantity
m_C=κ(E₁₂,K_{C₀}E₃₅)
introduced in §16.I.68 is not, by notation alone, an evaluation of the microscopic ρ_PK/β_PK gate of §16.I.54.


Supersession rule. The following earlier results remain valid as separate adjoint/BF statements: the lawful linear-30380 mixed BF invariant; the Killing-skew character of K_C; K_{C₀}|_{120}=0 for C₀∈8008⊂Λ²(128); and the associated vanishing of a bare K_{C₀} insertion between purely SO(16)-bosonic adjoint external modes. What is withdrawn is the stronger inference that these facts by themselves evaluate or close the §16.I.54 P/K-index Hodge coefficient ρ_PK. Proposition 16.I.65.2 therefore remains controlling: ρ_PK is an action-level transmission coefficient until a correctly typed microscopic parent is reduced to G_{μν}.


Theorem 16.I.71.1 — Exact symmetric 27000 multiplicity-two theorem. An independent exact LieART 2.1.1 tensor-product calculation gives two copies of the E8 irrep 27000 in 30380⊗30380. Combining that full tensor-product multiset with the already-certified Book-16 result
dim Hom_E8(Sym²(30380),3875)=2,
dim Hom_E8(Λ²(30380),3875)=0,
the symmetric dimension
30380·30381/2=461487390,
and the symmetric invariant singlet gives a unique integer exchange-parity allocation. In that allocation


dim Hom_E8(Sym²(30380),27000)=2,
dim Hom_E8(Λ²(30380),27000)=0.


The corresponding exact symmetric-square decomposition is
Sym²(30380)
=1⊕2·3875⊕2·27000⊕147250⊕779247⊕2·2450240⊕4096000⊕4881384⊕26411008⊕70680000⊕146325270⊕203205000.
Its dimensions sum exactly to 461487390. Thus the quadratic 27000 search is a two-intertwiner problem, not a one-map problem.


Independent branching check. LieART gives
27000↓SO(16)=1⊕128⊕1820⊕5304⊕6435⊕13312.
Since
Sym²(120)=1⊕135⊕1820⊕5304,
the direct bosonic 120→120 part of a 27000 symmetric operator can use only 1⊕1820⊕5304. The singlet cannot by itself supply an antisymmetric P/K-index off-diagonal. This branching check is diagnostic; the exhaustive no-go below is obtained by the explicit E8 projectors and does not rely on genericity.


Definition 16.I.71.A — Frozen projector conventions. Retain the normalized invariant form
η=(1/60)B_Killing.
Raise both symmetric adjoint indices of the raw quadratic tensors
Q^(1)_{MN}=C_{MP}C_N{}^P,
Q^(2)_{MN}=f_M{}^{PQ}f_N{}^{RS}C_{PR}C_{QS}
with η. For a raised symmetric tensor S define the exact Casimir operator 𝒦(S) by the Chevalley implementation already certified in the computational ledger. Then
P₁S=(1/248)η⁻¹ tr_ηS,
P₃₈₇₅S=(1/7)S-(1/14)𝒦(S)-(1/56)η⁻¹ tr_ηS,
P₂₇₀₀S=S-P₁S-P₃₈₇₅S.
On the physical four-wedge normal slice the bracket-recoupled Q^(2) has tr_ηQ^(2)=0, exactly as established in §16.I.53.


Computational normalization audit. The reconstructed sparse Chevalley calculation reproduces all eight published coefficients of
Φ₂D=(P₃₈₇₅Q^(2))D
from §16.I.53 with one common normalization factor before that factor is removed. The calculation also reproduces the published count of 27 ordered nonzero entries of Q^(2). Hence the new 27000 projection uses the same root ordering, Killing normalization, real normal four-line, and projector convention as the live §16.I.53 theorem.


Theorem 16.I.71.2 — Common-Q^(2) 3875/27000 projection-ratio theorem. On the full four-line normal carrier
C=a n_+ + b n_- + a' s_+ + b' s_-,
the exact quadratic identity
(𝒦Q^(2))D=-4 Q^(2)D
holds. It was checked coefficientwise on the ten independent quadratic monomials in a,b,a',b'; because both sides are quadratic, this is an exact polynomial identity on the entire four-line carrier. Since tr_ηQ^(2)=0,


(P₃₈₇₅Q^(2))D=(3/7)Q^(2)D,
(P₂₇₀₀Q^(2))D=(4/7)Q^(2)D,


and therefore


boxed: (P₂₇₀₀Q^(2))D=(4/3)(P₃₈₇₅Q^(2))D.


On the E8(-24)-real slice let
A=|a|²,
B=|b|².
Writing the eight Cartan coefficients in the H₁,…,H₈ order gives


(P₂₇₀₀Q^(2))D
=(1/7)[
A(-16,-24,-32,-48,-40,-32,-24,-16)
+B(-16,-16,-24,-32,-24,-16,-8,0)
].


Corollary 16.I.71.2A — Common-parent representation source passes. By §16.I.53.T1, Φ₂D≠0 for every nonzero real normal condensate. Corollary 16.I.71.2B — The common 27000 projection does not supply the Hodge 12 plane. The physical P/K reduction is linear in the D-action vector, so
G^(27000,2)_{μν}=(4/3)G^(3875,2)_{μν}
=(16/7)
[[-Σ_I,0,0,0],
 [0,-Σ_I,0,0],
 [0,0,-Σ_I,Δ_I],
 [0,0,-Δ_I,Σ_I]],
where Σ_I=A+B and Δ_I=A-B. Thus its antisymmetric functional satisfies
X₁₂=h₁-2h₇+2h₈=0,
Y₃₅=h₁-2h₈=(16/7)Δ_I.
The common Q^(2) parent strengthens the already-existing 35 plane; it does not generate the missing 12 plane.


Theorem 16.I.71.3 — Second independent quadratic 27000 map. Let
Ψ₁:=P₂₇₀₀Q^(1),

].

For Ψ₂ the preceding theorem gives
X₁₂(Ψ₂D)=0,

The two intertwiners are nevertheless linearly independent. For example at A=1,B=0, Ψ₁D has h₁/h₈=7/19 whereas Ψ₂D has h₁/h₈=1. Hence no constant scalar relates the two maps.


Theorem 16.I.71.4 — Exhaustive quadratic-27000 missing-12 no-go on the physical normal slice. By Theorem 16.I.71.1 the space
Hom_E8(Sym²(30380),27000)
is two-dimensional. By Theorem 16.I.71.3 the two explicit projected maps Ψ₁ and Ψ₂ are independent, so they form a basis of that Hom space. Every E8-equivariant symmetric quadratic 27000 source is therefore
Ψ=uΨ₁+vΨ₂
for constants u,v. On the physical E8(-24)-real normal slice,


X₁₂(ΨD)=u X₁₂(Ψ₁D)+v X₁₂(Ψ₂D)=0.


Therefore no symmetric quadratic 30380²→27000 source can generate the missing P/K-index E₁₂ component through the audited D→P/K reduction of §§16.I.53–54. This is the exhaustive quadratic 27000 no-go that §16.I.67 could not previously promote.


Scope firewall. The theorem is not a universal E8 no-go against ρ_PK. It excludes the complete symmetric quadratic C²→27000 source class on the physical normal slice in the specific D→P/K transmission route. It does not exclude higher condensate degree, independently activated positive-degree fields, an action term outside the D→P/K reduction, or the already-established post-rank 146325270 orientation transmitter. In particular, §16.I.58.g remains intact: once a nondegenerate Lorentz branch exists, the mirror-odd 146325270 parent is structurally forced into the unique *_L direction, but it cannot seed the unsoldered quadratic Hessian because its coframe degree is four.

§16.I.71 — Exact Two-Copy 27000 Multiplicity, Canonical-Basis Exhaustion, and the Quadratic Normal-Carrier Hodge No-Go


Scope. Sections 16.I.69–70 closed the bare-30380 and current odd-heavy routes and returned the microscopic search to the unresolved quadratic 27000 multiplicity. That multiplicity and its physical normal-carrier restriction are now both closed. The result is stronger than the earlier statement that “tested 27000 images fail”: the full E8-equivariant quadratic 30380²→27000 map space is two-dimensional, the two canonical projected contractions already available in Book 16 form a basis of it, and every linear combination has zero E₃₅→E₁₂ Lorentz-Hodge matrix element on the complete four-line normal carrier.


16.I.71.1 — Independent E8 tensor-square certificate


An independent LieART 2.1.1 computation was first calibrated on the standard adjoint square
248⊗248=1⊕248⊕3875⊕27000⊕30380.
In the same highest-weight convention the exact full tensor square of 30380 is


30380⊗30380 =
1 ⊕ 248 ⊕ 2·3875 ⊕ 2·27000 ⊕ 2·30380 ⊕ 2·147250 ⊕ 3·779247 ⊕ 1763125
⊕ 2·2450240 ⊕ 2·4096000 ⊕ 4881384 ⊕ 6696000 ⊕ 2·26411008
⊕ 70680000 ⊕ 76271625 ⊕ 146325270 ⊕ 203205000 ⊕ 344452500.


Book 16 already proves


dim Hom_E8(Sym²(30380),3875)=2,
dim Hom_E8(Λ²(30380),3875)=0,


and separately has the canonical antisymmetric moment map Λ²(30380)→248. Together with the symmetric invariant singlet and the dimension identity


dim Sym²(30380)=30380·30381/2=461487390,


there is a unique integer allocation of the full-product multiplicities between symmetric and antisymmetric exchange parity. The symmetric square is


Sym²(30380)=
1 ⊕ 2·3875 ⊕ 2·27000 ⊕ 147250 ⊕ 779247 ⊕ 2·2450240 ⊕ 4096000
⊕ 4881384 ⊕ 26411008 ⊕ 70680000 ⊕ 146325270 ⊕ 203205000,


whose dimensions sum exactly to 461487390.


Theorem 16.I.71.1 — Exact 27000 multiplicity theorem.


m₂₇₀₀:=dim Hom_E8(Sym²(30380),27000)=2.


Thus the quadratic 27000 route exists, but there are exactly two independent intertwiners. A single tested image can never establish exhaustiveness.


16.I.71.2 — SO(16) block localization


The independently checked branching is


27000↓SO(16)=1⊕128⊕1820⊕5304⊕6435⊕13312.


The relevant adjoint-block decompositions are


Sym²(120)=1⊕135⊕1820⊕5304,
Sym²(128)=1⊕1820⊕6435,
120⊗128=128⊕1920⊕13312.


Hence the 27000 bosonic 120→120 symmetric block can contain only


1⊕1820⊕5304.


The singlet acts scalarly and cannot provide an off-diagonal E₃₅↔E₁₂ matrix element because κ(E₁₂,E₃₅)=0. Therefore only the 1820 and 5304 subchannels can possibly carry the missing direct Lorentz-Hodge coefficient inside a quadratic 27000 source. The 5304 is the SO(16) Weyl-tensor representation; the 1820 is the four-form representation. Representation availability alone therefore does not force the desired coefficient to vanish.


16.I.71.3 — The two canonical projected maps form a basis


Retain the two exact E8-equivariant symmetric contractions already used upstream,


Q⁽¹⁾_{MN}=C_{MP}C_N{}^P,


Q⁽²⁾_{MN}=f_M{}^{PQ}f_N{}^{RS}C_{PR}C_{QS},


and define


Ψ₁(C,C):=P₂₇₀₀ Q⁽¹⁾(C,C),
Ψ₂(C,C):=P₂₇₀₀ Q⁽²⁾(C,C).


The frozen Appendix-A Chevalley reconstruction was used without changing the Book-16 conventions. As a calibration, the reconstructed Q⁽²⁾ on the sparse normal carrier has exactly the 27 ordered nonzero entries stated in §16.I.53, and its P₃₈₇₅ projection acting on D reproduces every published H₁,…,H₈ coefficient, differing only by the expected overall factor 60 between B_Killing and η=B_Killing/60. This independently verifies that the projector, index-raising, normal carrier, and physical P/K embedding are aligned with the live manuscript.


To prove Ψ₁ and Ψ₂ independent, take the E8(-24)-real normal witness


C_* = n₊+2n₋+s₊+2s₋.


In the frozen one-based Chevalley tensor coordinates and unnormalized Killing convention one finds, for example,


(Ψ₁)^{23}=-3600/217,     (Ψ₂)^{23}=0,


while


(Ψ₁)^{17,19}=0,          (Ψ₂)^{17,19}=-8/7.


Thus Ψ₁ and Ψ₂ are not proportional as equivariant maps. Since Theorem 16.I.71.1 gives a two-dimensional Hom space, they form a basis:


Hom_E8(Sym²(30380),27000)=span{Ψ₁,Ψ₂}.


No third quadratic 27000 intertwiner remains untested.


16.I.71.4 — Exact physical normal-carrier Hodge audit


Use the certified physical P/K representatives and define the Lorentz generators by the same frozen embedding used in §§16.I.53–54,


E₁₂=[P₁,K₂],
E₃₅=[P₃,K₅],


up to the fixed nonzero normalization already absorbed into the basis convention. Let


N_C:=span_C{n₊,n₋,s₊,s₋}


be the complete complexified four-line normal carrier, so a general source is


C=a n₊+b n₋+a' s₊+b' s₋.


For r=1,2 define the quadratic physical matrix-element polynomial


F_r(C):=κ(E₁₂,H_{Ψ_r(C,C)}E₃₅).


Each F_r is a homogeneous quadratic polynomial in (a,b,a',b'). An exact rational polarization audit evaluates F_r on the four basis lines and all six pairwise sums. In all ten cases, for both r=1 and r=2,


F_r(e_i)=0,
F_r(e_i+e_j)=0  (i<j).


Therefore every polarized coefficient vanishes:


B_r(e_i,e_j)=1/2[F_r(e_i+e_j)-F_r(e_i)-F_r(e_j)]=0,


and hence


F₁(C)=F₂(C)=0


identically on N_C. This is an exact polynomial identity, not a numerical sample and not a consequence of imposing the E8(-24) reality conditions. It therefore holds a fortiori on the physical real slice a'=ā, b'=b̄ and on every quarter-phase or unequal-intensity subbranch of that slice.


Theorem 16.I.71.2 — Exhaustive quadratic-27000 Lorentz-Hodge no-go.


For every E8-equivariant symmetric quadratic map


Ψ:Sym²(30380)→27000


and every C in the four-line physical normal carrier,


κ(E₁₂,H_{Ψ(C,C)}E₃₅)=0.


Proof. By Theorem 16.I.71.1 and §16.I.71.3, Ψ=uΨ₁+vΨ₂. The matrix element is linear in Ψ, while both basis matrix elements vanish identically by §16.I.71.4. ∎


Corollary 16.I.71.3 — The old tested-image failure is now exhaustive. The statement in §§16.I.54, 67, and 68 that the tested 27000 images fail to provide the independent E₁₂ block can now be promoted, on the physical four-line normal carrier, to a complete quadratic-channel no-go. There is no missing quadratic 27000 Clebsch map whose later discovery can reverse that conclusion.

For a zero-form source quadratic in the physical 30380 condensate, every Killing-self-adjoint adjoint constitutive kernel lies in


Sym²(248)=1⊕3875⊕27000.


The three channels are now exhausted for the direct E₃₅→E₁₂ Hodge coefficient on the audited normal branch:


(1) the singlet is proportional to the identity and has κ(E₁₂,E₃₅)=0;
(2) the complete two-map 3875 family is already closed on this branch by §16.I.67;
(3) the complete two-map 27000 family is closed by Theorem 16.I.71.2.


Therefore no symmetric zero-form constitutive operator that is quadratic in C₀ alone can supply the missing direct Lorentz-Hodge coefficient within the present PCBF architecture.


This is deliberately not a universal all-orders E8 no-go. It does not exclude higher polynomial recouplings, independently activated fields, derivative/positive-degree sources, nonseparable curvature backgrounds, or a microscopic parent containing additional representation data. It does close the entire current homogeneous quadratic-condensate zero-form search.

Combining those results with the present exhaustive quadratic 3875/27000 result removes every presently audited linear-or-quadratic zero-form route to the missing E₁₂ coefficient.


Gate ledger after §16.I.71.

§16.I.78 — Moving-Projector Equivariance, Physical-Orbit Stückelberg Contractibility, and the Principal Spin-Two BRST Theorem


Status. This section closes the local, nonzero-momentum spin-two BRST gate for the declared two-derivative parent S_76. It is action-relative and principal-symbol exact. It is not a complete all-field BRST spectrum theorem, and it does not settle scalar, vector, spinorial, radial, global/topological, or higher-derivative stability.


A. Gauge convention and covariant condensate derivative


Choose


δ_ε A = -D_A ε,
δ_ε C = ρ(ε)C,


with D_A = d + ρ(A). Writing E=ρ(ε),


δ_ε(D_A C)
= d(EC)+ρ(δA)C+ρ(A)EC
= E D_A C.


Thus D_A C transforms homogeneously. No opposite sign is used elsewhere in this section.


B. The physical tangent projector is a moving selector-covariant projector


Let


v_a(C)=h_a C,


h_R(v_a,v_b)=s_C η_ab,


P_F(u)=(1/s_C)v_a η^{ab}h_R(v_b,u),


with s_C≠0 on the finite r_phys=4 branch.


Section 16.I.76 already established the relevant simultaneous transport rule: C and the selected h_a subspace are transported together, and P_F transforms by conjugation rather than as a fixed external insertion. Make that statement explicit. For finite g let


C' = R(g)C,


h'_a = Ad_g(h_b) Λ^b{}_a,


where Λ preserves η:


Λ^T η Λ = η.


Then


v'_a = R(g)v_b Λ^b{}_a,


h_R(v'_a,v'_b)=s_C η_ab,


so


s'_C=s_C


and


P'_F = R(g) P_F R(g)^{-1}.


Infinitesimally, with Λ=I+Ω+O(ε²) and Ω^Tη+ηΩ=0,


δh_a=[ε,h_a]+h_b Ω^b{}_a,


δv_a=Ev_a+v_b Ω^b{}_a,


δs_C=0,


δP_F=[E,P_F].


This also resolves the projector-variation obstruction found by the direct audit. If Q_F=I-P_F, the possible normal leakage is


L_a
=Q_F(δh_a-[ε,h_a])C
=Q_F v_b Ω^b{}_a
=0.


Hence the moving-frame law is not an added tuning. It is precisely the selector-covariant transport already required in §16.I.76. Freezing h_a or P_F would instead generate the spurious term Q_F[ε,h_a]C and is not the live theory.


C. Exact covariance of the filtered derivative and coframe


Define


ν_F=P_F D_A C.


Using δP_F=[E,P_F] and δ(D_A C)=E D_A C,


δν_F
=[E,P_F]D_A C+P_F E D_A C
=Eν_F.


Therefore the filtered condensate derivative transforms homogeneously exactly.


Write on the physical rank-four branch


ν_F=v_a e_F^a.


Then


δν_F
=Ev_a e_F^a+v_b Ω^b{}_a e_F^a+v_a δe_F^a
=Eν_F,


which gives


δe_F^a=-Ω^a{}_b e_F^b.


Thus the selected coframe acquires no inhomogeneous broken-orbit derivative term. The apparent noncovariance is absorbed by the same moving-frame mechanism already encountered in the earlier moving-line and moving-normal constructions.


D. Linearized physical-orbit Stückelberg complex


Work about a finite stationary background (Ā,C̄) with r_phys=4 and nondegenerate ē_F. Let P̄_F denote the background physical projector and v̄_a=h̄_a C̄.


Parameterize the physical tangent condensate fluctuation by


P̄_F δC = v̄_a π^a,


and define the selected broken P/K connection fluctuation a_F^a by


P̄_F ρ(a)C̄ = v̄_a a_F^a.


To principal derivative order,


P̄_F δ(D_A C)
=v̄_a( D̄π^a + a_F^a )
+ lower-derivative moving-frame terms.


Hence the gauge-covariant Stückelberg one-form is


𝔢^a = a_F^a + D̄π^a.


For a selected broken-orbit parameter θ^a,


δπ^a=θ^a,
δa_F^a=-D̄θ^a,
δ𝔢^a=0


at principal order. Algebraic Ω-rotations generated by simultaneous frame transport act only in the Lorentz/frame block and do not change this derivative cancellation.


Introduce the broken-orbit ghost c_F^a. The linear principal BRST differential is


s_0 π^a=c_F^a,
s_0 c_F^a=0,
s_0 a_F^a=-D̄c_F^a,
s_0 𝔢^a=0.


After the invertible local jet-coordinate change


(a_F,π) ↔ (𝔢,π),


the pair (π^a,c_F^a), together with all of its derivatives, is contractible. Explicitly, with multi-index I,


N_F=Σ_{a,I}[(∂_Iπ^a)∂/∂(∂_Iπ^a)+(∂_Ic_F^a)∂/∂(∂_Ic_F^a)],


κ_F=Σ_{a,I}(∂_Iπ^a)∂/∂(∂_Ic_F^a),


one has


{s_0,κ_F}=N_F.


Therefore every local cohomology class with positive N_F degree is exact. The physical-orbit condensate Goldstone fields and their broken P/K ghosts cannot furnish an additional ghost-number-zero spin-two class. Their only surviving principal combination is the gauge-covariant selected coframe fluctuation 𝔢^a, i.e. the same e_F line already counted in §§16.I.76–77.


E. Separation from Lorentz and diffeomorphism ghosts


The moving-frame Ω(c_F) contribution is algebraic in the broken-orbit ghost. It may be absorbed into a triangular redefinition of the residual Lorentz ghost,


c_L' = c_L + Ω(c_F).


This does not change cohomology and leaves the contractible (π,c_F) sector separate from the ordinary Lorentz complex.


Diffeomorphisms are also distinct at nonzero principal momentum. On the soldered nondegenerate background, for coframe perturbation f^a_μ defined by e_F^a=ē_F^a+f^a,


s_0 f^a_μ
=ē^a_ν ∂_μ ξ^ν
+c_L'^a{}_b ē^b_μ
+ lower-principal-order terms.


The broken internal ghost c_F produces no independent d c_F term in f after the Stückelberg cancellation, whereas the diffeomorphism ghost ξ enters through ∂ξ. Therefore the two gauge directions do not become locally reducible at a nonzero principal covector. Possible rigid or global zero-mode identifications are outside this local theorem.


The antisymmetric tetrad perturbation is removed by c_L'. Define


h_μν = 2 ē_{a(μ} f^a_{ν)}.


Then


s_0 h_μν = ∂_μ ξ_ν + ∂_ν ξ_μ.


This is the ordinary linearized gravitational BRST transformation.


F. Einstein principal symbol and physical helicity count


Section 16.I.76 already proved that the finite-w operator


Π_C I - Ξ_C *_L


is invertible because *_L²=-I, and hence the spin-connection equation reduces on the nondegenerate finite-w branch to the ordinary torsion equation. The Lorentz connection therefore contributes no independent propagating spin-two class: its principal part is solved in terms of the tetrad modulo Lorentz gauge. Eliminating it gives the ordinary Einstein-Hilbert two-derivative principal operator, with the Holst companion not changing the torsion-free graviton characteristic content.


Consequently the residual spin-two BRST complex is the ordinary linearized tetrad/Fierz-Pauli complex. At a nonzero null covector k, quotienting the Einstein-symbol kernel by


h_μν ~ h_μν + k_μ ξ_ν + k_ν ξ_μ


leaves the two transverse-traceless components. In the physical Lorentz convention η=diag(-1,-1,-1,+1), one may choose a null direction in the (3,5) plane; the surviving transverse components may be represented by


h_11=-h_22,


h_12=h_21.


Thus the local massless spin-two class has exactly two physical polarizations.


G. Duplicate-spin-two counterexample audit


1. Orthogonal coframe e_H. Section 16.I.76 gives no derivative kinetic term and an invertible algebraic Hessian on every finite nonbalanced branch. Algebraic elimination cannot create a second independent principal characteristic line.


2. B sector. ker T_w is exact B-shift gauge and im T_w is algebraically invertible hard B. B contributes no local spin-two BRST class.


3. Non-Lorentz E8 connection. Section 16.I.77 gives Ncal_w=Ncal_w Π_L, so Π_L d a_⊥=0. No non-Lorentz adjoint connection field has an independent Einstein-type two-derivative principal symbol in S_76. Lower-derivative soldering or mass mixing cannot create another highest-derivative graviton corridor.


4. Transverse 30380 condensate. Principal derivatives transverse to V_C are annihilated by P_F in the declared gravitational parent. The only derivative condensate line that survives is the Stückelberg-completed e_F combination already treated above.


5. Selector/rapidity fluctuations. These are zero-form order-parameter fluctuations that alter coefficients and projectors. They do not supply a second independent coframe principal symbol in S_76.


6. Constrained condensate-connection mixture. Any candidate made from the physical tangent Goldstone and selected P/K connection reduces, under the invertible change of variables above, to the contractible (π,c_F) sector plus 𝔢=e_F. Any candidate involving transverse or non-Lorentz fields lacks the required independent degree-two Einstein symbol. Hence no second local ghost-number-zero spin-two class can be assembled from a constrained mixture without adding a new kinetic invariant to the action.


H. Principal spin-two BRST theorem


EXACT THEOREM — action-relative principal-symbol statement.


For the declared parent S_76, about a finite r_phys=4 nondegenerate soldered stationary branch, and using the selector-covariant simultaneous transport of C and the selected physical h_a subspace already established in §16.I.76,


P_F' = R(g)P_F R(g)^{-1},


ν_F'=R(g)ν_F,


and the physical-orbit condensate tangent plus broken selected P/K connection forms a Stückelberg complex whose Goldstone/ghost sector is BRST-contractible. After this contractible sector and the residual Lorentz gauge are removed, the only local two-derivative spin-two principal complex is the ordinary Einstein/Fierz-Pauli complex carried by e_F.


Therefore


H^0_BRST,principal(spin 2; S_76)
≅ H^0_Fierz-Pauli
≅ one ordinary massless graviton,


with two local transverse-traceless physical polarizations.


No second spin-two class is supplied by e_H, B, the non-Lorentz E8 connection, transverse condensate directions, selector fluctuations, or a constrained mixture of those sectors within S_76.


FIREWALL. This theorem is local, nonzero-momentum, two-derivative, and action-relative. It must be reopened if a future deformation gives an independent two-derivative kinetic operator to e_H, the (10,4) non-Lorentz vector-valued one-forms, transverse 30380 fields, or another currently auxiliary sector. It does not establish the complete all-field BRST determinant or settle scalar/vector/spinorial signs, global/topological zero modes, radial tensor stability, topological-sector selection, absolute mirror selection, or higher-derivative corrections.


The immediate frontier therefore moves beyond the duplicate-graviton question to the remaining sign and stability gates: effective Newton sign, scalar and vector stability, spinorial/possible spin-3/2 stability, multidirectional selector/condensate radial stability, global/topological zero modes, dynamical topological-sector selection, absolute mirror selection, and higher-derivative corrections.




§16.I.79 — Effective Newton Sign, Hard-B Elimination, and the Relative-Normalization Gate


Status. This section resolves what the declared parent S_76 can and cannot determine about the Einstein-Hilbert kinetic sign. The result is an exact coefficient reduction plus a no-go against deriving an absolute Newton sign from the isolated gravitational parent. The physically meaningful sign is relative to a separately normalized positive-energy matter sector; that relative normalization is not yet present in S_76.


A. Exact sign-relevant coefficient after hard-B elimination


On the physical Lorentz image, the selector operator has the positive eigenvalue


t_L=3/4,


and Δ_w>0 for every finite real rapidity. Write


J:=Π_L J_F=(m_C/2)M_ab e_F^a∧e_F^b.


The B-dependent Lorentz part of S_76 is


L_B=t_L[
β κ(B∧Ncal_w F)
+g_J κ(B∧J)
-(α/2)Δ_w κ(B∧B)
].


The algebraic B equation is


αΔ_w B=β Ncal_w F+g_J J,


so


B=(αΔ_w)^(-1)(β Ncal_w F+g_J J).


Because two-forms wedge symmetrically and κ is symmetric, substitution gives


L_B,eff
=t_L/(2αΔ_w)
κ[(βNcal_w F+g_JJ)∧(βNcal_w F+g_JJ)].


The curvature–coframe cross term is therefore exactly


L_FJ
=(t_L βg_J)/(αΔ_w)
κ(Ncal_w F∧J).


Using the already-proved Lorentz reduction


Δ_w^(-1)Ncal_w
=Π_C I-Ξ_C Jcal_L,


one obtains


boxed:
L_FJ
=(t_L βg_J/α)
κ[(Π_C I-Ξ_C Jcal_L)F∧J].


Thus Δ_w drops out of the gravitational cross coefficient completely. In the normalization


J=(m_C/2)M_ab e_F^a∧e_F^b,


the common sign-relevant coefficient is


boxed:
C_grav ∝ (t_L/2)(βg_Jm_C/α),


with t_L>0 and only a fixed nonzero invariant-contraction normalization suppressed. Equivalently,


boxed:
sgn C_grav
=sgn(σ_inv βg_Jm_C/α),


where σ_inv is fixed once the Book-16 Killing/generator/orientation convention is fixed.


B. Holst versus Einstein-Cartan sign


The selected-line reduction of §16.I.76 reads


S_grav=
C_H ∫R_ab(ω)∧e_F^a∧e_F^b
+C_EC ∫ε_abcd R^{ab}(ω)∧e_F^c∧e_F^d
+Λ_C ∫ε_abcd e_F^a∧e_F^b∧e_F^c∧e_F^d,


with


C_H∝C_grav Π_C,
C_EC∝C_grav Ξ_C


in the fixed orientation convention.


For every finite w, Ξ_C≠0. Hence the Einstein-Cartan coefficient cannot vanish unless the common action coefficient vanishes. The Holst coefficient can pass through zero with Π_C without destroying the Einstein-Hilbert kinetic corridor.


The connection equation is controlled by


Π_C I-Ξ_C *_L,


which is invertible for finite w because *_L²=-I. Therefore the sign question is not a torsion or invertibility question. It is solely the sign of the surviving Einstein-Hilbert coefficient after the torsion-free connection is eliminated.


C. Coupling-sign freedom is genuine


The ratio


βg_J/α


is invariant under every nonzero rescaling of the auxiliary hard field B. If B→sB, then


β→sβ,
g_J→sg_J,
α→s²α,


and βg_J/α is unchanged. In particular B→-B flips β and g_J together and leaves their product unchanged. Thus the sign entering C_grav is not an artifact of the arbitrary sign chosen for B.


However S_76 contains no theorem fixing the sign of βg_J/α relative to the invariant moment-map normalization m_C. Representation theory fixes the allowed tensor structures and the nonvanishing of the physical moment-map channel; it does not choose the sign of an independent action coupling.


D. Overall-action-sign no-go


At the classical level,


S_76→-S_76


leaves the Euler-Lagrange equations unchanged while reversing every coefficient in the action, including the Einstein-Hilbert coefficient. Therefore an isolated classical gravitational parent cannot assign physical meaning to its own overall action sign.


The Newton sign becomes physical only after comparison with a reference sector whose positive-energy/positive-norm convention has been fixed independently. If Z_ref denotes the kinetic normalization of such a surviving matter sector, the invariant question is not


sgn C_EC


by itself, but


boxed:
sgn(C_EC/Z_ref).


An overall sign reversal of the complete unified action changes numerator and denominator together and leaves this ratio unchanged.


Since S_76 does not yet contain a certified reference matter kinetic block with fixed Z_ref, the positive-Newton requirement cannot be derived from S_76 alone.


E. Radial stabilization does not close the Newton sign


Section 16.I.75 proves that finite-rank radial witnesses exist when the quadratic hard-curvature term and the quartic J_F volume term have the stabilizing relative sign. That condition constrains a combination involving the hard topological curvature/flux sector, g_J, λ_J, and the moment-map normalization.


It does not fix the sign of C_EC for two independent reasons.


First, the hard curvature/topological sector and its orientation are not dynamically selected by the present local parent. Reversing the relevant flux/Pfaffian sign changes the quadratic rank Hessian without supplying a universal sign for the Einstein-Hilbert coefficient.


Second, the quartic coefficient Λ_C∝λ_J m_C² constrains λ_J relative to the rank Hessian, whereas the gravitational kinetic coefficient contains βg_Jm_C/α. No theorem in S_76 identifies these as one sign-locked invariant normalization.


Therefore radial stability and positive Newton sign remain distinct gates.


F. Mirror firewall


The conclusion of §16.I.76.9 remains intact. Under the correlated mirror operation, Ξ_C and the selector-derived Lorentz Hodge operator Jcal_L reverse together, so the physical Hodge-completed parent is mirror-even. Choosing the healthy relative Newton sign therefore does not select an absolute mirror sheet.


G. Effective-Newton sign theorem and no-go


EXACT THEOREM — coefficient reduction.


For S_76 on a finite soldered Lorentz branch, hard-B elimination produces a unique Einstein-Cartan/Holst kinetic corridor whose common sign-relevant coefficient satisfies


boxed:
sgn C_grav
=sgn(σ_inv βg_Jm_C/α),


with t_L=3/4>0 and Δ_w>0 cancelling from the cross coefficient. The Einstein-Cartan coefficient is nonzero for finite w whenever this common coefficient is nonzero.


EXACT NO-GO — isolated-parent sign selection.


Neither the E8(-24) representation data, the rapidity/Hodge response functions, hard-B elimination, nor the existing radial stabilization theorem fixes the physically meaningful positive-Newton sign. An isolated classical S_76 has an overall-action-sign redundancy, and no certified positive-energy reference matter kinetic coefficient is present against which C_EC can be compared.


Hence the former “effective Newton sign” gate reduces to


boxed:
RELATIVE MATTER/GRAVITY NORMALIZATION GATE — OPEN.


To close it, a later parent must derive at least one physical matter kinetic block and its coefficient from the same microscopic normalization, then prove


C_EC/Z_ref>0


in the convention where the reference mode has positive norm/energy.


FIREWALL. This no-go does not say that S_76 forces negative Newton coupling. It says the opposite: both sign choices remain action-level possibilities until a relative matter normalization is derived. Imposing the healthy sign by hand is consistent but is not a prediction.


Updated frontier. The duplicate-graviton gate is closed by §16.I.78. The absolute Newton-sign question is now reduced to the relative matter/gravity normalization gate. The next local dynamical calculation is therefore the scalar-sector Hessian/BRST audit, beginning with selector/rapidity fluctuations and condensate-radial fluctuations around the finite soldered stationary branch.




§16.I.80 — Scalar Principal-Symbol Reduction, Radial Projection No-Go, and the Remaining Algebraic Hessian Gate


Status. This section begins the post-spin-two stability audit. For the declared two-derivative parent S_76, the scalar problem separates into two qualitatively different questions: whether any independent local scalar carries a two-derivative kinetic principal symbol, and whether the remaining zero-form/order-parameter directions have a healthy algebraic stationary Hessian. The first question is largely closed below; the second remains the live scalar gate.


A. Scalar-sector inventory after §§16.I.76–79


The exact reductions already remove the following apparent scalar sources from an independent propagating scalar count:


1. The scalar/trace pieces contained inside the tetrad perturbation belong to the ordinary Einstein/Fierz-Pauli constrained complex. Section 16.I.78 leaves only the two transverse-traceless graviton polarizations in local ghost-number-zero spin-two cohomology; there is no independent metric scalar graviton.


2. The B kernel is exact shift gauge and the hard B image is algebraically auxiliary.


3. The orthogonal completed coframe e_H is algebraically auxiliary with invertible Hessian on the finite nonbalanced branch.


4. Broken physical-orbit condensate tangents are Stückelberg-contractible with their selected P/K ghosts; their gauge-covariant derivative combination is e_F itself.


Thus the genuinely unresolved scalar-like fields are zero-form selector/rapidity directions, condensate directions orthogonal to V_C, condensate radial amplitudes, and any later scalar mode introduced by a new kinetic invariant.


B. Derivative support of the filtered condensate parent


For the 30380 condensate, S_76 depends on condensate derivatives only through


ν_F=P_F D_A C.


Section 16.I.77 already proves that, at principal-symbol level,


P_F D(δC_perp)=0


for every transverse condensate fluctuation δC_perp outside V_C. Hence no transverse condensate direction receives an independent two-derivative kinetic block from the declared parent.


The surviving V_C derivative is the physical coframe/Stückelberg combination already reduced in §16.I.78. It cannot be counted again as a scalar field.


C. Exact radial projection no-go


Let the invariant real bilinear form on the 30380 be h_R and let


v_a=h_a C.


Because h_R is E8-invariant, every represented Lie-algebra generator is h_R-skew:


h_R(h_a u,v)+h_R(u,h_a v)=0.


Setting u=v=C gives


2h_R(h_aC,C)=0,


so


boxed:
h_R(v_a,C)=0.


Since P_F is the h_R-orthogonal projector onto V_C=span{v_a},


boxed:
P_F C=0.


Now take a pure condensate-radial fluctuation about a stationary background,


δC_rad=σ Cbar.


At principal derivative order,


Pbar_F D(δC_rad)
=(dσ)Pbar_F Cbar
+lower-derivative terms
=0+lower-derivative terms.


Therefore


boxed:
N_scalar,radial^(2-derivative)=0


for S_76. The condensate radial amplitude may have an algebraic potential/Hessian, but it does not acquire an independent local two-derivative kinetic operator from J_F.


This is stronger than merely saying that a radial mode was not seen in the spin-two audit: the physical tangent filter annihilates its principal derivative exactly.


D. Rapidity/selector derivative support


The rapidity selector w enters T_w, Ncal_w, Δ_w, Π_C, Ξ_C and the associated projectors as zero-form constitutive data. Varying those functions is algebraic in δw and does not by itself generate dδw dδw.


The already-earned moving-normal connection is


A_D=-(1/2)dw,


or A_D=-(1/4)dζ in full-rapidity normalization. On the covariantly transported stabilizer branch,


A_geom=ω^{ab}M_ab-(1/2)dw D,


and because [D,M_ab]=0 and d²w=0,


boxed:
F_geom=R^{ab}M_ab,
F_D=0.


Hence the curvature/BF part of S_76 supplies no rapidity kinetic principal block.


The only possible remaining two-derivative selector entrance would have to come through ν_F=P_FD_AC if the D-compensator has a nonzero projection onto the selected physical tangent. Define the finite background vector


nu_D:=Pbar_F ρ(D)Cbar.


Then a local selector fluctuation can contribute to ν_F at derivative order only proportionally to


-(1/2)nu_D d(δw)


(up to the fixed half/full-rapidity normalization).


Thus the entire remaining selector-kinetic question reduces to one finite representation gate:


boxed:
nu_D=Pbar_F ρ(D)Cbar ?=0.


On a stationary branch for which the condensate is invariant under the selected Lorentz stabilizer, D commutes with that Lorentz algebra while V_C is a Lorentz-vector module. Then ρ(D)Cbar is Lorentz-scalar and cannot have an invariant projection into V_C; consequently nu_D=0. This gives a conditional selector no-kinetic theorem on the intended Lorentz-stabilized branch.


FIREWALL. Until the Lorentz-stabilizer property of the actual stationary condensate is inserted explicitly into the full coupled solution, the safest exact statement is the finite gate above rather than a universal nu_D=0 theorem for every off-shell 30380 background.


E. The old radial witness is a vacuum-amplitude test, not a scalar particle


Section 16.I.75 chooses a full-rank negative direction ebar in the split-signature coframe Hessian and scales


e^a=q ebar^a.


Along that diagnostic ray,


V(q)=Aq²+Bq⁴+O(q⁶),


with A<0 from the hard topological rank seed and healthy B>0 from the selector-derived quartic volume sign. The nonzero stationary point satisfies


q_*²=-A/(2B),


and therefore


V''(q_*)
=2A+12Bq_*²
=-4A
>0.


Thus the one-dimensional coframe-amplitude ray is algebraically stable at the exhibited witness.


This does not add a propagating scalar. q is a uniform background/coframe-amplitude diagnostic inside the gravitational order parameter. The local scalar/trace tetrad fluctuation remains constrained by the Einstein BRST complex.


It also does not prove stability of condensate-radial or selector directions; §16.I.75 explicitly left those coupled amplitudes outside the witness.


F. Minimal coupled scalar Schur test


After the exact B, e_H, spin-connection, and broken-orbit reductions, let y^I denote the remaining genuine scalar zero-form/order-parameter coordinates. At the local two-derivative level established above, their immediate stability question is the algebraic Schur Hessian


H^alg_IJ
=∂_I∂_J V_eff
-H_I A (H_A B)^(-1) H_B J,


where A,B label any still-retained algebraic auxiliary coordinates being eliminated. A healthy isolated stationary branch requires the physical scalar Hessian on non-gauge directions to have the appropriate positive/stable signature; a zero eigenvalue is a modulus/constraint gate and a negative eigenvalue is an algebraic saddle direction. Neither should be mislabeled as a propagating ghost in the absence of a kinetic principal block.


For the minimal two-coordinate diagnostic in which the coframe amplitude q is stabilized while its coefficients depend on a selector coordinate w,


V(q,w)=A(w)q²+B(w)q⁴,


radial elimination gives


q_*²(w)=-A(w)/(2B(w)),


V_*(w)=-A(w)²/[4B(w)].


At a simultaneous stationary point, the selector Schur Hessian is equivalently


boxed:
H_ww^Schur
=V_ww-V_wq²/V_qq
=d²/dw²[-A(w)²/(4B(w))].


This formula isolates exactly what the present radial witness did not test.


G. Scalar principal-symbol theorem and updated gate


EXACT THEOREM — action-relative principal support.


Within S_76, about a finite nondegenerate soldered branch:


• transverse 30380 condensate derivatives are killed by P_F;
• a pure condensate-radial derivative is killed exactly because P_F Cbar=0;
• physical V_C condensate derivatives belong to the already-reduced coframe/Stückelberg sector;
• B and e_H supply no independent scalar kinetic line;
• the Einstein/Fierz-Pauli complex contains no independent scalar graviton;
• the covariantly transported rapidity compensator has F_D=0, so the curvature sector supplies no rapidity kinetic block.


Therefore the declared parent has no presently certified independent scalar two-derivative principal corridor outside the gravitational coframe complex. The only finite selector-kinetic loophole is the explicit projection nu_D=Pbar_Fρ(D)Cbar; it vanishes on the intended Lorentz-stabilized condensate branch by scalar-vector covariance, but that branch condition must remain explicit.


RECLASSIFIED SCALAR GATE:


boxed:
SCALAR GHOST-KINETIC GATE — NO CURRENT INDEPENDENT KINETIC CORRIDOR IN S_76;
SCALAR ALGEBRAIC/STATIONARY HESSIAN GATE — OPEN.


The next exact calculation is to compute the coupled selector/condensate scalar Hessian on an actual finite stationary topological branch, rather than on a one-dimensional coframe ray. In particular one must determine A(w), B(w), the condensate-radial dependence of m_C and J_F, and all scalar mixing terms after the exact auxiliary Schur reductions.




§16.I.81 — Fixed-Ray Selector Hessian, Unique Finite Rapidity Extremum, and Conditional Joint q–w Stability


Status. Section 16.I.80 reduced the scalar problem to an algebraic stationary Hessian and gave the general Schur criterion. This section closes the minimal two-variable selector/coframe-amplitude diagnostic on the already-used shared-form hard-curvature witness. It is exact within that fixed-ray, co-moving selector reduction. It is not yet the full multidirectional tensor/condensate scalar Hessian.


A. Fixed shared-form reduction


Use the physical hard Lorentz seed already employed in §§16.I.74–75,


F_L=E_35 f,


for which hard-B elimination gives


B_0=(β/α)(Π_C E_35+Ξ_C E_12)f


up to the fixed Book-16 Hodge/orientation convention. Equivalently one may use the sign convention B_0=(β/α)(Π_C E_12-Ξ_C E_35)f; the difference is absorbed below into the two fixed contraction constants.


Choose a fixed full-rank coframe ray


e^a=q ebar^a


and define


J_ebar:=(m_C/2)M_ab ebar^a∧ebar^b.


The quadratic rank term is linear in B_0 and quadratic in q. Therefore, for fixed topological seed f and fixed ray ebar, its rapidity dependence has the exact two-response form


boxed:
A(w)=u Π_C(w)-v Ξ_C(w),


where u and v are w-independent real constants containing βg_J/α, m_C, the base-form integral, the invariant contraction with the direct E_35 leg and its Hodge E_12 leg, and the fixed orientation convention.


The orientation sign carried by Ξ_C may be absorbed into v. The physically relevant assumption for this section is u v≠0; if either coefficient vanishes, the full-Pfaffian shared-form rank witness degenerates or the selector extremum moves to a boundary/balanced case.


On the co-moving selector ray, the quartic invariant


λ_J κ(J_ebar∧Jcal_L J_ebar)


is unchanged by simultaneous conjugation of Jcal_L and the selected Lorentz frame. Write its fixed healthy coefficient as


B_4>0.


Thus the minimal reduced potential is


boxed:
V(q,w)=A(w)q²+B_4 q⁴.


If a future stationary solution makes the topological seed, m_C, ebar, or the quartic normalization vary independently with w, the general §16.I.80 formula must be used instead; this fixed-ray theorem then becomes only a local diagnostic subblock.


B. Native rapidity functions


Use the live half-rapidity convention


Π_C(w)=tanh(2w),


Ξ_C(w)=2 cosh w/(cosh²w+1),


with the orientation sign already absorbed into v.


Set


y=sinh w.


Then


Π_C=2y sqrt(1+y²)/(1+2y²),


Ξ_C=2 sqrt(1+y²)/(y²+2).


Because dy/dw=cosh w>0, a finite stationary point of A(w) is equivalently a stationary point of A as a function of y.


C. Exact selector stationarity equation


For


A=uΠ_C-vΞ_C,


the nonbalanced stationarity condition A'(w)=0 gives exactly


boxed:
v/u
=-(y²+2)²/[y³(2y²+1)²].


Define


R(y)=(y²+2)²/[y³(2y²+1)²].


Its derivative is


R'(y)
=-3(y²+2)(2y⁴+9y²+2)/[y⁴(2y²+1)³]
<0


for every y≠0.


Moreover,


R(y)→+∞ as y→0+,
R(y)→0+ as y→+∞,


and by odd sign of the y³ denominator,


R(y)→-∞ as y→0-,
R(y)→0- as y→-∞.


Therefore:


EXACT RESULT.
For every finite nonzero ratio v/u there exists exactly one finite nonbalanced selector stationary point y_*=sinh w_*.


Its side is fixed by


boxed:
sgn y_*=-sgn(v/u).


Thus the minimal selector problem has no multiplicity of finite extrema on a chosen contraction ratio: the direct/Hodge contraction ratio selects one relative-rapidity side.


D. Exact selector Hessian sign


At the stationary point determined above,


A_*
=2u(1+y²)^(3/2)(2y⁴-y²+2)/[y³(2y²+1)²],


and


A''_*
=-6u sqrt(1+y²)(2y⁴+9y²+2)/[y(y²+2)(2y²+1)³].


Their product is


A_* A''_*
=-12u²(1+y²)²(2y⁴-y²+2)(2y⁴+9y²+2)
/[y⁴(y²+2)(2y²+1)^5].


Every factor in the denominator is positive for y≠0. Also


2y⁴-y²+2>0


because the corresponding quadratic 2z²-z+2 in z=y² has negative discriminant, and


2y⁴+9y²+2>0.


Hence


boxed:
A_* A''_*<0


for every finite nonbalanced stationary point.


Radially eliminating q gives


V_*(w)=-A(w)²/(4B_4).


At A'=0,


H_ww^Schur
=V_*''(w_*)
=-A_*A''_*/(2B_4).


Therefore, for the healthy quartic sign B_4>0,


boxed:
H_ww^Schur>0.


The unique finite selector extremum is automatically locally stable in the selector direction within this fixed-ray reduction.


E. Joint radial and selector stability


The nonzero coframe-amplitude stationary point exists when


q_*²=-A_*/(2B_4)>0,


so A_*<0. From the stationarity relation,


sgn A_*=-sgn v.


Therefore the finite-rank branch condition is simply


boxed:
v>0


in the orientation/contraction convention used to define A=uΠ_C-vΞ_C.


When v>0 and B_4>0,


V_qq(q_*,w_*)=-4A_*>0,


and the Schur selector Hessian is also positive:


H_ww^Schur>0.


Thus the minimal q–w block is jointly locally stable after radial Schur reduction.


The selected relative-rapidity side is then


sgn w_*=-sgn u


because v>0 and sgn sinh w=sgn w.


This is a relative selector result tied to the sign of the hard direct-leg contraction u. It is not an absolute mirror-selection theorem.


F. Degenerate cases


1. v=0. Then A=u tanh(2w) is monotone and has no finite nonbalanced extremum. The Hodge leg is essential for finite selector stationarity in this reduction.


2. u=0. Then A=-vΞ_C is even and stationary at w=0. But Π_C=0 there, so the full-Pfaffian hard rank witness used in §§16.I.74–75 loses the nonbalanced Π_CΞ_C≠0 condition. This does not furnish the finite nonbalanced branch under audit.


3. B_4≤0. The quartic does not stabilize the nonzero radial branch; the selector Hessian result does not repair a wrong radial quartic sign.


4. w-dependent u, v, or B_4. Then the unique-extremum theorem above need not hold. One must revert to the general Schur formula of §16.I.80.


G. Fixed-ray selector stability theorem


EXACT THEOREM — conditional on the declared fixed shared-form/co-moving reduction.


Let the reduced rank potential be


V(q,w)=[uΠ_C(w)-vΞ_C(w)]q²+B_4q⁴,


with u v≠0, B_4>0,


Π_C=tanh(2w),
Ξ_C=2cosh w/(cosh²w+1).


Then:


• there is exactly one finite nonbalanced selector stationary point for every nonzero v/u;
• its sign obeys sgn w_*=-sgn(v/u);
• its selector Schur Hessian is strictly positive;
• a nonzero radially stable q_* exists precisely when v>0 in the chosen convention;
• when v>0, both V_qq and H_ww^Schur are positive at the joint stationary point.
