Hence the minimal q–w scalar/vacuum-amplitude block does not contain a local saddle once the healthy Hodge quartic sign and the finite-rank v>0 condition are imposed.


FIREWALL. This theorem does not close the complete scalar Hessian of S_76. The actual topological branch may allow w-dependent seed orientation, coframe ray, condensate radial normalization m_C, or additional scalar mixing. It also does not promote w to a propagating scalar; §16.I.80 found no independent certified scalar kinetic corridor. The theorem closes only the fixed-ray algebraic selector/coframe-amplitude subproblem.


Updated frontier. The next scalar calculation is no longer the abstract q–w Hessian. It is the condensate-radial and transverse scalar Schur block on the actual stationary topological branch, together with the finite selector projection nu_D=P_Fρ(D)C. After that, the audit should move to vector and spinorial sectors unless a new scalar kinetic invariant is introduced.




§16.I.82 — Quasi-Linear Projectivization, Degree-Zero Physical Moment Map, and the Radial-Scale BRST Doublet


Status. The normalization issue exposed after §16.I.81 has the same mathematical architecture as the earlier quasi-linear/projective reductions, but it must not be confused with an already-existing gauge symmetry of S_76. This section first derives the exact degree-zero soldering carrier on the nonzero physical branch, then defines and audits a distinct quasi-linearly normalized parent. The result is a CONDITIONAL ACTION MECHANISM: on s_C≠0 the condensate radial normalization can be promoted to an exact local projective redundancy without disturbing the previously closed one-spin-two structure. The zero section and any future terms that depend on the unnormalized condensate remain separate gates.


A. Exact radial homogeneity of the physical tangent data


Write locally on a connected nonzero branch


C=ρ Ĉ,


with ρ>0 and Ĉ a fixed radial representative. Then


v_a(C)=h_a C=ρ vhat_a,


and, because


h_R(v_a,v_b)=s_C η_ab,


one has


s_C=ρ² shat_C.


The physical tangent projector is homogeneous of degree zero:


P_F(C)
=(1/s_C)v_a η^{ab}h_R(v_b,·)
=P_F(Ĉ).


The coframe is also exactly degree zero. Since E8-invariance of h_R gives


h_R(h_a Ĉ,Ĉ)=0,


the radial derivative term drops out:


D_A C=(dρ)Ĉ+ρD_AĈ,


and therefore


e_F^a(C)
=(1/s_C)η^{ab}h_R(v_b,D_AC)
=(1/shat_C)η^{ab}h_R(vhat_b,D_AĈ)
=e_F^a(Ĉ).


Thus


boxed:
C→ρC leaves P_F and e_F invariant.


By contrast,


ν_F=P_FD_AC


has degree one,


ν_F(C)=ρ νhat_F,


and hence


J_F=(1/2)μ(ν_F∧ν_F)


has degree two:


J_F(C)=ρ² Jhat_F.


This agrees with the already-proved physical moment-map normalization


m_C=λ_L s_C,


P_LJ_F=(m_C/2)M_ab e_F^a∧e_F^b.


B. Degree-zero quasi-linear current


On s_C≠0 define


boxed:
mathbb J_F:=J_F/s_C.


Equivalently, with


vtilde_a=v_a/sqrt(|s_C|),
νtilde_F=ν_F/sqrt(|s_C|),
σ_C=sgn(s_C),


one may write


mathbb J_F
=σ_C (1/2)μ(νtilde_F∧νtilde_F).


Its physical Lorentz projection is canonical:


boxed:
P_L mathbb J_F
=(λ_L/2)M_ab e_F^a∧e_F^b.


All dependence on the condensate radial magnitude has disappeared. The geometric objects


P_F,
e_F,
mathbb J_F


are degree-zero/projective data, while s_C carries the separate radial magnitude.


This is precisely the normalization architecture already used by the quasi-linear branch: common homogeneous scale is separated from projective/angular transport. It is not another qSaw iteration and does not alter the established half-rapidity degree-doubling rules.


C. Exact local projective-scale transformation


Consider the local positive rescaling


δ_σ C=σ(x)C,
δ_σ A=0,


with selector/rapidity data held fixed. Then


δ_σ s_C=2σ s_C,
δ_σ P_F=0.


Moreover


δ_σ(D_AC)=dσ C+σD_AC,


so, using P_FC=0,


δ_σν_F
=P_F(dσ C+σD_AC)
=σν_F.


Therefore


δ_σJ_F=2σJ_F,


and hence


boxed:
δ_σ mathbb J_F=0.


Since ν_F=v_a e_F^a and both ν_F and v_a have weight one,


boxed:
δ_σ e_F^a=0.


Thus the quasi-linearly normalized physical soldering data are invariant under arbitrary local positive changes of condensate normalization.


D. Why S_76 itself does not have this scale symmetry


The declared parent uses the homogeneous current J_F, not mathbb J_F:


S_76⊃
g_J κ(B∧T_wP_LJ_F)
+λ_J κ(P_LJ_F∧Jcal_LP_LJ_F).


Writing J_F=s_C mathbb J_F makes the radial weights explicit:


S_76⊃
g_J s_C κ(B∧T_wP_Lmathbb J_F)
+λ_J s_C² κ(P_Lmathbb J_F∧Jcal_LP_Lmathbb J_F).


Therefore


C→e^{σ(x)}C


is not a symmetry of S_76. The apparent r–q flat direction found by keeping only the hard-seed J_F corridor is a kernel of that reduced parametrization, not an already-earned E8/BRST gauge orbit of the full declared parent.


EXACT NO-GO:
one may not simply quotient the condensate radius out of S_76 and call the resulting zero Hessian eigenvalue gauge.


E. Quasi-linearly normalized parent


The user-identified normalization pattern suggests a distinct branchwise parent. On the already-certified s_C≠0 physical hard-orbit branch define


S_82=
∫[
 β κ(B∧T_wNcal_wF)
 -(α/2)Δ_w κ(B∧T_wB)
 +g_Q κ(B∧T_wP_Lmathbb J_F)
 +λ_Q κ(P_Lmathbb J_F∧Jcal_LP_Lmathbb J_F)
].


This differs from S_76 only by quasi-linear radial normalization of the physical moment-map source and its quartic stabilizer. It is rational/projective in C and is intentionally outside a globally polynomial description at s_C=0.


Because mathbb J_F is E8-covariant and invariant under the local radial rescaling above, S_82 has both the original gauge covariance and the additional exact local projective symmetry


boxed:
C(x)→e^{σ(x)}C(x)


on every connected fixed-sign s_C≠0 branch.


F. Radial BRST doublet


Introduce the local radial coordinate


r_C=(1/2)ln|s_C|.


Under the new scale symmetry,


δ_σr_C=σ.


Introduce an abelian scale ghost c_R. The corresponding principal/local BRST differential contains


s_R r_C=c_R,
s_R c_R=0,


while


s_RP_F=0,
s_Re_F=0,
s_Rmathbb J_F=0.


Hence (r_C,c_R), together with its jet prolongations, is a standard BRST-contractible pair. Locally,


boxed:
H^0_BRST contains no independent condensate-radial scalar from this normalization direction in S_82.


The scale BRST differential commutes with the E8 differential on the normalized variables because the radial rescaling is central in representation space and s_C is E8-invariant under the simultaneous moving-frame transport.


G. Hard-B, rank, and quartic audit


The matched B-kernel shift is unchanged because every B-dependent term still contains T_w adjacent to B:


δB=Pi_0(T_w)Λ_2.


The hard B image remains algebraically invertible for α≠0 and finite w.


On the physical tangent,


P_Lmathbb J_F
=(λ_L/2)M_ab e_F^a∧e_F^b,


so a hard topological B_0 couples to exactly the same canonical e_F∧e_F tensor as before, now without an independent condensate-radius coefficient. The split-signature full-Pfaffian Hessian theorem therefore survives up to the nonzero overall coupling g_Qλ_L.


The normalized Hodge quartic reduces to


λ_Q κ(P_Lmathbb J_F∧Jcal_LP_Lmathbb J_F)
=Lambda_Q ε_abcd e_F^a∧e_F^b∧e_F^c∧e_F^d,


with Lambda_Q proportional to λ_Qλ_L² and no s_C magnitude.


The unrotated square generated algebraically by hard-B elimination vanishes on the canonical tetrad moment map:


boxed:
κ(P_Lmathbb J_F∧P_Lmathbb J_F)=0.


Indeed the Lorentz Killing contraction is proportional to


(η_acη_bd-η_adη_bc)
e^a∧e^b∧e^c∧e^d,


and each metric contraction forces a repeated coframe one-form. The nonzero quartic is specifically the Hodge-rotated Jcal_L contraction.


Therefore the hard-flux quadratic instability and the lawful Hodge quartic stabilizer survive the quasi-linear normalization without introducing a second independent quartic.


H. One-coframe and spin-two re-audit for S_82


The filter P_F is unchanged and mathbb J_F depends only on e_F. Therefore the P/K curvature decomposition still has one selected gravitational source line and the orthogonal e_H line remains outside the moment map.


The B shift and hard-B auxiliary reduction are unchanged. The finite-w Lorentz operator


Pi_CI-Xi_C*_L


is unchanged and remains invertible. Eliminating the torsion connection therefore again produces the ordinary Einstein/Fierz-Pauli two-derivative principal structure on e_F.


The moving-projector/Stückelberg proof of §16.I.78 is also unchanged because it depends on P_F, ν_F/v_a=e_F, and the broken-orbit gauge complex, not on the radial magnitude multiplying J_F. The only new local ghost is c_R, paired contractibly with r_C.


Hence, after explicit re-audit rather than blind inheritance,


boxed:
H^0_BRST,principal(spin 2;S_82)
≅ one ordinary massless graviton,


with two local transverse-traceless polarizations.


I. Newton normalization after projectivization


Hard-B elimination now gives the selected gravitational cross coefficient


C_grav,Q proportional to
(t_L/2)(βg_Qλ_L/α),


t_L=3/4>0,


with no factor m_C or |C|². Thus the condensate radial normalization no longer masquerades as a Newton normalization in the normalized parent.


The §16.I.79 relative-normalization firewall nevertheless remains. The physical sign and magnitude of gravity are meaningful only relative to a derived matter kinetic normalization. S_82 removes an internal condensate normalization ambiguity; it does not derive the matter/gravity ratio.


J. Relation to the §16.I.81 q–w theorem


In S_76, allowing the condensate radius to vary makes the diagnostic J_F potential scale schematically as


s_C A(w)q²+s_C²B(w)q⁴,


which depends only on the composite amplitude sqrt(|s_C|)q on a fixed-sign branch and therefore produces an apparent normalization valley in that truncated corridor.


In S_82 the quasi-linear current removes s_C before the action is formed. The fixed-ray potential is directly


V_Q(q,w)=A_Q(w)q²+B_Q(w)q⁴,


with no condensate-radius coordinate. Therefore the exact unique-selector-extremum and positive Schur-Hessian theorem of §16.I.81 applies without an additional r_C–q normalization degeneracy, subject to the same fixed-ray/topological-seed firewalls.


K. Regularity and branch firewall


S_82 is defined only where


s_C≠0.


It therefore cannot explain why the theory occupies a nonzero physical condensate orbit rather than the zero section. It projectivizes the normalization after the nonzero branch is available; it does not dynamically create that branch.


Accordingly:


EXACT THEOREM:
on s_C≠0, P_F and e_F are degree zero, J_F/s_C is a gauge-covariant degree-zero physical moment map, and the normalized parent has an exact local radial-scale symmetry whose logarithmic radius/ghost pair is BRST-contractible.


CONDITIONAL ACTION MECHANISM:
S_82 lawfully implements the recurring quasi-linear normalization pattern and removes the condensate radial normalization from the local gravitational/scalar spectrum while preserving the hard-flux rank mechanism and the one-graviton principal theorem.


OPEN PHYSICAL GATES:
1. justify the projective/quasi-linear parent from the microscopic E8(-24) theory rather than choosing it solely to remove a normalization;
2. explain or otherwise control the exclusion/selection of the s_C=0 section;
3. require every future matter or higher-order term either to respect the local radial scaling with consistent weights or to reopen the condensate-radial scalar audit;
4. compute the relative matter/gravity kinetic normalization;
5. continue the vector and spinorial stability audits and the global/topological zero-mode analysis.


This is the precise sense in which the normalization problem is “the quasi-linear again”: the correct object is the projective degree-zero carrier, but promoting that normalization to a symmetry is an explicit branchwise action choice, not a theorem about the unnormalized S_76.




§16.I.83 — Vector Principal-Symbol No-Go, Safe Residual-Internal Yang–Mills Corridor, and the Newton-Normalization Pairing


Status. Sections 16.I.77–82 remove the duplicate-graviton and condensate-normalization problems at the declared principal level. The next question is whether any non-gravitational spin-one field already propagates in S_76 or in the quasi-linearly normalized S_82. The answer is an exact action-relative no-go: neither parent contains an independent Maxwell/Yang–Mills two-derivative principal block outside the gravitational coframe complex. This absence is spectrum-safe but phenomenologically incomplete. It identifies a narrow post-soldering vector extension that can also supply the matter normalization needed by §16.I.79.


A. Connection decomposition on the soldered branch


Under Spin(10)×Spin(4,2),


248=(45,1)⊕(1,15)⊕(10,6)⊕(16,4bar)⊕(16bar,4).


Under the physical Lorentz subgroup,


15=6_L⊕4_P⊕4_K⊕1_D.


The 4_P and 4_K lines have already been reorganized into the selected gravitational e_F line and the orthogonal auxiliary e_H line. The remaining one-form connection sectors include


• the physical Lorentz connection omega;
• the dilation one-form a_D;
• the internal Spin(10) adjoint one-forms a_10 in (45,1);
• the mixed Lorentz-vector-valued one-forms, including the (10,4) block inside (10,6);
• the spinorial one-form sectors.


B. Exact derivative-support theorem


For either S_76 or S_82, the only curvature numerator entering the BF/BB corridor is


Ncal_w=D_XN_ZPi_L-D_ZN_XJcal_L,


and therefore


boxed:
Ncal_w=Ncal_wPi_L.


Let a_perp be any connection fluctuation whose internal label is outside the physical Lorentz six-plane. At principal order,


δF_perp=d a_perp+lower order.


The derivative preserves the internal label, so


Pi_L d a_perp=0,


and hence


boxed:
Ncal_w d a_perp=0.


The moment-map terms do not repair this. J_F in S_76 and mathbb J_F in S_82 contain the connection through D_AC algebraically as a one-form, but contain no derivative d a_perp. Their quartics are likewise algebraic in the non-Lorentz connection fluctuations.


Because hard B is algebraic, eliminating B cannot manufacture a highest-derivative block that is absent from its sources. Therefore


boxed:
N_spin1,candidate^(2-derivative, non-grav)=0


for the declared S_76 and S_82 parents.


This statement includes the dilation, internal Spin(10), mixed (10,4), and spinorial connection sectors insofar as ordinary bosonic vector principal symbols are concerned.


C. Gravitational vector-looking components are not extra spin one


The selected e_F one-form carries the tetrad. Its longitudinal/vector-looking components belong to the ordinary Lorentz+diffeomorphism constrained gravitational complex and §16.I.78 leaves only the two TT graviton polarizations.


The orthogonal e_H one-form has no derivative kinetic term and is algebraically eliminable.


The physical-orbit condensate tangent and broken P/K gauge field form the Stückelberg-contractible complex of §16.I.78. No extra spin-one class arises from splitting that pair differently.


In S_82 the additional radial scale ghost is a scalar projective ghost and forms its own contractible pair with r_C; it does not create a vector mode.


D. Dilation one-form


The moving-normal branch already carries


A_D=-(1/2)dw


in half-rapidity normalization. Because [D,M_ab]=0 and d²w=0,


F_D=0


on the covariantly transported stabilizer background.


A generic fluctuation δa_D could have dδa_D, but its internal label remains D and is annihilated by Pi_L in Ncal_w. Thus S_76/S_82 supplies no Maxwell principal operator for a_D. Any future decision to make the dilation connection propagating is a new vector action choice, not an inherited consequence of the moving-normal compensator.


E. Internal Spin(10) gauge sector


The same theorem applies to a_10 in (45,1):


Pi_L d a_10=0.


Therefore the currently declared microscopic parent does not yet produce ordinary propagating Spin(10) gauge bosons. This is not a ghost instability; it is an absence of a gauge-field kinetic corridor.


Algebraic or lower-derivative mass/mixing terms can still constrain these one-forms. Until their algebraic Hessian is computed, one should not call every zero-principal-symbol direction a physical zero mode. What is exact is narrower:


boxed:
no Yang–Mills pole is present for the residual internal gauge connection in S_76/S_82.


F. Mixed (10,4) firewall


The (10,4) block is especially important. A spacetime one-form carrying an additional Lorentz-vector internal index can decompose under the diagonal Lorentz group into scalar, antisymmetric, and symmetric-traceless rank-two pieces. Section 16.I.77 already proved that S_76 gives this block no Einstein principal symbol, and S_82 does not alter that result.


Consequently a future generic “full E8 Yang–Mills” kinetic term would not be spectrum-neutral: if it kinetizes the (10,4) block, the spin-two audit must be reopened.


The safe vector extension must therefore be projected onto a sector that is Lorentz-scalar internally, rather than onto all non-Lorentz E8 directions.


G. Safe post-soldering residual-internal Yang–Mills corridor


The transported selector pair (T_w,D) already has residual continuous stabilizer algebra


so(10)⊕so(1,3)


at the representation level. Once the nondegenerate e_F branch is established, the derived metric/coframe supplies a lawful spacetime Hodge star *_g.


A minimal spectrum-safe vector candidate is therefore


S_YM,10
=-(Z_10/2)
∫ κ_10(F_10∧*_g F_10),


where F_10 is the curvature projected onto the residual internal (45,1) algebra and κ_10 is its inherited invariant form.


This is a CONDITIONAL ACTION MECHANISM, not yet derived from S_82. Its important structural property is that the internal 45 is a Lorentz scalar. Thus its connection is an ordinary spacetime vector and does not carry the extra Lorentz-vector label responsible for the (10,4) spin-two danger.


At a background with vanishing internal field strength, the principal quadratic blocks are the ordinary Einstein/Fierz-Pauli block and the ordinary Yang–Mills vector block; metric–vector mixing begins below the independent highest-derivative blocks. Therefore this restricted extension does not by itself create a second graviton.


FIREWALL:
kinetizing the mixed (10,4), e_H, transverse 30380, or spinorial one-form sectors requires separate audits and is not authorized by this safe internal-vector corridor.


H. Obstruction complementarity with the Newton-sign gate


Section 16.I.79 showed that a physically meaningful Newton sign requires comparison with a separately normalized positive-energy matter sector:


sgn(C_EC/Z_ref).


The missing vector kinetic term now supplies a natural candidate for that reference. If one microscopic post-soldering parent derives both the gravitational coefficient C_EC and the residual-internal Yang–Mills coefficient Z_10 in one inherited normalization, then the two formerly separate gates become one finite relative-sign calculation:


boxed:
C_EC/Z_10>0


in the convention where the Yang–Mills vector has positive norm/energy.


Thus the vector-sector incompleteness and the Newton-normalization ambiguity are obstruction-complementary: the structure needed to make internal gauge bosons propagate can simultaneously provide the normalization against which the sign of gravity becomes physical.


This does not prove the inequality. It identifies the correct shared-parent target.


I. Vector-sector theorem and updated gate


EXACT THEOREM — action-relative principal-symbol statement.


For S_76 and S_82 on the finite soldered branch:


• the Lorentz connection is the torsion connection and contributes no independent spin-one particle;
• e_F belongs to the gravitational BRST complex;
• e_H is auxiliary;
• broken P/K tangent/connection variables are Stückelberg-contractible;
• non-Lorentz connection derivatives are annihilated by Pi_L in Ncal_w;
• moment-map terms contain those one-forms only algebraically;
• hard-B elimination cannot generate a missing non-Lorentz derivative block.


Hence there is no presently certified propagating non-gravitational spin-one principal corridor in the declared parent.


OPEN PHYSICAL VECTOR GATE:
derive a healthy kinetic invariant for the desired residual internal gauge algebra without kinetizing the dangerous mixed Lorentz-vector sectors.


CONDITIONAL SAFE CORRIDOR:
a post-soldering, coframe-Hodge Yang–Mills term projected only onto the residual Lorentz-scalar internal algebra.


NEXT LIVE CALCULATION:
search the already-earned selector/projector algebra for a canonical broken-phase projector onto the residual (45,1) internal algebra and test whether one common E8(-24) invariant can generate Z_10 together with the gravitational normalization. If that common-parent coefficient exists, compute C_EC/Z_10. If it does not, record the normalization as an independent coupling and move to the spinorial/spin-3/2 audit.




§16.I.84 — Canonical Residual-Internal Projector and the No-Go for Symmetry-Forced Gravity/Yang–Mills Normalization


Status. Section 16.I.83 reduced the vector frontier to two questions: whether the already-earned selector data canonically isolate the residual internal gauge algebra, and whether the same microscopic normalization that produces gravity also forces a nonzero Yang–Mills coefficient there. The first question closes at broken-phase projector level. The second does not: S_82 gives the internal sector zero derivative coefficient, and residual symmetry permits an independent Yang–Mills invariant. Thus the desired relative normalization is not fixed by the present theorem stack.


A. Stabilizer subalgebra from the live selector data


Section 16.I.60 proves


boxed:
stab(T_w,D)=so(10)⊕so(1,3).


Let


h_w:=stab(T_w,D)⊂e8(-24).


The two simple ideals are the residual internal algebra


g_10≅so(10)


and the selected physical Lorentz algebra


l≅so(1,3).


Because h_w is semisimple, the restriction of the E8 Killing form κ to h_w is nondegenerate. Therefore there exists a unique κ-orthogonal projector


Pi_h:e8(-24)→h_w.


Under simultaneous E8 transport of the selector data,


(T_w,D)→(Ad_g T_w Ad_g^{-1},Ad_gD),


one has


h_w→Ad_g h_w,


and Killing invariance gives


boxed:
Pi_h→Ad_g Pi_h Ad_g^{-1}.


Thus Pi_h is a canonical broken-phase selector-covariant projector, even though an explicit low-degree polynomial in T_w and ad D has not yet been written.


A basis-independent computational expression is obtained by choosing any local basis {X_A} of h_w, forming


G_AB=κ(X_A,X_B),


and setting


Pi_h(u)=X_A(G^{-1})^{AB}κ(X_B,u).


The result is independent of the chosen stabilizer basis.


B. Exact internal projector


The physical Lorentz projector Pi_L is already constructed in §16.I.74 and its image lies inside h_w. The two ideals g_10 and l commute, and any invariant bilinear cross-pairing between distinct simple ideals vanishes. Hence they are κ-orthogonal inside h_w.


Therefore define


boxed:
Pi_10:=Pi_h-Pi_L.


Because


Pi_hPi_L=Pi_LPi_h=Pi_L,


one finds


Pi_10²=Pi_10,


Pi_10Pi_L=Pi_LPi_10=0.


Its image is exactly


boxed:
im Pi_10=g_10≅so(10),
rank Pi_10=45.


Under simultaneous selector transport,


Pi_10→Ad_gPi_10Ad_g^{-1}.


Thus the representation/projector gate for a spectrum-safe residual-internal vector kinetic term is closed at broken-phase level.


FIREWALL. This is an exact existence/uniqueness projector theorem. It is not yet a theorem that Pi_10 is a low-degree polynomial of the primitive selector operators. If microscopic locality requires such a primitive-polynomial realization rather than the stabilizer-kernel construction above, that stronger realization remains to be supplied.


C. The current parent gives Z_10=0


For S_76 and S_82,


Ncal_w=Ncal_wPi_L.


Hence


Ncal_wPi_10=0.


At quadratic principal order in an internal connection fluctuation a_10,


δF_10=d a_10+lower order,


so the BF/BB source contains no d a_10. The moment-map sources J_F and mathbb J_F contain a_10 only algebraically through D_AC and likewise supply no d a_10² term.


Therefore, in the presently declared parents,


boxed:
Z_10(S_76)=Z_10(S_82)=0


at the local two-derivative principal level.


This is an exact action-relative statement, not a choice of normalization.


D. Post-soldering Yang–Mills invariant is lawful but independent


Once e_F is nondegenerate, its metric supplies the spacetime Hodge star *_g. With the exact broken-phase internal projector above, the local invariant


I_YM,10
=-(1/2)∫κ(Pi_10F∧*_gPi_10F)


is selector-covariant and residual-gauge invariant. Multiplying it by a coefficient Z_10 gives the safe vector corridor of §16.I.83.


However this invariant is algebraically independent of the S_82 moment-map/BF invariants. The residual symmetry permits


C_EC S_EC


and


Z_10 I_YM,10


with independent coefficients. The direct-sum stabilizer contains no symmetry exchanging so(10) with so(1,3): the two ideals have different dimensions and different real-form roles, and Pi_10 and Pi_L are separately selector-covariant invariants.


Therefore residual symmetry alone cannot force a relation


Z_10=f(C_EC).


E. Why the inherited E8 Killing form is not enough


Both sectors inherit their quadratic contractions from the same E8 Killing form, so once a single common microscopic monomial exists its relative group-theoretic normalization can in principle be computed from the embedding indices.


But the existence of the common bilinear form does not force coefficients of distinct action monomials to coincide. Gravity in S_82 arises through the BF/moment-map cross term and the Hodge-completed coframe structure, whereas internal Yang–Mills propagation requires a spacetime-Hodge curvature square after soldering. These are distinct local invariants with different field/form structure.


Thus “both use κ_E8” is insufficient to close the Newton/Yang–Mills normalization gate.


F. Common-parent no-go for the presently declared action


Suppose no new action term is added. Then the internal derivative coefficient is exactly zero while the gravitational coefficient is nonzero on the healthy branch:


Z_10=0,
C_EC≠0.


Therefore S_82 cannot itself furnish the reference matter normalization required by §16.I.79.


If the Yang–Mills invariant is added with an arbitrary new coefficient, then


C_EC/Z_10


is simply a new independent coupling ratio. It becomes calculable only if a stronger microscopic construction derives both terms from one coefficient before or through symmetry breaking.


Accordingly:


boxed:
CURRENT SELECTOR/RESIDUAL SYMMETRY DOES NOT FIX C_EC/Z_10.


G. What a genuine common-parent success would require


To improve on the independent-coupling status, one must exhibit a single E8(-24)-invariant microscopic term with one coefficient whose reduction simultaneously yields


1. the already-certified Lorentz Einstein-Cartan/Holst corridor; and
2. a nonzero positive-energy Pi_10 Yang–Mills block;
3. zero principal support on the dangerous mixed (10,4) sector;
4. no new e_H or transverse-condensate kinetic block.


A generic full-E8 curvature square fails condition 3 unless an additional selector projector is present. A purely Lorentz-supported Ncal_w fails condition 2. Therefore the needed common parent must distinguish Pi_L and Pi_10 while still deriving their relative coefficient from one upstream invariant rather than assigning two couplings by hand.


No such single monomial is currently present in S_76 or S_82.


H. Projector theorem and normalization no-go


EXACT THEOREM — broken-phase projector availability.


The selector pair (T_w,D), together with the already-earned Pi_L, canonically determines


Pi_10=Pi_stab(T_w,D)-Pi_L,


whose image is exactly the residual so(10) algebra and which transforms covariantly under simultaneous E8 transport.


EXACT NO-GO — current-parent normalization.


S_76/S_82 gives Pi_10F no independent derivative principal block, so Z_10=0 there. Adding the lawful post-soldering Yang–Mills invariant introduces a coefficient not related to C_EC by residual symmetry alone. The common E8 Killing form fixes contractions, not the relative coefficient of distinct allowed monomials.


OPEN PHYSICAL GATE:
find a stronger single-coefficient microscopic parent that yields both gravity and safe internal Yang–Mills propagation, or accept C_EC/Z_10 as an independent parameter.


UPDATED FRONTIER:
The vector representation/projector problem is closed. The vector normalization/unification problem remains open but sharply localized. Unless a pre-existing Book-16 invariant is found that already meets the four common-parent conditions above, the next independent spectrum audit is the spinorial one-form sector, especially the possibility of a spin-3/2 principal block after soldering.




§16.I.85 — Spinorial Connection Decomposition, Bosonic Vector-Spinor Firewall, and the No-Propagating-Spin-3/2 Result


Status. Section 16.I.84 closes the safe internal-vector projector while leaving spinorial one-form sectors for independent audit. Those sectors do contain Lorentz spin-3/2 representation content after soldering. However, they are components of an ordinary bosonic E8(-24) connection. The declared S_76/S_82 parents give them no derivative principal block, which is spectrum-protective. A future kinetic term cannot simply reinterpret these commuting vector-spinors as physical fermions.


A. Lorentz restriction of the conformal spinor


The adjoint decomposition contains


(16,4bar)⊕(16bar,4)


under Spin(10)×Spin(4,2).


Under the physical Spin(3,1) subgroup, a complex chiral conformal spinor 4 (and similarly 4bar, with the dilation/chirality assignments exchanged) restricts to a four-component Lorentz Dirac carrier,


4↓Spin(3,1)
≅(1/2,0)⊕(0,1/2).


The internal 16 or 16bar is a multiplicity carrier with respect to physical Lorentz transformations.


A connection component in one of these sectors is also a spacetime one-form. Therefore its Lorentz representation is


(1/2,1/2)⊗[(1/2,0)⊕(0,1/2)].


Using Clebsch decomposition,


(1/2,1/2)⊗(1/2,0)
=(1,1/2)⊕(0,1/2),


(1/2,1/2)⊗(0,1/2)
=(1/2,1)⊕(1/2,0).


Hence each internal spinor label contains


• gamma-traceless vector-spinor pieces (1,1/2)⊕(1/2,1), carrying spin-3/2 content;
• gamma-trace pieces (0,1/2)⊕(1/2,0), carrying spin-1/2 content.


Thus:


boxed:
spin-3/2 representation availability is real in the spinorial E8 connection blocks.


This is only a representation statement.


B. Statistics firewall


The E8(-24) gauge algebra is an ordinary Lie algebra, not a Lie superalgebra. Its connection


A=A^I T_I


is an even/bosonic one-form. Consequently the coefficient fields multiplying generators in the (16,4bar) and (16bar,4) subspaces remain commuting bosonic fields. A generator transforming as a subgroup spinor does not change the Grassmann parity of its gauge-field coefficient.


Therefore these sectors are bosonic vector-spinors, not fermionic Rarita-Schwinger fields.


Under the standard assumptions of local relativistic unitary quantum field theory, a propagating half-integer-spin particle requires fermionic statistics. A conventional Rarita-Schwinger kinetic operator placed directly on these bosonic connection components would therefore not provide a healthy physical gravitino or matter fermion merely by representation relabeling.


boxed:
subgroup spinor representation ≠ fermionic field statistics.


Any proposal to identify these connection components with physical fermions must supply an independent grading mechanism, Grassmann-odd matter variables, a genuine superconnection, or another explicit quantization structure. None is present in S_76/S_82.


C. Principal-symbol no-propagation theorem


The derivative curvature of a spinorial connection fluctuation a_sp is


δF_sp=d a_sp+lower order,


and its internal label lies outside the physical Lorentz six-plane. Therefore


Pi_L d a_sp=0,


and because


Ncal_w=Ncal_wPi_L,


one has


boxed:
Ncal_w d a_sp=0.


The J_F or mathbb J_F terms contain a_sp only algebraically through D_AC. Their quartics contain no derivative d a_sp.


Hard B is algebraic, so its elimination cannot generate a missing spinorial derivative block.


Therefore


boxed:
N_spin3/2,candidate^(principal)=0,
boxed:
N_spin1/2,candidate^(connection principal)=0


for these bosonic E8 connection sectors in S_76/S_82.


This does not say the algebraic fields vanish. It says they do not carry propagating half-integer-spin poles in the declared parent.


D. Gauge ghosts do not change the statistics conclusion


The E8 gauge ghost associated with a spinorially transforming generator is Grassmann odd because all Faddeev-Popov ghosts of a bosonic gauge algebra are odd. But that ghost is a spacetime scalar in the gauge complex; it does not convert the original bosonic vector-spinor connection into a physical fermionic field.


Thus the ordinary BRST grading of the bosonic E8 gauge symmetry cannot be used to evade the field-statistics firewall.


E. Algebraic sector remains to be constrained


Because the spinorial one-forms enter D_AC algebraically, a soldered condensate can in principle generate algebraic mass/mixing matrices for them. Without an explicit quadratic Hessian, three outcomes remain possible:


1. the algebraic block is invertible and the fields are auxiliary;
2. residual gauge constraints remove a kernel;
3. an algebraic zero direction survives, signaling a modulus/strong-coupling constraint rather than an ordinary propagating fermion.


The present principal-symbol theorem does not distinguish these algebraic possibilities.


OPEN LOCAL ALGEBRAIC GATE:
compute the spinorial connection Hessian induced by the soldered condensate and normalized moment-map sector before declaring these directions fully eliminated.


F. Generic full-E8 Yang–Mills term is doubly unsafe


Section 16.I.83 already showed that a generic full-E8 Yang–Mills kinetic term would kinetize the mixed (10,4) block and reopen the spin-two audit. The present section adds a second obstruction: the same generic term would also kinetize bosonic vector-spinors in the (16,4bar)⊕(16bar,4) sectors.


Therefore the safe post-soldering internal vector term must remain projected onto Pi_10. It should not be replaced by an indiscriminate kinetic term for the full E8 connection.


G. Lawful physical-fermion corridor is separate


Once a nondegenerate coframe is available, genuine Grassmann-odd matter fields may admit ordinary Dirac or, if independently justified, Rarita-Schwinger kinetic operators. Such fields must be introduced or derived with explicit odd parity.


For example, a Grassmann-odd Spin(10) spinor ψ in a genuine matter bundle could have a post-soldering Dirac action schematically


S_Dirac=Z_ψ∫vol_e barψ i gamma^a e_a^μ D_μψ,


with its own positivity/reality audit.


This is not a license to identify ψ with the bosonic E8 connection component a_sp. Establishing the microscopic origin, representation, chirality, and normalization of any physical fermion field is a separate matter-sector gate.


H. Spinorial principal theorem


EXACT REPRESENTATION RESULT:
the spinorial E8 connection blocks contain vector-spinor Lorentz representations with spin-3/2 and spin-1/2 pieces after soldering.


EXACT ACTION-RELATIVE PRINCIPAL RESULT:
S_76/S_82 gives these sectors no independent derivative principal block because their derivative curvatures are annihilated by the Lorentz-supported numerator.


PHYSICAL FIREWALL:
because the ordinary E8 connection is bosonic, these commuting vector-spinors may not be promoted to healthy physical fermions merely from their subgroup spinor labels.


CONDITIONAL SAFE INTERPRETATION:
keep them auxiliary/constrained unless a separate graded microscopic structure changes the field-statistics assignment.


OPEN GATES:
• algebraic spinorial-connection Hessian;
• microscopic origin of genuine Grassmann-odd matter fields;
• fermion kinetic normalization and its relation to the gauge/gravity coefficients;
• global/topological and higher-derivative effects.


UPDATED FRONTIER:
The local two-derivative bosonic spectrum of S_82 is now unusually sparse: one graviton principal corridor, no independent scalar kinetic corridor, no non-gravitational vector corridor, and no spinorial/vector-spinor connection corridor. The remaining local questions are algebraic auxiliary Hessians and the derivation of lawful matter kinetic terms. The next exact calculation should therefore test the algebraic Hessian/rank of the non-Lorentz connection sectors on the soldered condensate before moving to global/topological zero modes.




§16.I.86 — Algebraic Non-Lorentz Connection Hessian, Condensate-Response Rank Bound, and the 41-Direction Internal Null Theorem


Status. Sections 16.I.83–85 prove absence of independent non-gravitational derivative principal symbols in the declared S_76/S_82 parents. That result does not imply that every remaining connection one-form is algebraically auxiliary. At zero momentum the non-Lorentz connection can still enter through two distinct mechanisms: its action on the condensate inside the filtered moment map, and Lorentz-valued commutators inside the curvature. This section separates those mechanisms exactly and closes the residual-internal rank question on the same internal-flat soldered geometric background used for the local spectrum witness.


A. Non-Lorentz connection response map


Fix a soldered background (Cbar,Abar,Bbar) with s_C!=0 and let a be a one-form fluctuation valued outside the physical Lorentz six-plane. Holding Cbar fixed, the filtered tangent changes by


δ_a nu_F=P_F(a·Cbar).


Define the pointwise condensate-response map


boxed:
R_C: e8(-24) -> im P_F,
R_C(X)=P_F(X·Cbar).


For a one-form a=a_mu dx^mu, R_C acts on the internal coefficient at each spacetime index.


Because rank P_F=4,


boxed:
rank R_C <=4.


This rank bound is independent of the dimension of the connection sector being tested.


For the normalized parent,


mathbb J_F=J_F/s_C,
J_F=(1/2)mu(nu_F wedge nu_F),


and s_C is fixed under an A-variation. Therefore, up to the fixed convention for the symmetric bilinearization of mu,


δ_a mathbb J_F
=(1/s_C)mu(nubar_F wedge R_C(a)),


δ^2_{a,b} mathbb J_F
=(1/s_C)mu(R_C(a) wedge R_C(b)).


Hence every quadratic contribution from the normalized moment-map source and Hodge quartic factors through R_C.


boxed:
a in ker R_C => the entire mathbb J_F sector is algebraically blind to a at quadratic order.


The ordinary condensate stabilizer


stab(Cbar)={X | X·Cbar=0}


is contained in ker R_C, but equality need not hold: a generator can move Cbar only in a direction removed by P_F.


B. Curvature visibility is independent of R_C


The full algebraic Hessian does not factor only through R_C. Expand


F(Abar+a)
=Fbar+Dbar a+(1/2)[a wedge a],


with


Dbar a=d a+[Abar wedge a].


The principal derivative d a is killed for a non-Lorentz fluctuation by the already-proved relation


Ncal_w=Ncal_w Pi_L.


At zero momentum, however, define the linear curvature-response map


K_Abar(a):=Pi_L[Abar wedge a].


There is also a genuinely quadratic Lorentz bracket


Q_L(a,b):=Pi_L[a wedge b].


Before eliminating hard B, the BF term contains the seed-weighted second variation


beta kappa(Bbar wedge T_w Ncal_w Q_L(a,a)),


while eliminating the algebraic B image additionally produces quadratic terms built from K_Abar(a). Thus an R_C-null direction can still acquire an algebraic Hessian through curvature commutators.


C. Exact structural form of the zero-momentum Hessian


After the already-certified hard-B Schur reduction, the zero-momentum quadratic form on a non-Lorentz connection sector can be organized schematically but invariantly as


Q_alg[a]
=<K_Abar a,H_FF K_Abar a>
+2<K_Abar a,H_FJ R_C a>
+<R_C a,H_JJ R_C a>
+L_Bbar(a,a),


where H_FF,H_FJ,H_JJ are finite algebraic matrices fixed by the background and couplings, and


L_Bbar(a,b)
:=beta integral kappa(Bbar wedge T_w Ncal_w Pi_L[a wedge b])


is the direct seed-weighted bracket bilinear. The displayed decomposition records support, not a new assumption about the numerical coefficients.


Consequently a sufficient exact algebraic-null criterion is


R_C(a)=0,
K_Abar(a)=0,
L_Bbar(a,b)=0 for every b in the tested quadratic sector.


Any direction satisfying all three conditions lies in the kernel of the declared zero-momentum quadratic Hessian.


This is the correct replacement for the false inference “no derivative principal symbol implies invertible auxiliary field.”


D. Residual internal Spin(10) sector on the geometric background


Now restrict to the residual internal algebra


g_10=im Pi_10 ~= so(10),
dim g_10=45.


On the same geometric background used by the local gravity witness, take the background connection to have no nonzero internal/mixed/spinorial one-form expectation value. Its geometric part lies in the conformal factor (1,15). Because the subgroup algebra is a direct sum,


[(45,1),(1,15)]=0.


Therefore for a_10 in g_10,


boxed:
K_Abar(a_10)=0.


Moreover


[g_10,g_10] subset g_10,


and Pi_L annihilates g_10, so


boxed:
Pi_L[a_10 wedge b_10]=0


for any internal a_10,b_10. Hence


boxed:
L_Bbar(a_10,b_10)=0.


The Lorentz BF/curvature corridor is therefore completely blind to the pure residual-internal quadratic block on this background. Its only possible algebraic response in S_76/S_82 is the condensate map R_C.


E. 41-direction internal null theorem


Restrict R_C to g_10:


R_10:=R_C|_{g_10}:g_10 -> im P_F.


Since dim g_10=45 and dim im P_F=4,


rank R_10 <=4.


Rank-nullity gives


boxed:
dim ker R_10 >=41.


Every element of ker R_10 is also invisible to the curvature mechanisms just proved to vanish in the pure internal block. Therefore, on the declared internal-flat soldered background,


boxed:
dim ker H_alg^(g_10) >=41


at the level of internal generator directions, with the corresponding one-form multiplicity carried at each spacetime point.


This conclusion does not assume that Spin(10) leaves Cbar invariant. Even if the condensate breaks the entire residual internal algebra, the rank-four filtered tangent can algebraically see at most four independent internal generator combinations.


This is stronger than the previous condensate-stabilizer question.


F. Condensate-stabilizer refinement


Let


h_C,10:=g_10 intersect stab(Cbar).


Then


h_C,10 subset ker R_10,


so every unbroken internal generator is certainly among the algebraically invisible directions. But ker R_10 can be much larger because P_F discards transverse condensate motion.


Two cases must therefore be distinguished.


1. If g_10 subset stab(Cbar), then R_10=0 and the full 45-dimensional internal connection sector is algebraically null in S_82 before a Yang–Mills term is added. This is the expected behavior of an unbroken gauge connection whose kinetic term is absent.


2. If Cbar breaks some or all of g_10, the normalized moment map can supply Higgs/Stueckelberg-type algebraic response to at most four independent internal generator combinations. At least 41 directions remain null anyway.


The live source stack has not yet proved which exact Spin(10) subalgebra, if any, stabilizes the physical 30380 condensate. That representation-specific stabilizer remains open, but it can no longer rescue the current parent from the 41-direction rank obstruction.


G. Dilation and other one-dimensional non-Lorentz directions


For the dilation generator D, the self-bracket vanishes and its commutator with a purely conformal geometric background remains inside the conformal non-Lorentz subspace rather than the Lorentz six-plane at the linear level relevant here. Thus its pure zero-momentum quadratic block is controlled primarily by R_C(D), together with possible cross-sector mixing.


If R_C(D)=0 after the moving-normal constraint is imposed, the generic dilation fluctuation is algebraically null in the declared parent. If R_C(D)!=0, the moment-map sector can constrain it. The existing identity A_D=-(1/2)dw on the transported branch is a geometric compensator relation, not by itself the missing Hessian calculation.


H. Mixed and spinorial sectors are qualitatively different


The 41-direction theorem does not extend automatically to the mixed (10,6) and spinorial blocks. Their Lie brackets can have bosonic conformal/Lorentz output, so


Pi_L[a wedge b]


need not vanish. Consequently the nonzero hard/topological background can supply algebraic bilinears even when R_C(a)=0.


The next finite calculation is therefore not another principal-symbol count. It is the seed-weighted E8 bracket Hessian:


(a,b) -> kappa(Bbar wedge T_w Ncal_w Pi_L[a wedge b])


on the mixed and spinorial modules, followed by the low-rank R_C correction and Schur test.


I. Gate ledger


PASS: exact condensate-response factorization of the normalized moment-map Hessian through R_C.


PASS: rank R_C<=4.


PASS: exact separation between condensate response and curvature-commutator response.


PASS on the internal-flat soldered geometric background: the pure residual Spin(10) sector has K_Abar=0 and zero Lorentz bracket bilinear.


NO-GO: S_76/S_82 can algebraically constrain at most four independent residual-internal generator combinations through the filtered condensate tangent; at least 41 internal generator directions remain zero modes of the declared local quadratic action before a separate Yang–Mills or other kinetic/constraint structure is supplied.


OPEN: exact stabilizer of the physical 30380 condensate inside Spin(10).


OPEN: mixed (10,6) seed-weighted bracket Hessian.


OPEN: opposite-spinorial and same-spinorial seed-weighted bracket Hessians.


OPEN: cross-sector Schur cancellations and global gauge/topological zero-mode interpretation.


Interpretation firewall. The 41 null directions are zero directions of the declared local quadratic action, not automatically 41 physical particles. If they are unbroken gauge connections, the correct interpretation is an under-kinetized gauge sector; if they are broken but not gauge-removable, the nulls signal strong coupling or an incomplete action. BRST and the missing matter/Yang–Mills terms decide which interpretation applies.


NEXT EXACT CALCULATION:
Use the E8 bracket under Spin(10)×Spin(4,2) to compute the Lorentz-valued quadratic bracket on the (10,4) mixed vector block. Test whether the hard/Hodge gravitational seed gives ten copies of the already-audited 16×16 vector-one-form Hessian, and then perform the analogous conjugate-spinorial pairing test.




§16.I.87 — Mixed (10,6) Bracket Hessian, Ten-Copy Vector Auxiliary Resource, and the 61-Direction Connection-Block Null Bound


Status and scope correction to §16.I.86. The rank-nullity theorem of §16.I.86 is exact for the connection–connection algebraic Hessian block with the condensate held fixed. A direction in that block can still be Schur-lifted in the full coupled (connection,condensate) Hessian if nonzero mixed A–C second variations and an invertible transverse-condensate block exist. Therefore every phrase in §16.I.86 that calls the >=41 directions “zero modes of the local action” is to be read more narrowly as “null directions of the fixed-condensate connection block” until the coupled Schur complement is computed. This section preserves that firewall and strengthens the fixed-condensate rank theorem.


A. Orthogonal-algebra origin of the mixed bracket


The bosonic subalgebra relevant to the live branching contains


so(10)⊕so(4,2)⊕(10,6),


with the (10,6) realized as the off-diagonal block of the corresponding orthogonal algebra. Let V_10 carry the internal metric delta_ij and let W_6 carry the conformal metric eta_AB. For basis elements


X_{iA}=E_i wedge U_A


in V_10 tensor W_6, the orthogonal-algebra commutator gives, up to the single fixed nonzero generator-normalization coefficient,


[X_{iA},X_{jB}]
=c_10 eta_AB M_ij
+c_6 delta_ij M_AB,


where M_ij is in so(10) and M_AB is in so(4,2). The precise signs of c_10,c_6 depend on generator convention; neither coefficient vanishes.


The Lorentz projection therefore has the exact form


boxed:
Pi_L[X_{iA},X_{jB}]
=c_6 delta_ij Pi_L(M_AB).


No additional E8 Clebsch search is required for this bosonic block: it is the ordinary off-diagonal orthogonal bracket.


B. Lorentz-four-plane decomposition


The transported selector data split the conformal vector carrier as


W_6=L_4⊕N_2,


where L_4 is the physical Lorentz vector plane and N_2=span{n_+,n_-} is the two-normal plane selected by the dilation generator D=n_+ wedge n_- up to convention.


Accordingly


(10,6)
=(10,4)⊕(10,1)_+⊕(10,1)_-.


Write the corresponding connection one-forms as


nu_i^a X_{ia},
y_i^+ X_{i+},
y_i^- X_{i-}.


For a,b in L_4,


boxed:
Pi_L[X_{ia},X_{jb}]
=c_6 delta_ij M_ab.


By contrast,


Pi_L[X_{i+},X_{j+}]=0,
Pi_L[X_{i-},X_{j-}]=0,
Pi_L[X_{i+},X_{j-}]=0,


because the only conformal output is respectively zero or the normal-plane bivector D. Likewise


Pi_L[X_{i±},X_{ja}]=0,


because n_± wedge L_4 is a P/K-type conformal vector generator rather than a Lorentz bivector.


Thus the hard Lorentz seed distinguishes the (10,4) block sharply from the two normal (10,1) copies.


C. Ten copies of the previously audited vector-one-form Hessian


After eliminating hard B, the selected gravitational/moment-map cross corridor in S_82 has the already-proved Lorentz structure


C_grav,Q integral kappa(
 [Pi_C I-Xi_C Jcal_L] Pi_LF
 wedge M_cd e_F^c wedge e_F^d).


Insert the quadratic mixed curvature contribution


Pi_LF|_(10,4)^2
=c_6 sum_i M_ab nu_i^a wedge nu_i^b.


Then the pure seed contribution to the mixed algebraic quadratic form is


Q_mix,seed
=k_mix sum_{i=1}^{10}
integral kappa(
 [Pi_C I-Xi_C Jcal_L]M_ab nu_i^a wedge nu_i^b
 wedge M_cd e_F^c wedge e_F^d),


with


k_mix=c_6 C_grav,Q


times the fixed convention normalization.


For each internal index i, this is exactly the same Lorentz/spacetime tensor functional that governed the orthogonal coframe line e_H in §16.I.76.7. Therefore its 16×16 Hessian determinant is inherited with only the overall coefficient changed:


boxed:
det H_mix,seed^(i)
=-3 times 2^16 k_mix^16 Pi_C^10(Pi_C^2+4Xi_C^2)^3.


Consequently, whenever


k_mix!=0,
Pi_C!=0,


one has


boxed:
rank H_mix,seed^(i)=16


for every i=1,...,10.


Hence the curvature/Hodge seed supplies a direct sum of ten nondegenerate 16×16 algebraic vector-one-form blocks:


boxed:
rank H_mix,seed=160.


This is an algebraic auxiliary resource, not a derivative kinetic term. The bosonic (10,4) fields still have no independent propagating principal pole.


D. Full (10,4) connection block and the low-rank condensate correction


The normalized moment-map sector adds an algebraic correction that factors through


R_C|_(10,4).


At each spacetime covector its internal rank is at most four. Therefore the full fixed-condensate (10,4) Hessian has the form


H_mix=H_mix,seed+R_mix^T M_C R_mix


plus the already-declared finite background cross matrices, with H_mix,seed invertible on the Pi_C!=0 branch.


By the matrix determinant lemma, any loss of invertibility caused by the condensate correction is equivalent to a finite determinant condition on the image of R_mix rather than a 160-dimensional determinant:


det H_mix
=det H_mix,seed
 det(I+M_C R_mix H_mix,seed^{-1}R_mix^T)


in a basis where the remaining finite cross terms have been absorbed into M_C.


Thus:


EXACT RESOURCE PASS:
the hard/Hodge curvature seed by itself makes the entire (10,4) connection block algebraically nondegenerate for Pi_C!=0.


FULL-BLOCK STATUS:
generic invertibility is available, but an exact theorem for the total S_82 block still requires the small condensate-response determinant; a tuned cancellation cannot be excluded merely from representation theory.


E. Two-normal mixed sector has no Lorentz seed


Define


N_20:=(10,1)_+⊕(10,1)_-,


dim N_20=20


at the internal-generator level.


The bracket identities above show that N_20 has zero Lorentz-seed quadratic pairing with itself and with the (10,4) block. Its linear zero-momentum curvature response also remains in the (10,6) module under a purely geometric conformal background and is killed by Pi_L.


Therefore the fixed-condensate connection Hessian on N_20 factors entirely through


R_N:=R_C|_{N_20}:N_20 -> im P_F.


Since dim im P_F=4,


boxed:
rank R_N<=4,


dim ker R_N>=16.


Every generator direction in ker R_N is invisible to both the normalized moment-map connection block and the Lorentz curvature seed.


F. Strengthened common null bound with the residual internal algebra


Now combine


W_65:=g_10⊕N_20,


dim W_65=45+20=65.


For any pair of generators in W_65, the E8 bracket has no physical Lorentz-bivector component:


• [g_10,g_10] lies in g_10;
• [g_10,N_20] lies in (10,6);
• [N_20,N_20] lies only in internal and normal-plane conformal directions, not the Lorentz six-plane.


Moreover, on the internal/mixed-flat geometric background, the linear commutator with Abar does not map W_65 into the Lorentz adjoint.


Thus the entire fixed-condensate connection block on W_65 factors through one common map


R_W:=R_C|_{W_65}:W_65 -> im P_F.


Not two independent rank-four maps, but one.


Therefore


rank R_W<=4,


and rank-nullity yields the stronger exact bound


boxed:
dim ker H_AA|_{W_65}
>=dim ker R_W
>=65-4
=61.


So the earlier >=41 residual-internal bound was not the strongest available statement. Once the two conformal-normal mixed copies are included,


boxed:
at least 61 internal-generator directions lie in the kernel of the fixed-condensate non-Lorentz connection–connection Hessian of S_76/S_82 on the declared geometric background.


Each such generator carries the usual one-form spacetime multiplicity.


G. Optional dilation extension


If the dilation one-form is treated as an independent fluctuation rather than eliminated by the transported relation A_D=-(1/2)dw, it can be adjoined to W_65. Its self-bracket is zero, its bracket with g_10 is zero, and its bracket with N_20 remains in N_20 rather than the Lorentz adjoint. The corresponding 66-dimensional generator space still couples to the fixed-condensate connection block only through R_C, giving


boxed:
dim ker >=62


for the enlarged connection block.


Because the live moving-normal branch may constrain A_D rather than treat it as an independent field, the canonical unconditional count retained in this section is >=61, not >=62.


H. Coupled-condensate Schur firewall


The >=61 theorem is deliberately a statement about the A–A block with C held fixed. The full local Hessian contains


H_full=
[[H_AA,H_AC],[H_CA,H_CC]].


Even when a lies in ker H_AA, a nonzero H_CA a can generate the Schur term


-H_AC H_CC^{-1}H_CA


if the relevant condensate block is invertible. Conversely, if the transverse-condensate Hessian itself has a kernel, the coupled system may remain strongly coupled.


Therefore


boxed:
>=61 connection-block null directions != >=61 physical zero modes.


The full nullity question is now localized to the transverse-condensate Schur map on this 61-dimensional connection kernel.


I. Updated gate ledger


PASS: exact orthogonal-algebra bracket for (10,6).


PASS: Lorentz projection of the (10,4) self-bracket is delta_ij M_ab up to one nonzero normalization.


PASS: hard/Hodge seed gives ten copies of the known 16×16 invertible vector-one-form Hessian for k_mix Pi_C !=0.


CONDITIONAL FULL PASS: total (10,4) connection block is invertible unless the low-rank condensate correction hits the explicit finite determinant-zero locus.


PASS: two normal (10,1) copies have no Lorentz seed.


PASS: strengthened fixed-condensate connection-block null bound dim ker>=61 on g_10⊕(10,1)_+⊕(10,1)_-.


OPEN: small determinant for the total (10,4) block.


OPEN: transverse-condensate Schur lifting of the >=61 kernel.


OPEN: conjugate-spinorial seed-weighted bracket Hessian.


OPEN: global BRST interpretation of any surviving kernel after lawful matter/Yang–Mills completion.


NEXT EXACT CALCULATION:
Compute the E8 bracket pairing between the conjugate spinorial connection modules. Determine the Lorentz-bivector mass operator induced by the same Hodge/coframe seed, decompose it into spin-3/2 and spin-1/2 vector-spinor pieces, and test its algebraic rank. The statistics firewall of §16.I.85 remains in force regardless of that rank.




§16.I.88 — Conjugate-Spinorial Seed Hessian, Full-Rank Bosonic Vector-Spinor Auxiliary Pairing, and the Statistics Closure


Status. Section 16.I.85 established that the spinorial E8 connection blocks contain Lorentz spin-3/2 and spin-1/2 representation content but carry bosonic statistics and no derivative principal symbol. Section 16.I.87 left their zero-momentum algebraic rank open. This section closes the pure hard/Hodge seed pairing and reduces the full fixed-condensate correction to a low-rank determinant. The result is protective: the seed is algebraically nondegenerate on the entire bosonic vector-spinor connection sector, while the statistics firewall remains absolute.


External representation-theory input, independently checked. The standard E8 construction


e8=so(16)⊕S_16^+


has spinor bracket


[Y^A,Y^B]=(1/4)Gamma^{IJ}_{AB}X^{IJ}


up to the chosen generator normalization. This is the ordinary E8 Clifford bracket; it was independently checked against the explicit so(16)-covariant commutation relation in the mathematical literature. The present section uses only its representation support and nondegeneracy, not the compact-real-form numerical normalization.


A. Spin(10)×Spin(4,2) branching of the E8 spinor


On the live real-form branch the 128 spinorial carrier is written in complex bookkeeping as


S_16^+
-> (16,4bar)⊕(16bar,4).


Call these conjugate modules S_+ and S_-.


Representation products immediately separate the possible bracket outputs.


For the same-module products,


16 tensor 16 contains 10,
4bar tensor 4bar contains 6,


and similarly for the conjugate module. The E8 bracket therefore has the supported channel


[S_+,S_+] subset (10,6),
[S_-,S_-] subset (10,6).


Hence


boxed:
Pi_L[S_+,S_+]=0,
Pi_L[S_-,S_-]=0.


For the conjugate product,


16 tensor 16bar contains 1⊕45⊕...,
4bar tensor 4 contains 1⊕15,


so the E8 bracket contains


[S_+,S_-] subset (45,1)⊕(1,15).


The physical Lorentz bivector lies in the (1,15) term. Therefore


boxed:
the hard Lorentz seed pairs the two conjugate spinorial connection modules off-diagonally; it does not produce independent same-module diagonal masses.


B. Lorentz-bivector cross bracket


Let Psi_mu^{alpha A} denote the one-form coefficients in (16,4bar) and Chi_mu_{alpha B} those in the conjugate (16bar,4), with alpha the paired Spin(10) spinor index and A,B conformal-spinor indices.


The physical Lorentz part of the E8 Clifford bracket has the unique invariant form


boxed:
Pi_L[Psi,Chi]
=c_sp <Psi_alpha,Sigma_ab Chi^alpha> M^{ab},


where Sigma_ab is the physical Lorentz spinor bivector generator, < , > is the nondegenerate conjugate Spin(10) pairing, and c_sp!=0 is the fixed real-form/generator normalization.


This uniqueness follows because the Lorentz-adjoint channel occurs once in the singlet-internal part of the conjugate product.


C. Hodge/coframe seed induces the standard vector-spinor algebraic operator


Insert this bracket into the already-derived normalized gravitational cross corridor


C_grav,Q integral kappa(
 [Pi_C I-Xi_C Jcal_L]Pi_LF
 wedge M_cd e_F^c wedge e_F^d).


On a nondegenerate coframe background, use e_F to identify internal Lorentz and spacetime indices. The spinorial quadratic seed becomes, up to one nonzero overall coefficient k_sp and harmless convention-dependent signs,


Q_sp,seed
=k_sp integral vol_e
 <Psi_mu, M^{mu nu}_sp Chi_nu>.


The vector-spinor operator can be written, up to multiplication by a fixed invertible chirality convention matrix, as


boxed:
M_sp^{mu nu}
=gamma^{mu nu} C_S,


where


C_S=Pi_C I-Xi_C j_S,


and j_S is the spinorial representation of the Lorentz-bivector Hodge complex structure. It satisfies


j_S^2=-I


on the real doubled spinor carrier and commutes with the Lorentz-equivariant vector-spinor decomposition. Therefore


boxed:
C_S^{-1}
=(Pi_C I+Xi_C j_S)/(Pi_C^2+Xi_C^2)


whenever Pi_C^2+Xi_C^2!=0.


D. Exact gamma-trace eigenvalues


Define the purely Lorentz vector-spinor map


(T psi)^mu:=gamma^{mu nu}psi_nu.


Using


gamma^{mu nu}=gamma^mu gamma^nu-eta^{mu nu}


in the standard antisymmetrized convention gives


(T psi)^mu
=gamma^mu(gamma·psi)-psi^mu.


The vector-spinor decomposes into the gamma-traceless spin-3/2 carrier and the gamma-trace spin-1/2 carrier.


If


gamma·psi=0,


then


boxed:
T psi=-psi.


If


psi^mu=gamma^mu chi,


then the Clifford identity


gamma^{mu nu}gamma_nu=3gamma^mu


in four dimensions gives


boxed:
T psi=3psi.


Thus T has no kernel:


boxed:
T|spin-3/2=-I,
T|spin-1/2=3I.


This is the decisive rank result. The bosonic vector-spinor seed does not hide a gamma-trace or gamma-traceless zero direction.


E. Full hard/Hodge seed rank


Because T and C_S are both invertible whenever


k_sp!=0,
Pi_C^2+Xi_C^2!=0,


the conjugate cross map between S_+ and S_- one-form coefficients is nondegenerate.


Equivalently, in a real basis for the full 128-generator spinorial E8 sector, the seed Hessian on its four spacetime one-form components has


boxed:
rank H_sp,seed=128 times 4=512.


The off-diagonal complex bookkeeping form and the real 512-dimensional statement are the same nondegenerate pairing expressed in different bases.


Therefore:


boxed:
the hard/Hodge gravitational seed algebraically lifts the entire bosonic spinorial E8 connection sector at fixed condensate.


It still supplies no derivative kinetic term.


F. Low-rank normalized-moment-map correction


The mathbb J_F contribution to the spinorial connection block factors through


R_sp:=R_C|spinorial.


At each spacetime covector,


rank R_sp<=4.


On the full one-form field space the induced correction therefore has rank at most 16. The total fixed-condensate block has the form


H_sp=H_sp,seed+R_sp^T M_C R_sp


up to the finite background matrices already absorbed into M_C.


Since H_sp,seed is invertible, the rank inequality gives


boxed:
nullity H_sp<=16


for any such rank-16 correction, even on a tuned determinant-zero locus.


Generically the block remains fully invertible. Exact failure of full rank reduces by the determinant lemma to a determinant on the at-most-16-dimensional image of R_sp.


Thus the remaining calculation is small; there is no 512-dimensional mystery determinant.


G. Cross-sector curvature firewall


The E8 bracket of a spinorial module with the bosonic mixed (10,6) or internal (45,1) modules transforms again in a spinorial module, not in the physical Lorentz adjoint. Therefore the Lorentz BF seed does not introduce a quadratic curvature cross term that mixes one spinorial fluctuation with one mixed/internal fluctuation.


Any such fixed-condensate cross mixing in S_82 must again enter through the low-rank condensate-response map R_C. This preserves the usefulness of the seed-block decomposition above.


H. Statistics theorem survives algebraic nondegeneracy


The new rank result does not make these fields physical fermions. Quite the opposite: it provides a lawful way for the declared bosonic connection to remove the dangerous vector-spinor representations algebraically.


The E8 connection remains Grassmann even. The spin-3/2 and spin-1/2 labels above refer only to Lorentz representation content. A nondegenerate bosonic vector-spinor auxiliary Hessian does not become a Rarita-Schwinger or Dirac kinetic operator.


Therefore


boxed:
spinorial connection representation content: PRESENT;
propagating half-integer-spin principal pole: ABSENT;
hard/Hodge algebraic auxiliary resource: FULL RANK;
physical fermion interpretation: FORBIDDEN without an independent odd/graded structure.


I. Coupled-condensate firewall


Because the seed connection block is already invertible, the spinorial one-forms can be Schur-eliminated algebraically on the generic branch before analyzing the remaining condensate Hessian. On a tuned locus where the low-rank correction makes H_sp singular, the coupled system must be revisited.


This is qualitatively better than the W_65 sector of §16.I.87, whose fixed-condensate connection block has an unavoidable >=61-dimensional kernel before transverse-condensate mixing is considered.


J. Gate ledger


PASS: standard E8 spinor bracket into so(16) independently verified.


PASS: same-module spinor brackets have zero physical-Lorentz projection; conjugate modules pair into the Lorentz adjoint.


PASS: induced seed operator is gamma^{mu nu} times an invertible Hodge/chiral factor.


PASS: gamma^{mu nu} has eigenvalue -1 on gamma-traceless spin-3/2 and +3 on gamma-trace spin-1/2.


PASS: hard/Hodge seed rank 512 on the full real bosonic spinorial connection one-form sector for k_sp!=0 and Pi_C^2+Xi_C^2!=0.


CONDITIONAL FULL PASS: normalized-moment-map correction can spoil full rank only on an at-most-16-dimensional tuned determinant locus; generic block remains auxiliary and invertible.


FIREWALL: no physical fermion is derived from these bosonic connection components.


OPEN: exact small determinant including the R_sp correction if one wants a nongeneric all-coupling theorem.


OPEN: coupled transverse-condensate Hessian and Schur complement on the unavoidable W_65 kernel.


OPEN: microscopic origin and normalization of genuinely Grassmann-odd matter.


UPDATED FRONTIER:
At fixed condensate, the dangerous (10,4) and spinorial connection sectors now possess explicit nondegenerate hard/Hodge algebraic seed blocks, while the residual-internal plus two-normal W_65 sector has an unavoidable >=61 connection-block kernel. The next calculation should therefore stop auditing already-lifted representations and attack the coupled transverse-condensate Schur map on W_65. If that map has rank less than 61 after all gauge identities, an unavoidable local strong-coupling/incomplete-action kernel survives S_82 and the missing Yang–Mills/matter completion becomes mandatory rather than optional.




§16.I.89 — Contact-Term Exclusion, Projective Transverse Schur Rank, and the 228-Component Coupled-Kernel Theorem


Status. Sections 16.I.86–88 isolate an unavoidable fixed-condensate connection-block kernel on


W_65 = so(10) ⊕ (10,1)_+ ⊕ (10,1)_-,


dim W_65=65,


with generator-level nullity at least 61, while the mixed Lorentz-vector and bosonic spinorial connection sectors possess nondegenerate hard/Hodge seed Hessians. The remaining question was whether transverse 30380 condensate fluctuations could Schur-lift the W_65 kernel. The live S_82 action and the earlier scalar-protection gate now make that question finite. The answer is a rank no-go: on the certified c_contact=0 branch, condensate mixing can reach at most a 16-dimensional one-form/tangent corridor, so it cannot lift the full W_65 one-form kernel.


A. The Hermitian 30380 contact is not an available S_82 Hessian resource


The live microscopic audit states that an E8-invariant Hermitian 30380 contact/four-fermion operator is lawful, that minimal unbroken adjoint exchange does not determine its coefficient, and that


boxed:
c_contact=0


remains a separate ultraviolet obligation unless a stronger microscopic symmetry, critical/fixed-surface theorem, or equivalent structural principle forbids it.


Therefore the lawful contact operator must not be silently promoted into a transverse condensate potential in the certified S_82 parent. Doing so would reopen the scalar-tilt problem that the current branch explicitly leaves protected only under c_contact=0.


This removes the previously tempting but illegitimate shortcut


“use the 30380 Hermitian contact as an invertible H_CC.”


No such independent H_CC is presently part of the certified parent.


B. Exact C-dependence of the declared S_82 parent


On s_C!=0 the normalized parent is


S_82 = integral [
 beta kappa(B wedge T_w Ncal_w F)
 -(alpha/2) Delta_w kappa(B wedge T_w B)
 +g_Q kappa(B wedge T_w P_L mathbb J_F)
 +lambda_Q kappa(P_L mathbb J_F wedge Jcal_L P_L mathbb J_F)
].


It differs from S_76 only by the quasi-linear radial normalization of the physical moment-map source and its quartic stabilizer.


The condensate enters these declared interaction terms through


nu_F=P_F D_A C,


J_F=(1/2)mu(nu_F wedge nu_F),


mathbb J_F=J_F/s_C.


The first BF and BB terms carry selector/rapidity data but no independent 30380 self-potential. Thus, on the c_contact=0 branch, every condensate contribution relevant to the present A–C Schur problem is mediated by the normalized filtered physical tangent and its moment map.


The manuscript already proves that transverse condensate derivatives obey


boxed:
P_F D(delta C_perp)=0,


so no independent transverse condensate derivative principal symbol is available from the filtered parent.


C. Radial projective direction cannot lift the connection kernel


S_82 has the exact local projective symmetry


C(x) -> exp(sigma(x)) C(x)


on s_C!=0. Equivalently, with


r_C=(1/2)log|s_C|,


the normalized geometric data P_F, e_F and mathbb J_F are invariant under the radial shift, and r_C may be placed in the already-defined BRST-contractible radial doublet when the scale is treated as gauge.


For a radial variation chi=sigma C,


delta_chi P_F=0.


If a lies in ker R_C, then


P_F(a chi)=sigma P_F(a C)=0.


Hence the radial condensate direction gives no mixed A–C response on the W_65 kernel. Any possible Schur lifting must be projectively transverse.


D. Mixed second variation of the filtered tangent


Let


K_W:=ker(R_C|W_65).


Section 16.I.87 proves


dim K_W>=61.


For a one-form fluctuation a with coefficients in K_W,


P_F(a C)=0.


Let chi=delta C be an arbitrary projectively transverse condensate fluctuation. At fixed background A and selector data, the mixed variation of the filtered tangent is


boxed:
B_C(a,chi)
:=delta_chi delta_a nu_F
=(delta_chi P_F)(a C)+P_F(a chi).


Both terms lie in im P_F. Therefore


boxed:
B_C(a,chi) in Omega^1(M) tensor V_C,
V_C=im P_F,
dim V_C=4.


Using the explicit rank-four projector, the first term can be written on a P_F-null vector y=a C as


(delta_chi P_F)y
=(1/s_C) v_a eta^{ab} h_R(h_b chi,y),


up to the already-fixed moving-frame convention. This is the second-fundamental-form response of the selected physical four-plane. The second term P_F(a chi) has the same codomain.


Thus the moving-projector correction does exist, but its image cannot escape the physical four-plane.


E. Factorization of the mixed Hessian


Because delta_a mathbb J_F=0 for a in K_W, every nonzero A–C second variation of the normalized moment-map source or quartic is obtained by feeding B_C(a,chi) into the first variation of mathbb J_F around the soldered background. Variations of the normalization 1/s_C multiply delta_a J_F=0 and therefore do not open an additional channel on K_W.


Consequently the mixed Hessian restricted to the W_65 kernel factors as


K_A
 --B--> Omega^1 tensor V_C
 --L_C--> T_C^*,


where


K_A:=Omega^1 tensor K_W


is the one-form connection kernel and L_C is a finite background-dependent linear map determined by the normalized moment-map/Hodge terms.


Pointwise in four spacetime dimensions,


dim(Omega^1 tensor V_C)=4 times 4=16.


Therefore


boxed:
rank(H_CA|K_A)<=16.


This bound is independent of the dimension of the full 30380 condensate representation and independent of whether its transverse algebraic Hessian is invertible.


F. 228-component coupled-kernel theorem


Since dim K_W>=61 and each connection generator carries four one-form components,


boxed:
dim K_A>=4 times 61=244.


Rank-nullity applied to the mixed Hessian gives


nullity(H_CA|K_A)
>=244-16
=228.


Let a lie in this >=228-dimensional kernel. By construction,


H_AA a=0,
H_CA a=0.


Since the bosonic Hessian is symmetric after the already-certified hard-B Schur reduction, the coupled variation (a,0) is annihilated by the full (A,C) quadratic block:


H_(A,C) (a,0)^T =0.


Therefore


boxed:
dim ker H_(A,C)|_(W_65 plus C) >=228


pointwise, before BRST/gauge quotienting and on the declared c_contact=0 S_82 branch.


This is stronger than the previous conditional Schur question. No assumption that H_CC is invertible is needed.


G. Schur-complement corollary


If one nevertheless assumes that a chosen projectively transverse condensate block H_CC is invertible after lawful gauge fixing, the Schur correction on K_A is


Delta H_K
=-H_AC H_CC^{-1} H_CA.


Its rank obeys


boxed:
rank Delta H_K<=rank(H_CA|K_A)<=16.


Hence even a perfectly invertible transverse H_CC can lift at most 16 independent combinations of the >=244-dimensional W_65 one-form kernel.


Therefore


boxed:
nullity H_eff|K_A >=228.


The old proposed criterion “rank S_C>=61” was stated at generator level and was too optimistic. Once the actual one-form multiplicity and the rank-four physical tangent are kept, the correct local Schur bottleneck is 16 component directions versus at least 244 connection components.


H. What the theorem does and does not mean


The >=228 number is a local quadratic-Hessian component count, not a count of 228 physical particles. Gauge transformations, BRST quartets, algebraic constraints, and global/topological identifications can remove null directions from the physical state space.


But gauge fixing cannot turn the absent gauge-invariant kinetic structure into a derived Yang–Mills principal symbol. The theorem says something narrower and harder:


boxed:
the certified normalized condensate sector is too low-rank to make the entire W_65 connection sector algebraically auxiliary.


Thus S_82 by itself does not close the non-Lorentz gauge sector.


I. Consequence for completion


The current parent now has three qualitatively different non-gravitational connection classes:


1. (10,4) mixed Lorentz-vector block: nondegenerate hard/Hodge algebraic seed resource, no independent derivative pole.


2. Bosonic spinorial block: full-rank hard/Hodge algebraic seed resource, no independent derivative pole, no fermionic interpretation.


3. W_65=so(10) plus the two conformal-normal (10,1) copies: at least 228 coupled A–C Hessian null components survive the normalized condensate Schur mechanism before BRST quotienting.


Therefore a lawful completion must do one of the following:


• supply a separate gauge kinetic/constraint structure on the surviving W_65 sector;
• prove that BRST/topological gauge structure removes the entire residual kernel without introducing local particles;
• enlarge the physical condensate tangent/filter beyond rank four, which would change the one-coframe architecture and therefore requires a fresh spin-two audit;
• or introduce new microscopic fields/interactions and re-run the scalar-protection gates.


A generic full-E8 Yang–Mills term remains disallowed by the earlier spectrum audit because it kinetizes the already-protected mixed and bosonic spinorial sectors. Any Yang–Mills completion must therefore be selectively supported on the surviving lawful gauge subalgebra after the condensate/stabilizer and BRST analysis.


J. Gate ledger


PASS: the Hermitian 30380 contact is lawful but not an available certified S_82 Hessian resource; c_contact=0 remains the active scalar-protection obligation.


PASS: S_82 condensate dependence in the declared parent is mediated through the normalized filtered moment map.


PASS: radial projective condensate variation cannot lift a W_65 connection-kernel direction.


PASS: transverse A–C mixed response on K_W factors through Omega^1 tensor im P_F, of pointwise dimension 16.


PASS: rank(H_CA|K_A)<=16.


NO-GO: at least 228 local one-form component directions remain in the coupled (A,C) Hessian kernel on the c_contact=0 S_82 branch before BRST quotienting.


NO-GO: an invertible transverse H_CC alone cannot rescue the W_65 sector; its Schur correction has rank at most 16 on K_A.


OPEN: exact BRST quotient and stabilizer of the physical 30380 condensate inside the W_65 gauge sector.


OPEN: identification of the maximal subalgebra on which a selective Yang–Mills term can be added without re-kinetizing the (10,4) and bosonic spinorial auxiliary sectors.


OPEN: microscopic odd matter action and its compatibility with the same selective gauge completion.


UPDATED FRONTIER:
The transverse-condensate Schur gate is no longer the main uncertainty. Its rank is insufficient by construction. The next live problem is the selective gauge-completion theorem: determine the exact residual/stabilizer algebra represented inside the >=228 kernel, quotient pure BRST/topological directions, and construct or falsify a projector-supported Yang–Mills term whose principal symbol lives only on the surviving legitimate gauge sector.




§16.I.90 — Primitive so(11,1) Spectral Closure, Selector-Cartan Metric, and the Spectrum-Safe 45+20 Completion Candidate


Status. Section 16.I.89 proves that the normalized rank-four 30380 condensate corridor cannot Schur-lift the full W_65 connection kernel: at least 228 one-form components remain null in the coupled (A,C) Hessian on the certified c_contact=0 S_82 branch before BRST quotienting. The next question is therefore whether the already-earned selector algebra itself supplies a lawful completion without re-kinetizing the dangerous mixed (10,4), Lorentz-gravitational, or bosonic spinorial sectors. It does. The transported carrier T_w and grading generator D canonically isolate a 66-dimensional so(11,1) Lie algebra, determine its Cartan involution, close the primitive-polynomial realization of the residual so(10) projector left open in §16.I.84, and supply a conditional post-soldering mechanism in which only the desired internal 45 receives Yang–Mills propagation while the 20 conformal-normal directions receive an invertible algebraic selector constraint rather than a new vector pole.


A. Exact rank-66 spectral support from T_w


The §16.I.60 bosonic spectrum is


lambda=0 on L wedge a, dim 4,
lambda=1/4 on U_(10,1) wedge a, dim 11,
lambda=3/4 on Lambda^2 L, dim 6,
lambda=1 on U_(10,1) tensor L, dim 44,
lambda=5/4 on Lambda^2 U_(10,1), dim 55,


with a spacelike, b timelike, and


U_(10,1)=I_10 direct-sum <b>.


The full-adjoint multiplicities show that the lambda=1/4 and lambda=5/4 eigenspaces have dimensions exactly 11 and 55 respectively. Therefore their Lagrange spectral projectors are


Pi_(1/4)(T)
=-(2/3) T(T-I)(4T-5I)(4T-3I),


Pi_(5/4)(T)
=(2/5) T(T-I)(4T-3I)(4T-I).


Define


boxed:
Pi_G:=Pi_(1/4)(T_w)+Pi_(5/4)(T_w).


Equivalently,


boxed:
Pi_G=-(4/15)T_w(T_w-I)(4T_w-11I)(4T_w-3I).


Then


Pi_G^2=Pi_G,
rank Pi_G=66.


Its image is


im Pi_G
=Lambda^2 U_(10,1) direct-sum (U_(10,1) wedge a)
=Lambda^2(I_10 direct-sum <a,b>).


Because I_10 is positive definite, a is spacelike, and b is timelike, the twelve-dimensional carrier has signature (11,1). Therefore


boxed:
g_G:=im Pi_G ~= so(11,1).


This is not merely a dimension coincidence. The orthogonal bivector bracket obeys


[Lambda^2 U,Lambda^2 U] subset Lambda^2 U,
[Lambda^2 U,U wedge a] subset U wedge a,
[U wedge a,U wedge a] subset Lambda^2 U,


so im Pi_G is a Lie subalgebra.


FIREWALL. Pi_G has zero support on the lambda=0 physical null-coframe sector, the lambda=3/4 Lorentz bivectors, and the lambda=1 block containing both the mixed (10,4) vectors and the bosonic spinorial eigenspace. Hence a Pi_G-supported completion does not automatically reopen the duplicate-spin-two or bosonic-vector-spinor principal blocks.


B. Relation to the §16.I.89 problematic sector


Write


N_20:=(10,1)_+ direct-sum (10,1)_-.


In the real orthogonal basis,


N_20=(I_10 wedge a) direct-sum (I_10 wedge b).


The §16.I.89 generator space is


W_65=so(10) direct-sum N_20.


The remaining generator in g_G is the normal boost/dilation


D=a wedge b.


Therefore


boxed:
g_G=W_65 direct-sum R D
