as a vector-space decomposition, and the minimal Lie closure of W_65 is exactly so(11,1). No (10,4), Lorentz, or spinorial generator is forced into this closure.


On the live moving-normal branch A_D=-(1/2)dw is a compensator relation rather than an independent vector fluctuation. Thus the unconditional connection-kernel count remains the W_65 count of §16.I.89; adjoining D is optional when testing an unconstrained dilation one-form.


C. Primitive rank-20 normal projector from D


On g_G the adjoint action of D=a wedge b satisfies


(ad D)^2=0 on so(10) direct-sum R D,
(ad D)^2=I on N_20,


with the present unit spacelike/timelike normalization. Explicitly, ad D exchanges the two members of each pair


e_i wedge a <-> e_i wedge b


up to the fixed orthogonal-sign convention, so its square is the identity on that twenty-dimensional subspace.


Define


boxed:
Pi_N:=(ad D)^2 Pi_G.


Then


Pi_N^2=Pi_N,
rank Pi_N=20,
im Pi_N=N_20.


Because Pi_N preserves the two T_w eigenspaces, it commutes with Pi_(1/4), Pi_(5/4), and Pi_G.


D. Primitive rank-one D projector and explicit so(10) projector


Since D is non-null under the inherited Killing form, define the rank-one selector-covariant projector


boxed:
Pi_D(X)=D kappa(D,X)/kappa(D,D).


Then


rank Pi_D=1,
im Pi_D=R D,
Pi_D Pi_N=Pi_N Pi_D=0.


Consequently


boxed:
Pi_10^(prim):=Pi_G-Pi_N-Pi_D


obeys


(Pi_10^(prim))^2=Pi_10^(prim),
rank Pi_10^(prim)=45,
im Pi_10^(prim)=so(10).


Thus


boxed:
Pi_10^(prim)=Pi_10


for the canonical residual-internal projector already constructed abstractly in §16.I.84 from the stabilizer kernel.


This closes the §16.I.84 primitive-realization firewall: the desired residual internal projector is not merely guaranteed by a stabilizer-basis construction; it can be written directly from the already-earned primitive selector data T_w, D, kappa, and finite polynomial functional calculus.


E. Selector-derived Cartan involution on so(11,1)


The noncompact algebra g_G cannot possess a fixed positive-definite Ad-invariant bilinear form. The selector data nevertheless admit a covariant Cartan metric because they determine the time direction rather than attempting to make the Killing form itself positive.


Define on g_G


A_T:=Pi_(5/4)-Pi_(1/4),
B_D:=Pi_G-2Pi_N.


Both square to Pi_G and commute. Set


boxed:
Theta_G:=A_T B_D.


Its action on the four irreducible blocks is


Theta_G=+1 on so(10),
Theta_G=+1 on I_10 wedge a,
Theta_G=-1 on I_10 wedge b,
Theta_G=-1 on R D.


Therefore


k_G:=so(10) direct-sum (I_10 wedge a) ~= so(11)


is the +1 eigenspace, while


p_G:=(I_10 wedge b) direct-sum R D


is the -1 eigenspace. The orthogonal brackets satisfy


[k_G,k_G] subset k_G,
[k_G,p_G] subset p_G,
[p_G,p_G] subset k_G.


Equivalently Theta_G is induced by the reflection b->-b of the twelve-dimensional (11,1) vector carrier. Hence


boxed:
Theta_G is the Cartan involution of so(11,1).


F. Positive internal metric and covariance firewall


Let the overall inherited Killing-form sign be fixed once on one compact generator. Then the Cartan bilinear


boxed:
h_G(X,Y)=-kappa(X,Theta_G Y)


with the corresponding global sign convention is positive definite on g_G. In particular the sign reversal on p_G removes the ordinary noncompact Yang–Mills internal ghost that would arise from using kappa alone on so(11,1).


This does not contradict the theorem that a noncompact simple algebra has no fixed positive Ad-invariant form. h_G is selector-covariant, not fixed. Under simultaneous E8 transport of the selector data,


T_w -> Ad_g T_w Ad_g^(-1),
D -> Ad_g D,
Pi_G -> Ad_g Pi_G Ad_g^(-1),
Theta_G -> Ad_g Theta_G Ad_g^(-1).


Therefore for covariantly transforming X,Y,


h_G(Ad_g X,Ad_g Y)=h_G(X,Y)


when the selector is transported simultaneously. In a unitary selector gauge Theta_G becomes the ordinary fixed Cartan involution and only the stabilizer remains manifest.


G. Full rank-66 Yang–Mills escape exists but is not the preferred minimal spectrum


Once e_F is nondegenerate, the derived metric supplies the spacetime Hodge star *_g. One lawful broken-phase invariant is


I_YM,G
=-(Z_G/2) integral kappa(Pi_G F wedge *_g Theta_G Pi_G F),


with the global sign chosen so Z_G>0 corresponds to positive vector energy.


At principal quadratic order this gives a healthy internal kinetic metric on all 66 g_G generators and has zero support on the dangerous lambda=0,3/4,1 sectors.


Thus the naive noncompact-Killing ghost is not an unavoidable obstruction: the already-earned selector pair supplies a covariant Cartan-metric escape.


However this term kinetizes, in addition to the desired 45 internal vectors, the twenty N_20 normal vectors and an independent D vector if D is treated as free. That is a new physical field-content choice and is not forced by S_82. It therefore remains a broader conditional completion, not the minimal safe corridor.


H. Spectrum-safe 45+20 completion candidate


The more conservative completion uses the exact primitive Pi_10 only for vector propagation and treats N_20 algebraically through the selector D.


Retain the lawful post-soldering internal Yang–Mills term


I_YM,10
=-(Z_10/2) integral kappa(Pi_10 F wedge *_g Pi_10 F),


with the compact so(10) sign fixed so Z_10>0 is healthy.


Now define the selector-covariant one-form


K_D:=Pi_G D_A D.


Because D is a zero-form selector, D_A D transforms homogeneously, and so does K_D. Add the broken-phase term


boxed:
I_N=(M_N^2/2) integral h_G(K_D wedge *_g K_D),


with M_N^2>0.


In local unitary selector gauge dD=0 and with the connection decomposed on g_G,


A_G=A_10+A_N+A_D D,


one has


K_D=[A_N,D],


because


[A_10,D]=0,
[D,D]=0.


Since ad D is invertible on N_20 and (ad D)^2=I there,


boxed:
rank Hess_(A_N) I_N=20.


No derivative of A_N occurs. Thus I_N supplies an invertible algebraic Hessian for all twenty conformal-normal one-forms without creating twenty new Yang–Mills poles.


The term has no support on Pi_10, Pi_L, the mixed (10,4) block, the physical coframe/null-coframe pair, or the bosonic spinorial one-forms after the explicit Pi_G output projection.


I. Relation to the moving-normal compensator


The remaining g_G/so(10) generator is D itself. On the live transported branch the manuscript already imposes geometrically


A_D=-(1/2)dw


in half-rapidity normalization, or A_D=-(1/4)d zeta in full-rapidity normalization. This relation is a transport/compensator identity, not a Hessian theorem, and §16.I.89 correctly did not count D as an unconditional independent connection direction.


Therefore the minimal 45+20 candidate does not require a new D-vector kinetic term. If a future formulation instead promotes A_D to an independent gauge field, a separate selector/Stueckelberg audit is required; the full Pi_G Yang–Mills escape above is available but carries extra propagating-vector content.


J. Quadratic completion of the W_65 obstruction


On the declared extension


S_90^cond
:=S_82+Z_10 I_YM,10+I_N,


the W_65 sector splits exactly as


W_65=so(10) direct-sum N_20.


The so(10) block receives the ordinary Yang–Mills two-derivative principal symbol. The N_20 block receives a rank-20 algebraic Hessian and no independent derivative pole.


Therefore the specific §16.I.89 W_65 under-kineticization obstruction is removed at quadratic broken-phase level:


boxed:
45 healthy vector principal directions + 20 algebraically auxiliary normal directions.


The previous >=228 coupled-kernel theorem remains exact for bare S_82; S_90^cond escapes it only because I_YM,10 and I_N are genuinely new lawful post-soldering resources outside that parent.


K. What is and is not closed


PASS: the minimal Lie closure of W_65 is the selector spectral algebra so(11,1)=im Pi_G.


PASS: Pi_G is an explicit degree-four polynomial in T_w.


PASS: Pi_N=(ad D)^2 Pi_G is an exact rank-20 projector onto the two normal 10s.


PASS: Pi_D is an exact rank-one projector onto D.

PASS: Theta_G=(Pi_(5/4)-Pi_(1/4))(Pi_G-2Pi_N) is the Cartan involution of so(11,1).


PASS: the selector-Cartan metric h_G is positive definite on g_G and transforms covariantly with the selector, so a noncompact internal-sign ghost is avoidable without kinetizing the forbidden E8 sectors.


CONDITIONAL ACTION MECHANISM: Pi_10 Yang–Mills plus the selector term I_N gives a 45-propagating + 20-auxiliary quadratic completion of W_65 after soldering.


FIREWALL: I_N is a new post-soldering selector-orbit term, not already contained in S_82. Its coefficient M_N^2 is not derived by the present microscopic parent.


FIREWALL: treating selector orientation as a dynamical sigma-model field requires a BRST/Goldstone audit. In a gauge where D is fixed, A_N is algebraic; before gauge fixing the same term is a gauged selector-orbit kinetic invariant.

The W_65 rank deficit no longer forces either a full-E8 Yang–Mills term or a noncompact ghost. The selector spectrum itself provides the exact decomposition needed for a spectrum-safe escape. The next finite calculation is the BRST/Stueckelberg audit of I_N: linearize the selector orbit and A_N simultaneously, show whether the twenty selector angular modes are entirely contractible/algebraically removed with A_N, and then join that result to the already-nondegenerate bosonic spinorial auxiliary Hessian and the still-missing odd matter action.




§16.I.91 — Normal-Selector Stückelberg Cancellation and Local BRST Triviality of the Twenty-Dimensional N_20 Sector


Status. Section 16.I.90 constructs the exact primitive decomposition


g_G~=so(11,1)=so(10) direct-sum N_20 direct-sum R D


and proposes the spectrum-safe post-soldering extension in which so(10) alone receives a Yang–Mills principal symbol while


I_N=(M_N^2/2) integral h_G(Pi_G D_A D wedge *_g Pi_G D_A D)


supplies a rank-20 algebraic Hessian for N_20. The remaining question was whether treating the selector orientation covariantly turns those twenty auxiliary directions into hidden scalar poles. At local quadratic order around a covariantly constant selector background, it does not. The selector-orbit fluctuations and the N_20 connection form a Stückelberg pair whose invariant combination is algebraic. Integrating out the connection removes the selector angular kinetic term exactly; unitary selector gauge gives the same result. The corresponding linear BRST sector is contractible and contains no ghost-number-zero pole.


A. Selector-orbit coordinates


Fix a background selector D_0 with


D_0=a wedge b,


and let N_20 be the rank-20 image of


Pi_N=(ad D_0)^2 Pi_G.


The stabilizer of D_0 inside g_G is


so(10) direct-sum R D_0,


so the local adjoint orbit of D_0 inside g_G has tangent space N_20.


Choose a local orbit coordinate


pi(x) in N_20.


To first order,


boxed:
D(x)=D_0+[pi(x),D_0]+O(pi^2).


The same group-orbit fluctuation transports every selector-covariant object constructed from the common selector data. In particular T_w and the projectors vary by the corresponding adjoint conjugation. No independent twenty-component fluctuation of Pi_G or Pi_10 is introduced.


B. Linearized covariant derivative


Decompose the g_G-valued connection near the background as


A=A_10+A_N+A_D D_0+...


with


A_N in Omega^1 tensor N_20.


At a background with D_0 constant in unitary selector coordinates,


D_A D=dD+[A,D].


Using


dD=[d pi,D_0]+O(2),
[A_10,D_0]=0,
[D_0,D_0]=0,


one obtains


boxed:
Pi_G D_A D
=[d pi+A_N,D_0]+O(2),


up to the fixed sign convention for the gauge covariant derivative.


Define the linear Stückelberg combination


boxed:
B_N:=d pi+A_N


with the corresponding sign changed everywhere if the opposite D_A convention is used.


Then


Pi_G D_A D=ad_(D_0) B_N+O(2).


C. Exact quadratic reduction of I_N


On N_20,


(ad D_0)^2=I.


Moreover the selector-Cartan metric h_G is positive definite and ad D_0 is invertible on this sector. Therefore the quadratic selector term is, up to the fixed positive normalization inherited from ad D_0,


boxed:
I_N^(2)
=(M_N^2/2) integral h_N(B_N wedge *_g B_N),


where h_N is the positive restriction of h_G to N_20.


There is no derivative acting on A_N. Hence A_N is an algebraic field even before gauge fixing.


Its Euler–Lagrange equation is


boxed:
B_N=0,


or


boxed:
A_N=-d pi.


Substituting this solution back gives


boxed:
I_N^(2)|_(A_N solved)=0.


Thus the apparent derivative term for the selector angle pi does not produce a scalar propagator. It is exactly cancelled by the algebraic N_20 connection.


D. Unitary selector gauge gives the same result


Under an infinitesimal N_20 gauge transformation with parameter epsilon_N,


D -> D+[epsilon_N,D],


so at linear order the orbit coordinate shifts as


pi -> pi-epsilon_N


up to convention. The connection transforms as


A_N -> A_N+d epsilon_N


with the matching convention. Therefore


boxed:
B_N=d pi+A_N


is gauge invariant at linear order.


Choose unitary selector gauge


pi=0.


Then


I_N^(2)
=(M_N^2/2) integral h_N(A_N wedge *_g A_N),


and the algebraic field equation gives


boxed:
A_N=0.


Hence integrating out A_N before gauge fixing and fixing pi before integrating out A_N are equivalent at quadratic order.


E. Linear BRST complex


Introduce an N_20 ghost c_N. The linear BRST differential may be written


s pi=c_N,
s A_N=-d c_N,
s c_N=0,


again up to the same global sign convention. Then


s B_N=0.


For unitary selector gauge introduce the standard antighost/multiplier pair


s bar(c)_N=b_N,
s b_N=0.


A gauge fermion proportional to


Psi_N=integral bar(c)_N pi


gives an algebraic Faddeev–Popov operator because


delta pi/delta epsilon_N=I


at the background. The N_20 ghost therefore has no derivative pole in this gauge.


The pairs


(pi,c_N),
(bar(c)_N,b_N)


are BRST doublets. Their local cohomology is contractible. The remaining A_N dependence is a positive algebraic Gaussian with no momentum pole.


Therefore


boxed:
H_BRST^0(N_20 local quadratic sector) contains no propagating scalar or vector class.


This is a local quadratic cohomology statement, not a global anomaly theorem.


F. Principal-symbol and pole audit


The combined extension


S_91^cond
=S_82+Z_10 I_YM,10+I_N


has, on a background with zero internal field strength and covariantly constant selector orientation,


so(10): ordinary Yang–Mills principal symbol,


N_20: no connection derivative principal symbol; positive algebraic Hessian,


pi_N selector angles: no surviving principal symbol after algebraic A_N elimination,


N_20 ghosts: algebraic in unitary selector gauge.


Thus the twenty-direction selector mechanism does not introduce a hidden second vector sector or twenty physical scalar modes.


G. Interaction with the primitive projector


Because Pi_10 is transported by the same selector orbit,


Pi_10 -> Ad_g Pi_10 Ad_g^(-1),


its Yang–Mills term is fully selector-covariant before unitary gauge. Expanding around a background with


Pi_10 F_0=0


produces no quadratic pi-A_10 principal mixing from variation of Pi_10: every such term is multiplied by the vanishing background internal curvature or appears at interaction order.


Therefore the independent highest-derivative blocks remain


one gravitational Einstein/Cartan block,
one compact so(10) Yang–Mills block,


with no N_20 pole.


FIREWALL. A nonzero internal background field strength can generate lower-order or mixed quadratic terms through the moving projector. That case requires a separate background-specific Hessian and is not included in the present zero-internal-flux spectrum theorem.


H. Relation to the §16.I.89 rank no-go


The §16.I.89 theorem remains exact for bare S_82: normalized condensate mixing alone cannot lift more than sixteen component combinations of the W_65 connection kernel.


The present extension bypasses that no-go by adding a new selector-orbit invariant whose rank is controlled by


ad D_0:N_20->N_20,


not by the rank-four condensate tangent. Since ad D_0 is invertible on all twenty normal generators, its one-form algebraic Hessian has full normal rank independently of H_CA.


Thus the two mechanisms are logically distinct:


condensate Schur corridor: rank-limited and insufficient;
selector-orbit constraint: full rank on N_20 and algebraic.


I. Dilation/rapidity direction remains separate


The N_20 Stückelberg theorem does not treat the generator D_0 itself. D_0 stabilizes itself, so D_A D cannot constrain an A_D component. The live transported branch instead carries


A_D=-(1/2)dw


in half-rapidity normalization.


Whether the w/A_D relation is a pure gauge/Stückelberg identity, a relational physical rapidity degree of freedom, or a mixture once the compensated exchange datum Xcal is included must be audited separately. No conclusion about the physical status of w follows from the N_20 theorem.


J. Gate ledger


PASS: the D_0 adjoint orbit inside g_G has exactly twenty tangent directions N_20.


PASS: linear selector fluctuations satisfy D=D_0+[pi,D_0]+O(pi^2).


PASS: Pi_G D_A D=[d pi+A_N,D_0]+O(2).


PASS: I_N^(2) is a positive Stückelberg square for B_N=d pi+A_N.


PASS: A_N is algebraic and enforces B_N=0.


PASS: integrating out A_N removes the apparent pi kinetic term exactly at quadratic order.


PASS: unitary selector gauge pi=0 yields the equivalent algebraic equation A_N=0.


PASS: the linear N_20 BRST selector/ghost sector is contractible and carries no ghost-number-zero momentum pole.


CONDITIONAL CLOSURE: on zero internal background flux, the 45+20 extension leaves one so(10) Yang–Mills vector block and no N_20 scalar/vector pole.


OPEN: nonlinear/global BRST cohomology and measure/anomaly audit for the selector-orbit gauge fixing.


OPEN: independent audit of the D/rapidity compensator channel once the relational Xcal datum is retained.

The twenty normal directions are no longer the live local spectrum obstruction. The next selector-sector calculation is the one-dimensional D/rapidity channel: retain both T_w and the compensated exchange involution Xcal, determine the exact gauge-invariant relational coordinate, and test whether A_D=-(1/2)dw removes a pure gauge direction or whether one physical rapidity scalar survives. That answer must then be joined to the odd physical matter action and the final complete BRST Hessian.




§16.I.92 — Relational Rapidity Survives D-Gauge, the Moving-Normal D Connection Is Flat, and No Rapidity Scalar Pole Exists in the Declared Parent


Status. Section 16.I.91 closes the twenty-dimensional N_20 selector orbit locally: its selector angles and algebraic normal connection form a contractible Stückelberg sector. One selector direction remains conceptually distinct: the rapidity/D direction. The transported carrier T_w is generated by D and therefore its coordinate w is not measurable from T_w alone. However Book 16 already introduced the compensated exchange involution Xcal precisely because T_w-only conjugation invariants are w-blind. Once T_w and Xcal are treated as relational selector data, the response scalar Pi_C is invariant under simultaneous E8 conjugation and reconstructs w uniquely on every finite real branch. Therefore the surviving rapidity is not pure D gauge. At the same time, the moving-normal D connection A_D=-(1/2)dw is flat and the declared S_82/S_91 parent contains no independent derivative kinetic invariant for the relational rapidity. The result is a gauge-invariant but presently nonpropagating zero-form order parameter.


A. T_w alone does not define a physical rapidity


The transported carrier is


T_w=exp[-(w/2)ad D] T_0 exp[(w/2)ad D]


in Killing-raised endomorphism language.


Therefore every full-adjoint trace constructed only from T_w, kappa, powers of T_w, and ad D is independent of w. This is the already-established conjugacy-orbit no-go.


Equivalently, under a local change of D-frame generated by rho(x)D,


T_w -> Ad_(exp(rho D)) T_w Ad_(exp(-rho D)),


which on the physical P/K doublet shifts the coordinate assigned to T alone as


w_T -> w_T-2rho


with the present half-rapidity normalization.


Thus


boxed:
w_T is not by itself a gauge-invariant scalar.


B. The compensated exchange involution supplies the relational reference


The already-earned compensated full-E8 involution Xcal obeys on the physical P/K sector


Xcal P_mu=K_mu,
Xcal K_mu=P_mu,
Xcal D=-D,


so that


Xcal|_(P/K)=X,
(ad D)|_(P/K)=Z,
XZ+ZX=0.


Book 16 defines the simultaneous-conjugation trace data


N_Z,D_Z,N_X,D_X


from T_w,D,Xcal and the physical P/K projector. Their exact reductions are


boxed:
N_Z/D_Z=Pi_C=tanh(2w),


boxed:
N_X/D_X=Xi_C=2 epsilon_P cosh(w)/(cosh^2(w)+1).


Under simultaneous E8 conjugation of T_w,D,Xcal and the associated projectors, both ratios are scalar traces. Therefore Pi_C and Xi_C are gauge invariant relational observables of the selector configuration.


C. Exact gauge-invariant relational rapidity


For finite real w, tanh(2w) is one-to-one. Define


boxed:
w_rel:=(1/2) artanh(Pi_C).


Then


boxed:
s w_rel=0


under the simultaneous selector BRST/gauge action, because s Pi_C=0.


The orientation sheet is separately read from the sign carried by Xi_C on the nonzero branch. Thus the relational selector data determine


continuous rapidity: w_rel,
orientation sheet: epsilon_P.


This distinguishes two notions that had previously shared the same symbol in unitary coordinates:


w_T = coordinate of T_w relative to a chosen D-frame,


w_rel = invariant relative coordinate between the transported carrier and the compensated exchange datum.


In the gauge where Xcal is fixed to its canonical representative, the two coincide:


w_T=w_rel.


But a simultaneous D-gauge transformation moves both T_w and Xcal and leaves w_rel unchanged. Therefore


boxed:
the physical relational rapidity cannot be gauged away by the lawful simultaneous E8 action.


D. The moving-normal D connection is a flat compensator


On the transported geometric branch the connection contains


A_geom=omega^(ab) M_ab-(1/2)dw D.


In the canonical Xcal-fixed representative, w may be read as w_rel. Hence


boxed:
A_D=-(1/2)dw_rel.


Because


[D,M_ab]=0,
d^2 w_rel=0,
[D,D]=0,


its curvature is


boxed:
F_D=0,


and


F_geom=R^(ab)M_ab.


Thus A_D is a moving-frame compensator connection. Its nonzero local value does not constitute an independent D-vector field strength or a Yang–Mills pole.


E. No rapidity derivative principal symbol in S_82 or the 45+20 extension


The declared normalized parent S_82 contains w through selector-covariant zero-form coefficient data such as


T_w,
Delta_w,
Ncal_w,
Pi_L,
Jcal_L,
Pi_C,
Xi_C,


but it contains no independent invariant of the form


dw_rel wedge *_g dw_rel,


D_A T_w wedge *_g D_A T_w,


or


F_D wedge *_g F_D.


The added terms of §§16.I.90–91 are


Pi_10 Yang–Mills,
I_N proportional to h_G(Pi_G D_A D,*_g Pi_G D_A D),


and neither produces a surviving dw_rel kinetic term after the N_20 auxiliary field is eliminated. The moving-normal D curvature is identically zero on the transported branch.


Therefore the quadratic Hessian in the relational rapidity direction has no k^2 principal term:


boxed:
principal_symbol_(w_rel)(S_91^cond)=0.


Background curvature and coefficient variation may still generate an algebraic w_rel Hessian or mixing. The exact statement is the absence of an independent rapidity propagation pole.


F. BRST interpretation


There are two different BRST statements.


For the coordinate of T_w alone, a D-gauge ghost can shift the chosen frame coordinate w_T. That coordinate is gauge-dependent.


For the relational trace scalar,


Pi_C=N_Z/D_Z,


simultaneous covariance gives


s Pi_C=0,


and therefore


s w_rel=0.


No local selector ghost c_D can make w_rel a BRST doublet without also changing the relational Xcal data in a way that changes Pi_C, which ordinary simultaneous gauge transport does not do.


Thus w_rel is BRST-closed at ghost number zero. The present theorem does not prove that it defines a nontrivial global quantum cohomology class, but it does rule out identifying the already-earned relational rapidity with the pure gauge coordinate of T_w alone.


G. Physical status: invariant order parameter, not yet a propagating scalar


The current action therefore places w_rel in a precise intermediate category:


1. it is not gauge removable as a relational selector scalar;
2. it has no independent two-derivative kinetic term;
3. it may enter algebraic stationarity conditions and background-dependent masses through Pi_C,Xi_C and the response coefficients;
4. it does not presently contribute a new local scalar pole.


This is consistent with the earlier manuscript statement that selector/rapidity variables are zero-form order-parameter fields whose coefficient variations do not by themselves create a second gravitational principal symbol.


H. Kinetic-promotion firewall


A future term such as


I_w=(Z_w/2) integral dw_rel wedge *_g dw_rel


would be gauge invariant because w_rel is relationally invariant. But it would be a genuinely new action invariant and would promote the rapidity order parameter into a physical scalar field.


That promotion would require a new audit of


scalar sign and normalization,
scalar mass and potential,
mixing with the coframe trace,
mixing with the 30380 transverse/radial sector,
contact/criticality protection,
and BRST cohomology.


No such term is authorized by the current S_82/S_91 theorem stack.


I. Relation to the one-dimensional D connection


Since A_D is already constrained by


A_D=-(1/2)dw_rel


on the transported branch and F_D=0, the current local spectrum contains neither


an independent propagating D gauge boson


nor


a propagating rapidity scalar.


Nevertheless w_rel can vary as an auxiliary/order-parameter field when the algebraic field equations or boundary data permit it. Flatness of A_D does not force w_rel to be constant; it only removes local D curvature.


J. Gate ledger


PASS: T_w alone is a conjugacy orbit and cannot define a gauge-invariant rapidity scalar.


PASS: the compensated exchange datum Xcal supplies the necessary relational reference.


PASS: Pi_C=N_Z/D_Z is invariant under simultaneous E8 selector transport and equals tanh(2w) on the physical branch.


PASS: w_rel=(1/2)artanh(Pi_C) is the exact finite-branch gauge-invariant relational rapidity.


PASS: w_rel is BRST-closed under simultaneous selector gauge transport and is not the pure-gauge T_w coordinate.


PASS: A_D=-(1/2)dw_rel is a flat moving-normal compensator with F_D=0 on the transported branch.


PASS: S_82 plus the §16.I.90–91 45+20 extension contains no independent w_rel derivative principal symbol.


PASS: the current selector spectrum contains no independent D-vector pole and no rapidity-scalar pole.

FIREWALL: adding a kinetic invariant for w_rel would create one new physical scalar and requires a fresh scalar/contact/BRST audit.

OPEN: odd physical matter action and final complete BRST Hessian.


UPDATED FRONTIER:
The local even-bosonic selector/vector spectrum is now sharply organized: one gravitational Einstein/Cartan block; one compact so(10) Yang–Mills block if the conditional extension is adopted; twenty normal connections in a locally contractible algebraic selector sector; no independent D-vector pole; and no rapidity scalar pole in the declared action. The next independent frontier is therefore the odd physical matter action and its BRST coupling to this completed even-sector architecture, followed by the full quantum measure/anomaly audit.




§16.I.93 — D-Grade Odd-Matter Projectors, the Weyl-16 Corridor, and the Chirality/Product-Duality Tension


Status. Sections 16.I.85 and 16.I.88 prove that the spinorially transforming components of the ordinary E8 connection are bosonic vector-spinors and must not be reinterpreted as physical fermions. The live microscopic construction nevertheless already requires genuine Grassmann/two-fermion exchange structure: the common ordered spinor pair generates both the 1820 symmetric channel and the 8008⊂30380 antisymmetric condensate channel. The remaining question is whether the same primitive spinorial carrier can support lawful odd matter after soldering. The exact five-grading by D answers the representation part positively, but it exposes a new chirality constraint: one D-grade supplies one four-dimensional chiral Spin(10) 16 corridor, whereas the full two-normal 126 condensate uses same-grade squares from both D grades. Thus a naive deletion of one mirror grade is incompatible with the already-earned two-normal condensate architecture. Whether product duality Q_Y lifts to the primitive fermion action and protects c_contact=0 remains open.


A. Exact D-grade projectors on the spinorial 128


Let Z:=ad D. The exact E8 five-grading has root grades n_1=±2 with 14 roots each, n_1=±1 with 64 roots each, and n_1=0 with 84 roots, with the eight Cartan directions in grade zero. Hence the full spinorial 128 is exactly the sum of the two rank-64 grade spaces g_+1 and g_-1.


Define


Pi_sp=(1/3) Z^2(4I-Z^2),
Pi_+=(1/2)(I+Z)Pi_sp,
Pi_-=(1/2)(I-Z)Pi_sp.


Then Pi_sp is the rank-128 projector onto the ±1 grades, Pi_± are orthogonal rank-64 projectors, and im Pi_±=g_±1. Under simultaneous E8 transport D->Ad_gD they transform by conjugation. Thus the odd grade subbundles are selector-covariant moving subbundles. No new fixed E8 spinor projector is required after D is earned.


FIREWALL. A Grassmann-odd field satisfying Psi_±=Pi_±Psi is a selector-dependent broken-phase matter bundle carried by the adjoint vector space, not a new fixed irreducible representation of unbroken full E8.


B. Lorentz/Weyl content of one grade


The live branching is


128 -> (16,4bar)⊕(16bar,4)


under Spin(10)×Spin(4,2). Standard conformal-spinor restriction gives, in the Book-16 integer grade normalization and up to the overall D-sign convention,


4bar -> S_R^(+1)⊕S_L^(-1),
4 -> S_L^(+1)⊕S_R^(-1).


Thus one rank-64 real grade contains one complex four-dimensional Weyl field in the 16 of Spin(10), together with its real-form/CPT-conjugate bar16 component; the opposite grade is the mirror Weyl corridor. One grade has complex dimension 2×16=32, or 64 real components, exactly the local carrier size of one chiral Spin(10) family.


C. Lawful post-soldering Weyl kinetic corridor


Once e_F is nondegenerate, a genuine Grassmann-odd field psi_+ in one selected grade admits the ordinary first-order Weyl action


S_F,+
=(i Z_F/3!)∫epsilon_abcd e_F^a∧e_F^b∧e_F^c∧psi_+^dagger barsigma^d D_(omega,A10)psi_+,


with the conjugate real-form term understood and Z_F>0 in the conventional positive-energy normalization. The opposite grade admits the mirror action. The principal symbol is the ordinary Weyl symbol. This is a CONDITIONAL ACTION MECHANISM; S_82 does not already contain it and Z_F is not yet microscopically derived.


D. Same primitive fermion square reproduces the live condensate channels


Book 16 already establishes


Sym^2(128)=1⊕1820⊕6435,
Lambda^2(128)=120⊕8008,


with j_8008:8008->30380. For an ordered microscopic pair T=psi⊗phi,


F(T)=P_1820 Sym(T),
C(T)=j_8008 P_8008 Alt(T).


Thus the same genuine odd/two-fermion parent supplies the symmetric selector/flux descendant and antisymmetric 30380 condensate descendant. A commuting rank-one replacement kills the 8008 branch.


Under Spin(10)×Spin(4,2), the same fermion square contains


(10,6)⊕(126,6)⊕(120,10bar),


with the (126,6) channel in the 30380 bracket kernel and supplying the physical normal condensate precursor.


E. D-grade decomposition of the conformal six-vector


Because the conformal vector six is the exterior square of the chiral four-spinor,


6 -> 1_(+2)⊕4_0⊕1_(-2)


in Book-16 grade normalization. The two Lorentz scalars are the two normal lines n_+,n_- and arise from same-grade Weyl squares, while the cross-grade product supplies the Lorentz vector 4_0.


Therefore, up to the global +/− convention,


C_+ ~ P_126(psi_+ wedge phi_+)⊗n_+,
C_- ~ P_126(psi_- wedge phi_-)⊗n_-.


F. Two-normal condensate requires both grade sectors


The live E8(-24)-real condensate is


C_0=a n_+ + b n_- + abar s_+ + bbar s_-


inside (126,6)⊕(126bar,6). The physical quarter-orientation branch uses


Re(a bbar)=0,
Im(a bbar)!=0,


so both |a| and |b| are nonzero. By the grade decomposition, the two nonzero normal amplitudes require microscopic same-grade pair support in both D-grade fermion corridors.


Hence


boxed: full nonzero n_+⊕n_- condensate support requires both microscopic D-grade fermion sectors.


Deleting one grade before condensation can produce at most one Lorentz-scalar normal 126 component. This is an exact representation/grade statement, not a statement about which fermion poles remain light afterward.


G. Chirality/product-duality tension


One grade is sufficient for a chiral Weyl-16 kinetic corridor, while the full two-normal condensate requires both grades. The already-earned Q_Y=*10 tensor(E_N H_N) acts nontrivially on the two normal condensate components and induces the Y reflection on the physical quotient. Therefore any microscopic lift of Q_Y must act consistently on the primitive structures generating both same-grade squares.


What is exact: both grades are required for the full nonzero condensate; one grade supplies one chiral Weyl corridor; Q_Y is complete at representation level on the heavy scalar sector.


What remains open: a specific fermionic Q_Y lift; equality or relation of the two grade kinetic normalizations; and a mechanism that removes one mirror pole from the low-energy spectrum without destroying Q_Y/contact protection.


This is a tension, not a no-go against chiral matter.


H. Lawful resolution classes


Possible resolutions include: primitive condensate fermions distinct from light matter; both grades present microscopically with one mirror sector later gapped/confined/paired/BRST-topologically removed; or a nonlinear/emergent Q_Y realization on the pair/heavy quotient. None is yet earned.


I. Interaction with the N_20 auxiliary selector sector


The twenty normal generators have grades ±2. Their opposite-grade action can map one spinorial grade into the other. If both microscopic odd grades are retained before unitary selector gauge, the algebraic N_20 connection can therefore couple off-diagonally to mirror fermion currents. Eliminating A_N through the §16.I.91 selector term can generate a local four-fermion interaction schematically J_N^2/M_N^2.


This is not automatically covered by the earlier minimal-unbroken-adjoint theorem that annihilates the 30380 anomalous-pair channel: N_20 exchange is selector-broken, algebraic, and carries the specific normal Clifford structure.


OPEN FINITE GATE: compute the Fierz/projection coefficient of this induced N_20 current-current operator into the 8008⊂30380 anomalous-pair channel. A zero coefficient would preserve c_contact=0 at tree level; a nonzero coefficient would regenerate the forbidden Hermitian 30380 contact unless Q_Y or another microscopic symmetry cancels it.


J. Gate ledger


PASS: exact D-grade projectors Pi_sp and Pi_± from ad D.
PASS: rank64 per grade and selector-covariant moving odd subbundles.
PASS: one grade has the local carrier size of one chiral Spin(10) Weyl-16 family plus its real-form/CPT conjugate.
CONDITIONAL ACTION MECHANISM: post-soldering Weyl kinetic term using e_F, omega, and Pi_10A.
PASS: same primitive ordered pair retains the certified 1820 and 8008->30380 descendants.
PASS: 6=1_(+2)⊕4_0⊕1_(-2); the two normal scalar 126 components are same-grade pair squares.
PASS: the nonzero quarter-branch condensate requires both microscopic D grades.
TENSION, NOT NO-GO: naive ultraviolet deletion of one mirror grade is incompatible with the full two-normal condensate, but low-energy chirality may still emerge later.
OPEN: microscopic Q_Y fermion/pair lift, odd-action measure anomaly, N_20-exchange 8008 Fierz coefficient, and dynamical mirror removal.


UPDATED FRONTIER: the odd-matter representation problem is no longer to find a fermion carrier; the exact D grading supplies it. The next calculation is the induced N_20 four-fermion operator and its 8008/30380 projection. That decides whether the spectrum-safe 45+20 even-sector completion is compatible with the scalar-contact firewall or whether a microscopic Q_Y lift is already mandatory at tree level.




§16.I.94 — Normal-Connection Exchange Factors Through the E8 Bracket and Cannot Regenerate the 8008/30380 Contact


Status. Section 16.I.93 left a finite compatibility test: eliminating the algebraic N_20 connection in the presence of both microscopic odd D grades generates a four-fermion current-current term. On the selector-covariant adjoint-action odd corridor, its group factor is the N_20 projection of the E8 bracket. Since 30380 is exactly the bracket kernel, the physical 8008 current vanishes before the selector metric is applied. Therefore N_20 exchange cannot regenerate the forbidden physical 30380 Hermitian contact at tree level.


Let beta:Lambda^2(248)->248 be beta(X wedge Y)=[X,Y]. Exact E8 representation theory gives


Lambda^2(248)=248⊕30380,
ker beta=30380,
8008⊂Lambda^2(128)⊂30380.


Define the normal current map


beta_N:=Pi_N beta,


with Pi_N=(ad D)^2Pi_G. Because beta_N is a postcomposition of beta,


ker beta⊂ker beta_N,


and therefore


boxed: beta_N|8008=0.


The §16.I.91 selector term gives a positive algebraic normal Hessian. In the presence of odd matter, after fixing/eliminating the selector angle one has schematically


(M_N^2/2)<A_N,A_N>_hN+<A_N,J_N>,


so


A_N=-M_N^(-2)h_N^(-1)J_N


and


Delta S_4F,N=-(1/(2M_N^2))<J_N,h_N^(-1)J_N>.


In the anomalous-pair channel the internal operator therefore factors as


Q_N=beta_N^dagger h_N^(-1) beta_N.


For C_8008 in the physical 8008,


Q_N C_8008=0,


hence


boxed: P_8008 Q_N P_8008=0,
boxed: Delta c_contact^(N20 tree)=0.


The two-component Lorentz Fierz rearrangement cannot change this exact zero of the internal group map.


Spin(10) shadow: 16 tensor16=10_s⊕120_a⊕126_s. The N_20 adjoint current is the internal 10 projection of the same-grade bracket, while the physical normal condensate uses the 126 component. The full-E8 kernel statement is stronger and controls the result independently of basis.


FIREWALLS. This does not forbid the independent lawful E8-invariant Hermitian 30380 contact; c_contact=0 remains a separate UV obligation. It does not prove selector-broken loops cannot generate a contact, and it assumes the odd coupling is inherited from the adjoint action rather than a new direct-30380 Yukawa vertex.


PASS: N_20 current factors through beta_N=Pi_N beta.
PASS: beta_N annihilates the full physical 8008.
PASS: Cartan-metric reweighting preserves the kernel.
PASS: tree-level N_20 elimination has zero 8008/30380 Hermitian-contact coefficient.
OPEN: radiative selector-sector contact generation; microscopic Q_Y fermion/pair lift; Q_Y measure anomaly; dynamical mirror removal.


UPDATED FRONTIER: the 45+20 spectrum completion is tree-level compatible with the active c_contact=0 branch. The next microscopic calculation is the fermionic lift of Q_Y: construct or falsify an operator on the two D-grade odd subbundles whose pair action reproduces Q_Y=*10 tensor(E_N H_N), then audit its square, odd kinetic invariance, and fermion measure.




§16.I.95 — Compensated-Exchange Hodge-Chirality Obstruction to the Primitive Q_Y Lift


Status. EXACT REPRESENTATION-LEVEL FALSIFICATION OF THE CURRENT CANDIDATE. This section tests only the proposed primitive operator


q_Y = exp[iπ(ad D)/4] 𝓧,


with 𝓧 = Ad(g_X). It does not falsify the existence of a different primitive product-duality lift. Because the candidate already fails on the complexified physical normal-pair representation, no odd kinetic, contact, or fermion-measure claim is promoted here.


Let


Z := ad D.


From §16.I.60.12 and the later D-grade audit,


𝓧 Z 𝓧^{-1} = -Z,


𝓧^2 = I


on the relevant physical odd carrier. Hence


q_Y^2 = I


on the complexified D = ±1 odd bundle, and q_Y exchanges the two grade subbundles.


On the same-grade normal pair lines the induced grade has eigenvalues ±2. Write


Z_N n_+ = +n_+,


Z_N n_- = -n_-.


Then


Z^(2) = 2 Z_N,


and the induced quarter-phase is


exp(iπ Z^(2)/4) = exp(iπ Z_N/2) = i Z_N.


The live normal operators are


E_N = X_N,


H_N = -Z_N,


R_prod := E_N H_N = Z_N E_N = iY,


with


R_prod n_+ = -n_-,


R_prod n_- = +n_+,


and


R_prod^2 = -I.


Let R_I denote the action of the internal compensator r_I on the real five-form carrier


F = Λ^5 R^10.


By construction r_I is a product of an odd number of mutually orthogonal internal reflections and is therefore orientation-reversing on the internal ten-space. Hodge naturality gives


R_I *_10 R_I^{-1} = -*_10.


After complexification,


F_C = 126_(+i) ⊕ overline{126}_(-i),


so R_I exchanges the two Hodge eigenspaces:


R_I : 126_(+i) ↔ overline{126}_(-i).


On the physical (126,6) normal pair sector the conformal reflection in 𝓧 supplies the normal exchange E_N, while the internal compensator supplies R_I. Up to the single global sign ε = ±1 allowed by pair-order convention,


Λ^2 𝓧 = ε R_I ⊗ E_N.


Therefore


Λ^2 q_Y
= exp(iπ Z^(2)/4) Λ^2 𝓧
= ε R_I ⊗ (i Z_N E_N)
= ε i R_I ⊗ R_prod.


The earned heavy-sector product duality is


Q_Y = *_10 ⊗ R_prod.


Since R_prod is invertible, equality up to an allowed overall sign would require


i R_I = ± *_10.


That is impossible. The operator R_I anticommutes with *_10 and exchanges the ±i Hodge eigenspaces, whereas *_10 commutes with itself and preserves each eigenspace.


Explicitly, for u ∈ 126_(+i),


Q_Y(u ⊗ n_+) = -i u ⊗ n_-,


whereas


Λ^2 q_Y(u ⊗ n_+) = -ε i (R_I u) ⊗ n_-,


with


R_I u ∈ overline{126}_(-i).


The normal quarter phase is the desired one, but the internal representation is wrong. An overall convention sign cannot turn a 126 target into an overline{126} target.


Theorem 16.I.95.T1 — Current primitive candidate is not the Q_Y lift.


The candidate


q_Y = exp[iπ(ad D)/4] 𝓧


does not satisfy


Λ^2 q_Y |_(physical normal pair) = Q_Y.


It is therefore rejected as the primitive product-duality symmetry, despite q_Y^2 = I on the complexified odd bundle.


Corollary 16.I.95.C1 — Scalar grade dressing of the same compensated exchange cannot repair the obstruction.


For any candidate


q_f = f(Z) 𝓧


with f(Z) scalar on the D-grade eigenspaces, the induced pair action retains the Hodge-antilinear R_I internal factor. Such a scalar grade dressing can change pair phases but cannot convert 126 ↔ overline{126} exchange into the Hodge-linear action *_10. Reversing the order of the quarter-phase and 𝓧 changes the phase convention but not this obstruction.


Real-structure disposition. The unresolved question whether exp(iπZ/4) can be realized as a lawful real operator remains a genuine issue for any future candidate that reaches it. It is not reached for the present q_Y because the candidate already fails after complexification.


Updated frontier. The next representation-level gate is narrower: classify lawful real grade-exchanging involutions on S_+ ⊕ S_- whose induced action on the same-grade five-form normal pair is Hodge-linear and equals *_10 ⊗ R_prod. The internal piece of a viable primitive map must induce *_10 on the 126 ⊕ overline{126} five-form pair rather than an orientation-reversing Hodge-eigenspace exchange. No action modification is authorized by this result. Odd kinetic invariance, microscopic 30380-contact transformation, fermion measure/discrete anomaly, radiative contact generation, and low-energy mirror removal remain open and downstream.


Book-16 live gate.


CURRENT CANDIDATE q_Y = exp[iπ(ad D)/4]𝓧: FALSIFIED AT REPRESENTATION LEVEL.


EXISTENCE OF A DIFFERENT PRIMITIVE Q_Y LIFT: OPEN.


KINETIC / CONTACT / MEASURE TESTS: NOT REACHED.




§16.I.96 — Real Primitive Product-Duality Lift by Lorentz Compensation, and the Hermitian-Contact No-Go


Status. EXACT REPRESENTATION-LEVEL EXISTENCE WITH A NONCANONICAL LORENTZ CHOICE; EXACT HERMITIAN-CONTACT NO-GO; CONDITIONAL KINETIC COROLLARY. The representation and real-structure gate for a primitive product-duality lift closes positively in the following precise sense: after choosing a suitable physical Lorentz reflection direction, there is a lawful real involution on the D=±1 odd bundle whose same-grade normal-pair action is the already-earned Q_Y up to the single global pair-order sign. The construction is not yet a canonical microscopic symmetry because the Lorentz direction is not selected intrinsically and the reflection reverses physical Lorentz orientation. Separately, the independent Hermitian 30380 contact gate closes negatively: every primitive lift whose pair action is the earned orthogonal Q_Y leaves the pure Hermitian norm contact even. No selected-vacuum, complete-action, measure, anomaly, or infrared-mirror theorem is promoted here.


A. Lorentz-compensated grade exchange


Use the live orthogonal split


V_{12,4}=I_{10}⊕L_{1,3}⊕⟨a,b⟩,


with


D=a∧b,


and let


Z:=ad D.


Choose a physical Lorentz vector ℓ∈L_{1,3}, orthogonal to a and b, with normalization


ℓ²=-a².


The physical Lorentz signature contains vectors of both signs, so such a choice is representation-theoretically available. Define


g_ℓ:=r_ℓ r_a,


and let


𝒫_ℓ:=ρ(g_ℓ)


be its action on the real D8 chiral spinorial odd carrier.


Because g_ℓ is a product of two reflections, it has even total Clifford parity and therefore acts within the same real chiral Spin module. Unlike the rejected compensated exchange of §16.I.95, it contains no internal reflection. Hence it preserves the internal 16/overline{16} chirality labels.


For orthogonal Clifford vectors,


(r_ℓr_a)²=-ℓ²a².


With ℓ²=-a² and the live unit normalization of the reflection vectors,


(r_ℓr_a)²=+1,


so


𝒫_ℓ²=I.


The r_a factor reverses the conformal normal entering D while r_ℓ commutes with D. Therefore


𝒫_ℓ Z 𝒫_ℓ^{-1}=-Z.


The Lorentz reflection reverses four-dimensional Weyl chirality while preserving the internal Spin(10) chirality. Thus, up to the global D-sign convention already fixed in §16.I.93,


16⊗S_R^(+1) ↔ 16⊗S_L^(-1),


overline{16}⊗S_L^(+1) ↔ overline{16}⊗S_R^(-1).


On the Lorentz-scalar same-grade normal-pair sector, the Lorentz reflection contributes only the invariant scalar-pair normalization, while r_a exchanges the two D=±2 normal lines. Consequently, up to the single global pair-order convention sign ε_ℓ=±1,


Λ²𝒫_ℓ |_(physical normal pair)
=
ε_ℓ I_F⊗E_N,


where F=Λ⁵R¹⁰ is the real internal five-form carrier.


This closes the grade-exchange obstruction that killed the internal-reflection candidate: the new compensator flips Lorentz chirality without exchanging 126 and overline{126} internally.


B. A lawful real realization of the quarter phase


Let J_I be the real-linear endomorphism of the odd spinor carrier induced by the oriented internal ten-dimensional Clifford volume element. On the real internal chiral spinor carrier,


J_I²=-I.


It is Spin(10)-equivariant, commutes with Z, and commutes with 𝒫_ℓ because 𝒫_ℓ acts only in the Lorentz/conformal factors. After complexification, choose the internal orientation convention so that


J_I|_{16}=+i,


J_I|_{overline{16}}=-i.


Thus J_I is the already-available real complex structure whose complex eigenvalue notation is ±i; no multiplication of the E8(-24)-real odd bundle by an external complex scalar is required.


On the D=±1 odd bundle,


(ZJ_I)²=-I.


Define the real grade dressing


R_D
:=
exp[(π/4)ZJ_I]
=
(1/√2)(I+ZJ_I),


and define


q_ℓ
:=
R_D 𝒫_ℓ
=
(1/√2)(I+ZJ_I)𝒫_ℓ.


This operator is manifestly real-linear. Since


𝒫_ℓ Z 𝒫_ℓ^{-1}=-Z,


[𝒫_ℓ,J_I]=0,


one has


𝒫_ℓ R_D 𝒫_ℓ^{-1}=R_D^{-1}.


Therefore


q_ℓ²
=
R_D𝒫_ℓR_D𝒫_ℓ
=
R_DR_D^{-1}𝒫_ℓ²
=
I.


Moreover


q_ℓ Z=-Zq_ℓ,


and hence


q_ℓΠ_±=Π_∓q_ℓ.


The real-structure warning left open after §16.I.95 therefore closes positively for this representation-level construction.


C. Exact pair intertwiner


On a primitive internal 16 state, J_I has eigenvalue +i; on an overline{16} state it has eigenvalue -i. The same-grade pair dressing therefore squares the primitive quarter phase. On the D=+2 normal line it produces the +i/-i internal five-form Hodge eigenvalue, while on the D=-2 normal line it produces its negative. Equivalently,


Λ²R_D |_(physical normal pair)
=
*_{10}⊗Z_N,


where


Z_Nn_+=+n_+,


Z_Nn_-=-n_-.


Using


Λ²𝒫_ℓ=ε_ℓ I_F⊗E_N,


we obtain


Λ²q_ℓ |_(physical normal pair)
=
ε_ℓ *_{10}⊗(Z_NE_N).


The live normal identities are


E_N=X_N,


H_N=-Z_N,


E_NH_N=Z_NE_N=R_prod.


Hence


Λ²q_ℓ |_(physical normal pair)
=
ε_ℓ *_{10}⊗(E_NH_N)
=
ε_ℓ Q_Y.


Theorem 16.I.96.T1 — Noncanonical real primitive representation lift.


Given a physical Lorentz reflection direction ℓ satisfying the conditions above, the real-linear operator


q_ℓ=(1/√2)(I+ZJ_I)𝒫_ℓ


is an involution on S_+⊕S_- satisfying


q_ℓΠ_±=Π_∓q_ℓ,


q_ℓ²=I,


and


Λ²q_ℓ |_(physical normal pair)=ε_ℓQ_Y.


Thus the primitive product-duality lift exists at representation level, up to the allowed global pair-order sign. This is REPRESENTATION / TOPOLOGICAL AVAILABILITY, not yet an exact microscopic symmetry: the direction ℓ has not been selected canonically or dynamically, and the complete action and selected vacuum have not been shown invariant.


D. Limited uniqueness of the quarter dressing


Within the ansatz consisting of a grade-exchanging involution that preserves internal 16/overline{16} chirality, followed by a real grade-diagonal Spin(10)-equivariant dressing, let the complexified grade eigenvalues be λ_+ and λ_-.


Involutivity requires


λ_+λ_-=1.


Matching the five-form Hodge eigenvalues on same-grade pairs requires


λ_+²=+i,


λ_-²=-i,


up to the single common primitive sign convention. Therefore the conjugate quarter phases e^{±iπ/4}, equivalently the real operator


R_D=(1/√2)(I+ZJ_I),


are essentially forced within this ansatz. This is not a global uniqueness theorem for all possible primitive lifts.


E. Conditional kinetic corollary


Let


S_F=Z_+S[ψ_+]+Z_-S[ψ_-]


be the conditional post-soldering odd kinetic sector of §16.I.93. At the principal Weyl-symbol level, with the coframe and Lorentz spin connection transformed covariantly by 𝒫_ℓ, the Pin reflection exchanges the two grade Weyl operators. The internal dressing R_D is unitary with respect to the natural real/complex spinor pairing. Therefore the two principal kinetic terms are interchanged.


Absent an additional transformed background factor, invariance requires


Z_+=Z_-.


This equality is not derived from the present bosonic parent; it is the coefficient relation required if q_ℓ is promoted to a symmetry of the odd kinetic extension.


The complete action test remains OPEN. In particular, r_ℓ reverses physical Lorentz orientation while the differential-form Weyl action contains ε_abcd. The transformation of the soldered coframe orientation, Lorentz connection, selected Spin(10) connection, selector D, bridge terms, pair/Yukawa couplings, and selected vacuum must all be audited before q_ℓ can be called a microscopic symmetry.


F. Exact Hermitian-contact no-go


Let C denote the physical 8008→30380 fermion-pair tensor and let the independent lawful Hermitian contact be


O_30380:=⟨C,C⟩_H.


Suppose any primitive transformation q — not necessarily q_ℓ — induces the already-earned pair action


C↦Q_YC,


with


Q_Y=*_{10}⊗R_prod.


On the real five-form carrier, *_{10} is orthogonal. On the physical two-normal plane, R_prod=E_NH_N is an orthogonal quarter-turn. Hence Q_Y is orthogonal with respect to the induced Hermitian/real pairing. Therefore


O_30380
↦
⟨Q_YC,Q_YC⟩_H
=
⟨C,C⟩_H
=
O_30380.


Thus the pure Hermitian 30380 norm is Q_Y-even.


Theorem 16.I.96.T2 — Q_Y cannot forbid the pure Hermitian 30380 norm by an ordinary field-only Ward parity.


For every primitive field transformation whose induced pair action is exactly the earned orthogonal product-duality involution Q_Y, the independent Hermitian contact


O_30380=⟨C,C⟩_H


is invariant. Therefore an ordinary Q_Y Ward symmetry acting only on the fields cannot force


c_contact=0


by making this operator odd.


A sign-changing spurion multiplying c_contact would be additional microscopic structure and is not derived here. The result does not prove that c_contact is nonzero; it proves that Q_Y alone is not the structural reason for setting it to zero.


This theorem is independent of the noncanonical ℓ used in q_ℓ. It applies to any primitive lift that induces the same orthogonal Q_Y on the physical pair tensor.


G. Updated live frontier


EXACT / REPRESENTATION LEVEL


• A real primitive product-duality lift exists after choosing a suitable Lorentz reflection direction ℓ.


• q_ℓ is real-linear, exchanges the D=±1 odd subbundles, and satisfies q_ℓ²=I.


• Its same-grade physical normal-pair action is ε_ℓQ_Y.


• The quarter phase has a lawful real realization through the internal volume complex structure J_I.


• Any primitive lift inducing orthogonal Q_Y leaves the pure Hermitian 30380 norm contact even.


REPRESENTATION / TOPOLOGICAL AVAILABILITY CAVEAT


• The construction is noncanonical because no preferred physical Lorentz reflection direction ℓ has been selected.


• The Lorentz reflection reverses physical orientation, so covariance of the complete soldered action is not automatic.


CONDITIONAL ACTION MECHANISM


• At principal Weyl-symbol level the two grade kinetic terms exchange; q_ℓ invariance would require Z_+=Z_- absent another compensating background transformation.


OPEN PHYSICAL GATES


• canonical/covariant realization of the Lorentz compensator, or a different lift without a chosen ℓ;


• invariance of the complete odd action and selected vacuum;


• transformation of all bridge, selector, pair, Yukawa, and gauge couplings;


• fermion-measure/discrete-anomaly consistency if a true microscopic q symmetry is obtained;


• an independent structural, critical, or fixed-surface reason for c_contact=0;


• selector-broken radiative generation of the 30380 contact;


• dynamical removal of the unwanted low-energy mirror grade;


• final complete fermionic spectrum and stability.


Book-16 live gate.


PRIMITIVE Q_Y REPRESENTATION LIFT: AVAILABLE, REAL, BUT NONCANONICAL.


Q_Y-ONLY HERMITIAN CONTACT PROTECTION: FALSIFIED.


MICROSCOPIC ACTION / VACUUM / MEASURE SYMMETRY: OPEN.




§16.I.97 — Gauge-Canonical Outer-Class Lift, Vectorlike Measure Cancellation, and the Remaining Pin/Wilsonian Gates


Status. EXACT GAUGE-CLASS CANONICALIZATION; EXACT FINITE-MODE JACOBIAN TRIVIALITY; CONDITIONAL LOCAL/PERTURBATIVE MEASURE CANCELLATION IN THE DOUBLED Z_+=Z_- CORRIDOR; PARTIAL BOSONIC PARITY COMPATIBILITY; CONDITIONAL POWER-COUNTING COROLLARY; FULL TWISTED PIN GLOBAL ANOMALY AND FINITE WILSONIAN 30380 GENERATION OPEN. Section 16.I.96 established a real primitive lift q_ℓ but left its physical Lorentz reflection direction ℓ apparently noncanonical. The first result below sharpens that status: once the already-fixed positive physical time orientation is respected, the freedom in ℓ is only connected-Lorentz gauge conjugacy. The primitive lift therefore defines one gauge-canonical outer class rather than a preferred observer. The doubled D=±1 odd corridor also closes the ordinary finite-mode measure gate and admits the expected vectorlike perturbative cancellation under a q-symmetric regulator. None of this settles the genuinely global Pin/gauge anomaly. Separately, the inherited E8 bracket still gives c_contact=0 exactly at tree level, while ordinary four-dimensional power counting supplies only a conditional statement about divergent counterterms, not an all-orders Wilsonian protection theorem.


A. The Lorentz compensator is gauge-representative freedom


The construction of §16.I.96 uses


g_ℓ=r_ℓr_a,


𝒫_ℓ=ρ(g_ℓ),


q_ℓ=R_D𝒫_ℓ,


R_D=(1/√2)(I+ZJ_I),


with


ℓ²=-a².


In the live conformal split, a is spacelike. Hence ℓ is timelike. Book 16 already fixes the positive physical time orientation, so restrict ℓ to the future unit timelike sheet with the required norm.


Let ℓ and ℓ′ be any two such choices. The proper orthochronous Lorentz group acts transitively on the future unit hyperboloid, so there exists


Λ∈SO^+(1,3)


such that


ℓ′=Λℓ.


Choose a spin lift


U∈Spin^+(1,3).


On Clifford vectors,


r_{ℓ′}=Ur_ℓU^{-1},


up to the common central sign of the spin lift. Since U acts only on the physical Lorentz factor,


[U,r_a]=0,


[ρ(U),Z]=0,


[ρ(U),J_I]=0,


and therefore


[ρ(U),R_D]=0.


It follows that


q_{ℓ′}
=
±ρ(U)q_ℓρ(U)^{-1}.


The sign is the already-allowed global primitive sign and drops out of the same-grade pair action.


Theorem 16.I.97.T1 — Gauge-canonical primitive outer-class lift.


After fixing the already-present positive physical time orientation, all admissible future-timelike representatives ℓ of the §16.I.96 construction are related by connected local Lorentz gauge conjugacy. Consequently the primitive product-duality lift defines a gauge-canonical class


[q]


with


Λ²q|_(physical normal pair)=±Q_Y,


while no preferred physical observer or timelike direction is selected by the construction itself.


Correction of language. The word “noncanonical” in §16.I.96 should now be read only at the representative level. A particular q_ℓ requires a Lorentz-frame representative, but the Lorentz-gauge equivalence class [q] is canonical once time orientation and the already-fixed internal/conformal structures are given.


B. Why an outer Lorentz class is unavoidable


The connected physical Lorentz spin group is


Spin^+(1,3)≅SL(2,C).


Its left- and right-handed Weyl modules are inequivalent complex representations. Therefore no nonzero complex-linear intertwiner that commutes with the full connected Spin^+(1,3) action can map


S_L↔S_R.


But every primitive lift of the present type must exchange the D grades while also exchanging the physical Weyl chirality in order to preserve the internal 16/overline{16} assignment established in §16.I.96.


Hence, absent an additional Lorentz tensor background that itself carries the chirality-changing information, such a lift cannot lie wholly inside the connected Lorentz group. It must represent the nontrivial disconnected/Pin outer class of the Lorentz action.


This explains the appearance of a reflection representative. The reflection is not evidence for a preferred frame; it is a representative of the unavoidable chirality-exchanging Lorentz outer class.


This is a limited uniqueness statement. It does not prove uniqueness among arbitrary field-dependent, nonlocal, or enlarged-group primitive lifts.


C. Compatibility with the existing bosonic grade-reversal skeleton


The earlier Book-16 grade-reversal analysis already established the bosonic transformation


n_+↔-n_-,


and therefore


D→-D.


Under that same reversal the linear selector source obeys


J_D→-J_D,


while quadratic expressions in J_D are even.


The new primitive class [q] has precisely the same defining selector action because


qZq^{-1}=-Z


and Z is generated by D. Thus the primitive odd lift does not introduce a disconnected new parity idea; it extends the already-present bosonic grade-reversal skeleton to the odd carrier.


This compatibility is only partial. A true symmetry of the complete soldered theory still requires a simultaneous audit of the coframe orientation, ε_abcd, Lorentz spin connection, selected Spin(10) connection, selector potential and source sector, bridge terms, pair/Yukawa couplings, and the selected vacuum.


D. The doubled D-grade corridor is vectorlike


After complexification the live odd decomposition is


S_+^C
=
(16⊗S_R)⊕(overline{16}⊗S_L),


S_-^C
=
(16⊗S_L)⊕(overline{16}⊗S_R).


The 16-valued pieces therefore combine across the two D grades into a four-dimensional Dirac corridor,


Ψ_16
:=
(χ_L^-,χ_R^+),


with the overline{16} pieces supplied by the real structure as the conjugate corridor rather than as an unrelated second family.


The primitive q class exchanges the left and right components,


χ_L^-↔χ_R^+,


up to the fixed internal quarter dressing. When the conditional kinetic relation from §16.I.96 holds,


Z_+=Z_-,


the principal kinetic sector of this doubled corridor is vectorlike with respect to the selected Spin(10) connection.


This closes the ordinary local chiral-gauge-anomaly concern for the doubled UV corridor at the representation level: the left/right contributions occur with opposite four-dimensional chirality and identical internal representation data. It does not establish the anomaly status of an infrared theory in which one grade has been dynamically removed.


E. Finite-mode q Jacobian


Let V_+ and V_- denote the real D=+1 and D=-1 odd subspaces. Each has real dimension 64. Any grade-exchanging involution q can be represented in block form as


q
=
[ 0   A
  A^{-1}  0 ],


with A:V_-→V_+ invertible.


For equal block dimension N,


det q=(-1)^N det(A A^{-1}).


Here N=64, so


boxed(det q=+1).


For the specific primitive representative of §16.I.96, the additional dressing


R_D=exp[(π/4)ZJ_I]


is a real orthogonal rotation. Its determinant is +1, consistently with the block result.


Therefore every finite spectral truncation respecting the D-grade pairing has trivial Berezin Jacobian under q:


Dψ→D(qψ)=Dψ.


Theorem 16.I.97.T2 — Finite-mode Jacobian triviality and local doubled-corridor measure cancellation.


On the complete real 128-dimensional odd carrier, the primitive grade-exchanging involution has determinant +1. Consequently its finite-mode Grassmann Jacobian is exactly trivial. If the regulator preserves the q pairing of the two D grades, the ordinary local/Fujikawa perturbative measure phase cancels between the vectorlike partner corridors.


Scope. This theorem is deliberately narrower than a full discrete-anomaly theorem. It closes the finite-dimensional Jacobian and ordinary paired perturbative measure gate. It does not evaluate a five-dimensional η invariant, a bordism class, or an anomaly on an unorientable background.


F. The genuine global Pin/gauge anomaly remains open


The primitive class [q] is orientation reversing in the physical Lorentz factor. To formulate the global anomaly problem unambiguously one must first determine the exact disconnected symmetry group acting on the complete microscopic fields. The unresolved choices include at least:


• whether the lift realizes Pin^+ or Pin^- after all signature and central-sign conventions are fixed;


• whether q² is identified strictly with 1 or with a central fermion-parity/gauge element on the complete field content;


• how the Spin(10) center is quotiented with the Lorentz/fermion center;


• how the conformal-normal reflection and selector D are incorporated in the disconnected group;


• whether the true microscopic symmetry lives in a normalizer inside the E8(-24) structure rather than a direct Pin×Spin(10) product.


Only after that group is fixed is the correct five-dimensional η-invariant or bordism computation well posed.


Therefore the present anomaly ledger is:


finite-mode q Jacobian: TRIVIAL;


ordinary local doubled-corridor perturbative measure phase: CANCELED UNDER q-SYMMETRIC REGULATION;


full twisted Pin/global anomaly: OPEN.


G. The 30380 contact: exact tree-level zero, no Q_Y Ward protection


The inherited E8 bracket remains


B:Λ²128→120,


with


Λ²128=120⊕8008,


8008=ker B.


The inherited N20 algebraic exchange factors through B. Therefore its tree-level quartic tensor lies in the bracket image and has no independent 8008-kernel Hermitian contact coefficient. Thus, in the inherited microscopic tree-level action,


boxed(c_contact=0).


This remains exact at that level and is independent of the failed Q_Y Ward-protection idea. Section 16.I.96 proved that the lawful Hermitian norm


O_30380=⟨C,C⟩_H


is Q_Y-even, so Q_Y alone cannot symmetry-forbid it.


There is nevertheless a narrower power-counting observation. In four post-soldering dimensions a Weyl field has canonical engineering dimension


[ψ]=3/2.


A local four-fermion contact therefore has


[ψ⁴]=6,


and its coefficient has mass dimension -2.


If — as an additional microscopic assumption, not yet a theorem of the full gravity/selector theory — the odd extension is truncated to local vertices of canonical dimension at most four, then the superficial degree of divergence of a one-particle-irreducible graph with four external fermions is at most


ω=4-(3/2)×4=-2


after the usual subdivergences are removed. No divergent local dimension-six ψ⁴ counterterm is then required by ultraviolet renormalization.


Corollary 16.I.97.C1 — Conditional UV-counterterm stability of the 30380 contact.


Under a local canonical-dimension-≤4 microscopic truncation of the odd sector, the independent 30380 Hermitian four-fermion operator is not required as a divergent counterterm by ordinary four-dimensional power counting. Hence the inherited tree-level surface c_contact=0 is stable against divergent renormalization within that restricted truncation.


This is not an all-orders Wilsonian zero theorem. Nonrenormalizable gravitational, selector, soldering, or bridge vertices can invalidate the power-counting premise; integrating out heavy fields can generate finite dimension-six operators; and finite threshold terms need not vanish even when no divergence exists. Therefore


all-orders Wilsonian c_contact=0: OPEN.


H. Selector-vacuum implication


Because q acts by


D→-D,


an exactly q-invariant selector potential with degenerate nonzero extrema ±D has paired vacua. Choosing one sign of D spontaneously breaks q unless the two vacua are gauge-identified by a larger exact symmetry.


This observation is useful but does not solve the infrared mirror problem. Spontaneous q breaking can distinguish the two grade orientations, but no calculation here shows that one D-grade fermion corridor becomes heavy, confined, projected out, or otherwise absent from the observed low-energy spectrum.


In particular, an exact unbroken q symmetry and a low-energy theory containing only one of the exchanged grades are incompatible. Any successful one-grade infrared reduction must either break q, gauge-identify the exchanged sectors, or supply an additional dynamical mechanism.

§16.I.98 — Primitive Fermionic Dihedral Double Cover of D4, Exact q-Square, and the Reduced Global-Anomaly Group Gate


Status. EXACT PRIMITIVE GROUP PRESENTATION; EXACT FERMIONIC CENTRAL EXTENSION AND D4 PAIR DESCENT; q² AMBIGUITY CLOSED; CONNECTED ODD SPIN-GAUGE QUOTIENT DERIVED AT REPRESENTATION LEVEL; FULL ACTION SYMMETRY, FULL SELECTOR/CONFORMAL/E8 EXTENSION, AND TWISTED BORDISM/η ANOMALY OPEN.


Section 16.I.97 correctly isolated the disconnected-symmetry group as the prerequisite for a global anomaly calculation, but it left the primitive square and the relation between the fermionic lift and the already-earned bosonic D4 orbit too loosely described in Pin language. The identities already proved in §16.I.96 close a substantial part of that gate. The primitive quarter dressing and the grade-reversing reflection do not generate an arbitrary disconnected extension. They generate a specific finite fermionic dihedral group whose central fermion sign disappears on pair data, leaving exactly an order-eight dihedral quotient. The primitive q reflection has square +I exactly, not merely modulo a center.


A. Exact primitive algebra


Set


K:=ZJ_I,


R:=R_D=exp[(π/4)K],


P:=𝒫_ℓ,


q:=q_ℓ=RP.


The §16.I.96 identities are


K²=-I,


P²=I,


PZP^{-1}=-Z,


[P,J_I]=0.


Therefore


PKP^{-1}=-K,


and hence


PRP^{-1}
=
exp[-(π/4)K]
=
R^{-1}.


Because K²=-I,


R²
=
exp[(π/2)K]
=
K,


R⁴
=
K²
=
-I,


R⁸
=
I.


Moreover


q²
=
RPRP
=
R(PRP^{-1})P²
=
RR^{-1}
=
I.


These are identities on the complete real odd carrier, not merely on the physical pair quotient.


Theorem 16.I.98.T1 — Exact primitive order-16 dihedral presentation.


On the real 128-dimensional odd carrier, the operators R_D and 𝒫_ℓ generate the group


\widetilde D_4^{\,f}
:=
⟨R,P\mid R⁸=1,\;P²=1,\;PRP^{-1}=R^{-1}⟩.


This is the ordinary dihedral group with rotation subgroup of order eight and total order sixteen. In notation that records the rotation order, it may be denoted Dih_8. It is not the dicyclic, binary-dihedral, or quaternion extension: the distinction is fixed by the exact relation


P²=I,


rather than P²=R⁴.


The primitive product-duality reflection


q=RP


is itself an involution:


boxed(q²=I).


Thus the §16.I.97 possibility that q² might close only up to fermion parity or an internal gauge-center element is eliminated for this primitive lift.


B. Fermionic central sign and pair descent


The fourth power of the primitive rotation is


z_F:=R⁴=-I.


It is central, has order two, and acts as the common minus sign on the odd carrier. On any same-grade fermion pair,


Λ²z_F
=
Λ²(-I)
=
+I.


Therefore the pair representation factors through the central quotient


\widetilde D_4^{\,f}/⟨z_F⟩.


In this quotient the image \bar R of R has order four, the image \bar P of P has order two, and


\bar P\bar R\bar P^{-1}=\bar R^{-1}.


Hence


\widetilde D_4^{\,f}/⟨z_F⟩
≅
D_4,


where D_4 denotes the project’s order-eight dihedral group acting on the pair/polarization orbit.


Equivalently there is an exact central extension at primitive representation level,


1
→
Z_2^F
→
\widetilde D_4^{\,f}
→
D_4
→
1,


with


Z_2^F=⟨-I⟩.


Theorem 16.I.98.T2 — Fermionic central extension and D4 pair descent.


The primitive quarter-phase structure is a spinorial double cover of the order-eight pair-level dihedral algebra: the primitive rotation has order eight, its fourth power is the fermionic central sign, and the induced pair rotation has order four. The primitive reflection q descends on the certified physical normal pair as


Λ²q|_(physical normal pair)
=
ε_ℓQ_Y.


Thus the ±Q_Y pair reflection is not an isolated quotient operation. It is the even/pair shadow of an exact order-16 fermionic dihedral representation.


Scope. The theorem identifies the finite group generated by the already-certified primitive operators and its pair quotient. It does not by itself prove that every global RP1/D4 chart generator is realized by the same microscopic operator on every field of the full action. That stronger identification remains an action-level extension problem.


C. Why the primitive quarter phase has to double the bosonic order


On the odd carrier,


R
=
exp[(π/4)ZJ_I]


acts with primitive quarter phases. Two primitive factors in a same-grade pair square that phase. This is exactly why §16.I.96 found


Λ²R|_(physical normal pair)
=
*_{10}⊗Z_N,


while the complete primitive operator still has


R⁴=-I.


The central sign is invisible to even pair data. Consequently the bosonic/pair quarter-turn has order four even though its primitive fermionic lift has order eight.


This resolves a structural feature that had previously appeared as bookkeeping: the e^{±iπ/4} primitive dressing is the correct spinorial half-angle of the pair-level ±i Hodge/quarter-turn phase. The order doubling is therefore algebraic, not an extra postulate.


D. The connected odd spin-gauge kernel


The live complexified odd decomposition is


S_+^C
=
(16⊗S_R)⊕(overline{16}⊗S_L),


S_-^C
=
(16⊗S_L)⊕(overline{16}⊗S_R).


The central element -1 of Spin^+(1,3) acts as -1 on a Weyl spinor. The central element -1 of Spin(10) acts as -1 on its spinor representations. Their diagonal product therefore acts trivially on every tensor-product summand above:


(-1,-1)↦(+1).


Hence the connected spin-gauge action on the odd carrier factors through


G_{0,odd}
:=

The internal order-four spin-center phase does not enlarge this diagonal kernel. In the manuscript convention the internal volume complex structure acts as

whereas the Lorentz center supplies only the real signs ±1. Thus the nontrivial ±i internal central action cannot be canceled uniformly by a Lorentz central element on the complete 16⊕overline{16} carrier.

boxed(
G_{0,odd}
≅

This is a representation-level statement about the odd carrier. It is not yet a theorem identifying the global gauge group of the complete microscopic theory after all E8(-24), selector, conformal-normal, and vacuum identifications are imposed.


E. Action of q on the connected group


The internal operator J_I is Spin(10)-equivariant. The grade operator Z commutes with the selected Spin(10) action, and 𝒫_ℓ acts in the physical Lorentz/conformal factors while preserving the internal Spin(10) chirality. Therefore q centralizes the connected Spin(10) action.


On the physical Lorentz factor, q contains the chirality-reversing reflection representative. Its conjugation action on Spin^+(1,3) is therefore the usual disconnected Lorentz outer automorphism exchanging left and right Weyl modules.


Consequently the restricted disconnected symmetry acting on the odd spin-gauge representation has the split form


G_{odd}^{(q)}
=

where


q²=1,

This split extension is exact for the restricted odd spin-gauge representation because q²=I exactly.


Firewall. The complete microscopic discrete operation is larger than a bare physical reflection: q also contains the conformal-normal grade reversal and the internal quarter dressing R_D. Therefore G_{odd}^{(q)} is not yet the full selector/conformal/E8(-24) symmetry group. The full action may require a normalizer extension or additional identifications not visible in the restricted odd representation.


F. Correction of the bare Pin± question


Section 16.I.97 listed Pin^+ versus Pin^- as part of the unresolved global-anomaly data. That wording was intentionally cautious but is now too broad for the primitive lift actually derived.

q

§16.I.99 — Orientation-Compensated q Action Audit: Exact Selected-Line Gravity Pass, Conditional Odd-Kinetic Pass, and the Vacuum/Action Split


Status. EXACT ORIENTATION-SIGN AUDIT FOR THE EARNED HOLST–EINSTEIN–CARTAN–VOLUME LINE; EXACT MATCH TO THE Y-REFLECTION CHARACTERS OF THE DERIVED GRAVITATIONAL COEFFICIENTS; EXACT RANK-ONE COFRAME-PROJECTOR COMPATIBILITY AT THE QUARTER-Y BALANCE BRANCH; PRINCIPAL ODD KINETIC INVARIANCE CONDITIONAL ON Z_+=Z_- AND q-COVARIANT BACKGROUND TRANSPORT; SELECTOR SOURCE PARITY COMPATIBLE AT THE CERTIFIED LEVEL; NONZERO SELECTOR VACUUM GENERICALLY q-BROKEN; BRIDGE/YUKAWA/COMPLETE MICROSCOPIC ACTION STILL OPEN.


Sections 16.I.96–16.I.98 established a gauge-canonical primitive reflection class [q], its exact involutive square, its order-16 fermionic dihedral closure, and its order-eight D4 pair quotient. Those are representation-level results. The next question is stricter: can the same q be promoted to a symmetry of the already-earned post-soldering action without undoing the rank-one gravity result or reintroducing a second spin-2 kinetic line?


The answer is now partially sharp. The gravitational sign problem closes if q is realized globally as an orientation-compensated parity operation: an improper physical Lorentz/frame reflection accompanied by an orientation-reversing spacetime map. Under that realization, the Holst, Einstein–Cartan, and volume integrals acquire exactly the parities required by the already-derived Y-reflection characters of their coefficients. An internal improper frame reflection by itself does not give the required matching. The fermion principal kinetic sector passes only conditionally, with the already-isolated requirement Z_+=Z_-. The bridge/Yukawa sector remains insufficiently specified for certification.


A. Action covariance is not vacuum invariance


The manuscript has repeatedly distinguished the D4-covariant parent family from one selected vacuum. That distinction becomes essential here.


Let Φ denote the complete field/background data on which q acts. A microscopic q symmetry requires action covariance


S[q·Φ]=S[Φ].


This does not require a chosen vacuum Φ_0 to satisfy


q·Φ_0=Φ_0.


If instead


q·Φ_0=Φ_0',


with Φ_0' a degenerate partner vacuum, then the action may be exactly q-covariant while the selected vacuum spontaneously breaks q.


This is precisely the situation suggested by the certified selector reversal


D→-D.


Therefore all subsequent statements must keep two ledgers separate:


ACTION / PARENT q COVARIANCE,


versus


SELECTED-VACUUM q INVARIANCE.


Conflating them would incorrectly turn spontaneous symmetry breaking into an action-level obstruction.


B. The global orientation-compensated realization


The primitive q contains the physical Lorentz chirality-reversing outer class. To test the earned gravitational action, choose a representative consisting of


1. an orientation-reversing spacetime map p:M→M with


deg(p)=-1,


2. an improper physical frame transformation Λ with


det Λ=-1,


3. the already-certified odd-carrier primitive action q_ℓ=R_D𝒫_ℓ, including the conformal-normal grade reversal and internal quarter dressing.


On the coframe and curvature take


e'^a=Λ^a{}_b p^*e^b,


R'^{ab}=Λ^a{}_cΛ^b{}_d p^*R^{cd}.


This subsection does not claim that the spacetime map p has already been derived as an E8(-24) gauge operation. It is the global parity realization required to test whether the disconnected primitive class can act as a symmetry of the post-soldering spacetime theory.


C. Certified Y-reflection characters of the gravitational coefficients


On the earned D4 polarization carrier


P_C=(Π_C,Ξ_C)^T,


the Y reflection acts as


Π_C→-Π_C,


Ξ_C→Ξ_C.


The selected-line gravitational coefficients already have the dependence


C_H=16λ_H Π_C,


C_EC=16λ_H Ξ_C,


while the cosmological coefficient has the certified condensate dependence


Λ_C∝λ_J m_C².


For q-even microscopic couplings λ_H and λ_J this gives

Thus the coefficient character under the pair-level Y reflection is


(C_H,C_EC,Λ_C)
→
