§16.I.119 — Inverse Post-Soldering Critical Hyperbola, Reciprocal Saw RG Coordinates, and Tilted-Branch Common-Phase Survival


Status. EXACT REDUCED/POST-SOLDERED SYNTHESIS USING THE ALREADY-EARNED PHASE/KINETIC CO-MATCHING LAW AND THE ALREADY-EARNED Φ₂/G₄ HARMONIC. NO MICROSCOPIC RG TANGENCY THEOREM IS PROMOTED. THE TILTED RANK-ONE BRANCH NEED NOT BE A q-FIXED VACUUM; EXACT q-FIXED NULLITY REMAINS THE STRONGER SPECIAL POINT.


1. Inverse-matching reset.


The post-soldered theory is taken as boundary data rather than as an output to be re-derived by successive speculative UV mechanisms. The minimal physical requirements are:


• exactly one nondegenerate spin-2 kinetic line;
• the 45 internal gauge directions propagating with the 20 normal directions algebraic rather than as additional vector poles;
• the odd kinetic fixed surface Z_+=Z_- when exact reduced q is imposed;
• a nonzero common-phase fourth harmonic carried by G₄;
• a scalar/relative-phase sector compatible with the one-spin-2 condition.


The microscopic problem is therefore an inverse matching problem: determine which parent couplings can land simultaneously on this post-soldered coefficient/rank surface.


2. Exact tilted one-spin-2 critical surface.


Let


C_sc:=2 Re(a b̄),
P:=2 Im(a b̄),
r:=|g_P/g_A|.


The already-earned reduced gravitational kernel obeys


det K_Σ=(4/N²)[g_A² C_sc² +(g_A²-g_P²)P²].


Hence, on the nonzero orientation branch P≠0, exact rank one is equivalent to


C_sc²=(r²-1)P²,


with r≥1 on the real tilted branch. The exact scalar-null/common-normalization point is the special case


C_sc=0,
r=1.


Using the stationary relative-phase equation of §16.I.55.31,


s_Ω c_S
=
tanδ [ μ-2βR_Σ cosδ ],


and the rank-one condition


r=secδ,


define


D_*:=μ-(2βR_Σ)/r,
u:=s_Ω c_S/D_*.


Then


u=tanδ,
r=secδ,


so the entire post-soldered phase/gravity co-matching condition is the unit hyperbola


boxed:
r²-u²=1.


Equivalently,


c_S²=(r²-1)[ μ-(2βR_Σ)/r ]².


This is exact within the already-declared reduced phase potential and kinetic completion. It is not yet a microscopic E8 action theorem.


3. Reciprocal Saw coordinates on coupling space.


Define


R_+:=r+u,
R_-:=r-u.


Then exactly


R_+R_-=1,


and on the physical |δ|<π/2 branch


R_±=secδ±tanδ.


Thus the post-soldered rank-one critical surface reconstructs the native reciprocal cxp/crx pair in coupling space. Introducing


ω_crit:=ln R_+,


one has


R_±=e^{±ω_crit},
r=coshω_crit,
u=sinhω_crit.


The symmetric product-duality/null point is


ω_crit=0.


Firewall. ω_crit is a newly derived rapidity coordinate on the post-soldered coupling/criticality surface. It is not identified with the original Book-16 geometric rapidity w or ζ without an additional theorem.


4. Exact RG tangency condition.


Let the renormalization-scale derivative be denoted by β. For


F_crit:=r²-u²-1,


one has


β_F=2r β_r-2u β_u.


Therefore the tilted one-spin-2 surface is RG-invariant iff


boxed:
r β_r=u β_u.


Since u=s_Ω c_S/D_*, this is equivalently


boxed:
β_(c_S)/c_S - β_(D_*)/D_*
=
r β_r/(r²-1)


away from c_S=0.


In reciprocal variables the same condition is


β_(R_+)/R_+ + β_(R_-)/R_-=0.


If a microscopic parent renormalizes the two channels through one running boost coordinate,


R_±=e^{±ω_crit},


then


β_(R_±)=±β_ω R_±,


and the rank-one critical hyperbola is preserved identically.


This identifies the sharp microscopic target: the scalar tilt and kinetic-normalization mismatch must arise as odd/even components of one determinant-one transport, rather than as unrelated running counterterms.


5. Small-spurion limit.


For |ω_crit|≪1,


u=ω_crit+O(ω_crit³),
r=1+(1/2)ω_crit²+O(ω_crit⁴).


Hence


r-1=(1/2)u²+O(u⁴)


and therefore


r-1
=
c_S²/(2D_*²)+O(c_S⁴),


which recovers the earlier even-normalization/odd-scalar-spurion scaling as the Taylor expansion of the exact critical hyperbola.


6. Relative and common phases separate exactly.


Parameterize the two nonzero normal amplitudes by


a=A exp[i(σ+θ/2)],
b=B exp[i(σ-θ/2)],


where σ is the common phase and θ is the relative phase. Then


C_sc=2AB cosθ,
P=2AB sinθ,


whereas the same-chirality 54 source satisfies


Y_54∝ab M(u)
=AB e^{2iσ} M(u).


Therefore the relative phase θ cancels identically from the holomorphic 54 source. Consequently


G₄=B_3875(Y_54,Y_54)


contains the fourth common-phase harmonic


A₄ e^{4iσ}+Ā₄ e^{-4iσ}


with A₄≠0 whenever A,B≠0. The already-proved nonzero A₄ does not depend on imposing scalar nullity; the quarter-null condition was used only to simplify the displayed phase on that branch.


7. Survival of the fourth harmonic on the tilted rank-one branch.


From


4A²B²=C_sc²+P²


and the rank-one relation


C_sc²=(r²-1)P²,


one obtains


boxed:
4A²B²=r²P².


Thus on every nonzero-orientation tilted rank-one branch,


boxed:
|A₄|∝A²B²=r²P²/4>0.


The tilted one-spin-2 compensation therefore cannot extinguish the common-phase selector.


8. Common-phase minimum and half-tilt of the individual legs.


On the exact null branch the healthy fourth-harmonic sign gives the odd-quarter leg phase, equivalently a common-phase minimum


σ_*=0 mod π/2


in the above average/relative parametrization. Along the continuous tilted branch the stationary relative phase may be written


θ=π/2+s_Ω δ mod π.


Because G₄ depends on σ but not θ, the common minimum remains


boxed:
σ_*=0 mod π/2.


The individual leg phases shift only through the relative tilt:


φ_(a,*)=π/4+(s_Ω δ)/2 mod π/2,
φ_(b,*)=-π/4-(s_Ω δ)/2 mod π/2.


Thus the individual odd-quarter positions acquire exactly half of the relative phase displacement while the common fourth-harmonic minimum itself does not move.


9. Angular Hessian decoupling.


For a phase potential of the form


V(θ,σ)=V_rel(θ)+H(θ) cos(4σ+φ),


one has


∂²V/(∂θ∂σ)=-4H'(θ) sin(4σ+φ).


At every common-phase extremum,


sin(4σ+φ)=0,


so


boxed:
V_(θσ)=0.


The relative one-spin-2 stabilization and the common G₄ phase stabilization therefore decouple at quadratic angular order at their joint stationary point.


10. Strong q-fixed point versus tilted rank-one pair.


The exact reduced q/product-duality operation reverses the dangerous scalar overlap and therefore exchanges the two tilted branches ±δ. A generic δ≠0 vacuum is thus not individually q-fixed even though it can retain exact rank-one gravitational kinetics.


The strong q-fixed point is


δ=0,
C_sc=0,
r=1,


supplemented in the odd kinetic sector by the already-earned fixed surface


Z_+=Z_-.


The weaker tilted one-spin-2 family is


δ≠0,
r=secδ,


with the common G₄ fourth harmonic still nonzero and its common-phase minimum unchanged. Whether the odd Dirac kinetic sector is blind to ω_crit, or instead develops a kinetic mismatch Z_+-Z_- when the relative vacuum tilts, is not yet derived.


11. Contact/criticality firewall.


The lawful Hermitian 30380 contact is Q_Y-even, so a field-only q Ward identity cannot forbid it. The reduced coefficient c_S is an effective scalar-tilt parameter whose detailed microscopic decomposition includes contact, heavy spectral contrast, and higher retained contributions. Therefore the existence of the critical hyperbola does not by itself prove that a generated microscopic contact renormalizes into c_S and r in the required correlated fashion.


The correct inverse-matching question is whether the full renormalized post-soldered coefficients satisfy


F_crit=0


as a fixed/critical surface. Exact operator-level c_contact=0 remains a stronger sufficient condition, especially at the q-fixed center, but it is not the only reduced route to one-spin-2 rank once tilted co-matching is allowed.


12. Updated backward frontier.


CLOSED EXACTLY AT REDUCED/POST-SOLDERED LEVEL:
• one-spin-2 tilted critical surface r²-u²=1;
• reciprocal Saw coordinates R_±=secδ±tanδ with R_+R_-=1;

NOT CLOSED MICROSCOPICALLY:

• proof that the tilted branch preserves the odd kinetic equality Z_+=Z_- rather than spontaneously exchanging two q-related vacua;
• identification, if any, of ω_crit with an already-earned upstream rapidity.


NEXT FINITE BACKWARD GATE:
compute the dependence of the two-grade odd kinetic coefficients Z_± on the tilted critical coordinate ω_crit and determine whether


boxed:
Z_+-Z_-=0


holds identically along the rank-one hyperbola or only at ω_crit=0.


No full-E8 automorphism, pre-soldering activation, absolute mirror selection, or physical cosmology is promoted by this section.


§16.I.120 — Tilted-Branch Odd-Kinetic Spurion Audit, Vacuum-Adapted Reflection, and the Exact-q Central-Point Boundary


Status. EXACT REDUCED q-COVARIANT KINETIC-SPURION CLASSIFICATION; EXACT REPRESENTATION-LEVEL VACUUM-ADAPTED REFLECTION CONSTRUCTION; NO GENERIC CONTINUOUS AXIAL/MEASURE SYMMETRY IS PROMOTED. THE COMMON-PHASE G₄ SECTOR IS GRADE-BLIND, BUT A GENERIC q-ODD RELATIVE BACKGROUND CAN SOURCE KINETIC MISMATCH.


1. Exact kinetic mismatch variable.


Section 16.I.102 defines


bar Z:=sqrt(Z_+ Z_-),
delta_Z:=(1/2)log(Z_+/Z_-),


so that the odd kinetic matrix is


cal Z=bar Z exp(delta_Z Z).


Under the primitive reduced reflection q,


bar Z→bar Z,
delta_Z→-delta_Z.


Therefore delta_Z is the exact q-odd kinetic spurion and


Z_+=Z_-


is the unique positive q-fixed kinetic surface.


After grade-dependent canonical normalization the primitive exchange becomes


q_can=exp(delta_Z Z)q,


and on same-grade pairs its normal action is


Lambda² q_can
=±exp(2 delta_Z Z_N) Q_Y.


Thus a real kinetic mismatch dresses the pair involution by an opposite real rescaling of the two normal lines. The exact earned unit-normalized Q_Y quotient survives iff delta_Z=0.


2. q parities of the condensate Bloch coordinates.


For


S:=2 Re(a bbar),
P:=2 Im(a bbar),
Z_B:=|a|²-|b|²,
N:=|a|²+|b|²,


the same-chirality pair product duality acts as


(S,P,Z_B,N)
→
(-S,+P,-Z_B,+N).


Hence the two q-odd condensate background coordinates are S and Z_B, while P and N are q even.


3. General q-covariant kinetic response.


Any reduced Spin(10)-scalar two-grade principal kinetic matrix has the form


cal Z_eff=A I+B Z.


q covariance requires A to be even and B to be odd under


(S,Z_B)→(-S,-Z_B).


Therefore the most general analytic mismatch near the symmetric branch is


delta_Z
=
S F_S(S²,Z_B²,S Z_B,P,N,...)
+
Z_B F_Z(S²,Z_B²,S Z_B,P,N,...),


with F_S and F_Z q-even scalar functions.


Consequently a tilted relative-phase background S≠0 generically permits a nonzero kinetic mismatch. q covariance by itself implies only


Z_+(S,Z_B)=Z_-(-S,-Z_B),


not equality of Z_+ and Z_- at one selected tilted vacuum.


On the one-coordinate equal-amplitude subbranch Z_B=0 this reduces to


delta_Z(omega_crit)
=a_1 omega_crit+a_3 omega_crit³+...,


because omega_crit is itself q odd.


4. The G₄ common-phase sector is grade blind.


For


a=A exp[i(sigma+theta/2)],
b=B exp[i(sigma-theta/2)],


one has


ab=AB e^{2i sigma},


and therefore the holomorphic 54 source and strict phase scalar depend on the common phase and on the symmetric product A B, but not on the relative phase theta:


Y_54∝AB e^{2i sigma}M,
G₄∝A²B² e^{4i sigma}.


Under grade exchange a↔b these structures are invariant. Their radial factor may be written


A²B²=(N²-Z_B²)/4,


which is q even.


Therefore the isolated G₄ common-phase sector cannot by itself supply the q-odd coefficient B multiplying the kinetic grading operator Z. The potential kinetic splitting must come from the relative/q-odd sector or from other q-odd microscopic/background spurions.


5. Tree-level versus radiative status on the tilted branch.


The declared §16.I.102 kinetic ansatz treats Z_± as independent post-soldered kinetic coefficients. The tilted critical coordinate of §16.I.119 does not algebraically force delta_Z≠0 at tree level. Thus one may impose


Z_+=Z_-


at a tilted classical vacuum.


However, if the selected vacuum has S≠0 or Z_B≠0, the vacuum breaks the primitive reflection q. The renormalized effective action is then only required to relate the +tilt and -tilt branches. It does not force the same-vacuum self-energies to remain equal. Exact radiative protection of Z_+=Z_- therefore requires an additional grade-blindness theorem or a stronger residual symmetry.


6. Vacuum-adapted dressed reflection at representation level.


Let


K:=Z J_I,


so that R_D=exp[(pi/4)K] and the primitive reflection conjugates K to -K. For any real alpha define


q_alpha:=exp(alpha K)q.


Then


q_alpha²=1.


On the same-chirality pair normal slice the dressing acts as opposite unit phases on the two normal lines, so the pair involution is


Q_(Y,alpha)
=
exp(2 i alpha Z_N)Q_Y.


Its +1 fixed line satisfies


arg(a)-arg(b)
=
pi/2+2 alpha
mod pi,


with no real kinetic rescaling. Therefore a tilted relative-phase vacuum


theta=pi/2+s_Omega delta


is fixed at representation level by choosing


boxed:
alpha=(s_Omega delta)/2.


This shows that the tilted one-spin-2 branch can be viewed algebraically as a reflected line in a continuously rotated family of reduced odd reflections while maintaining Z_+=Z_- at principal kinetic level.


7. Finite-group and measure firewall.


The earned primitive fermionic symmetry is the finite group generated by the quarter rotation


R_D=exp[(pi/4)K]


and the reflection q. The generic q_alpha above belongs to that earned finite dihedral group only when alpha is an integer multiple of pi/4. A generic small tilted delta therefore requires a continuous axial/grade rotation not contained in the already-certified finite group.


Moreover, Book 16 proves exact oriented Fujikawa closure for the specific quarter axial rotation R_D and local/vectorlike measure cancellation for the certified q corridor. It does not establish anomaly-free measure invariance for an arbitrary continuous exp(alpha K). Thus q_alpha is presently a representation-level construction, not a promoted quantum symmetry for generic alpha.


8. Exact-q central point versus q-broken tilted family.


Within the already-earned finite primitive symmetry, the generic tilted branch is exchanged with its opposite tilt rather than fixed. The exact q-fixed center is


boxed:
omega_crit=0,
S=0,
r=1,
delta_Z=0,
Z_+=Z_-.


If exact within-vacuum q symmetry and its kinetic protection are retained as nonnegotiable post-soldered boundary data, the current theorem stack therefore selects the central point of the critical hyperbola.


If one permits spontaneous or explicit relative-q breaking, the full tilted one-spin-2 hyperbola remains available and the common G₄ phase selector remains nonzero, but kinetic equality becomes an additional matching condition rather than a symmetry theorem.


9. Updated backward fork.


STRONG EXACT-q BRANCH:
• omega_crit=0;
• scalar-null relative phase;
• common gravitational normalization r=1;
• Z_+=Z_- with the §102 conditional RG protection;
• common G₄ fourth harmonic active independently.


TILTED ONE-SPIN-2 BRANCH:
• omega_crit≠0 and r=cosh omega_crit;
• exact rank-one gravity preserved by co-matching;
• common G₄ harmonic survives unchanged in phase;
• primitive q exchanges ±omega_crit branches;
• delta_Z is generically allowed as an odd function of omega_crit;
• maintaining Z_+=Z_- requires a separate grade-blindness condition or a non-certified vacuum-adapted continuous reflection.


NEXT FINITE BACKWARD GATE:
classify the actual post-soldered interactions by whether their fermion two-point corrections are grade blind. In particular, determine whether every retained operator outside the common G₄ sector contributes to the q-odd kinetic coefficient B, and whether a single common-parent relation can force


boxed:
F_S=F_Z=0


on the tilted critical family.


No continuous axial symmetry, anomaly-free generic q_alpha, full-E8 lift, or physical cosmology is promoted by this section.


§16.I.119 — Three-Contact Oriented-Triangle Kinetic Recoupling


Status. The contact-induced odd-kinetic gate is now reduced from a representation/Fierz question to the microscopic origin and protection of the independent Hermitian 30380 contact coefficient.


Perturbative-order correction. The condensate-dependent kinetic operator


O_{10,S}=S \bar\Psi Z i\!\not D\Psi


is a six-fermion operator before the condensate background is inserted, since S is quartic in the primitive odd fields. Two four-fermion contacts can leave six external fermion legs only through a one-particle-reducible connection. Therefore the first pure-contact 1PI topology capable of generating O_{10,S} is the one-loop triangle built from three Hermitian 30380 contacts. Its coefficient begins at order c_contact^3. This is dimensionally exact: [c_contact^3]=-6, the coefficient dimension required for a dimension-ten operator.


Exact 126 one-leg recoupling. On one D-grade the complex local fermion carrier is 16⊗2, of dimension 32. The physical Lorentz-scalar same-grade pair channel is 126⊗Λ^2(2)≅126. Let P_126 denote the idempotently normalized projector onto that pair channel. Its one-leg partial trace commutes with Spin(10)×Lorentz and therefore equals α I_32. Taking the trace gives 32α=Tr P_126=126, hence


α=126/32=63/16≠0.


Thus the relevant 126 Fierz/recoupling coefficient cannot vanish. A different normalization of the primitive contact rescales the coefficient but cannot turn it into zero.


Quarter-mass grade tensor. With


M_θ=[[0,e^{-iθ}],[e^{+iθ},0]],
D_C=diag(a,b),
a\bar b=(S+iP)/2,


and the charge-conjugated anomalous routing


K_θ=D_C M_{-θ} D_C^† M_θ,


one obtains


K_θ=1/2(S cos2θ-P sin2θ)I + i/2(S sin2θ+P cos2θ)Z.


For the q-fixed mass angle θ=π/4,


K_{π/4}=-P I/2 + i S Z/2.


Hence the quarter-twisted mass sends the q-even relative coordinate P into the common-grade I channel and the q-odd coordinate S into the grade-odd Z channel.


Orientation selection in the 1PI triangle. Let F(p) denote one oriented fermion-loop contribution. Reversing loop orientation complex-conjugates the grade tensor and reverses the external momentum, so the paired contribution has the schematic form


K F(p)+K^† F(-p).


Writing F(p)=F_0+\not p F_1+O(p^2), the scalar term selects K+K^† whereas the kinetic term selects K-K^†. At θ=π/4,


K+K^†=-P I,
K-K^†=i S Z.


Therefore the derivative part of the oriented triangle lies in exactly the required S Z kinetic channel rather than the P channel.


Nonzero momentum coefficient. For the two-mass/one-kinetic numerator routing, the Euclidean vector integral may be reduced to


J_μ(p)=∫d^4k/(2π)^4 (k+p)_μ /[(k^2+m^2)^2((k+p)^2+m^2)].


Feynman parametrization and the shift ℓ=k+(1-x)p give


J_μ(p)=p_μ/(48π^2m^2)+O(p^3).


The two mass numerators supply m^2, so the mass cancels and the derivative form factor is nonzero:


m^2 J_μ(p)=p_μ/(48π^2)+O(p^3).


Combining the direct 126 channel with the oriented grade tensor gives schematically


Γ_tri^(6) ⊃ N_Wick (63/16) c_contact^3/(48π^2) sin(2θ_m) S \bar\Psi Z i\!\not D\Psi,


up to the chosen normalization of O_30380 and the nonzero Wick/symmetry multiplicity N_Wick. At θ_m=π/4, sin(2θ_m)=1.


Theorem 16.I.119.T1 — Three-contact oriented-triangle theorem. In the q-paired Dirac-16 corridor with the q-fixed quarter-angle mass and a nonzero independent Hermitian 30380 contact, the first pure-contact 1PI six-fermion derivative amplitude occurs at order c_contact^3. Its reverse-orientation sum projects the relative condensate scalar S into the D-grade kinetic tensor Z, the direct 126 Fierz coefficient is nonzero, and the momentum form factor is nonzero. Therefore the local operator S \bar\Psi Z i\!\not D\Psi is generated within this retained reduced interaction class whenever c_contact≠0, absent an independently demonstrated cancellation with another operator.


Corollary 16.I.119.C1 — Linear rapidity mismatch. On the rank-one gravity branch S=±P sinh(ω_crit). Near the strong center,


S=±P ω_crit+O(ω_crit^3).


Therefore a nonzero independent contact generates


δ_Z=κ_30380 c_contact^3 P ω_crit+O(ω_crit^3),


with κ_30380 nonzero in the direct 126 channel. Thus the generic odd expansion δ_Z=a_1ω_crit+a_3ω_crit^3+⋯ has a concrete nonzero microscopic source for a_1 whenever c_contact≠0.


Updated inverse ledger. Heavy 10/6 exchange remains grade blind under the retained vertex assumptions: h_S^heavy=0. Direct contact tree and leading one-leg Hermitian contractions remain phase blind in S/P: h_S^(contact,tree)=0 and h_S^(contact,1leg)=0. The 126 Fierz zero is closed negatively: C_126=63/16≠0. The first pure-contact 1PI kinetic recoupling is nonzero at order c_contact^3. Therefore tilted Z_+=Z_- is radiatively natural only if c_contact=0 is itself microscopically protected, or if another common-parent contribution enforces an exact cancellation.


Live gate after §16.I.119. The next calculation is the contact-origin theorem: classify every retained parent mediator and invariant to decide whether the independent Hermitian 30380 norm is exactly absent, finitely generated, or coefficient-locked to another term whose induced kinetic triangle cancels the contribution above. The old Q_Y Ward-protection route remains closed because O_30380 is Q_Y-even. The inherited E8-bracket/N_20 route still gives zero tree contact but does not by itself forbid finite Wilsonian generation of the independent Hermitian contact.




§16.I.120 — Crossed N20 Recoupling and Finite Wilsonian Regeneration of the Physical 8008 Contact


Status. EXACT SPIN(10) CLIFFORD CROSSING IDENTITY; EXACT NONZERO 126 CROSSED RECOUPLING; NONZERO WILSONIAN SHELL SOURCE CONDITIONAL ONLY ON THE RETAINED NONZERO INHERITED N20 ODD-MATTER CURRENT. FULL-E8 ISOTROPIC PACKAGING OF THE GENERATED BROKEN-PHASE OPERATOR AND ITS OVERALL NORMALIZATION REMAIN SEPARATE.


A. Starting point: the tree bracket zero


Section 16.I.94 proves that elimination of the algebraic normal connection gives


ΔS_4F,N=-(1/(2M_N^2))<J_N,h_N^(-1)J_N>,


with anomalous-pair channel operator


Q_N=β_N^† h_N^(-1)β_N.


Because the physical 8008 lies in ker β,


β_N P_8008=0,


and therefore


Q_N P_8008=0.


Thus Δc_contact^(N20 tree)=0 remains exact. The question here is whether this kernel is preserved after crossed fermion recoupling at the next Wilsonian order.


B. Physical Spin(10) Clifford form


On the physical same-grade Dirac-16 corridor, one normal internal ten acts through the chiral Clifford intertwiners


Γ^I:16→16bar,
barΓ^I:16bar→16,
I=1,…,10,


normalized by


barΓ^I Γ^J+barΓ^J Γ^I=2δ^{IJ}I.


The one-exchange pair map is


X:=Σ_I Γ^I⊗Γ^I.


On the symmetric same-grade pair space


Sym^2(16)=10⊕126,


the physical condensate occupies the 126. Since the corresponding target contains 126bar rather than 126, equivalently by the already-earned bracket-kernel theorem,


X P_126=0.


C. Direct versus crossed two-normal recoupling


Define the direct two-exchange operator


D_N:=X^†X
=Σ_{I,J}(barΓ^IΓ^J)⊗(barΓ^IΓ^J),


and the crossed operator


C_N:=Σ_{I,J}(barΓ^IΓ^J)⊗(barΓ^JΓ^I).


The Clifford relation gives


barΓ^JΓ^I=2δ^{IJ}I-barΓ^IΓ^J.


Therefore


C_N
=20I-D_N.


Since D_N P_126=X^†X P_126=0,


boxed:
P_126 C_N P_126=20 P_126.


Thus the same Clifford structure whose direct one-exchange projection vanishes has an exactly nonzero crossed two-exchange recoupling on the physical 126. The number 20 is in the canonical Clifford normalization; any allowed rescaling changes the overall coefficient but not the nonzero result.


Theorem 16.I.120.T1 — Crossed-N20 recoupling theorem. The inherited N20 bracket kernel is stable under direct repeated exchange but not under crossed two-exchange recoupling. On the physical same-grade 126 channel the direct operator vanishes and the crossed operator acts as 20 times the identity.


D. Lorentz-scalar Cooper projection is nonzero


The N20 connection is a one-form and its odd-matter coupling is vector-current type. In two-component notation the exact Weyl identity


barσ^μ_(dotα α) barσ_{μ(dotβ β)}=2 ε_(dotα dotβ) ε_(αβ)


shows that the crossed vector-current contraction has a nonzero Lorentz-scalar Cooper component. For the two internal Weyl propagators,


ε^{αβ}(k·σ)_(α dotα)(k·σ)_(β dotβ)=k^2 ε_(dotα dotβ)


up to the conventional overall signature sign. Hence the scalar pair projection of the crossed bubble carries the nonvanishing radial integrand


k^2/(k^2+m^2)^2.


E. Wilsonian shell coefficient


For a Euclidean momentum shell μ<|k|<Λ define


I_shell(m;Λ,μ)
:=∫_shell d^4k/(2π)^4 k^2/(k^2+m^2)^2.


Direct integration gives


I_shell
=1/(16π^2)[
(Λ^2-μ^2)
-2m^2 ln((Λ^2+m^2)/(μ^2+m^2))
+m^4(1/(μ^2+m^2)-1/(Λ^2+m^2))
].


For Λ>μ this is nonzero, and in the high-scale massless limit


I_shell(0;Λ,μ)=(Λ^2-μ^2)/(16π^2).


Let G_N denote the canonically normalized coefficient of the N20 current-current operator after eliminating A_N; schematically G_N~g_N^2/M_N^2, with the positive selector-Cartan metric weights included. The crossed one-loop Wilsonian matching therefore contains


boxed:
Δc_8008^(N20,1-loop)
= N_N ·20·G_N^2·I_shell,


where N_N is the nonzero convention-dependent Lorentz/Wick/normalization factor. Residual Spin(10) covariance and positivity of h_N prevent the ten-dimensional metric contraction from annihilating the result. Thus


boxed:
Δc_8008^(N20,1-loop)≠0


whenever the inherited N20 odd-matter coupling is nonzero.


At a matching scale Λ~M_N with m,μ≪M_N,


Δc_8008^(N20,1-loop)
~N_N ·20/(16π^2) · g_N^4/M_N^2,


up to the normalization already absorbed into G_N.


Theorem 16.I.120.T2 — Selector-broken Wilsonian contact regeneration. In the spectrum-safe 45+20 completion with both microscopic odd grades retained and a nonzero inherited N20 matter current, the exact tree-level bracket zero does not define an all-orders Wilsonian c_contact=0 surface. The first crossed two-N20 recoupling generates a nonzero local Hermitian contact on the physical 8008/126 corridor at one fermion loop. The generated broken-phase operator need not be the full isotropic E8 Hermitian norm away from the physical corridor; its nonzero 8008 projection is sufficient for the Book-16 scalar/contact and odd-kinetic gates.


F. Symmetry audit


The generated Hermitian pair norm is Q_Y-even, so the primitive product-duality Ward symmetry cannot forbid it. It is also neutral under the accidental reduced vector U(1)_F because C and C^† carry opposite charges. The already-closed fermion-measure anomaly gate supplies no compensating anomaly selection rule. Therefore no presently earned reduced symmetry forces the crossed coefficient above to vanish.


G. Consequence for the odd-kinetic gate


Section 16.I.119 proves that any nonzero physical Hermitian contact supplies a nonzero three-contact oriented-triangle route


S→Z i\!\not D.


Combining the two sections gives the certified cascade


N20 selector coupling
→ crossed one-loop Δc_8008≠0
→ three-contact oriented triangle
→ h_S≠0
→ δ_Z∝S on the tilted branch.


This cascade proves nonzero radiative communication; it is not asserted to be the lowest perturbative order for an N20-assisted kinetic mismatch. A direct N20-assisted six-fermion derivative graph may occur earlier and is the next finite calculation.


Updated contact-origin ledger. Minimal adjoint exchange: exact tree zero. Algebraic N20 exchange: exact tree zero. Declared S_82 B/selector parent: no independent 30380 self-potential at tree level. N20 crossed Wilsonian recoupling: NONZERO on the physical 8008 at one fermion loop. Therefore c_contact=0 is not radiatively natural in the retained selector-completed two-grade theory without an additional cancellation, vanishing N20 matter coupling, or a microscopic mechanism that removes the relevant mirror-grade channel before this threshold.


Live gate after §16.I.120. Compute the lowest-order direct N20-assisted contribution to S \barΨ Z i\!\not D Ψ and compare it with the indirect contact cascade. If it is nonzero, the selector completion itself supplies the leading microscopic kinetic-mismatch source. If it vanishes, classify whether the vanishing is an exact grade/Clifford theorem or only a topology-specific cancellation; the indirect §16.I.120→§16.I.119 route remains nonzero regardless.




§16.I.121 — Direct N20-Assisted Odd-Kinetic Recoupling and the Selector–Fermion Compatibility Gate


Status. EXACT NONZERO FULLY CROSSED INTERNAL 126 COEFFICIENT; EXACT NONZERO LORENTZ-SCALAR CYCLIC COEFFICIENT; EXACT QUARTER-MASS S→Z PROJECTION; NONZERO DIRECT N20-ASSISTED KINETIC MISMATCH CONDITIONAL ONLY ON A NONZERO INHERITED N20 ODD-MATTER COUPLING AND NONZERO q-FIXED DIRAC MASS. OVERALL VERTEX/WICK NORMALIZATION REMAINS CONVENTION-DEPENDENT.


A. Minimal 1PI topology and axial-spurion counting


The target operator is


O_{10,S}=S \barΨ Z i\!\not D Ψ.


Before condensate insertion, S is quartic in the primitive odd fields, so the target is a six-fermion derivative operator. For V four-fermion N20 current-current vertices and six external fermions,


I=2V-3,
L=I-V+1=V-2.


Thus V=2 is one-particle reducible and the first 1PI topology has


V=3, I=3, L=1.


The massless N20 current vertex is compatible with the reduced axial spurion generated by gamma_5. The relative condensate coordinate a\bar b carries axial charge four, so two equal-orientation q-fixed mass insertions are the minimum spurion content needed to neutralize the linear S/P channel. Therefore the first direct N20-assisted candidate has schematic order


G_N^3 m^2 S,


with three N20 current-current vertices and two quarter-mass numerator insertions.


B. Internal fully crossed coefficient


Let


A_{IJ}:=barΓ_I Γ_J


on the chiral Spin(10) 16 and define the fully crossed cyclic operator


T_N^(3)=Σ_{I,J,K} A_{IJ}⊗A_{JK}⊗A_{KI}.


Project the first pair onto the physical 126 and define


B_{IK}=Σ_J Tr_{16⊗16}[P_126(A_{IJ}⊗A_{JK})].


Using P_126=P_sym-P_10 and the canonical chiral Spin(10) traces gives


B_{IK}^{sym}=208 δ_{IK},
B_{IK}^{10}=-44 δ_{IK},


hence


boxed:
B_{IK}=252 δ_{IK}.


Closing the remaining normal index gives the unnormalized partial trace


Tr_{12}[(P_126⊗I)T_N^(3)]=2520 I_16.


Dividing by dim 126 gives the normalized invariant coefficient


boxed:
C_{N,int}^{(3)}=20.


The reverse cyclic orientation gives the same real coefficient. The mixed/direct routings reduce to the already-earned tree-kernel structure and vanish on P_126. Hence no cancellation occurs between the two surviving cyclic orientations.


Theorem 16.I.121.T1 — Fully crossed three-N20 internal theorem. On the physical same-grade 126 pair channel, the first 1PI cyclic three-N20 recoupling is nonzero and acts with canonical normalized coefficient 20. The only routings killed by the §16.I.94 bracket kernel are the direct/mixed contractions; cyclic and anticyclic crossed contractions survive.


C. Lorentz-scalar cyclic coefficient


On the Weyl two-spinor define


L_{μν}=barσ_μ σ_ν


and let P_0=P_{Λ^2 2} project the first pair onto the antisymmetric Lorentz scalar. Then


Σ_ν Tr_{2⊗2}[P_0(L_{μν}⊗L_{νρ})]
=-2 δ_{μρ}.


After the canonical normalization of the scalar line, the cyclic Lorentz coefficient is


boxed:
C_{N,Lor}^{(3)}=-8,


up to the overall signature convention. Its nonvanishing is convention-independent.


Therefore the factorized physical 126⊗Λ^2 2 channel has


boxed:
C_{N,cyc}^{(3)}=20(-8)=-160≠0.


D. Quarter-mass grade projection


The q-fixed mass is


L_{m,q}=m \barΨ exp[-i(pi/4)gamma_5] Ψ.


With a\bar b=(S+iP)/2, two equal-oriented quarter-mass insertions produce the previously established grade tensor


K_{pi/4}=-P I/2+i S Z/2.


Reverse orientation of the derivative amplitude selects the anti-Hermitian difference,


K-K^†=i S Z.


Thus the surviving -160 cyclic channel lands exactly in


S \barΨ Z i\!\not D Ψ.


E. Momentum form factor and Wilsonian threshold behavior


For the two-mass/one-kinetic routing, the full Euclidean derivative integral satisfies


F_1^{full}=1/(48 pi^2 m^2),


so


m^2 F_1^{full}=1/(48 pi^2).


For a Wilsonian shell μ<|k|<Λ,


I_3^{shell}=∫_shell d^4k/(2pi)^4 1/(k^2+m^2)^3


=1/(16 pi^2)[
1/(μ^2+m^2)-1/(Λ^2+m^2)
+(m^2/2)(1/(Λ^2+m^2)^2-1/(μ^2+m^2)^2)
].


The derivative routing carries


m^2 F_{1,shell}=(2m^2/3) I_3^{shell}.


For m<<μ<Λ,


m^2 F_{1,shell}
≈m^2/(24 pi^2)(1/μ^2-1/Λ^2),


so the UV shell contribution vanishes as m→0, as required by the axial-spurion counting. The finite full 1/(48pi^2) coefficient emerges only after the Wilsonian flow reaches the fermion-mass scale.


F. Direct kinetic coefficient


For one canonically normalized ordered cyclic routing,


h_{S,N}^{cyc}
=N_vert (-160) G_N^3/(48 pi^2)
=-N_vert [10/(3 pi^2)] G_N^3,


where N_vert is the nonzero convention-dependent vertex/Wick normalization. Therefore


boxed:
h_S^{N,direct}≠0


whenever the retained N20 odd-matter coupling and q-fixed mass are nonzero.


Theorem 16.I.121.T2 — Direct selector-induced odd-kinetic theorem. The spectrum-safe N20 selector completion directly generates the q-odd kinetic operator S \barΨ Z i\!\not D Ψ at the first allowed 1PI three-current-current topology. No internal 126 Fierz zero, Lorentz scalar zero, orientation zero, or momentum-form-factor zero remains.


G. Relative order versus the indirect contact cascade


With G_N~g_N^2/M_N^2,


h_S^{N,direct}~g_N^6/M_N^6,


whereas the crossed one-loop generated contact scales schematically as


c_8008^N~g_N^4/(16pi^2 M_N^2),


and feeding that contact through the §16.I.119 three-contact triangle begins parametrically at order g_N^12/M_N^6 times additional loop factors. Thus the direct N20 route is the leading selector-induced kinetic mismatch at weak coupling.


H. Rapidity consequence


On the rank-one gravity branch,


S=±P sinh(omega_crit)
=±P omega_crit+O(omega_crit^3).


Hence


boxed:
delta_Z^N=a_1^N omega_crit+O(omega_crit^3),
a_1^N≠0,


for nonzero N20 matter coupling and q-fixed mass. The strong center S=0 remains automatically immune.


I. Selector–fermion compatibility gate


Protecting c_contact=0 is no longer sufficient. Even an exact ultraviolet absence of the independent Hermitian 30380 contact does not remove the direct N20-assisted mismatch. The retained two-grade selector completion is compatible with tilted Z_+=Z_- only if at least one stronger condition is derived:


1. the physical N20 odd-matter coupling vanishes;
2. the relevant mirror-grade channel is removed before the N20 threshold;
3. an exact common-parent contribution cancels the nonzero direct coefficient.


The heavy 10/6 channel cannot provide this cancellation under the retained assumptions because its odd-kinetic coefficient remains zero. Primitive Q_Y cannot forbid the operator because S and Z are both q-odd, so their product is q-even. The accidental vector U(1)_F also does not forbid the mass-dressed neutral operator.


Live gate after §16.I.121. Determine whether the N20 odd-matter coupling is compulsory in the inherited selector-covariant derivative. If it is fixed nonzero by the same parent connection that supplies the spectrum-safe 45+20 completion, the tilted two-grade branch is microscopically incompatible with exact Z_+=Z_- unless mirror removal occurs before the N20 threshold or a new coefficient-locked cancellation is derived. If the N20 matter current can be consistently projected out while retaining the Spin(10) gauge coupling, selector BRST structure, and q-paired mass corridor, construct that reduced covariant derivative explicitly and audit its closure.




§16.I.122 — No Selective N20 Decoupling, No Declared Mirror-Mass Escape, and the Tilted Exact-QY Trilemma


Status. EXACT LIE-ALGEBRA NO-GO FOR SELECTIVELY REMOVING THE N20 MATTER ACTION FROM THE INHERITED SELECTOR-COVARIANT DERIVATIVE WHILE RETAINING NONTRIVIAL SPIN(10) CHARGE; EXACT CURRENT-LEDGER NO-GO FOR LIFTING ONLY ONE MIRROR GRADE BY A DECLARED SPIN(10)-SINGLET BILINEAR; REDUCED POST-GAUGE SPIN(10)-ONLY DERIVATIVE REMAINS LAWFUL AS A DIFFERENT EFFECTIVE THEORY BUT CANNOT ERASE WILSONIAN THRESHOLDS ALREADY GENERATED BY THE INHERITED PARENT.


A. Selector algebra and inherited matter derivative


The spectrum-safe selector completion has the exact Lie algebra


g_G ≅ so(11,1)
=so(10)⊕N_20⊕R D


as a vector-space decomposition, with N_20=(U∧a)⊕(U∧b), U≅R^10, and D=a∧b. Before unitary selector gauge the odd matter corridor uses the inherited selector-covariant derivative


D_A Ψ=dΨ+[A,Ψ].


Thus A_N acts on the paired odd bundle through the same adjoint representation as the retained Spin(10) connection.


B. Exact bracket generation


Choose an orthonormal internal basis e_i and normal basis a,b with a^2=+1, b^2=-1, <a,b>=0. Define


P_i=e_i∧a,
K_i=e_i∧b,
J_{ij}=e_i∧e_j,
D=a∧b.


The orthogonal bivector bracket gives, up to the fixed overall sign convention,


[P_i,P_j]=-J_{ij},
[K_i,K_j]=+J_{ij},
[P_i,K_j]=-δ_{ij}D.


Hence


boxed:
[N_20,N_20]=so(10)⊕R D


in the sense that the brackets span the entire indicated subspace. Together with [so(10),N_20]⊂N_20 and [D,N_20]⊂N_20, the minimal Lie closure is all of so(11,1).


C. Exact no-selective-decoupling theorem


Let rho be any nontrivial representation of g_G on the paired odd matter carrier which retains the Spin(10) action. Suppose rho(N_20)=0. Then for X,Y∈N_20,


rho([X,Y])=[rho(X),rho(Y)]=0.


Because [N_20,N_20] spans so(10)⊕R D, this forces rho(so(10))=rho(D)=0. Therefore rho is trivial on the complete Lie closure, contradicting retained nontrivial Spin(10) matter charge.


Theorem 16.I.122.T1 — No selective N20 decoupling. Within the inherited selector-covariant g_G matter representation, it is impossible to set the N20 matter action to zero while retaining a nontrivial Spin(10) gauge action.


D. Relative-rescaling rigidity


Let phi_s act identically on so(10)⊕R D and rescale N20 by s. Bracket preservation gives s^2=1 from [P_i,P_j], hence only s=±1 is allowed. The sign is the symmetric-pair involution; s=0 is not a Lie-algebra homomorphism. There is no continuous selector-covariant limit in which the N20 matter coupling vanishes while the Spin(10) coupling remains finite.


E. BRST form


Before unitary selector gauge, truncating the fermion transformation to the Spin(10) ghost while retaining the full selector ghosts yields a BRST square containing rho(P_so10[c_N,c_N])Ψ. Because [N20,N20] has a nonzero so(10) component, this does not vanish for Spin(10)-charged fermions. A Spin(10)-only derivative is consistent only after passing to the genuinely reduced residual theory, not as the same selector-covariant parent representation.


F. Post-gauge reduction does not erase thresholds


Section 16.I.103 certifies a minimal reduced Spin(10) fermion derivative compatible with q. But if that reduced derivative is obtained by integrating out/gauge-fixing the inherited algebraic N20 sector, the Wilsonian thresholds already generated remain. Sections 16.I.120–121 give those nonzero local thresholds explicitly. Avoiding them requires changing the ultraviolet parent coupling assignment before the N20 threshold; it is not a field-redefinition or gauge-choice escape.


G. No declared mirror-only singlet mass


The internal product


16⊗16=10_s⊕120_a⊕126_s


contains no singlet. Therefore one chiral Spin(10) 16 has no gauge-invariant bilinear Majorana mass. The declared q-fixed singlet mass pairs the conjugate/chiral grades in the Dirac corridor and cannot lift only one grade. Book 16 also contains no fully declared independent Yukawa with a named bosonic representation and fixed intertwiner that could provide a selective mirror mass.


Theorem 16.I.122.T2 — Declared-sector mirror-mass no-go. Within the current Spin(10)-preserving reduced odd action, there is no available local gauge-singlet bilinear or declared Yukawa mechanism that removes only one D-grade mirror fermion before the N20 threshold.


H. Tilted exact-QY trilemma


Section 16.I.102 proves that the exact unit-normalized pair quotient Q_Y survives canonical fermion normalization iff delta_Z=0. The tilted branch permits delta_Z as an odd function of omega_crit, while §16.I.121 gives delta_Z^N=a_1^N omega_crit+O(omega_crit^3) with a_1^N≠0 for nonzero inherited N20 matter coupling and q-fixed mass.


The two obvious removal routes are closed within the declared inherited theory: N20 decoupling is incompatible with the selector-covariant so(11,1) matter representation, and mirror-only mass removal is unavailable in the declared Spin(10)-preserving odd sector. Therefore a tilted branch can retain exact unit-normalized Q_Y only if another common-parent contribution cancels the selector-induced odd kinetic coefficient order by order. Absent such cancellation, the exact-QY fixed solution is the strong center omega_crit=0, S=0, delta_Z=0, Z_+=Z_-.


This is not a no-go for the tilted one-spin-2 branch itself. That branch remains lawful with mirror-covariant delta_Z(-omega)=-delta_Z(omega). What fails without cancellation is simultaneous tilted vacuum, inherited selector-covariant matter coupling, and exact within-vacuum unit-normalized Q_Y.


Live gate after §16.I.122. Audit the remaining common-parent cancellation possibility at the first nonzero order G_N^3. Enumerate every declared interaction capable of contributing to the same dimension-ten S \barΨ Z i\!\not D Ψ operator with the same coupling and threshold order. If no independent declared term exists at that order, the nonzero §16.I.121 coefficient is the leading Wilsonian matching invariant and the exact-QY tilted cancellation route closes negatively within the current action ledger. If another term exists, derive its coefficient from the same parent normalization rather than tuning an unrelated Wilson coefficient.




§16.I.123 — Exact Gaussian N20 Elimination and the Leading-Coefficient Cancellation No-Go


Status. EXACT WITHIN THE DECLARED SPECTRUM-SAFE N20 SELECTOR/ODD-MATTER SUBSECTOR; NO CLAIM THAT AN UNDECLARED OR INDEPENDENTLY COEFFICIENT-LOCKED PARENT SECTOR CANNOT ADD A SEPARATE CANCELLING TERM.


The spectrum-safe N20 selector term contains no derivative on A_N. After fixing/eliminating the contractible selector angle, its matter-dependent local action is Gaussian,


L_N=(M_N^2/2)<A_N,h_N A_N>+<A_N,J_N>,


so


A_N=-M_N^{-2}h_N^{-1}J_N,
Delta L_N=-1/(2M_N^2)<J_N,h_N^{-1}J_N>.


The A_N determinant is fermion-current independent. Hence the complete local fermionic memory of the N20 selector field in this subsector is the single exact current-current kernel. There is no hidden second N20-sector matter vertex whose coefficient could cancel its Wilsonian consequences.


Let G_N be its normalized coefficient. The first pure-N20 1PI six-fermion derivative term is cubic in G_N, and §16.I.121 proves the coefficient of S \barPsi Z i\!\not D Psi is nonzero. Therefore the pure G_N^3 Taylor coefficient is unique and nonzero within this Gaussian subsector. Any exact cancellation must come from another microscopic sector whose coefficient is tied to G_N by an independently derived common-parent identity.


The current ledger supplies no such certified relation: Spin(10) gauge dynamics, the independent Hermitian contact, S82 B/current structures, broader PCBF C^2B^2 candidates, and any future Yukawa carry separate or presently open coefficients. q/Q_Y and U(1)_F permit the target operator. Thus no currently earned common-parent identity cancels the nonzero pure G_N^3 coefficient.


Live gate: propagate the nonzero h_S into the exact two-grade kinetic matrix rather than assuming a linear rapidity transport.




§16.I.124 — Nonlinear Kinetic-Rapidity Map, Positivity Bound, and No Constant-Transport Absorption


Status. EXACT WITHIN THE LEADING LOCAL KINETIC TRUNCATION A I+h_S S Z; HIGHER ODD KINETIC OPERATORS CAN MODIFY THE GLOBAL FUNCTION BUT NOT THE NONZERO LINEAR SOURCE.


Let A_0>0 be the common grade-even kinetic coefficient and retain


Z_eff=A_0 I+h_S S Z.


Then


Z_+=A_0+h_S S,
Z_-=A_0-h_S S,


delta_Z=(1/2)ln(Z_+/Z_-)
=artanh(h_S S/A_0)


whenever both eigenvalues are positive.


On the rank-one branch S=s P sinh(omega_crit), define eta_N=s h_S P/A_0. Then


boxed:
delta_Z(omega_crit)=artanh[eta_N sinh(omega_crit)].


Near the strong center,


delta_Z=eta_N omega_crit+[eta_N/6+eta_N^3/3]omega_crit^3+O(omega_crit^5).


Hence a_1=eta_N and a_3=eta_N/6+eta_N^3/3 in this leading truncation.


Positivity requires


|eta_N sinh(omega_crit)|<1,


so for eta_N neq 0,


|omega_crit|<arsinh(1/|eta_N|).


At the boundary one kinetic eigenvalue vanishes and |delta_Z| diverges.


A constant transport ansatz delta_Z=nu omega_crit cannot reproduce this nonzero selector map. Matching the linear term gives nu=eta_N; vanishing of the cubic deviation would require eta_N/6+eta_N^3/3=0, whose only real solution is eta_N=0. Therefore the only exact constant-weight rapidity transport is the trivial zero-mismatch case.


Canonical normalization gives q_can=exp(delta_Z Z)q and pair action ±exp(2delta_Z Z_N)Q_Y. With x=eta_N sinh omega_crit,


exp(2delta_Z)=(1+x)/(1-x),


so the normal lines receive reciprocal Cayley dressings (1+x)/(1-x) and (1-x)/(1+x). This is distinct from unit-normalized Q_Y unless x=0.


The tilted one-spin-2 branch is not thereby falsified; it naturally carries a separate nonlinear q-odd kinetic coordinate. The strong center is the unique point where the selector-induced dressing vanishes automatically.


Live gate after §16.I.124. Audit pole counting and canonical mass mixing for the q-fixed two-grade Dirac system with Z_+ neq Z_- and determine whether the mismatch creates an additional fermion pole, merely renormalizes the existing Dirac pole, or obstructs any proposed low-energy mirror removal.




§16.I.125 — Paired Dirac Pole Theorem, Kinetic Hyperbola, and the Failure of Mismatch-Driven Mirror Removal


Status. EXACT FOR THE q-FIXED TWO-GRADE DIRAC QUADRATIC OPERATOR WITH POSITIVE Z_±; NO ADDITIONAL FERMION POLE IS GENERATED BY KINETIC MISMATCH.


For one internal Spin(10) component the inverse Weyl operator is


D(p)=
[[ Z_+ p·barsigma, -m e^{-i theta}],
 [ -m e^{+i theta}, Z_- p·sigma ]],


theta=pi/4.


Using (p·barsigma)(p·sigma)=p^2I gives


boxed:
det D(p)=[Z_+Z_-p^2-m^2]^2.


Thus unequal grade kinetic coefficients produce one paired Dirac pole only,


m_phys=m/sqrt(Z_+Z_-)=m/bar Z,


and the quarter mass phase cancels from the pole equation.


Canonical normalization hat psi_±=sqrt(Z_±)psi_± leaves a standard Dirac quadratic operator with mass m/bar Z. The relative mismatch remains physical relative to the earned q/Q_Y composite structure but is not a second free-particle pole.


For A=(Z_++Z_-)/2, B=(Z_+-Z_-)/2,


A=bar Z cosh(delta_Z),
B=bar Z sinh(delta_Z),
A^2-B^2=bar Z^2.


Hence the kinetic coefficients possess their own exact rapidity hyperbola without implying delta_Z=omega_crit.


In the leading selector truncation A=A_0, B=h_SS, x=h_SS/A_0=tanh delta_Z,


bar Z=A_0sqrt(1-x^2)=A_0 sech delta_Z,


so


m_phys=(m/A_0)cosh delta_Z.


On x=eta_N sinh omega_crit,


m_phys=(m/A_0)[1-eta_N^2sinh^2 omega_crit]^{-1/2},


which is mirror even. Near the center the first correction is +(eta_N^2/2)omega_crit^2.


As |x|→1 one kinetic eigenvalue vanishes, bar Z→0, |delta_Z|→∞, and for nonzero q-fixed mass the only Dirac pole is pushed to infinite mass. The algebraic grade then removes its mass-coupled partner rather than leaving a finite single chiral pole. Thus kinetic mismatch cannot solve mirror removal by itself.


If m=0 exactly, one chiral grade could remain at the kinetic boundary, but the selector-induced odd mismatch mechanism itself uses the q-fixed mass as axial-breaking spurion and vanishes in the strict massless ultraviolet limit. Therefore that route is not self-consistent within the same perturbative mechanism.


Live gate: canonically normalize the interaction vertices and determine whether delta_Z produces physically unequal N20/composite couplings even though it creates no extra free pole.




§16.I.126 — Canonical Interaction Dressing, N20 Current Neutrality, and the Noninvariance of Naive Pair Balance


For cal Z=bar Z exp(delta_Z Z), define hat Psi=bar Z^(1/2)exp[(delta_Z/2)Z]Psi. Every N20 generator anticommutes with Z, so


exp[-delta_Z Z/2] T_N exp[-delta_Z Z/2]=T_N.


Hence


J_N(Psi)=bar Z^(-1)J_N(hat Psi),
G_N^can=G_N/bar Z^2.


There is no residual exp(±delta_Z) splitting inside the off-diagonal N20 current. The q-fixed mass behaves the same way and becomes m/bar Z with its quarter phases unchanged. The residual Spin(10) gauge derivative remains canonically normalized because its generator commutes with Z and the same kinetic matrix multiplies derivative and gauge vertex.


Same-grade pair operators do feel the mismatch:


C_+=Z_+^(-1)hat C_+,
C_-=Z_-^(-1)hat C_-.


If a,b are their expectation amplitudes, hat a=Z_+a and hat b=Z_-b, so


hat S=bar Z^2S,
hat P=bar Z^2P,


and S/P plus the scalar-null condition are invariant up to common scale. But separate pair intensities are not invariant. On an originally equal-amplitude slice,


hat Z_B/hat N=tanh(2delta_Z).


Thus naive equal-amplitude balance depends on field normalization once delta_Zneq0. The strong center is the unique point where bare and canonical pair balance coincide.

§16.I.127 — Pair-Intensity Rapidity, the Normalization-Invariant Combination Upsilon_B, and the Kinetic-Feedback Equation


For pair amplitudes a,b define N=|a|^2+|b|^2, Z_B=|a|^2-|b|^2, S=2Re(a conjugate(b)), P=2Im(a conjugate(b)). Then N^2-Z_B^2=S^2+P^2. Let z_B=Z_B/N and y_B=artanh(z_B)=(1/2)ln(|a|^2/|b|^2).


Under a positive grade rescaling Psi_+→e^{lambda/2}Psi_+, Psi_-→e^{-lambda/2}Psi_-, the pair amplitudes transform a→e^lambda a, b→e^{-lambda}b. Therefore (N,Z_B) undergo a 1+1 boost of rapidity 2lambda,


z_B'=(z_B+tanh2lambda)/(1+z_B tanh2lambda),
y_B'=y_B+2lambda.


The kinetic rapidity transforms oppositely, delta_Z'=delta_Z-lambda. Hence


boxed:
Upsilon_B:=y_B+2delta_Z


is exactly normalization invariant.


Bare equal amplitude y_B=0 becomes canonical hat y_B=2delta_Z; canonical equal amplitude is the invariant condition Upsilon_B=0, not the coordinate condition Z_B=0. The strong center is the unique point where all notions coincide without compensating imbalance.


A covariant local mismatch expansion should therefore be organized using projective phase data and Upsilon_B rather than raw Z_B alone. Near a fixed radial background one may write delta_Z=A_s s+A_y Upsilon_B+... . On a bare-equal coordinate branch Upsilon_B=2delta_Z, giving


delta_Z=[A_s/(1-2A_y)]s+...


when 1-2A_yneq0. The surface 2A_y=1 is a possible kinetic susceptibility criticality, but A_y is not yet microscopically known.


A minimal nonlinear diagnostic is delta_Z=A_s s+lambda_B tanh(2delta_Z). At zero scalar tilt, nonzero mirror-paired kinetic solutions can bifurcate only if the slope condition 2lambda_B>1 is met for positive lambda_B. This remains conditional.


Prior results depending only on S/P, phase, scalar-nullity, or rank-one S/P relations survive canonical normalization. Results depending separately on Z_B=0 must be restated in terms of Upsilon_B.


Live gate: compute the canonical linear response coefficient A_y from the retained N20/contact interactions.




§16.I.128 — Massless N20 Intensity-Feedback Triangle and the Nonzero Canonical A_y Coefficient


The q-odd intensity coordinate Z_B is neutral under the axial bookkeeping that forced two q-fixed mass insertions in the S channel. Therefore the first three-current-current N20 1PI topology can generate


O_{10,Z}=Z_B \barPsi Z i\!\not D Psi


without mass insertions. Since G_N^3 already has dimension -6, the derivative loop is logarithmic.


The pure + same-grade physical 126 background inherits the nonzero fully crossed internal coefficient 20 of §16.I.121 and closes on the same one-fermion carrier; q supplies the mirror - result. Their sum decomposes into a common NI kinetic term plus a nonzero grade-odd Z_B Z term. Hence the canonical intensity-feedback channel is not representation-zero.


The universal vector shell integral reduces to


I_mu(p)=∫_shell d^4k/(2pi)^4 (k+p)_mu/[k^2(k+p)^2]
=p_mu/(16pi^2)ln(Lambda/mu)+O(p^3).


Therefore


h_{Z_B,N}(mu)
=N_y [20G_N^3/(32pi^2)]ln(Lambda^2/mu^2)+finite,


where N_y is the nonzero convention-dependent Lorentz/Wick factor. The signed normalization is not yet fixed.


In a canonical fermion scheme, Upsilon_B=y_B at the matching point and Z_B=N_*y_B+O(y_B^3), so


A_y^N(mu)
=(N_*/A_0)h_{Z_B,N}(mu)
=N_y[20N_*G_N^3/(32pi^2A_0)]ln(Lambda^2/mu^2)+... neq0.


Thus the normalization-invariant intensity susceptibility A_y is nonzero already at order G_N^3. In a perturbative regime where the loop expansion parameter is small, |A_y|<<1, so the feedback cannot generically reach the critical surface 2A_y=1; it only renormalizes the already-nonzero S susceptibility. A kinetic bifurcation would require strong coupling, a large logarithm, or additional coherent sectors.


The remaining finite gate is the signed Wick/current-current audit. A_y<0 is stabilizing, A_y>0 enhances the mismatch, and only sufficiently large positive A_y can approach the 1/2 threshold.




§16.I.129 — Signed N20 Intensity Feedback: Positive A_y in the Canonical Euclidean Convention


Freeze the declared spectrum-safe N20 parent in Euclidean signature with positive selector metric h_N and positive algebraic mass M_N^2:


S_E,N=∫[(M_N^2/2)<A_N,h_NA_N>+g_N<A_N,J_N>].


The A_N propagator is positive, while the order-G_N^3 six-fermion 1PI graph contains one closed fermion cycle and therefore one universal Grassmann minus sign. The fully crossed physical 126 x Lambda^2(2) cyclic tensor has the already-derived canonical coefficients C_int=+20 and C_Lor=-8, hence C_cyc=-160. The logarithmic shell coefficient is positive for Lambda>mu.


Therefore


(-1)_F x (-160)=+160,


and all remaining factors are positive. Thus


boxed:
h_{Z_B,N}>0,
A_y^N>0.


Writing n_y>0 for the positive combinatorial multiplicity,


A_y^N(mu)=n_y[20N_*G_N^3/(32pi^2A_0)]ln(Lambda^2/mu^2)+...>0,


with G_N interpreted as the positive Gaussian exchange magnitude g_N^2/M_N^2.


Hence the N20 intensity feedback amplifies rather than damps the existing S-induced kinetic mismatch. The direct S and intensity channels inherit the same loop/cyclic orientation and do not self-cancel.


Neglecting running of the prefactors over a narrow perturbative interval, A_y increases toward the infrared and the linear susceptibility chi_kin=1/(1-2A_y) is enhanced. This does not prove the threshold 2A_y=1 is reached; in the controlled weak-coupling regime A_y<<1.

§16.I.130 — Positive-Feedback Fixed Points, Pitchfork Threshold, and the Pair-Balance Firewall


For the minimal positive-feedback model


delta=h+a tanh(2delta),
with h=A_s s and a=A_y>0, define


V(delta)=1/2 delta^2-(a/2)ln cosh(2delta)-h delta.


Then V'=0 is the fixed-point equation and V''=1-2a sech^2(2delta) gives local stability.


At h=0, if a<=1/2 the unique solution is delta=0; it is strictly stable for a<1/2 and marginal-quartically-stable at a=1/2. If a>1/2, delta=0 becomes unstable and exactly one stable mirror pair ±delta_* appears, with delta_*=a tanh(2delta_*). Near threshold,


delta_*^2=3(2a-1)/(8a)+... .


For nonzero h and a>1/2 the pitchfork is imperfect. The spinodal coordinate is delta_s=(1/2)arcosh sqrt(2a), with t_s=sqrt(1-1/(2a)) and source magnitude h_c=a t_s-delta_s. For |h|<h_c there are two minima and one unstable maximum; |h|>h_c leaves one minimum.


Below threshold the linear response is delta=h/(1-2a)+..., so positive A_y enhances the kinetic susceptibility.


If a(mu)=C_N ln(Lambda^2/mu^2) with frozen prefactors, the formal a=1/2 scale is mu_c=Lambda exp[-1/(4C_N)]. This is only a leading-log diagnostic, not a prediction.


Crucial firewall: this feedback equation is physical only if microscopic pair dynamics actually pin a noninvariant pair coordinate such as y_B in the primitive-q frame. If instead the invariant physical balance condition is Upsilon_B=y_B+2delta_Z=0, the intensity source vanishes on that branch and A_y does not autonomously create a kinetic bifurcation. Rank-one gravity constrains S/P but does not itself choose either pair-balance condition.


Live gate: derive the pair-intensity equation of motion from the declared condensate/selector action and determine what, if anything, pins y_B or Upsilon_B.




§16.I.131 — Reciprocal Pair-Scaling Flat Direction, Positive Hermitian Contact Pinning, and the Conditional Equal-Amplitude Theorem


For nonzero pair amplitudes define rho=|a||b| and y_B=ln(|a|/|b|). Then |a|^2=rho e^{y_B}, |b|^2=rho e^{-y_B}, N=2rho cosh y_B, Z_B=2rho sinh y_B, and S^2+P^2=4rho^2.


The reciprocal scaling a→e^lambda a, b→e^{-lambda}b shifts y_B by 2lambda while leaving ab, a conjugate(b), S, P, |ab| and their phase harmonics invariant. Thus any product/cross-only condensate potential, including a strict fourth-harmonic term with radial dependence |ab|^4, is exactly flat in y_B at fixed rho.


The Hermitian 8008/30380 norm is different. For q-related orthogonal same-grade 126 tensors C_± of equal positive Hermitian norm kappa_126,


C=aC_++bC_-,


and


boxed:
<C,C>_H=kappa_126(|a|^2+|b|^2)=2kappa_126 rho cosh y_B.


The previously unfixed sign of the §16.I.120 crossed N20 contact is positive in the same canonical Euclidean convention used for the A_y sign audit. With S_4=-(G_N/2)∫J_N^2, the second Wilsonian cumulant contributes one minus sign and the crossed high-fermion loop contributes a second; the +20 internal coefficient, scalar Cooper contraction and shell integral are positive. Therefore


Delta c_8008=n_c·20·G_N^2 I_shell>0


for n_c>0.


On a nonzero fixed-product branch,


V_contact=2kappa_126 Delta c_8008 rho cosh y_B,


so y_B=0 is the unique finite minimum and


K_y=V''(0)=2kappa_126 Delta c_8008 rho>0.


Thus equal amplitude |a|=|b| is dynamically pinned in the primitive microscopic normalization, conditional on a separately sustained nonzero product/radial condensate. The contact alone does not create that condensate; if rho is freely varied with no competing radial dynamics it favors rho=0.


After canonical fermion normalization, hat y_B=y_B+2delta_Z, so the contact-pinned primitive branch gives Upsilon_B=2delta_Z. Hence the positive A_y feedback of §§16.I.127–130 is physically active rather than removed by canonical balancing, and the linear response is (1-2A_y)delta_Z=A_s s+... . The one-loop regime still has A_y<<1, so the pitchfork threshold is not yet earned.

§16.I.132 — N20 Breaking of the S82 Projective Radius, Radial No-Go, and the Minimal Positive-Higher-Order Threshold


The quasi-linear S82 parent has an exact local radial symmetry C(x)→e^{sigma(x)}C(x) on s_C≠0 and makes the condensate logarithmic radius BRST-contractible. But the earned N20 Wilsonian Hermitian contact c_H<C,C>_H with c_H>0 scales as e^{2sigma} and therefore breaks that bare projective symmetry unless a compensating completion is supplied. Thus the §16.I.82 radial BRST theorem is action-relative and is now explicitly reopened by the retained matter sector.


On the §16.I.131 equal-amplitude line |a|=|b|=t,


<C,C>_H=2kappa_126 t^2,


so


V_H=mu_H^2 t^2,
mu_H^2=2kappa_126 c_H>0.


Because the normalized S82 moment-map/gravity sector is degree zero in the condensate radius, strict S82 plus the earned N20 contact has


V_rad=V_0+mu_H^2t^2,


with no finite nonzero radial stationary point. The force points toward t=0, which is precisely the projective boundary excluded from the S82 chart.


If the lawful stable-54 fourth-harmonic selector interaction is also activated, its phase minimum gives


V_4^ind=-beta_4t^4,
beta_4=lambda_2^2K_R/m_S^2>0.


Hence


V_rad=mu_H^2t^2-beta_4t^4.


The nonzero stationary point t_*^2=mu_H^2/(2beta_4) has


V''(t_*)=-4mu_H^2<0,


so it is a barrier maximum, and the truncation runs to -infinity at large t. The previously noted negative-semidefinite selector quartic therefore does not stabilize the microscopic pair radius.


A positive sextic is only a diagnostic for what a future lawful completion would need. For


x=t^2,
V=mu_H^2x-beta_4x^2+gamma_6x^3,
gamma_6>0,


nonzero extrema exist iff beta_4^2≥3gamma_6mu_H^2, with


x_±=[beta_4±sqrt(beta_4^2-3gamma_6mu_H^2)]/(3gamma_6).


The nonzero minimum becomes degenerate with the origin at


x_coex=beta_4/(2gamma_6),
mu_H^2=beta_4^2/(4gamma_6).


No +gamma_6 operator is presently earned; these are threshold diagnostics only. The earlier polynomial S76 radial witness cannot be imported into S82 without abandoning the projective action choice and reopening the full condensate-radial/BRST Hessian.


Live gate: derive a lawful positive large-radius or bounded completion from an already-required microscopic sector, preferably higher N20 Wilsonian terms, an actually declared non-Gaussian selector sector, a full-E8 higher invariant on the physical 8008/30380 corridor, or a fully re-audited polynomial S76-type parent. Do not add a free sextic merely because its sign would stabilize the toy potential.




§16.I.133 — Projective Gauge–Radius Incompatibility and the U_F-Neutral N20 Radial Tower


Let U=<C,C>_H>0. Under the bare S82 local scaling C→e^sigma C, U→e^{2sigma}U. Exact invariance of any differentiable C-only radial potential V(U) under arbitrary local sigma implies 2UV'(U)=0, so V is constant on U>0. Therefore exact projective gauge redundancy and dynamical selection of an absolute C radius are incompatible unless the symmetry is broken/deformed or a compensating field/spurion is introduced. The earned N20 contact has already broken the bare S82 scaling, so the radius is a reopened physical action direction.


The reduced vector symmetry gives q_F(C)=+2 and q_F(C^dagger)=-2, hence U is U_F neutral. It is also q even and Q_Y even. Therefore every Hermitian power O_n=U^n is a lawful reduced radial invariant. With [Psi]=3/2, [C]=3 and [U]=6, one has [O_n]=6n and coefficient dimension 4-6n. In particular U, U^2 and U^3 have dimensions 6, 12 and 18.


The N20 auxiliary field is algebraic Gaussian and has no declared cubic or quartic self-interaction. After Gaussian elimination its interaction is a four-fermion current-current vertex G_N. For a connected graph contributing to U^n, E=4n external fermion legs. With V four-fermion vertices, I internal fermion lines and L loops,


4V=E+2I,
L=I-V+1=V-2n+1.


A connected tree at V=2n-1 is not 1PI, so the leading local 1PI contribution has V=2n and L=1. Thus the leading N20 coefficient of U^n occurs at order G_N^(2n) in a one-loop 2n-vertex fermion polygon.


At large momentum the shell integral scales as


I_n~∫d^4k/(2pi)^4 1/(k^2)^n.


Hence


I_1=(Lambda^2-mu^2)/(16pi^2),
I_2=ln(Lambda^2/mu^2)/(16pi^2),
I_3=(mu^{-2}-Lambda^{-2})/(16pi^2)


in the massless shell approximation. The q-even Dirac mass cuts off the infrared behavior. Write


c_n^(N)=R_n G_N^(2n)I_n+... .


R_1 is already fixed positive. Symmetry and power counting do not determine R_2 or R_3.


On the equal-amplitude branch, the stable-54 phase selector contributes -b_2U^2 with b_2>0, so


V_rad(U)=c_1U+(c_2-b_2)U^2+c_3U^3+...,


with c_1>0. Defining B_2=b_2-c_2, a bounded nonzero minimum in the minimal Hermitian tower requires B_2>0 and c_3>0. Nonzero extrema require B_2^2≥3c_1c_3, and the nonzero minimum lies below the origin only for B_2^2>4c_1c_3.


Thus the first neutral Hermitian coefficient whose sign can close the current positive-quadratic/negative-quartic radial architecture is the dimension-18 coefficient c_3. Its invariant form (<C,C>_H)^3 is lawful, but its microscopic coefficient is not earned.


Live gate: compute the signed cyclic recouplings R_2 and R_3 in the canonical physical 126/N20 normalization. R_2 decides whether the attractive quartic survives after neutral Wilsonian dressing; R_3 decides whether the pure Gaussian N20 tower can provide the minimal bounded radial completion.

The standard downstream identities are


(1/2π) ∫₀²π exp(−iu cos φ) dφ = J₀(u),
2 ∫₀¹ r J₀(ur) dr = 2J₁(u)/u.


A candidate conic character is exp(−iuQ)=exp(−iuΔ/Σ), but it remains conditional until the compact-coordinate bridge is closed. The Airy/jinc amplitude A(u)=2J₁(u)/u is therefore a provisional descendant test, not an established Projection Craft theorem.


Next calculation: prove or falsify the exact (p,q) ↔ (Q,S) map, then push the rotational character completely backward into native UNA/Saw variables. If that requires an independent external primitive, classify the Airy correspondence as analogy rather than inheritance.




VOLUME III SYNCHRONIZATION UPDATE — SEPTEMBER 15, 2026


CORRECTED C8 NORMALIZATION BOOKKEEPING — CONTROLLING STATUS


This correction supersedes numerical closure claims made during the normalization discussion. The values c_ord=1, N_1820=2 (or a closed residual count of zero), and K_parent=1 have not been established by the ordered contraction. UPSTREAM CARRIER AND WITNESS
Book 15's Canonical Round-Trip Normalization Theorem fixes the common relative carrier scalar to 1 under its canonical UNA/Saw endpoint hypotheses. It does not fix action strength k_0=beta^2/(2 alpha), microscopic reduced coefficients, or eta_(-4).
Use F_C8=(1/2)F_0 and Q_F=P_54 Q_4(F_C8,F_C8)=(6/5)T, where T=(1,1,-1/4,...,-1/4) has eight -1/4 entries. Then <T,T>=5/2 and <Q_F,Q_F>=18/5. The certified local 1820 component is already absorbed in this witness; Q_4(F_C8,F_C8)=(1/4)Q_4(F_0,F_0). This alone does not count the projections in the complete graph.


ORDERED WITNESS READOUT
Define the stripped ordered response by
Rtilde^t_2222(F,F)=a_2222 T+R_perp, with <T,R_perp>=0.
Here stripped excludes the separately displayed contact magnitude, crossed Wick sign, m_2^(-4), parent/action factor, state defect, and charged UV datum. Actual ordered Clifford/projector factors belong inside this response.
S_F:=<Q_F,Rtilde^t_2222(F,F)>=3a_2222.
c_ord:=S_F/(18/5)=(5/18)<Q_F,Rtilde^t_2222(F,F)>.
Therefore S_F=(18/5)c_ord and a_2222=(6/5)c_ord.
These are definitions and exact conversions, not a calculation of c_ord or an assertion that the complete maps are proportional.


NEUTRAL COEFFICIENT AND CONTACT PLACEMENT
Retain the inherited coefficient convention:
nHat_54=-(sqrt(10)/1536)S_F m_2^(-4)
       =-(sqrt(10)/512)a_2222 m_2^(-4)
       =-(3sqrt(10)/1280)c_ord m_2^(-4).
Equivalently,
nHat_54=(-1)(1/256)(sqrt(10)/6)S_F m_2^(-4).
The crossed-t Wick sign -1 and the four-contact magnitude (1/4)^4=1/256 are already included. Never multiply nHat_54 by either again. The remainder sqrt(10)/6 is an arithmetic decomposition of the inherited prefactor, not a newly derived Clebsch.


ORDERED 1820 MULTIPLICITY
N_1820 remains OPEN until the actual cyclic word (E,B,E,O,E,B,E,O) is reconstructed and every projection/component extraction is identified. STATE, ACTION, AND CHARGE
W=(r_1 s_2-s_1 r_2)/2 is the primitive oriented variable.
Delta_C8=W^2/16=Delta_12^2/256, with W=Delta_12/4.
Use exactly one of these equivalent state-defect expressions. The state-conversion denominator 256 and the contact denominator 256 have different ancestry; neither substitutes for the other.
K_parent is kept outside nHat_54 and remains OPEN. Identifying it as a function of k_0 alone requires the actual parent-action matching. The formal common-action count k_0^(V-I)=1 for V=I=8 applies only if k_0 multiplies the complete relevant microscopic action uniformly; the PCBF relation alone does not establish that premise.
q_F(C_chi)=2, q_F(Y_54)=4, q_F(eta_(-4))=q_F(y_2)=-4.
Matching remains schematic: y_2 proportional to eta_(-4) K_parent nHat_54. A corresponding matrix element carries Delta_C8 separately with the matched tensor. Neutral recoupling and W cannot supply the missing charge.


ADJUNCTION FIREWALL AND NEXT GATE
For an isometric channel inclusion J, J^sharp J=I removes an intermediate basis ambiguity. It does not imply J^sharp C_t J=I for the actual ordered contraction C_t. Hermiticity, equivariance, or multiplicity one alone cannot set c_ord=1.
